# file opened: zpolice.asm
   1  0000              ;==============================================================================
   2  0000              ; Z-POLICE  -  3D vector city flight for the ZX Spectrum Next
   3  0000              ;
   4  0000              ;  Z80N assembler (sjasmplus --zxnext).  Layer 2 256x192x8bpp, double buffered.
   5  0000              ;  The 3D viewport is the top 256x128; the bottom third is an instrument panel.
   6  0000              ;  Maths: world positions are 16-bit integers (one city cell = 256 units),
   7  0000              ;         trig values are S7.8 fixed point (1.0 = $0100).
   8  0000              ;  Hidden line removal: painter's algorithm. Buildings are walked over the
   9  0000              ;  grid far-to-near (no sort needed). Each visible face is filled in its
  10  0000              ;  shade (roof lighter, side turned away darker) and optionally outlined, so
  11  0000              ;  nearer buildings overwrite those behind them. Faces with every corner on
  12  0000              ;  screen use cached projections; faces crossing the view frustum are
  13  0000              ;  clipped in 3D (Sutherland-Hodgman, 5 planes). Rasterisation then only
  14  0000              ;  ever sees on-screen 8-bit coordinates.
  15  0000              ;
  16  0000              ;  Controls: Q/A speed up/down   Z air brake O/P turn left/right
  17  0000              ;            W/S climb/descend   E/D nose up/down
  18  0000              ;            SPACE fire          1-5 select weapon slot
  19  0000              ;            0 change the centre slot's weapon
  20  0000              ;
  21  0000              ;  Source files: zpolice.asm (resident main code), memory_bank_defs.asm
  22  0000              ;  (slots and pages).  Game logic pages, swapped into slot 6 at $C000:
  23  0000              ;  player.asm (input/movement), weapons.asm, menu.asm (main menu).  Core
  24  0000              ;  function pages, swapped into slot 7 at $E000: city.asm (Layer 2
  25  0000              ;  drawing), hud.asm (ULA HUD), input.asm (redefinable keys, joysticks), sprites.asm
  26  0000              ;  (hardware sprites).  Always mapped: zombie.asm at $2000, human.asm and
  27  0000              ;  map.asm (the city map) at $7000.
  28  0000              ;
  29  0000              ;  Memory while running:
  30  0000              ;    $0000-$1FFF  Layer 2 back buffer, one 8K section at a time (slot 0)
  31  0000              ;    $2000-$3FFF  zombie page (slot 1)
  32  0000              ;    $4000-$7FFF  occlusion data; cell colours, humans, map (slots 2-3)
  33  0000              ;    $8000-$9FFF  ULA screen (slot 4)
  34  0000              ;    $A000-$BFFF  resident code, tables, variables, stack (slot 5)
  35  0000              ;    $C000-$DFFF  game logic page (slot 6)
  36  0000              ;    $E000-$FFFF  core function page (slot 7)
  37  0000              ;==============================================================================
  38  0000
  39  0000                      DEVICE ZXSPECTRUMNEXT
  40  0000                      SLDOPT COMMENT WPMEM, LOGPOINT, ASSERTION
  41  0000                      CSPECTMAP zpolice.map
  42  0000                      OPT --zxnext=cspect --syntax=a --reversepop
  43  0000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  44  0000              ; Build options
  45  0000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  46  0000              ; DRAW_LINES: outline building faces with EDGE_COL vectors.  Comment it out for solid-colour buildings only; the HUD
  47  0000              ; is always drawn.  Can also be set from the command line: -DDRAW_LINES
  48  0000                      ;DEFINE DRAW_LINES
  49  0000
  50  0000              ; WAIT_FOR_VBLANK: wait for the raster to leave the display area before
  51  0000              ; flipping the Layer 2 buffers (no tearing).  Comment it out to flip as soon
  52  0000              ; as a frame is drawn: faster, but the flip may tear mid-screen.
  53  0000              ; Command line: -DWAIT_FOR_VBLANK
  54  0000                      ;DEFINE WAIT_FOR_VBLANK
  55  0000
  56  0000              ; DRAW_FPS: measure frames per second from the raster (vtick) and show it at
  57  0000              ; the top left of the ULA screen.  Comment it out to drop the measuring and
  58  0000              ; the display.  Command line: -DDRAW_FPS
  59  0000                      ;DEFINE DRAW_FPS
  60  0000
  61  0000              ; SKY_IS_WALL: the camera can never rise above the perimeter wall, so fill
  62  0000              ; the sky in the wall's colour and draw wall cells only up to camera height
  63  0000              ; (their top edge is then exactly the horizon).  No sky is ever seen.
  64  0000              ; Command line: -DSKY_IS_WALL
  65  0000                      DEFINE SKY_IS_WALL
  66  0000
  67  0000              ; OCCLUSION_CULL: in level flight, record the buildings, then cull those
  68  0000              ; completely hidden behind nearer ones (a per-column cover buffer, front to
  69  0000              ; back) before drawing the rest.  Uses pages PAGE_OCC0/1 at $4000-$7FFF.
  70  0000              ; Command line: -DOCCLUSION_CULL
  71  0000                      DEFINE OCCLUSION_CULL
  72  0000
  73  0000              ; LEVEL_FAST_PATH: while pitch is 0 (level flight) a building's top corners
  74  0000              ; have the same camera X and Z as the bottom corners below them, so each top
  75  0000              ; corner reuses its bottom corner's screen x and 1/Z and only works out its
  76  0000              ; screen y.  Comment it out to project every corner in full.
  77  0000              ; Command line: -DLEVEL_FAST_PATH
  78  0000                      DEFINE LEVEL_FAST_PATH
  79  0000
  80  0000                      INCLUDE "memory_bank_defs.asm"  ; MMU slots, registers, addresses, pages
# file opened: memory_bank_defs.asm
   1+ 0000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ 0000              ; Z-POLICE - memory bank definitions
   3+ 0000              ;
   4+ 0000              ; The Next maps its RAM into the Z80's 64K as eight 8K MMU slots, each set by
   5+ 0000              ; a NextReg ($50-$57) to an 8K page (page n = half of 16K bank n/2).  This
   6+ 0000              ; file names the slots, their registers and addresses, what each slot is
   7+ 0000              ; used for, and which page holds what.  Use:
   8+ 0000              ;   nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_CITY            ; page the city code in at $E000
   9+ 0000              ;   MMU CODE_SLOT e, PAGE_CITY, CODE_ADDR ; (sjasmplus) assemble into it
  10+ 0000              ;
  11+ 0000              ; Memory map (see README.md):
  12+ 0000              ;   slot 0 $0000  Layer 2 sections (32 rows each) while drawing; the sine
  13+ 0000              ;                 table (PAGE_SIN) whenever get_sin reads it
  14+ 0000              ;   slot 1 $2000  zombie page, always mapped: zombies, humans, the HUD
  15+ 0000              ;                 map, the IM 2 table
  16+ 0000              ;   slot 2 $4000  occlusion data
  17+ 0000              ;   slot 3 $6000  the city bank (always mapped): city_col at $6000,
  18+ 0000              ;                 city_map at $7000, loaded from the level's city file
  19+ 0000              ;   slot 4 $8000  ULA screen (page 10, the first half of bank 5)
  20+ 0000              ;   slot 5 $A000  resident main code, variables, stack (page 11, the unused
  21+ 0000              ;                 second half of bank 5)
  22+ 0000              ;   slot 6 $C000  game logic pages, swapped: player, weapons, menu, level
  23+ 0000              ;                 loading
  24+ 0000              ;   slot 7 $E000  core function pages, swapped: city (Layer 2 drawing), HUD,
  25+ 0000              ;                 keys, sprites; a sprite bank while it is loaded; the
  26+ 0000              ;                 start-up set-up (initialise.asm) once
  27+ 0000              ; So game logic in slot 6 can call any core page in slot 7: main_loop pages
  28+ 0000              ; the keys in at $E000 while the player and weapons pages run at $C000.
  29+ 0000              ; At start-up the NEX loader's default mapping is in place (slot 6 = page
  30+ 0000              ; 0), so the entry point is a stub in PAGE_BOOT at $C000 (boot, zpolice.asm)
  31+ 0000              ; that maps the resident page into slot 5 and jumps to start.
  32+ 0000              ;==============================================================================
  33+ 0000
  34+ 0000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  35+ 0000              ; MMU slots: number, NextReg, address
  36+ 0000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  37+ 0000              MEMBANK_SIZE            equ $2000       ; 8K a slot / page
  38+ 0000
  39+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_0              equ 0
  40+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_1              equ 1
  41+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_2              equ 2
  42+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_3              equ 3
  43+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_4              equ 4
  44+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_5              equ 5
  45+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_6              equ 6
  46+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_7              equ 7
  47+ 0000
  48+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_0_REGISTER     equ $50
  49+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_1_REGISTER     equ $51
  50+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_2_REGISTER     equ $52
  51+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_3_REGISTER     equ $53
  52+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_4_REGISTER     equ $54
  53+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_5_REGISTER     equ $55
  54+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_6_REGISTER     equ $56
  55+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_7_REGISTER     equ $57
  56+ 0000
  57+ 0000              membank0                equ $0000
  58+ 0000              membank1                equ $2000
  59+ 0000              membank2                equ $4000
  60+ 0000              membank3                equ $6000
  61+ 0000              membank4                equ $8000
  62+ 0000              membank5                equ $A000
  63+ 0000              membank6                equ $C000
  64+ 0000              membank7                equ $E000
  65+ 0000
  66+ 0000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  67+ 0000              ; What each slot is used for: <use>_SLOT, <use>_MMU (its NextReg) and
  68+ 0000              ; <use>_ADDR (the ORG of code or data assembled for it)
  69+ 0000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  70+ 0000              ; slot 0: one 32-row section of a Layer 2 buffer at a time (seg_map etc.)
  71+ 0000              L2SEC_SLOT              equ MMU_SLOT_0
  72+ 0000              L2SEC_MMU               equ MMU_SLOT_0_REGISTER
  73+ 0000              L2SEC_ADDR              equ membank0
  74+ 0000              ; slot 0 also: the sine table, paged in by get_sin (resident).  Only the
  75+ 0000              ; game logic calls it, never the drawing, and every Layer 2 writer maps its
  76+ 0000              ; own section first, so the two never meet.  Nothing may write to
  77+ 0000              ; $0000-$1FFF while it is mapped.
  78+ 0000              SIN_SLOT                equ MMU_SLOT_0
  79+ 0000              SIN_MMU                 equ MMU_SLOT_0_REGISTER
  80+ 0000              SIN_ADDR                equ membank0
  81+ 0000
  82+ 0000              ; slot 1: zombie.asm, human.asm, map.asm, interrupts.asm, always mapped
  83+ 0000              ZOMBIE_SLOT             equ MMU_SLOT_1
  84+ 0000              ZOMBIE_MMU              equ MMU_SLOT_1_REGISTER
  85+ 0000              ZOMBIE_ADDR             equ membank1
  86+ 0000
  87+ 0000              ; slot 2: occlusion buffer, draw list and building records (OCCLUSION_CULL)
  88+ 0000              OCC_SLOT                equ MMU_SLOT_2
  89+ 0000              OCC_MMU                 equ MMU_SLOT_2_REGISTER
  90+ 0000              OCC_ADDR                equ membank2
  91+ 0000
  92+ 0000              ; slot 3: the city bank, always mapped: city_col at $6000, city_map at
  93+ 0000              ; $7000, read from the level's city file (level_load.asm) in one go
  94+ 0000              TABLES_SLOT             equ MMU_SLOT_3
  95+ 0000              TABLES_MMU              equ MMU_SLOT_3_REGISTER
  96+ 0000              TABLES_ADDR             equ membank3            ; city_col
  97+ 0000              CITYMAP_ADDR            equ membank3+$1000      ; city_map
  98+ 0000
  99+ 0000              ; slot 4: the ULA screen (bank 5's first 8K: bitmap and attributes)
 100+ 0000              ULA_SLOT                equ MMU_SLOT_4
 101+ 0000              ULA_MMU                 equ MMU_SLOT_4_REGISTER
 102+ 0000              ULA_ADDR                equ membank4
 103+ 0000
 104+ 0000              ; slot 5: resident main code (zpolice.asm), tables, variables, stack
 105+ 0000              MAIN_SLOT               equ MMU_SLOT_5
 106+ 0000              MAIN_MMU                equ MMU_SLOT_5_REGISTER
 107+ 0000              MAIN_ADDR               equ membank5
 108+ 0000
 109+ 0000              ; slot 6: game logic pages, swapped (player, weapons, menu)
 110+ 0000              LOGIC_SLOT              equ MMU_SLOT_6
 111+ 0000              LOGIC_MMU               equ MMU_SLOT_6_REGISTER
 112+ 0000              LOGIC_ADDR              equ membank6
 113+ 0000              ; slot 6 at start-up: the boot stub (the NEX loader maps page 0 there)
 114+ 0000              BOOT_SLOT               equ MMU_SLOT_6
 115+ 0000              BOOT_ADDR               equ membank6
 116+ 0000
 117+ 0000              ; slot 7: core function pages, swapped (city / Layer 2, HUD, keys, sprites)
 118+ 0000              CODE_SLOT               equ MMU_SLOT_7
 119+ 0000              CODE_MMU                equ MMU_SLOT_7_REGISTER
 120+ 0000              CODE_ADDR               equ membank7
 121+ 0000              ; slot 7 also: a sprite pattern bank (sprite_data.asm), paged in to run
 122+ 0000              ; its own loader, spr_load_bank
 123+ 0000              SPRBANK_SLOT            equ MMU_SLOT_7
 124+ 0000              SPRBANK_MMU             equ MMU_SLOT_7_REGISTER
 125+ 0000              SPRBANK_ADDR            equ membank7
 126+ 0000
 127+ 0000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 128+ 0000              ; 8K pages
 129+ 0000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 130+ 0000              PAGE_BOOT               equ 0           ; the NEX entry stub (slot 6 at load)
 131+ 0000              PAGE_CITY               equ 1           ; city.asm: Layer 2 drawing (slot 7)
 132+ 0000              PAGE_HUD                equ 2           ; hud.asm: ULA (Layer 1) HUD (slot 7)
 133+ 0000              PAGE_PLAYER             equ 3           ; player.asm: input, movement,
 134+ 0000                                                      ; collision (slot 6)
 135+ 0000              PAGE_WEAPONS            equ 4           ; weapons.asm: player weapons (slot 6)
 136+ 0000              PAGE_ZOMBIE             equ 5           ; zombie.asm (slot 1, always)
 137+ 0000              PAGE_OCC0               equ 6           ; occlusion buffer, draw list and
 138+ 0000                                                      ; building records (slot 2)
 139+ 0000              PAGE_OCC1               equ 7           ; the city bank: city_col and
 140+ 0000                                                      ; city_map (slot 3, always)
 141+ 0000              PAGE_MENU               equ 8           ; menu.asm: main menu (slot 6)
 142+ 0000              PAGE_SPR0               equ 9           ; sprite_data.asm: sprite pattern
 143+ 0000                                                      ; bank 0 (slot 7 while loading);
 144+ 0000                                                      ; more banks: the next free pages
 145+ 0000              PAGE_ULA0               equ 10          ; the ULA screen, bank 5 (slot 4)
 146+ 0000              PAGE_MAIN               equ 11          ; zpolice.asm, resident (slot 5): the
 147+ 0000                                                      ; second half of bank 5
 148+ 0000              PAGE_INPUT              equ 12          ; input.asm: keyboard, joysticks (slot 7)
 149+ 0000              PAGE_SPRITES            equ 13          ; sprites.asm: hardware sprites
 150+ 0000                                                      ; (slot 7)
 151+ 0000              PAGE_SIN                equ 14          ; sin_tables.asm: the sine table
 152+ 0000                                                      ; (slot 0, read by get_sin)
 153+ 0000              PAGE_INIT               equ 15          ; initialise.asm: start-up set-up,
 154+ 0000                                                      ; initgame (slot 7, once)
 155+ 0000              PAGE_FILE               equ 16          ; file_handler.asm: SD card loading
 156+ 0000                                                      ; and saving (slot 7)
 157+ 0000              PAGE_AUDIO              equ 17          ; audio.asm: sound set-up (slot 7)
 158+ 0000              PAGE_SPECIAL            equ 31          ; specials.asm: special monsters
 159+ 0000                                                      ; (slot 6)
 160+ 0000              PAGE_LOADOUT            equ 32          ; loadout.asm: the load-out screen
 161+ 0000                                                      ; between levels (slot 6)
 162+ 0000              PAGE_KEYDEF             equ 33          ; keydef.asm: the redefine keys
 163+ 0000                                                      ; screen (slot 6)
 164+ 0000              PAGE_LEVEL              equ 30          ; level_load.asm: level and city
 165+ 0000                                                      ; files (slot 6); pages 18-29 are
 166+ 0000                                                      ; the Layer 2 buffers
 167+ 0000
 168+ 0000              ; Layer 2 double buffer, in 16K banks (NextReg $12 / $13 take banks; the
 169+ 0000              ; pages are bank * 2).  Layer 2 is clipped to 192 rows, so each buffer
 170+ 0000              ; uses three banks.
 171+ 0000              L2_BANK_A               equ 9           ; 16K banks 9-11 (pages 18-23)
 172+ 0000              L2_BANK_B               equ 12          ; 16K banks 12-14 (pages 24-29)
 173+ 0000              PAGE_L2_A               equ L2_BANK_A*2
 174+ 0000              PAGE_L2_B               equ L2_BANK_B*2
 175+ 0000
 176+ 0000              PAGE_ROM                equ $FF         ; MMU value: ROM in slots 0-1
 177+ 0000
# file closed: memory_bank_defs.asm
  81  0000
  82  0000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  83  0000              ; Tuning constants
  84  0000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  85  0000              ; Scale: one map tile (cell) is 20 m x 20 m = 256 world units, and one
  86  0000              ; height unit (building heights, the ceiling, the pad) is 10 m = 128 world
  87  0000              ; units.  World units are the same size on every axis, so 1 m = 12.8 units
  88  0000              ; and 1 unit = 7.8 cm.  The city is 64 x 64 tiles = 1280 m.
  89  0000              ; Speeds are per rendered frame, so real speed depends on the frame rate
  90  0000              ; (figures below are at 14 fps).
  91  0000              GRID            equ 64          ; the city is GRID x GRID tiles (gentables.py)
  92  0000              ; A city_map byte: bits 0-4 the cell code (0 road, 1-15 building height
  93  0000              ; level, PAD_CODE, TERRACE_CODE, VOID_CODE: out of bounds), bit 6 set while a human waits in the cell
  94  0000              ; (human.asm, hum_flags), bit 7 while a zombie or special monster stands in
  95  0000              ; it (zombie.asm, zflags; its type is in its record).  Bit 5 is free.  So
  96  0000              ; every reader masks with CELL_CODE before testing for road or a code.
  97  0000              CELL_CODE       equ %00011111
  98  0000              CELL_FLAGS      equ %11100000       ; (the marks, kept when the code changes)
  99  0000              CELL_HUMAN_B    equ 6
 100  0000              CELL_ZOMBIE_B   equ 7
 101  0000              CELL_HUMAN      equ 1<<CELL_HUMAN_B
 102  0000              CELL_ZOMBIE     equ 1<<CELL_ZOMBIE_B
 103  0000              TILE_M          equ 20          ; metres per tile (reference scale)
 104  0000              TILE_UNITS      equ 256         ; world units per tile
 105  0000              HEIGHT_M        equ 10          ; metres per height unit
 106  0000              HEIGHT_UNITS    equ 128         ; world units per height unit
 107  0000              NEAR            equ 16          ; near clip plane: 1.25 m
 108  0000              VIEW_R          equ 6           ; most draw distance: buildings within +/- 6
 109  0000                                              ; tiles (120 m).  Each level sets its own,
 110  0000                                              ; 2..VIEW_R (view_r; night, fog), and the
 111  0000                                              ; street dots' radius one less (dot_r)
 112  0000              PMAX            equ 24          ; pitch limit (256 = 360 deg) - match gentables.py
 113  0000              SPEED_MAX       equ 28          ; units/frame: 2.2 m/frame, ~31 m/s (110 km/h)
 114  0000              SPEED_MIN       equ -6          ; reverse: 0.47 m/frame, ~6.6 m/s
 115  0000              YAW_RATE        equ $0180       ; turn per frame, 8.8 fixed (1.5 units;
 116  0000                                              ; 256 units = 360 degrees)
 117  0000              YAW_FINE        equ 256*256/360 ; the first frame of a turn: 1 degree
 118  0000                                              ; (182: 0.711 units, 8.8 fixed)
 119  0000              CLIMB_RATE      equ 4           ; units/frame: 0.31 m/frame, ~4.4 m/s
 120  0000              SPEED_STEP      equ 1           ; Q / A: speed change a frame, units/frame
 121  0000                                              ; (1.1 m/s; player.asm uses inc / dec)
 122  0000                      ASSERT SPEED_STEP == 1
 123  0000              ; Air brake (Z): the speed falls by twice the Q / A step every frame, to 0
 124  0000              AIRBRAKE_STEP   equ 2*SPEED_STEP    ; 2 units/frame (2.2 m/s)
 125  0000              ; Booster module (a salvage reward, fitted at once: boost_fitted).  The
 126  0000              ; boost control (K_BOOST, X) puts the speed straight to BOOST_SPEED, twice
 127  0000              ; SPEED_MAX, if going forwards at BOOST_MIN or more with fuel left; again,
 128  0000              ; or out of fuel, puts back the speed it had (boost_spd).  Q, A and Z don't
 129  0000              ; change the speed while boosting.  Each boosted frame burns 1 fuel
 130  0000              ; (boost_fuel, at most BOOST_FUEL_MAX); landed on the pad it refuels 1 a
 131  0000              ; frame (pad_service).  HUD: the B gauge, and the speed bar amber.
 132  0000              BOOST_SPEED     equ 2*SPEED_MAX     ; 56 units/frame
 133  0000              BOOST_MIN       equ SPEED_MAX/2     ; 14: at least half speed, forwards
 134  0000              BOOST_FUEL_MAX  equ 200
 135  0000                      ASSERT BOOST_SPEED < 128 && BOOST_FUEL_MAX < 256
 136  0000              ; Pitch damper: with neither E nor D held, the nose eases back to level.
 137  0000              ; Every PDAMP_FRAMES frames the 8.8 pitch moves towards 0 by
 138  0000              ; max(PDAMP_MIN, |pitch| * PDAMP_K) in 1/256ths of a pitch unit.  With
 139  0000              ; PDAMP_K 0 that is a steady PDAMP_MIN a frame: half a pitch unit, half the
 140  0000              ; rate of the nose keys (1 unit a frame), however far it is from level.  (A
 141  0000              ; PDAMP_K above 0 adds a share of what is left, faster when far off: 43, as
 142  0000              ; it was, returned up to 4 units a frame from full pitch.)
 143  0000              PDAMP_FRAMES    equ 1           ; frames between damping steps
 144  0000              PDAMP_K         equ 0           ; no share: a steady rate
 145  0000              PDAMP_MIN       equ 128         ; 128/256: half a pitch unit a frame
 146  0000              MIN_Y           equ 8           ; lowest flight: 0.6 m
 147  0000              MAX_Y           equ 3*HEIGHT_UNITS/2  ; ceiling 1.5 height units: 15 m
 148  0000              BLD_MAX_H       equ 7*HEIGHT_UNITS  ; tallest building 7.0 height units: 70 m
 149  0000                                              ; (htab level 15, gentables.py; 4.7 m a level)
 150  0000              WORLD_MIN       equ 64
 151  0000              WORLD_MAX       equ GRID*256-64
 152  0000              DMA_SPAN_MIN    equ 20          ; spans this wide or wider are filled by DMA
 153  0000              ; The VTOL (the player's craft): 10 m long by 3 m wide.  Its position
 154  0000              ; (cam_x, cam_z, the camera) is its centre; the hit box against buildings is
 155  0000              ; that rectangle turned to the heading (vtol_ground), and the camera stays
 156  0000              ; COLL_H above any roof under it.
 157  0000              VTOL_LEN_M      equ 10
 158  0000              VTOL_WID_M      equ 3
 159  0000              VTOL_HL         equ VTOL_LEN_M*TILE_UNITS/(TILE_M*2)  ; half length: 64 units
 160  0000              VTOL_HW         equ VTOL_WID_M*TILE_UNITS/(TILE_M*2)  ; half width: 19 units
 161  0000              COLL_H          equ 12          ; clearance above a roof: 0.94 m
 162  0000
 163  0000              ; Weapons: 5 slots, 2 bytes each (type, ammo) in wpn_slots.  Slot 0 is the
 164  0000              ; centre gun; slots 1-4 are the wing pylon hard points (limited ammo).
 165  0000              WPN_SLOTS       equ 5
 166  0000              WPN_CENTRE      equ 0           ; slot index of the centre gun
 167  0000              WPN_PYLON1      equ 1           ; first of the 4 wing pylons (slots 1-4)
 168  0000              WPN_TYPE        equ 0           ; offset of the type byte in a slot
 169  0000              WPN_AMMO        equ 1           ; offset of the ammo counter in a slot
 170  0000              AMMO_INF        equ 255         ; ammo counter 255 = infinite ammo (never
 171  0000                                              ; decremented); 0 = empty
 172  0000              ; weapon types (placeholders until the weapons exist)
 173  0000              WPN_NONE        equ 0           ; empty slot / nothing fitted
 174  0000              WPN_GUN         equ 1           ; machine gun (centre slot)
 175  0000              WPN_LASER       equ 2           ; pulse laser (centre slot)
 176  0000              WPN_CHAIN       equ 3           ; chain gun (centre slot)
 177  0000              WPN_ROCKETS     equ 4           ; unguided rocket pod (wing pylons)
 178  0000              WPN_MISSILE     equ 5           ; missile pod (wing pylons)
 179  0000              WPN_BOMB        equ 6           ; bomb (placeholder)
 180  0000              WPN_BOFORS      equ 7           ; Bofors cannon (wing pylons)
 181  0000              WPN_PROX        equ 8           ; proximity cannon (wing pylons)
 182  0000              WPN_LAST        equ WPN_PROX    ; (the highest type: the tables by type)
 183  0000              ; Weapon type details: wpn_cfg, WCFG_SIZE bytes per type (wpn_cfg_ptr)
 184  0000              WCFG_LOAD       equ 0           ; full load (AMMO_INF: infinite)
 185  0000              WCFG_RELOAD     equ 1           ; frames to reload a shot on the pad (0: none)
 186  0000              WCFG_SLOTS      equ 2           ; the slots it may be fitted in: bit n =
 187  0000                                              ; slot n (0 = the centre slot, key 1)
 188  0000              WCFG_SIZE       equ 3
 189  0000              WS_CENTRE       equ %00001      ; slot masks: the centre slot (key 1),
 190  0000              WS_PYLONS       equ %11110      ;   the wing pylons (keys 2-5),
 191  0000              WS_ANY          equ %11111      ;   any slot
 192  0000
 193  0000              ; Machine gun rounds: NBULLETS records of BREC bytes in `bullets`
 194  0000              NBULLETS        equ 24          ; rounds in flight (the chain gun needs most)
 195  0000              BREC            equ 27
 196  0000              B_STATE         equ 0           ; 0 free, 1 in flight, 2 hit (shown, then freed),
 197  0000                                              ; 3 laser beam; bit 7: fresh (fired this frame)
 198  0000              B_X             equ 1           ; position, world units
 199  0000              B_Y             equ 3
 200  0000              B_Z             equ 5
 201  0000              B_SIN           equ 7           ; heading sinY, cosY (S7.8) when fired
 202  0000              B_COS           equ 9
 203  0000              B_SLOPE         equ 11          ; tan(pitch) when fired (S7.8)
 204  0000              B_S             equ 13          ; horizontal distance per frame
 205  0000              B_PX            equ 15          ; start of this frame's path (drawn from here
 206  0000              B_PY            equ 17          ; to the position; the muzzle on its first)
 207  0000              B_PZ            equ 19
 208  0000              B_TDX           equ 21          ; path distance between x / z cell boundaries
 209  0000              B_TDZ           equ 23          ; (0 = not worked out yet)
 210  0000              B_DMG           equ 25          ; damage to a zombie it hits directly
 211  0000              B_KIND          equ 26          ; 0 = round or beam, BK_ROCKET = rocket,
 212  0000              BK_ROCKET       equ 1           ; BK_MISSILE = missile (both burst)
 213  0000              BK_MISSILE      equ 2
 214  0000              BK_BOFORS       equ 3           ; a Bofors shell: drawn as a round, bursts
 215  0000              BK_PROX         equ 4           ; a proximity shell: as Bofors, and bursts
 216  0000                                              ; passing near a monster (PROX_R)
 217  0000              NOMINAL_FPS     equ 14          ; frame rate the per-frame speeds assume
 218  0000              BULLET_MS       equ 500         ; muzzle velocity, m/s
 219  0000              BULLET_STEP     equ BULLET_MS*TILE_UNITS/(TILE_M*NOMINAL_FPS)  ; 457 units/frame
 220  0000              GUN_DY          equ -6          ; muzzle 0.5 m below the camera
 221  0000              GUN_INTERVAL    equ 4           ; machine gun: frames between rounds
 222  0000              ; Chain gun: 4x the machine gun's rate, one round every CHAIN_INTERVAL
 223  0000              ; frames.  Each round's heading and climb are turned by a random
 224  0000              ; -CHAIN_DEV..+CHAIN_DEV steps of 1/256 rad (0.22 deg): +/-2 degrees.
 225  0000              CHAIN_INTERVAL  equ 1           ; chain gun: frames between rounds
 226  0000              ; Centre gun tilt (gun_tilt, signed, 256 = 360 deg): R / F tilt the centre
 227  0000              ; slot's weapon up / down GUN_TILT_STEP a frame, at most GUN_TILT_UP up
 228  0000              ; (11: 15.5 deg, the nearest step to 15) and GUN_TILT_DN down (32: 45 deg);
 229  0000              ; V brings it back to the centre at twice the rate.  Its rounds (and laser)
 230  0000              ; fire at pitch + tilt; the gunsight moves to show where (sight_y).
 231  0000              GUN_TILT_STEP   equ 1           ; 1.4 deg a frame
 232  0000              GUN_TILT_UP     equ 11
 233  0000              GUN_TILT_DN     equ 32
 234  0000              GUN_CTR_STEP    equ 2*GUN_TILT_STEP
 235  0000              SIGHT_MARGIN    equ 6           ; the gunsight's arms stay in the view
 236  0000              ; Integrity panel (hud.asm draw_integrity): under the weapon slots,
 237  0000              ; "INTEGRITY" on character row INT_ROW, the VTOL's hit points as a
 238  0000              ; percentage below it (INT_ROW+1)
 239  0000              INT_ROW         equ 21
 240  0000              INT_COL         equ 11              ; the label's column (under the slots)
 241  0000              INT_PCT_COL     equ 12              ; "nnn%" from this column
 242  0000              INT_WARN        equ 50              ; below this percentage: amber,
 243  0000              INT_DANGER      equ 25              ;   below this: red (else green)
 244  0000              CHAIN_DEV       equ 9           ; spread: 9/256 rad = 2.01 degrees
 245  0000              ; Pulse laser: the beam is on for LASER_ON frames (0.1 s), then cools for
 246  0000              ; LASER_COOL frames (0.25 s) before it can fire again; holding SPACE repeats.
 247  0000              ; (Frame counts as specified; at 14 fps they last 0.21 s and 0.57 s.)  Each
 248  0000              ; beam frame it is traced from the muzzle along the view direction to the
 249  0000              ; first thing it meets, at most LASER_RANGE away.
 250  0000              LASER_ON        equ 3           ; beam frames
 251  0000              LASER_COOL      equ 8           ; cool-down frames
 252  0000              LASER_RANGE     equ 1536        ; 120 m (the draw distance)
 253  0000              ZHIT_R          equ 3           ; zombie hit box: 0.5 m wide (half 3.2 units)
 254  0000              ZHIT_H          equ 26          ; and 2 m tall
 255  0000
 256  0000              ; Damage, in hit points (zombies: ZHP / ZHP_FAST in zombie.asm; the base
 257  0000              ; tiles TERRACE_HP / PAD_HP; humans HUMAN_HP)
 258  0000              GUN_DMG         equ 4           ; machine gun, per round
 259  0000              CHAIN_DMG       equ 2           ; chain gun, per round
 260  0000              LASER_DMG       equ 1           ; pulse laser, per beam frame on a target
 261  0000
 262  0000              ; Rocket pod (WPN_ROCKETS, on a wing pylon): ROCKET_SHOTS rockets, one every
 263  0000              ; ROCKET_INTERVAL frames while SPACE is held, flying straight along the view
 264  0000              ; direction at 150 m/s.  Where one hits anything (a building, the ground, a
 265  0000              ; zombie) it bursts: BLAST_DMG to every zombie, human and base tile within
 266  0000              ; BLAST_R on each of the x, y and z axes (a box, not a sphere: cheaper, and
 267  0000              ; near enough).  A direct hit does no extra damage.
 268  0000              ROCKET_SHOTS    equ 12          ; a full pod
 269  0000              ROCKET_MS       equ 150         ; m/s
 270  0000              ROCKET_STEP     equ ROCKET_MS*TILE_UNITS/(TILE_M*NOMINAL_FPS)  ; 137 units/frame
 271  0000              ROCKET_INTERVAL equ 7           ; frames between rockets (0.5 s)
 272  0000              ROCKET_LEN      equ 13          ; drawn 1 m long (white),
 273  0000              ROCKET_TRAIL    equ 19          ; then a yellow trail to 1.5 m behind it
 274  0000              BLAST_R         equ 64          ; 5 m
 275  0000              BLAST_DMG       equ 20
 276  0000              BLAST_PARTS     equ 10          ; its burst: orange particles (FIRE_LIFE frames)
 277  0000
 278  0000              ; Missile pod (WPN_MISSILE, on a wing pylon): MISSILE_SHOTS missiles, fired
 279  0000              ; and flying as rockets but at half the speed (75 m/s).  The burst is bigger:
 280  0000              ; MISSILE_DMG to everything within MISSILE_R (1.0 tile) on each of the x, y
 281  0000              ; and z axes, and MISSILE_PARTS orange particles.
 282  0000              MISSILE_SHOTS   equ 4           ; a full pod
 283  0000              MISSILE_MS      equ ROCKET_MS/2 ; m/s
 284  0000              MISSILE_STEP    equ MISSILE_MS*TILE_UNITS/(TILE_M*NOMINAL_FPS)  ; 68 units/frame
 285  0000              MISSILE_INTERVAL equ ROCKET_INTERVAL    ; frames between missiles
 286  0000              MISSILE_R       equ TILE_UNITS  ; 1.0 tile (20 m)
 287  0000              MISSILE_DMG     equ 15
 288  0000              MISSILE_PARTS   equ 20
 289  0000
 290  0000              ; Bofors cannon (WPN_BOFORS, on a wing pylon): BOFORS_SHOTS shells, fired as
 291  0000              ; the machine gun's rounds (500 m/s, drawn as tracers) at a quarter of its
 292  0000              ; rate.  Each shell bursts where it hits anything: BOFORS_DMG to everything
 293  0000              ; within BOFORS_R (3 m) on each axis, and BOFORS_PARTS orange particles.
 294  0000              BOFORS_SHOTS    equ 50          ; a full magazine
 295  0000              BOFORS_INTERVAL equ GUN_INTERVAL*4      ; frames between shells (16)
 296  0000              BOFORS_R        equ 3*TILE_UNITS/TILE_M ; 3 m: 38 units
 297  0000              BOFORS_DMG      equ 4
 298  0000              BOFORS_PARTS    equ 3
 299  0000
 300  0000              ; Proximity cannon (WPN_PROX, on a wing pylon): the Bofors cannon, but its
 301  0000              ; shells also burst when they pass within PROX_R (2 m) of a zombie or
 302  0000              ; special monster, on each axis from its x, z and feet (cell_zombies: the
 303  0000              ; burst's own box, so the monster is always in it), where the shell comes
 304  0000              ; nearest it.  The burst: PROX_DMG within PROX_R on each axis.
 305  0000              PROX_SHOTS      equ BOFORS_SHOTS
 306  0000              PROX_INTERVAL   equ BOFORS_INTERVAL
 307  0000              PROX_R          equ 2*TILE_UNITS/TILE_M ; 2 m: 25 units
 308  0000              PROX_DMG        equ 3
 309  0000              PROX_PARTS      equ BOFORS_PARTS
 310  0000
 311  0000              ; Landed on the base pad (vtol_landed, the cell under it PAD_CODE) the VTOL
 312  0000              ; is repaired and its pods reloaded (pad_service, weapons.asm): 1 hit point
 313  0000              ; every REPAIR_FRAMES frames up to VTOL_HP, and each slot's weapon 1 shot
 314  0000              ; every its reload frames (wpn_cfg, WCFG_RELOAD) up to its full load.
 315  0000              REPAIR_FRAMES   equ 5
 316  0000              ROCKET_RELOAD   equ 15          ; frames a rocket
 317  0000              MISSILE_RELOAD  equ 30          ; frames a missile
 318  0000              BOFORS_RELOAD   equ 7           ; frames a Bofors shell
 319  0000              PROX_RELOAD     equ BOFORS_RELOAD   ; and a proximity shell

 321  0000              ; Humans to rescue (human.asm)
 322  0000              HUMAN_MAX       equ 20          ; room for this many
 323  0000              HUMANS_INIT     equ 5           ; placed at start
 324  0000              PASSENGER_MAX   equ 5           ; the craft carries this many
 325  0000              RESCUE_R        equ 512         ; 2.0 tiles (40 m): a landed craft this close
 326  0000              HUMAN_ROOF_MAX  equ MAX_Y-HEIGHT_UNITS  ; humans aren't placed on roofs
 327  0000                                              ; taller than the ceiling - 1.0 height unit
 328  0000              HUMAN_RUN       equ TILE_UNITS*10/(TILE_M*NOMINAL_FPS)  ; 10 m/s: 9 units/frame
 329  0000              HUMAN_HP        equ 10          ; hit points (a rocket blast kills)
 330  0000              VTOL_HP         equ 50          ; the VTOL's hit points (ghosts attack it)
 331  0000              ; At 0 hit points the VTOL is destroyed (vtol_dead): out of control, it
 332  0000              ; spins DEAD_SPIN (8.8 yaw) a frame as it falls, faster each frame up to
 333  0000              ; DEAD_FALL_MAX units, until it hits the ground (the road or roof under it).
 334  0000              ; DEAD_PAUSE frames later the game is over: the GAME OVER screen for 10
 335  0000              ; seconds, then the main menu.
 336  0000              DEAD_SPIN       equ $0800           ; 8 yaw units a frame: a turn in 32
 337  0000              DEAD_FALL_MAX   equ 8               ; units a frame (11 m/s at 14 fps)
 338  0000              DEAD_PAUSE      equ 100             ; frames on the ground before GAME OVER
 339  0000              DISEMBARK       equ NOMINAL_FPS ; landed on the base pad: one off a second
 340  0000
 341  0000              ; Particles (weapons.asm moves them, city.asm draws them).  A killed zombie
 342  0000              ; sprays NPART_BURST for PART_LIFE frames, bright green fading to dark; a
 343  0000              ; rocket or missile burst sprays orange ones (FIRE_COL0..) for FIRE_LIFE
 344  0000              ; frames.  Particle record: +0 age (0 = free, 1..its life), +1..+6 x, y, z
 345  0000              ; (world units), +7..+9 vx, vy, vz (signed units per frame), +10 its colour
 346  0000              ; at age 1 (each age after is 4 palette entries on), +11 its life (frames).

 500  0000              ; HL = HL * GRID  (a map row offset)
 501  0000                      MACRO ROWOFS
 502  0000 ~                    add hl,hl
 503  0000 ~                    add hl,hl
 504  0000 ~                    add hl,hl
 505  0000 ~                    add hl,hl
 506  0000 ~                    add hl,hl
 507  0000 ~                    add hl,hl
 508  0000                      ENDM
 509  0000
 510  0000              ; dst = src + vec  (three 16-bit components)
 511  0000                      MACRO VADD QD, QS, QV
 512  0000 ~                    ld hl,(QS)
 513  0000 ~                    ld de,(QV)
 514  0000 ~                    add hl,de
 515  0000 ~                    ld (QD),hl
 516  0000 ~                    ld hl,(QS+2)
 517  0000 ~                    ld de,(QV+2)
 518  0000 ~                    add hl,de
 519  0000 ~                    ld (QD+2),hl
 520  0000 ~                    ld hl,(QS+4)
 521  0000 ~                    ld de,(QV+4)
 522  0000 ~                    add hl,de
 523  0000 ~                    ld (QD+4),hl
 524  0000                      ENDM

 526  0000              ; Copy cached screen corner of face vertex (HL)+ into p2buf (DE)+ with the
 527  0000              ; outline flag for edge bit EB taken from df_mask.  B = high vtx.
 528  0000                      MACRO FACEVTX EB
 529  0000 ~                    ld a,(hl)
 530  0000 ~                    inc hl
 531  0000 ~                    add a,7
 532  0000 ~                    ld c,a
 533  0000 ~                    ld a,(bc)
 534  0000 ~                    ld (de),a
 535  0000 ~                    inc e
 536  0000 ~                    inc c
 537  0000 ~                    ld a,(bc)
 538  0000 ~                    ld (de),a
 539  0000 ~                    inc e
 540  0000 ~                    ld a,(df_mask)
 541  0000 ~                    and EB
 542  0000 ~                    ld (de),a
 543  0000 ~                    inc e
 544  0000 ~                    inc e
 545  0000                      ENDM

 547  0000              ;==============================================================================
 548  0000              ; NEX entry: the loader leaves its default mapping (slot 6 = page 0, slot 5
 549  0000              ; = page 5), so the entry point is this stub in PAGE_BOOT at $C000.  It maps
 550  0000              ; the resident page into slot 5 and jumps to start.
 551  0000              ;==============================================================================
 552  0000                      MMU BOOT_SLOT e, PAGE_BOOT, BOOT_ADDR
 553  C000                      ORG BOOT_ADDR
 554  C000              boot:
 555  C000 F3                   di
 556  C001 31 1B C0             ld sp,boot_stack
 557  C004 ED 91 55 0B          nextreg MAIN_MMU,PAGE_MAIN
 558  C008 C3 6B A7             jp start
 559  C00B 00 00 00...              ds 16                       ; the loader's stack, until start
 560  C01B              boot_stack:
 561  C01B
 562  C01B                      MMU MAIN_SLOT e, PAGE_MAIN, MAIN_ADDR
 563  A000                      ORG MAIN_ADDR

 565  A000                      ; page (PAGE_SIN, slot 0), below

   1+ A057              ; ---- generated by gentables.py - do not edit ----
   2+ A057
   3+ A057              ; The city constants shared by every city: the base and landing pad
   4+ A057              ; position, the perimeter wall's colour family.  The cities themselves
   5+ A057              ; (maps, cell colours, palettes) are data files, ZPDATA/CITYnn.ZPC,
   6+ A057              ; loaded at the start of each level (level_load.asm).
   7+ A057

   9+ A057              TERRACE_CODE    equ 17      ; the terrace round it (0.25 height units)
  10+ A057              PAD_H           equ 64     ; its height (0.5 height units)
  11+ A057              PAD_X           equ 32      ; base landing pad cell (gentables.py)
  12+ A057              PAD_COL         equ $FC     ; its city_col (colour family * 4)
  13+ A057              TERRACE_COL     equ $F8     ; the terrace's city_col
  14+ A057              TERRACE_R       equ 2       ; terrace: cells 1..TERRACE_R from the pad
  15+ A057              PAD_Z           equ 32
  16+ A057              BASE_YAW        equ 0      ; start heading: north up the boulevard
  17+ A057              WALL_FAMILY     equ 0       ; perimeter wall colour family (gentables.py)
  18+ A057                      ASSERT GRID == 64   ; gentables.py and zpolice.asm agree
  19+ A057              LEVEL_LAST      equ 50      ; the last level (gentables.py)
  20+ A057              CITY_LEVELS     equ 5       ; levels played in each city (gentables.py)
  21+ A057
# file closed: city_map.asm

 569  A057                      INCLUDE "levels.asm"            ; the current level's record
# file opened: levels.asm
   1+ A057              ;==============================================================================
   2+ A057              ; Z-POLICE - the current level's settings (resident, so any page can read
   3+ A057              ; them)
   4+ A057              ;
   5+ A057              ; level_cur holds the record of the level being played, loaded from its file
   6+ A057              ; ZPDATA/LEVELnn.ZPL by load_level (level_load.asm).  LV_SIZE bytes:
   7+ A057              ;   +0  LV_NUM          level number
   8+ A057              ;   +1  LV_NAME         city name, 8 characters (ASCII, space padded)
   9+ A057              ;   +9  LV_ZOMBIES      number of zombies a wave (at most NZOMB)
  10+ A057              ;   +10 LV_WAVES        number of zombie waves (1..LV_MAX_WAVES)
  11+ A057              ;   +11 LV_FAST         number of fast zombies (of LV_ZOMBIES)
  12+ A057              ;   +12 LV_VIEW_R       draw distance in tiles, 2..VIEW_R (fewer: night or
  13+ A057              ;                       fog); load_level sets view_r / dot_r from it
  14+ A057              ;   +13 LV_WAVE_DATA    the special monsters of each wave, waves 1 to
  15+ A057              ;                       LV_MAX_WAVES (wave 1 is the level's start), LV_WAVE_REC
  16+ A057              ;                       bytes each:
  17+ A057              ;         +0  LV_SPECIAL_ID   LV_SPEC_SLOTS special types (SPEC_*;
  18+ A057              ;                             SPEC_NONE, 255: none in that place)
  19+ A057              ;         +5  LV_SPECIAL      how many of each (in the same places)
  20+ A057              ; The levels and their cities are made by gentables.py (ZPDATA/); a level
  21+ A057              ; file can be replaced by a hand-made one (or its text form, .TXL, edited).
  22+ A057              ;==============================================================================
  23+ A057
  24+ A057              LV_NUM          equ 0
  25+ A057              LV_NAME         equ 1
  26+ A057              LV_NAME_LEN     equ 8
  27+ A057              LV_ZOMBIES      equ LV_NAME+LV_NAME_LEN
  28+ A057              LV_WAVES        equ LV_ZOMBIES+1
  29+ A057              LV_FAST         equ LV_WAVES+1
  30+ A057              LV_VIEW_R       equ LV_FAST+1
  31+ A057              LV_WAVE_DATA    equ LV_VIEW_R+1
  32+ A057              LV_MAX_WAVES    equ 10              ; waves a level can have specials for
  33+ A057              LV_SPEC_SLOTS   equ 5               ; special types a wave
  34+ A057              LV_SPECIAL_ID   equ 0               ; (in a wave's data) the types,
  35+ A057              LV_SPECIAL      equ LV_SPEC_SLOTS   ;   then how many of each
  36+ A057              LV_WAVE_REC     equ 2*LV_SPEC_SLOTS
  37+ A057              LV_SIZE         equ LV_WAVE_DATA+LV_MAX_WAVES*LV_WAVE_REC   ; 113 bytes
  38+ A057                      ASSERT LV_SIZE == 113           ; (gentables.py writes it)
  39+ A057              ; Special monster types in the level files (specials.asm: spec_types)
  40+ A057              SPEC_GHOST      equ 0
  41+ A057              SPEC_BANSHEE    equ 1
  42+ A057              SPEC_GHOUL      equ 2
  43+ A057              SPEC_IDS        equ 3               ; (how many types)
  44+ A057              SPEC_NONE       equ 255
  45+ A057
  46+ A057              ; -> HL = the current level's record
  47+ A057              level_entry:
  48+ A057 21 5B A0             ld hl,level_cur
  49+ A05A C9                   ret
  50+ A05B
  51+ A05B 00 00 00...  level_cur:  ds LV_SIZE                  ; the level being played (load_level)
  52+ A0CC
# file closed: levels.asm
 570  A0CC                      INCLUDE "text.asm"              ; text screens: menu, load-out
# file opened: text.asm
   1+ A0CC              ;==============================================================================
   2+ A0CC              ; Z-POLICE - text screens (resident): the ULA routines the menu screens
   3+ A0CC              ; (menu.asm) and the load-out screen (loadout.asm) share, from whichever
   4+ A0CC              ; page is in slot 6.  Layer 2 hidden, the ULA screen with opaque paper,
   5+ A0CC              ; 8x8 characters (font8, ASCII 32-95) by row and column with an attribute.
   6+ A0CC              ;==============================================================================
   7+ A0CC
   8+ A0CC              MENU_ATTR       equ P_PANEL<<3 | 7          ; white on the panel colour
   9+ A0CC              MENU_TITLE      equ P_PANEL<<3 | C_YEL      ; yellow
  10+ A0CC              MENU_OK         equ P_PANEL<<3 | C_OK       ; green
  11+ A0CC              MENU_BAD        equ P_PANEL<<3 | C_WARN     ; red
  12+ A0CC
  13+ A0CC              ; Hide Layer 2, silence the AY chips, and blank the ULA screen with opaque
  14+ A0CC              ; paper
  15+ A0CC              menu_clear:
  16+ A0CC ED 91 57 0D          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_SPRITES   ; no HUD sprites over the menu
  17+ A0D0 CD E5 E0             call spr_hide_all
  18+ A0D3 CD 2E E0             call spr_update
  19+ A0D6 ED 91 57 11          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_AUDIO     ; and silence: the engines off
  20+ A0DA CD 00 E0             call init_audio                 ; (audio.asm)
  21+ A0DD ED 91 57 0C          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_INPUT     ; (the menu reads the keys)
  22+ A0E1 01 3B 12             ld bc,$123B
  23+ A0E4 AF                   xor a
  24+ A0E5 ED 79                out (c),a
  25+ A0E7 21 00 80             ld hl,ULA_BMP
  26+ A0EA 11 01 80             ld de,ULA_BMP+1
  27+ A0ED 01 FF 17             ld bc,$1800-1
  28+ A0F0 36 00                ld (hl),0
  29+ A0F2 ED B0                ldir
  30+ A0F4 21 00 98             ld hl,ULA_ATTR
  31+ A0F7 11 01 98             ld de,ULA_ATTR+1
  32+ A0FA 01 FF 02             ld bc,$300-1
  33+ A0FD 36 0F                ld (hl),MENU_ATTR
  34+ A0FF ED B0                ldir
  35+ A101 C9                   ret
  36+ A102
  37+ A102              ; D = character row, E = column, C = attribute, HL -> text ending 0.
  38+ A102              ; Prints it; HL ends past the 0.
  39+ A102              menu_print:
  40+ A102 7E           .ch:    ld a,(hl)
  41+ A103 23                   inc hl
  42+ A104 B7                   or a
  43+ A105 C8                   ret z
  44+ A106 E5                   push hl
  45+ A107 D5                   push de
  46+ A108 C5                   push bc
  47+ A109 CD 12 A1             call menu_char
  48+ A10C C1                   pop bc
  49+ A10D D1                   pop de
  50+ A10E E1                   pop hl
  51+ A10F 1C                   inc e
  52+ A110 18 F0                jr .ch
  53+ A112
  54+ A112              ; A = ASCII 32-95, D = row, E = column, C = attribute: draw one character
  55+ A112              menu_char:
  56+ A112 D6 20                sub 32
  57+ A114 6F                   ld l,a
  58+ A115 26 00                ld h,0
  59+ A117 29                   add hl,hl
  60+ A118 29                   add hl,hl
  61+ A119 29                   add hl,hl
  62+ A11A D5                   push de
  63+ A11B 11 7B A1             ld de,font8
  64+ A11E 19                   add hl,de                       ; HL -> its 8 bytes
  65+ A11F D1                   pop de
  66+ A120 D5                   push de
  67+ A121 7A                   ld a,d                          ; attribute: ULA_ATTR + row*32 + col
  68+ A122 0F                   rrca
  69+ A123 0F                   rrca
  70+ A124 0F                   rrca
  71+ A125 47                   ld b,a
  72+ A126 E6 E0                and %11100000
  73+ A128 B3                   or e
  74+ A129 E5                   push hl
  75+ A12A 6F                   ld l,a
  76+ A12B 78                   ld a,b
  77+ A12C E6 03                and %00000011
  78+ A12E C6 98                add a,high ULA_ATTR
  79+ A130 67                   ld h,a
  80+ A131 71                   ld (hl),c
  81+ A132 E1                   pop hl
  82+ A133 D1                   pop de
  83+ A134 7A                   ld a,d                          ; bitmap: third, row in third, column
  84+ A135 E6 18                and %00011000
  85+ A137 C6 80                add a,high ULA_BMP
  86+ A139 47                   ld b,a
  87+ A13A 7A                   ld a,d
  88+ A13B E6 07                and 7
  89+ A13D 0F                   rrca
  90+ A13E 0F                   rrca
  91+ A13F 0F                   rrca
  92+ A140 B3                   or e
  93+ A141 5F                   ld e,a
  94+ A142 50                   ld d,b                          ; DE -> the cell's top line
  95+ A143 06 08                ld b,8
  96+ A145 7E           .l:     ld a,(hl)
  97+ A146 12                   ld (de),a
  98+ A147 23                   inc hl
  99+ A148 14                   inc d
 100+ A149 10 FA                djnz .l
 101+ A14B C9                   ret
 102+ A14C
 103+ A14C              ; Wait for the start of the next video frame (the raster reaching line 192)
 104+ A14C              menu_frame:
 105+ A14C CD 57 A1     .in:    call menu_line                  ; let any current blanking end
 106+ A14F 30 FB                jr nc,.in
 107+ A151 CD 57 A1     .out:   call menu_line                  ; then wait for the next one
 108+ A154 38 FB                jr c,.out
 109+ A156 C9                   ret
 110+ A157
 111+ A157              ; Carry clear if the raster is at line 192 or later (blanking).  The line is
 112+ A157              ; read as high byte ($1E), low byte ($1F), high byte again: if the high
 113+ A157              ; byte changed in between (line 255 -> 256) the pair is torn, so read again.
 114+ A157              menu_line:
 115+ A157 01 3B 24             ld bc,$243B
 116+ A15A 3E 1E                ld a,$1E
 117+ A15C ED 79                out (c),a
 118+ A15E 04                   inc b
 119+ A15F ED 58                in e,(c)                        ; high byte
 120+ A161 05                   dec b
 121+ A162 3E 1F                ld a,$1F
 122+ A164 ED 79                out (c),a
 123+ A166 04                   inc b
 124+ A167 ED 50                in d,(c)                        ; low byte
 125+ A169 05                   dec b
 126+ A16A 3E 1E                ld a,$1E
 127+ A16C ED 79                out (c),a
 128+ A16E 04                   inc b
 129+ A16F ED 78                in a,(c)
 130+ A171 BB                   cp e
 131+ A172 20 E3                jr nz,menu_line                 ; torn: again
 132+ A174 E6 01                and 1
 133+ A176 C0                   ret nz                          ; line >= 256 (carry clear)
 134+ A177 7A                   ld a,d
 135+ A178 FE C0                cp 192
 136+ A17A C9                   ret
 137+ A17B

 571  A37B                      INCLUDE "sfx_data.asm"          ; the sound effects (sfx_tick plays them)
# file opened: sfx_data.asm
   1+ A37B              ;==============================================================================
   2+ A37B              ; Z-POLICE - sound effect data (resident: the main page, slot 5, always
   3+ A37B              ; mapped, so the interrupt routine can read it whatever else is paged in)
   4+ A37B              ;
   5+ A37B              ; Played on AY 2 by sfx_tick (interrupts.asm); the game asks for one with
   6+ A37B              ; ld a,SFX_n / ld (sfx_req),a (the numbers are in audio.asm).  A sound is a
   7+ A37B              ; list of steps, one an interrupt (20 ms): each step is AY register / value
   8+ A37B              ; pairs, ended by SFX_STEP (the next step next interrupt) or SFX_END (over).
   9+ A37B              ; The Next's AYs run at 1.75 MHz: a tone of period P is 109375/P Hz; a
  10+ A37B              ; lower noise period is a brighter hiss.
  11+ A37B              ;==============================================================================
  12+ A37B
  13+ A37B              ; One step of a zap: channel A at tone period p, channel B 2 periods flat
  14+ A37B              ; (the two beat: a buzz), volumes v and v-2
  15+ A37B                  MACRO SFX_ZAP p, v
  16+ A37B ~                    db AY_TONE_A, low (p), AY_TONE_A+1, high (p)
  17+ A37B ~                    db AY_TONE_B, low ((p)+2), AY_TONE_B+1, high ((p)+2)
  18+ A37B ~                    db AY_VOL_A, v, AY_VOL_B, (v)-2, SFX_STEP
  19+ A37B                  ENDM
  20+ A37B
  21+ A37B              ; One step of a woosh: channel A's noise at period n, volume v
  22+ A37B                  MACRO SFX_WOOSH n, v
  23+ A37B ~                    db AY_NOISE, n, AY_VOL_A, v, SFX_STEP
  24+ A37B                  ENDM
  25+ A37B
  26+ A37B              ; An explosion: a deep noise (channel A, darkening: period 16 up to 30) over
  27+ A37B              ; a low rumble (channel B, BOOM_RUMBLE), both from volume peak (B 2 less)
  28+ A37B              ; fading to nothing over len steps.  (A falling level is an exponential fade:
  29+ A37B              ; the AY's levels are logarithmic.)
  30+ A37B              BOOM_RUMBLE     equ 2735            ; 109375 / 2735 = 40 Hz
  31+ A37B                  MACRO SFX_BOOM peak, len
  32+ A37B ~                    db AY_MIXER, SFX_MIX_BOOM       ; (channel C: the splats', left alone)
  33+ A37B ~                    db AY_TONE_B, low BOOM_RUMBLE, AY_TONE_B+1, high BOOM_RUMBLE
  34+ A37B ~            BOOM_I  DEFL 0
  35+ A37B ~                    DUP len
  36+ A37B ~                    db AY_NOISE, 16 + (BOOM_I*15)/(len)
  37+ A37B ~                    db AY_VOL_A, ((peak)*((len)-BOOM_I)+(len)-1)/(len)
  38+ A37B ~                    db AY_VOL_B, (((peak)-2)*((len)-BOOM_I)+(len)-1)/(len)
  39+ A37B ~                    db SFX_STEP
  40+ A37B ~            BOOM_I  DEFL BOOM_I+1
  41+ A37B ~                    EDUP
  42+ A37B ~                    db AY_VOL_A, 0, AY_VOL_B, 0, SFX_END
  43+ A37B                  ENDM
  44+ A37B
  45+ A37B              ; One step of a splat: channel C (only: see sfx_tick) at tone period p,
  46+ A37B              ; volume v
  47+ A37B                  MACRO SFX_SPLAT_STEP p, v
  48+ A37B ~                    db AY_TONE_C, low (p), AY_TONE_C+1, high (p), AY_VOL_C, v, SFX_STEP
  49+ A37B                  ENDM
  50+ A37B
  51+ A37B              ; The sounds, by number (SFX_*, from 1)
  52+ A37B              sfx_table:
  53+ A37B 91 A3                dw sfx_mg                       ; SFX_MG
  54+ A37D 07 A7                dw sfx_laser                    ; SFX_LASER
  55+ A37F B8 A3                dw sfx_chain                    ; SFX_CHAIN
  56+ A381 06 A4                dw sfx_rocket                   ; SFX_ROCKET
  57+ A383 41 A4                dw sfx_missile                  ; SFX_MISSILE
  58+ A385 DF A3                dw sfx_bofors                   ; SFX_BOFORS
  59+ A387 77 A5                dw sfx_boom_rocket              ; SFX_BOOM_ROCKET
  60+ A389 9A A4                dw sfx_boom_missile             ; SFX_BOOM_MISSILE
  61+ A38B 2A A6                dw sfx_boom_bofors              ; SFX_BOOM_BOFORS
  62+ A38D 89 A6                dw sfx_boom_prox                ; SFX_BOOM_PROX
  63+ A38F CC A6                dw sfx_splat                    ; SFX_SPLAT
  64+ A391                      ASSERT SFX_MG == 1 && SFX_LASER == 2 && SFX_CHAIN == 3
  65+ A391                      ASSERT SFX_ROCKET == 4 && SFX_MISSILE == 5 && SFX_BOFORS == 6
  66+ A391                      ASSERT SFX_BOOM_ROCKET == 7 && SFX_BOOM_MISSILE == 8
  67+ A391                      ASSERT SFX_BOOM_BOFORS == 9 && SFX_BOOM_PROX == 10 && SFX_SPLAT == 11
  68+ A391                      ASSERT ($ - sfx_table)/2 == SFX_LAST
  69+ A391
  70+ A391              ; The machine gun, heavy: a sharp crack (bright noise) dropping into a deep
  71+ A391              ; boom (noise, channel A) over a low thump (about 70 Hz, channel B), gone in
  72+ A391              ; 80 ms
  73+ A391              MG_THUMP        equ 1563            ; tone period: 109375 / 1563 = 70 Hz
  74+ A391              sfx_mg:
  75+ A391 07 35                db AY_MIXER, SFX_MIX_NOISE_A_TONE_B
  76+ A393 02 1B 03 06          db AY_TONE_B, low MG_THUMP, AY_TONE_B+1, high MG_THUMP
  77+ A397 0A 00                db AY_VOL_C, 0
  78+ A399 06 04 08 0D          db AY_NOISE, 4, AY_VOL_A, 13, AY_VOL_B, 11, SFX_STEP     ; crack
  78+ A39D 09 0B FE
  79+ A3A0 06 12 08 0A          db AY_NOISE, 18, AY_VOL_A, 10, AY_VOL_B, 9, SFX_STEP     ; boom
  79+ A3A4 09 09 FE
  80+ A3A7 06 1A 08 06          db AY_NOISE, 26, AY_VOL_A, 6, AY_VOL_B, 5, SFX_STEP
  80+ A3AB 09 05 FE
  81+ A3AE 08 03 09 02          db AY_VOL_A, 3, AY_VOL_B, 2, SFX_STEP
  81+ A3B2 FE
  82+ A3B3 08 00 09 00          db AY_VOL_A, 0, AY_VOL_B, 0, SFX_END
  82+ A3B7 FF
  83+ A3B8
  84+ A3B8              ; The chain gun: the machine gun's sound 25% higher in pitch (every period
  85+ A3B8              ; 4/5 of it: the thump about 88 Hz).  It fires every game frame, so each
  86+ A3B8              ; round cuts the last one's tail: a rattle.
  87+ A3B8              CHAIN_THUMP     equ MG_THUMP*4/5    ; 1250: 87.5 Hz
  88+ A3B8              sfx_chain:
  89+ A3B8 07 35                db AY_MIXER, SFX_MIX_NOISE_A_TONE_B
  90+ A3BA 02 E2 03 04          db AY_TONE_B, low CHAIN_THUMP, AY_TONE_B+1, high CHAIN_THUMP
  91+ A3BE 0A 00                db AY_VOL_C, 0
  92+ A3C0 06 03 08 0D          db AY_NOISE, 3, AY_VOL_A, 13, AY_VOL_B, 11, SFX_STEP     ; crack
  92+ A3C4 09 0B FE
  93+ A3C7 06 0E 08 0A          db AY_NOISE, 14, AY_VOL_A, 10, AY_VOL_B, 9, SFX_STEP     ; boom
  93+ A3CB 09 09 FE
  94+ A3CE 06 15 08 06          db AY_NOISE, 21, AY_VOL_A, 6, AY_VOL_B, 5, SFX_STEP
  94+ A3D2 09 05 FE
  95+ A3D5 08 03 09 02          db AY_VOL_A, 3, AY_VOL_B, 2, SFX_STEP
  95+ A3D9 FE
  96+ A3DA 08 00 09 00          db AY_VOL_A, 0, AY_VOL_B, 0, SFX_END
  96+ A3DE FF
  97+ A3DF
  98+ A3DF              ; The Bofors cannon (and the proximity cannon, its shells fired the same
  99+ A3DF              ; way): the machine gun's sound deeper (the thump 30 Hz, the noise periods
 100+ A3DF              ; doubled) and louder (every volume 2 levels up: about twice as loud).  The
 101+ A3DF              ; noise period can't go past 31, so the boom's later steps (18, 26 doubled)
 102+ A3DF              ; stop there, and the volume at 15.
 103+ A3DF              BOFORS_THUMP    equ 3646            ; 109375 / 3646 = 30 Hz
 104+ A3DF                      ASSERT BOFORS_THUMP < 4096
 105+ A3DF              sfx_bofors:
 106+ A3DF 07 35                db AY_MIXER, SFX_MIX_NOISE_A_TONE_B
 107+ A3E1 02 3E 03 0E          db AY_TONE_B, low BOFORS_THUMP, AY_TONE_B+1, high BOFORS_THUMP
 108+ A3E5 0A 00                db AY_VOL_C, 0
 109+ A3E7 06 08 08 0F          db AY_NOISE, 8, AY_VOL_A, 15, AY_VOL_B, 13, SFX_STEP     ; crack
 109+ A3EB 09 0D FE
 110+ A3EE 06 1F 08 0C          db AY_NOISE, 31, AY_VOL_A, 12, AY_VOL_B, 11, SFX_STEP    ; boom
 110+ A3F2 09 0B FE
 111+ A3F5 06 1F 08 08          db AY_NOISE, 31, AY_VOL_A, 8, AY_VOL_B, 7, SFX_STEP
 111+ A3F9 09 07 FE
 112+ A3FC 08 05 09 04          db AY_VOL_A, 5, AY_VOL_B, 4, SFX_STEP
 112+ A400 FE
 113+ A401 08 00 09 00          db AY_VOL_A, 0, AY_VOL_B, 0, SFX_END

 154+ A49A
 155+ A49A              ; Explosions (rocket_blast, weapons.asm; on AY 3).  Each one's loudness in
 156+ A49A              ; proportion to its blast's size, damage x reach (game units):
 157+ A49A              ;   missile 15 x 256 = 3840, rocket 20 x 64 = 1280, Bofors 4 x 38 = 152,
 158+ A49A              ;   proximity 3 x 25 = 75.
 159+ A49A              ; Sound level is logarithmic (about 2 AY levels each doubling), so the peak
 160+ A49A              ; is 15 + 2*log2(size / the missile's): 15, 12, 6, 4; and the bigger the
 161+ A49A              ; blast the longer it rumbles, 2 steps (40 ms) per level: 600, 480, 240 and
 162+ A49A              ; 160 ms.
 163+ A49A              sfx_boom_missile:
 164+ A49A                      SFX_BOOM 15, 30
 164+ A49A 07 31       >        db AY_MIXER, SFX_MIX_BOOM       ; (channel C: the splats', left alone)
 164+ A49C 02 AF 03 0A >        db AY_TONE_B, low BOOM_RUMBLE, AY_TONE_B+1, high BOOM_RUMBLE
 164+ A4A0             >BOOM_I  DEFL 0
 164+ A4A0             >        DUP 30
 164+ A4A0 06 10       >        db AY_NOISE, 16 + (BOOM_I*15)/(30)
 164+ A4A2 08 0F       >        db AY_VOL_A, ((15)*((30)-BOOM_I)+(30)-1)/(30)
 164+ A4A4 09 0D       >        db AY_VOL_B, (((15)-2)*((30)-BOOM_I)+(30)-1)/(30)
 164+ A4A6 FE          >        db SFX_STEP
 164+ A4A7             >BOOM_I  DEFL BOOM_I+1
 164+ A4A7 06 10       >        db AY_NOISE, 16 + (BOOM_I*15)/(30)
 164+ A4A9 08 0F       >        db AY_VOL_A, ((15)*((30)-BOOM_I)+(30)-1)/(30)
 164+ A4AB 09 0D       >        db AY_VOL_B, (((15)-2)*((30)-BOOM_I)+(30)-1)/(30)
 164+ A4AD FE          >        db SFX_STEP
 164+ A4AE             >BOOM_I  DEFL BOOM_I+1
 164+ A4AE 06 11       >        db AY_NOISE, 16 + (BOOM_I*15)/(30)
 164+ A4B0 08 0E       >        db AY_VOL_A, ((15)*((30)-BOOM_I)+(30)-1)/(30)
 164+ A4B2 09 0D       >        db AY_VOL_B, (((15)-2)*((30)-BOOM_I)+(30)-1)/(30)
 164+ A4B4 FE          >        db SFX_STEP
 164+ A4B5             >BOOM_I  DEFL BOOM_I+1
 164+ A4B5 06 11       >        db AY_NOISE, 16 + (BOOM_I*15)/(30)

 170+ A6B4 08 02       >        db AY_VOL_A, ((4)*((8)-BOOM_I)+(8)-1)/(8)
 170+ A6B6 09 01       >        db AY_VOL_B, (((4)-2)*((8)-BOOM_I)+(8)-1)/(8)
 170+ A6B8 FE          >        db SFX_STEP
 170+ A6B9             >BOOM_I  DEFL BOOM_I+1
 170+ A6B9 06 1B       >        db AY_NOISE, 16 + (BOOM_I*15)/(8)
 170+ A6BB 08 01       >        db AY_VOL_A, ((4)*((8)-BOOM_I)+(8)-1)/(8)
 170+ A6BD 09 01       >        db AY_VOL_B, (((4)-2)*((8)-BOOM_I)+(8)-1)/(8)
 170+ A6BF FE          >        db SFX_STEP
 170+ A6C0             >BOOM_I  DEFL BOOM_I+1
 170+ A6C0 06 1D       >        db AY_NOISE, 16 + (BOOM_I*15)/(8)
 170+ A6C2 08 01       >        db AY_VOL_A, ((4)*((8)-BOOM_I)+(8)-1)/(8)
 170+ A6C4 09 01       >        db AY_VOL_B, (((4)-2)*((8)-BOOM_I)+(8)-1)/(8)
 170+ A6C6 FE          >        db SFX_STEP
 170+ A6C7             >BOOM_I  DEFL BOOM_I+1
 170+ A6C7             >        EDUP
 170+ A6C7 08 00 09 00 >        db AY_VOL_A, 0, AY_VOL_B, 0, SFX_END
 170+ A6CB FF          >
 171+ A6CC                      ASSERT MISSILE_DMG*MISSILE_R == 3840 && BLAST_DMG*BLAST_R == 1280
 172+ A6CC                      ASSERT BOFORS_DMG*BOFORS_R == 152 && PROX_DMG*PROX_R == 75
 173+ A6CC
 174+ A6CC              ; A zombie hit, a squelchy splat (zdamage, weapons.asm; AY 3 channel C): a
 175+ A6CC              ; tone lurching down and up as it falls, a wet gargle, from about 680 Hz to
 176+ A6CC              ; 130 Hz over 160 ms, fading.  Each hit cuts the last one's.
 177+ A6CC              sfx_splat:
 178+ A6CC                      SFX_SPLAT_STEP 160, 12
 178+ A6CC 04 A0 05 00 >        db AY_TONE_C, low (160), AY_TONE_C+1, high (160), AY_VOL_C, 12, SFX_STEP
 178+ A6D0 0A 0C FE    >
 179+ A6D3                      SFX_SPLAT_STEP 320, 12
 179+ A6D3 04 40 05 01 >        db AY_TONE_C, low (320), AY_TONE_C+1, high (320), AY_VOL_C, 12, SFX_STEP
 179+ A6D7 0A 0C FE    >
 180+ A6DA                      SFX_SPLAT_STEP 210, 11
 180+ A6DA 04 D2 05 00 >        db AY_TONE_C, low (210), AY_TONE_C+1, high (210), AY_VOL_C, 11, SFX_STEP
 180+ A6DE 0A 0B FE    >
 181+ A6E1                      SFX_SPLAT_STEP 450, 9
 181+ A6E1 04 C2 05 01 >        db AY_TONE_C, low (450), AY_TONE_C+1, high (450), AY_VOL_C, 9, SFX_STEP
 181+ A6E5 0A 09 FE    >
 182+ A6E8                      SFX_SPLAT_STEP 280, 8
 182+ A6E8 04 18 05 01 >        db AY_TONE_C, low (280), AY_TONE_C+1, high (280), AY_VOL_C, 8, SFX_STEP
 182+ A6EC 0A 08 FE    >
 183+ A6EF                      SFX_SPLAT_STEP 620, 6
 183+ A6EF 04 6C 05 02 >        db AY_TONE_C, low (620), AY_TONE_C+1, high (620), AY_VOL_C, 6, SFX_STEP
 183+ A6F3 0A 06 FE    >
 184+ A6F6                      SFX_SPLAT_STEP 400, 4
 184+ A6F6 04 90 05 01 >        db AY_TONE_C, low (400), AY_TONE_C+1, high (400), AY_VOL_C, 4, SFX_STEP
 184+ A6FA 0A 04 FE    >
 185+ A6FD                      SFX_SPLAT_STEP 850, 2
 185+ A6FD 04 52 05 03 >        db AY_TONE_C, low (850), AY_TONE_C+1, high (850), AY_VOL_C, 2, SFX_STEP
 185+ A701 0A 02 FE    >
 186+ A704 0A 00 FF             db AY_VOL_C, 0, SFX_END
 187+ A707
 188+ A707              ; The pulse laser, a zap: a tone sweeping down from about 2.7 kHz to 470 Hz
 189+ A707              ; (channel A), with a second one a little flat beating against it (channel
 190+ A707              ; B), fading over 140 ms
 191+ A707              sfx_laser:
 192+ A707 07 3C                db AY_MIXER, SFX_MIX_TONE_AB
 193+ A709 0A 00                db AY_VOL_C, 0
 194+ A70B                      SFX_ZAP 40, 13
 194+ A70B 00 28 01 00 >        db AY_TONE_A, low (40), AY_TONE_A+1, high (40)
 194+ A70F 02 2A 03 00 >        db AY_TONE_B, low ((40)+2), AY_TONE_B+1, high ((40)+2)
 194+ A713 08 0D 09 0B >        db AY_VOL_A, 13, AY_VOL_B, (13)-2, SFX_STEP
 194+ A717 FE          >
 195+ A718                      SFX_ZAP 55, 13
 195+ A718 00 37 01 00 >        db AY_TONE_A, low (55), AY_TONE_A+1, high (55)
 195+ A71C 02 39 03 00 >        db AY_TONE_B, low ((55)+2), AY_TONE_B+1, high ((55)+2)
 195+ A720 08 0D 09 0B >        db AY_VOL_A, 13, AY_VOL_B, (13)-2, SFX_STEP
 195+ A724 FE          >
 196+ A725                      SFX_ZAP 75, 12
 196+ A725 00 4B 01 00 >        db AY_TONE_A, low (75), AY_TONE_A+1, high (75)
 196+ A729 02 4D 03 00 >        db AY_TONE_B, low ((75)+2), AY_TONE_B+1, high ((75)+2)
 196+ A72D 08 0C 09 0A >        db AY_VOL_A, 12, AY_VOL_B, (12)-2, SFX_STEP
 196+ A731 FE          >
 197+ A732                      SFX_ZAP 100, 11
 197+ A732 00 64 01 00 >        db AY_TONE_A, low (100), AY_TONE_A+1, high (100)
 197+ A736 02 66 03 00 >        db AY_TONE_B, low ((100)+2), AY_TONE_B+1, high ((100)+2)
 197+ A73A 08 0B 09 09 >        db AY_VOL_A, 11, AY_VOL_B, (11)-2, SFX_STEP
 197+ A73E FE          >
 198+ A73F                      SFX_ZAP 135, 9
 198+ A73F 00 87 01 00 >        db AY_TONE_A, low (135), AY_TONE_A+1, high (135)
 198+ A743 02 89 03 00 >        db AY_TONE_B, low ((135)+2), AY_TONE_B+1, high ((135)+2)
 198+ A747 08 09 09 07 >        db AY_VOL_A, 9, AY_VOL_B, (9)-2, SFX_STEP
 198+ A74B FE          >
 199+ A74C                      SFX_ZAP 180, 6
 199+ A74C 00 B4 01 00 >        db AY_TONE_A, low (180), AY_TONE_A+1, high (180)
 199+ A750 02 B6 03 00 >        db AY_TONE_B, low ((180)+2), AY_TONE_B+1, high ((180)+2)
 199+ A754 08 06 09 04 >        db AY_VOL_A, 6, AY_VOL_B, (6)-2, SFX_STEP
 199+ A758 FE          >
 200+ A759                      SFX_ZAP 235, 3
 200+ A759 00 EB 01 00 >        db AY_TONE_A, low (235), AY_TONE_A+1, high (235)
 200+ A75D 02 ED 03 00 >        db AY_TONE_B, low ((235)+2), AY_TONE_B+1, high ((235)+2)
 200+ A761 08 03 09 01 >        db AY_VOL_A, 3, AY_VOL_B, (3)-2, SFX_STEP
 200+ A765 FE          >

 574  A76B              ; Entry
 575  A76B              ;==============================================================================
 576  A76B              start:
 577  A76B F3                   di
 578  A76C 31 85 B5             ld sp,stack_top
 579  A76F ED 91 57 0F          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_INIT      ; hardware set-up and memory map
 580  A773 CD 00 E0             call initgame                   ; (initialise.asm)
 581  A776 ED 91 57 11          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_AUDIO     ; the AY chips silent, stereo
 582  A77A CD 00 E0             call init_audio                 ; (audio.asm)
 583  A77D CD E2 3B             call im2_init                   ; I -> the IM 2 vector table, IM 2
 584  A780                                                      ; (interrupts.asm; still disabled)
 585  A780 ED 91 57 10          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_FILE      ; the SD card's default drive
 586  A784 CD 00 E0             call fh_init                    ; (file_handler.asm)
 587  A787                      ; (the Layer 2 palette: each city's own, loaded with the level)
 588  A787
 589  A787 ED 5F                ld a,r                          ; random seed (xorshift: non-zero);
 590  A789 6F                   ld l,a                          ; the menu stirs it every frame
 591  A78A 26 A5                ld h,$A5
 592  A78C 22 65 34             ld (zseed),hl
 593  A78F ED 91 57 0C          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_INPUT     ; the extended keys only in their own
 594  A793 CD 9E E0             call kbd_double_on              ; banks (BREAK, the pause key, not
 595  A796                                                      ; also CAPS SHIFT + SPACE, fire)
 596  A796 ED 91 57 02          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_HUD
