 598  A79D ED 91 57 0D          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_SPRITES
 599  A7A1 CD 00 E0             call sprites_init               ; (sprites.asm) on, all hidden
 600  A7A4 ED 91 57 09          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_SPR0      ; sprite bank 0: the human status
 601  A7A8 CD 00 E0             call spr_load_bank              ; sprites (sprite_data.asm)
 602  A7AB ED 91 56 08          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_MENU     ; (the menu pages the keys in)
 603  A7AF FB                   ei                              ; interrupts on, from now on (the
 604  A7B0                                                      ; engines' sound: interrupts.asm)
 605  A7B0 C3 00 C0             jp main_menu                    ; (menu.asm) -> newGame
 606  A7B3
 607  A7B3              ;==============================================================================
 608  A7B3              ; New game: start level 1.  Start-up has set the hardware (speed, layers,
 609  A7B3              ; clip window, palettes, memory map), which the menu leaves alone; here the
 610  A7B3              ; game state and the screen contents are set up from scratch, so this also
 611  A7B3              ; works for a second game.
 612  A7B3              ;==============================================================================
 613  A7B3              newGame:
 614  A7B3 31 85 B5             ld sp,stack_top                 ; (entered by jp from the menu)
 615  A7B6 CD BC A7             call game_reset
 616  A7B9 C3 FA A9             jp loadout_go                   ; the load-out screen first, then the
 617  A7BC                                                      ; first level (next_level)
 618  A7BC
 619  A7BC              ; A new game's state: the player, weapons, store, salvage, booster (and the
 620  A7BC              ; debug load-out), level START_LEVEL.  (CONTINUE lays a save game over it:
 621  A7BC              ; continue_game.)
 622  A7BC              game_reset:
 623  A7BC 21 CB AE             ld hl,start_state               ; player, weapon slots, selection
 624  A7BF 11 23 AE             ld de,cam_x
 625  A7C2 01 1A 00             ld bc,start_state_end-start_state
 626  A7C5 ED B0                ldir
 627  A7C7 21 E5 AE             ld hl,bullets                   ; no rounds or beam in flight
 628  A7CA 11 E6 AE             ld de,bullets+1
 629  A7CD 01 72 05             ld bc,(NBULLETS+1)*BREC+NPART*PREC-1    ; and no particles
 630  A7D0 36 00                ld (hl),0
 631  A7D2 ED B0                ldir
 632  A7D4 3E 01                ld a,START_LEVEL
 633  A7D6 32 3D AE             ld (level),a
 634  A7D9 3E 32                ld a,VTOL_HP
 635  A7DB 32 40 AE             ld (vtol_hp),a                  ; the VTOL undamaged
 636  A7DE 3E 64                ld a,DEAD_PAUSE
 637  A7E0 32 44 AE             ld (dead_wait),a
 638  A7E3 AF                   xor a
 639  A7E4 32 41 AE             ld (vtol_dead),a
 640  A7E7 32 42 AE             ld (dead_down),a
 641  A7EA 32 43 AE             ld (dead_vy),a
 642  A7ED 21 00 00             ld hl,0
 643  A7F0 22 48 AE             ld (salvage_points),hl          ; no salvage yet
 644  A7F3 21 A7 AE             ld hl,wpn_stock                 ; and no weapons in store or
 645  A7F6 06 12                ld b,2*(WPN_LAST+1)             ; unlocked (wpn_unlocked follows)
 646  A7F8 36 00        .ws:    ld (hl),0
 647  A7FA 23                   inc hl
 648  A7FB 10 FB                djnz .ws
 649  A7FD 21 30 AE             ld hl,wpn_slots                 ; but those fitted at the start
 650  A800 06 05                ld b,WPN_SLOTS
 651  A802 7E           .wu:    ld a,(hl)
 652  A803 11 B0 AE             ld de,wpn_unlocked
 653  A806 ED 32                add de,a
 654  A808 3E 01                ld a,1
 655  A80A 12                   ld (de),a
 656  A80B 23                   inc hl
 657  A80C 23                   inc hl
 658  A80D 10 F3                djnz .wu
 659  A80F AF                   xor a
 660  A810 32 B0 AE             ld (wpn_unlocked+WPN_NONE),a    ; (an empty slot isn't a weapon)
 661  A813 32 B9 AE             ld (boost_fitted),a             ; no booster (a salvage reward)
 662  A816 32 BA AE             ld (boost_fuel),a
 663  A819 32 BB AE             ld (boost_on),a
 664  A81C                  IF DEBUG_LOADOUT
 665  A81C 21 A8 AE             ld hl,wpn_stock+1               ; debug: every weapon unlocked, and
 666  A81F 06 08                ld b,WPN_LAST                   ; DEBUG_STOCK of each in store (not
 667  A821 3E 09        .dbg:   ld a,WPN_LAST+1                 ; the bomb: none yet)
 668  A823 90                   sub b                           ; (the type)
 669  A824 FE 06                cp WPN_BOMB
 670  A826 28 0B                jr z,.dbn
 671  A828 36 04                ld (hl),DEBUG_STOCK
 672  A82A 11 09 00             ld de,wpn_unlocked-wpn_stock
 673  A82D 19                   add hl,de
 674  A82E 36 01                ld (hl),1
 675  A830 B7                   or a
 676  A831 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 677  A833 23           .dbn:   inc hl
 678  A834 10 EB                djnz .dbg
 679  A836 3E 01                ld a,1                          ; and the booster fitted, full
 680  A838 32 B9 AE             ld (boost_fitted),a
 681  A83B 3E C8                ld a,BOOST_FUEL_MAX
 682  A83D 32 BA AE             ld (boost_fuel),a
 683  A840                  ENDIF
 684  A840 C9                   ret
 685  A841
 686  A841              ;==============================================================================
 687  A841              ; Start the level `level`: from the load-out screen, after newGame and after
 688  A841              ; each level completed (level_complete_screen).  The VTOL goes back onto the base pad, level and
 689  A841              ; still; its hit points, weapons and ammo carry on.  The level's files are
 690  A841              ; loaded and everything in the city is set up afresh.
 691  A841              ;==============================================================================
 692  A841              next_level:
 693  A841 31 85 B5             ld sp,stack_top
 694  A844 21 CB AE             ld hl,start_state               ; position, heading, pitch, speed
 695  A847 11 23 AE             ld de,cam_x
 696  A84A 01 0D 00             ld bc,speed+1-cam_x
 697  A84D ED B0                ldir
 698  A84F 21 E5 AE             ld hl,bullets                   ; no rounds or beam in flight
 699  A852 11 E6 AE             ld de,bullets+1
 700  A855 01 72 05             ld bc,(NBULLETS+1)*BREC+NPART*PREC-1    ; and no particles
 701  A858 36 00                ld (hl),0
 702  A85A ED B0                ldir
 703  A85C 3A A0 AB             ld a,(resume_wave)              ; the level's first zombie wave (or a
 704  A85F 32 46 AE             ld (wave_number),a              ; save game's: continue_game)
 705  A862 3E 01                ld a,1
 706  A864 32 A0 AB             ld (resume_wave),a              ; (once)
 707  A867 AF                   xor a
 708  A868 32 BB AE             ld (boost_on),a                 ; (not boosting: start_state's speed)
 709  A86B 32 45 AE             ld (level_done),a
 710  A86E 32 47 AE             ld (game_over),a
 711  A871 ED 91 56 1E          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_LEVEL    ; the level's files: its record, its
 712  A875 3A 3D AE             ld a,(level)                    ; city's map, colours and palette,
 713  A878 CD 00 C0             call load_level                 ; the draw distance (level_load.asm)
 714  A87B 30 07                jr nc,.loaded
 715  A87D ED 91 56 08          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_MENU     ; couldn't: say so, back to the menu
 716  A881 C3 03 C4             jp load_error_screen            ; (menu.asm)
 717  A884              .loaded:
 718  A884 3A 65 A0             ld a,(level_cur+LV_WAVES)       ; (a save game's wave: within the
 719  A887 21 46 AE             ld hl,wave_number               ; level's waves)
 720  A88A BE                   cp (hl)
 721  A88B 30 01                jr nc,.wok
 722  A88D 77                   ld (hl),a
 723  A88E ED 91 57 11  .wok:   nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_AUDIO     ; the engines on (audio.asm; the
 724  A892 CD 41 E0             call engine_start               ; interrupt plays them)
 725  A895 CD DA 28             call base_init                  ; the pad and terrace, at full strength
 726  A898 ED 91 56 04          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_WEAPONS
 727  A89C CD E4 C3             call weapons_reset
 728  A89F
 729  A89F                      ; clear both buffers
 730  A89F ED 91 57 01          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_CITY
 731  A8A3 3E 40                ld a,VIEW_CY
 732  A8A5 32 58 B4             ld (horizon),a
 733  A8A8 3E 12                ld a,PAGE_L2_A
 734  A8AA 32 7F B4             ld (seg_base),a
 735  A8AD CD 66 EE             call clear_screen
 736  A8B0 3E 18                ld a,PAGE_L2_B
 737  A8B2 32 7F B4             ld (seg_base),a
 738  A8B5 CD 66 EE             call clear_screen
 739  A8B8 ED 91 12 09          nextreg $12,L2_BANK_A           ; Layer 2 active (displayed) bank
 740  A8BC ED 91 13 0C          nextreg $13,L2_BANK_B           ; Layer 2 shadow bank (drawn into)
 741  A8C0 01 3B 12             ld bc,$123B
 742  A8C3 3E 02                ld a,%00000010                  ; Layer 2 visible (the menu hid it)
 743  A8C5 ED 79                out (c),a
 744  A8C7 ED 91 57 02          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_HUD
 745  A8CB CD 6A E0             call ula_init                   ; ULA: FPS overlay, panel, city map
 746  A8CE CD 00 25             call zombie_init                ; place the zombies on the streets
 747  A8D1 CD 09 36             call human_init                 ; and the humans to rescue
 748  A8D4 ED 91 56 1F          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_SPECIAL  ; and the level's special monsters
 749  A8D8 CD 00 C0             call spec_init                  ; (specials.asm)
 750  A8DB
 751  A8DB              ;==============================================================================
 752  A8DB              ; Main loop
 753  A8DB              ;==============================================================================
 754  A8DB              main_loop:
 755  A8DB ED 91 57 0C          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_INPUT     ; input at $E000, while the logic runs
 756  A8DF CD 00 E0             call kbd_scan                   ; all key and joystick banks
 757  A8E2 3E 12                ld a,K_PAUSE                    ; paused until the pause control is
 758  A8E4 CD CF E0             call key_pressed                ; pressed again
 759  A8E7 C4 0F AA             call nz,game_pause
 760  A8EA ED 91 56 03          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_PLAYER   ; input/movement logic at $C000
 761  A8EE CD 00 C0             call update_player
 762  A8F1 CD B7 AC             call setup_frame
 763  A8F4 CD 07 26             call zombie_update              ; walk the zombies (page at $2000)
 764  A8F7 ED 91 56 1F          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_SPECIAL  ; and fly the special monsters
 765  A8FB CD AA C0             call spec_update                ; (specials.asm)
 766  A8FE ED 91 56 04          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_WEAPONS  ; weapons logic at $C000
 767  A902 CD 00 C0             call update_weapons             ; fire, move rounds, hits
 768  A905 CD A6 37             call human_update               ; map dots, rescues (page at $7000)
 769  A908 3A 47 AE             ld a,(game_over)
 770  A90B B7                   or a
 771  A90C 28 07                jr z,.play
 772  A90E ED 91 56 08          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_MENU     ; the pad is destroyed: game over
 773  A912 C3 54 C0             jp game_over_screen             ; (menu.asm) -> main menu
 774  A915 3A 45 AE     .play:  ld a,(level_done)
 775  A918 B7                   or a
 776  A919 28 07                jr z,.go
 777  A91B ED 91 56 08          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_MENU     ; the last wave wiped out: level
 778  A91F C3 83 C0             jp level_complete_screen        ; complete (menu.asm) -> next_level
 779  A922              .go:
 780  A922 CD 58 A9             call map_shadow
 781  A925                  IFDEF DRAW_FPS
 782  A925 ~                    call vtick
 783  A925                  ENDIF
 784  A925 ED 91 57 01          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_CITY      ; Layer 2 drawing code at $E000
 785  A929 CD 66 EE             call clear_screen
 786  A92C                  IFDEF DRAW_FPS
 787  A92C ~                    call vtick
 788  A92C                  ENDIF
 789  A92C CD B9 F4             call draw_dots
 790  A92F                  IFDEF DRAW_FPS
 791  A92F ~                    call vtick
 792  A92F                  ENDIF
 793  A92F CD C9 EE             call draw_city                  ; also calls vtick once per building
 794  A932 CD 75 F5             call draw_bullets               ; machine gun tracers
 795  A935 CD 88 F6             call draw_particles             ; zombie bursts
 796  A938 CD BE F7             call draw_gunsight
 797  A93B ED 91 57 02          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_HUD       ; ULA HUD code at $E000
 798  A93F CD FE E1             call draw_hud
 799  A942                  IFDEF WAIT_FOR_VBLANK
 800  A942 ~                    call wait_vblank
 801  A942                  ENDIF
 802  A942 CD 62 A9             call l2_flip
 803  A945                  IFDEF DRAW_FPS
 804  A945 ~                    call fps_tick                   ; HUD page is still at $E000
 805  A945                  ENDIF
 806  A945 ED 91 57 0D          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_SPRITES   ; the HUD sprites (humans, compass)
 807  A949 CD A0 E2             call hud_sprites                ; (hud_sprites.asm), with the new frame
 808  A94C C3 DB A8             jp main_loop
 809  A94F
 810  A94F              ;==============================================================================
 811  A94F              ; Frames per second, shown on the ULA layer above Layer 2.
 812  A94F              ;
 813  A94F              ; Interrupts stay off (the span filler borrows SP), so time is taken from the
 814  A94F              ; raster: vtick reads the current video line and counts a new 50Hz video
 815  A94F              ; frame whenever the line number has gone backwards.  It must be called at
 816  A94F              ; least once per video frame (~560K T at 28MHz); the main loop and draw_cell
 817  A94F              ; do that.  Every 50 video frames (1 second) the number of frames rendered
 818  A94F              ; becomes the FPS figure.
 819  A94F              ;==============================================================================
 820  A94F                  IFDEF DRAW_FPS
 821  A94F ~            vtick:
 822  A94F ~                    ld bc,$243B
 823  A94F ~                    ld a,$1E
 824  A94F ~                    out (c),a
 825  A94F ~                    inc b
 826  A94F ~                    in d,(c)                        ; line MSB
 827  A94F ~                    dec b
 828  A94F ~                    ld a,$1F
 829  A94F ~                    out (c),a
 830  A94F ~                    inc b
 831  A94F ~                    in e,(c)                        ; line LSB
 832  A94F ~                    dec b
 833  A94F ~                    ld a,$1E
 834  A94F ~                    out (c),a
 835  A94F ~                    inc b
 836  A94F ~                    in a,(c)
 837  A94F ~                    cp d
 838  A94F ~                    jr nz,vtick                     ; MSB changed mid-read: try again
 839  A94F ~                    and 1
 840  A94F ~                    ld d,a                          ; DE = video line 0..311
 841  A94F ~                    ld hl,(fps_line)
 842  A94F ~                    ld (fps_line),de
 843  A94F ~                    or a
 844  A94F ~                    sbc hl,de                       ; previous - current
 845  A94F ~                    ret c
 846  A94F ~                    ret z
 847  A94F ~                    ld hl,fps_vframes               ; line went backwards: new frame
 848  A94F ~                    inc (hl)
 849  A94F ~                    ret
 850  A94F ~
 851  A94F ~            ; Once per rendered frame, after the flip
 852  A94F ~            fps_tick:
 853  A94F ~                    call vtick
 854  A94F ~                    ld hl,fps_count
 855  A94F ~                    inc (hl)
 856  A94F ~                    ld a,(fps_vframes)
 857  A94F ~                    cp 50
 858  A94F ~                    ret c
 859  A94F ~                    sub 50
 860  A94F ~                    ld (fps_vframes),a
 861  A94F ~                    ld a,(fps_count)
 862  A94F ~                    ld (fps_value),a
 863  A94F ~                    xor a
 864  A94F ~                    ld (fps_count),a
 865  A94F ~                    jp fps_draw                     ; HUD page (paged in by main_loop)
 866  A94F                  ENDIF
 867  A94F
 868  A94F              ; A = NextReg number -> A = its value
 869  A94F              read_nextreg:
 870  A94F 01 3B 24             ld bc,$243B
 871  A952 ED 79                out (c),a
 872  A954 04                   inc b
 873  A955 ED 78                in a,(c)
 874  A957 C9                   ret
 875  A958
 876  A958              ; Select the shadow Layer 2 buffer for drawing: remember the 8K page of its
 877  A958              ; first row.  Sections are then paged in on demand by seg_map / line_seg.
 878  A958              map_shadow:
 879  A958 3E 13                ld a,$13
 880  A95A CD 4F A9             call read_nextreg
 881  A95D 87                   add a,a                         ; 16K bank -> 8K page
 882  A95E 32 7F B4             ld (seg_base),a
 883  A961 C9                   ret
 884  A962
 885  A962              ; Show the shadow buffer and make the old active buffer the new shadow
 886  A962              l2_flip:
 887  A962 3E 12                ld a,$12
 888  A964 CD 4F A9             call read_nextreg
 889  A967 5F                   ld e,a                          ; old active
 890  A968 3E 13                ld a,$13
 891  A96A CD 4F A9             call read_nextreg
 892  A96D ED 92 12             nextreg $12,a                   ; shadow -> active
 893  A970 7B                   ld a,e
 894  A971 ED 92 13             nextreg $13,a                   ; old active -> shadow
 895  A974 C9                   ret
 896  A975
 897  A975              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 898  A975              ; Layer 2 writes go through MMU slot 0 ($0000-$1FFF) only.  Each 8K page of
 899  A975              ; the buffer is a 32-row section, so row y is page seg_base + y/32 and its
 900  A975              ; pixels are at (y & 31)*256 + x.  Sections are paged in as drawing reaches
 901  A975              ; them.  Slots 4-5 keep the ULA screen.
 902  A975              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 903  A975
 904  A975              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 905  A975              ; Wait until the raster is outside the display area (line >= 192)
 906  A975              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 907  A975              wait_vblank:
 908  A975 01 3B 24             ld bc,$243B
 909  A978 3E 1E                ld a,$1E
 910  A97A ED 79                out (c),a
 911  A97C 04                   inc b
 912  A97D ED 78                in a,(c)
 913  A97F E6 01                and 1
 914  A981 C0                   ret nz                          ; line >= 256
 915  A982 05                   dec b
 916  A983 3E 1F                ld a,$1F
 917  A985 ED 79                out (c),a
 918  A987 04                   inc b
 919  A988 ED 78                in a,(c)
 920  A98A FE C0                cp 192
 921  A98C 38 E7                jr c,wait_vblank
 922  A98E C9                   ret
 923  A98F
 924  A98F              ; HL -> map byte for the cell the camera is in
 925  A98F              cam_cell_ptr:
 926  A98F 3A 28 AE             ld a,(cam_z+2)
 927  A992 6F                   ld l,a
 928  A993 26 00                ld h,0
 929  A995                      ROWOFS
 929  A995 29          >        add hl,hl
 929  A996 29          >        add hl,hl
 929  A997 29          >        add hl,hl
 929  A998 29          >        add hl,hl
 929  A999 29          >        add hl,hl
 929  A99A 29          >        add hl,hl
 930  A99B 3A 25 AE             ld a,(cam_x+2)
 931  A99E ED 31                add hl,a
 932  A9A0 11 00 70             ld de,city_map
 933  A9A3 19                   add hl,de
 934  A9A4 C9                   ret
 935  A9A5
 936  A9A5              ; A = damage to the VTOL (a building crash, a ghost): its hit points
 937  A9A5              ; (vtol_hp) fall by that, not below 0.  At 0 it is destroyed (vtol_dead): it
 938  A9A5              ; spins down out of control (vtol_wreck, player.asm), then the game is over.
 939  A9A5              vtol_damage:
 940  A9A5 B7                   or a
 941  A9A6 C8                   ret z
 942  A9A7 4F                   ld c,a
 943  A9A8 3A 40 AE             ld a,(vtol_hp)
 944  A9AB 91                   sub c
 945  A9AC 30 01                jr nc,.hp
 946  A9AE AF                   xor a
 947  A9AF 32 40 AE     .hp:    ld (vtol_hp),a
 948  A9B2 C0                   ret nz
 949  A9B3 3E 01                ld a,1
 950  A9B5 32 41 AE             ld (vtol_dead),a
 951  A9B8 C9                   ret
 952  A9B9
 953  A9B9              ; HL = the height the VTOL rests at: MIN_Y when its whole footprint is over
 954  A9B9              ; road, or just clear (COLL_H) of the highest roof under it
 955  A9B9              floor_y:
 956  A9B9 CD DF AB             call vtol_ground
 957  A9BC 21 08 00             ld hl,MIN_Y
 958  A9BF C8                   ret z
 959  A9C0 2A C9 AE             ld hl,(vg_max)
 960  A9C3 11 0D 00             ld de,COLL_H+1
 961  A9C6 19                   add hl,de
 962  A9C7 C9                   ret
 963  A9C8
 964  A9C8              ; Weapon type details, by WPN_*: its full load, reload frames a shot (0:
 965  A9C8              ; doesn't reload; the AMMO_INF weapons) and the slots it may be fitted in
 966  A9C8              ; (resident, for the weapons page and the load-out screen)
 967  A9C8 00 00 1F     wpn_cfg:    db 0, 0, WS_ANY                             ; WPN_NONE (empty)
 968  A9CB FF 00 01                 db AMMO_INF, 0, WS_CENTRE                   ; WPN_GUN
 969  A9CE FF 00 1F                 db AMMO_INF, 0, WS_ANY                      ; WPN_LASER
 970  A9D1 FF 00 01                 db AMMO_INF, 0, WS_CENTRE                   ; WPN_CHAIN
 971  A9D4 0C 0F 1E                 db ROCKET_SHOTS, ROCKET_RELOAD, WS_PYLONS   ; WPN_ROCKETS
 972  A9D7 04 1E 1E                 db MISSILE_SHOTS, MISSILE_RELOAD, WS_PYLONS ; WPN_MISSILE
 973  A9DA 00 00 00                 db 0, 0, 0                                  ; WPN_BOMB (none yet)
 974  A9DD 32 07 1E                 db BOFORS_SHOTS, BOFORS_RELOAD, WS_PYLONS   ; WPN_BOFORS
 975  A9E0 32 07 1E                 db PROX_SHOTS, PROX_RELOAD, WS_PYLONS       ; WPN_PROX
 976  A9E3                      ASSERT WPN_ROCKETS == 4 && WPN_MISSILE == 5 && WPN_BOFORS == 7
 977  A9E3                      ASSERT WPN_PROX == 8
 978  A9E3                      ASSERT $ - wpn_cfg == WCFG_SIZE*(WPN_LAST+1)
 979  A9E3
 980  A9E3              ; A = weapon type -> HL = its wpn_cfg entry.  Preserves BC, DE.
 981  A9E3              wpn_cfg_ptr:
 982  A9E3 6F                   ld l,a
 983  A9E4 87                   add a,a
 984  A9E5 85                   add a,l                         ; * WCFG_SIZE
 985  A9E6 21 C8 A9             ld hl,wpn_cfg
 986  A9E9 ED 31                add hl,a
 987  A9EB C9                   ret
 988  A9EC                      ASSERT WCFG_SIZE == 3
 989  A9EC
 990  A9EC              ; A = weapon type, C = slot (0 = the centre slot): carry set if the weapon
 991  A9EC              ; may be fitted in that slot (WCFG_SLOTS).  Preserves DE.
 992  A9EC              wpn_fits:
 993  A9EC CD E3 A9             call wpn_cfg_ptr
 994  A9EF 3E 02                ld a,WCFG_SLOTS
 995  A9F1 ED 31                add hl,a
 996  A9F3 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 997  A9F4 41                   ld b,c
 998  A9F5 04                   inc b
 999  A9F6 0F           .sh:    rrca                            ; bit C into carry
1000  A9F7 10 FD                djnz .sh
1001  A9F9 C9                   ret
1002  A9FA
1003  A9FA              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1004  A9FA              ; From the LEVEL COMPLETE screen (menu.asm, slot 6): page the load-out screen
1005  A9FA              ; into slot 6 (from here, resident) and show it; it goes on to next_level
1006  A9FA              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1007  A9FA              loadout_go:
1008  A9FA ED 91 56 20          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_LOADOUT
1009  A9FE C3 00 C0             jp loadout_screen
1010  AA01
1011  AA01              ; From the main menu (menu.asm, slot 6): the redefine keys screen
1012  AA01              keydef_go:
1013  AA01 ED 91 56 21          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_KEYDEF
1014  AA05 C3 00 C0             jp keydef_screen
1015  AA08
1016  AA08              ; Back to the main menu, from another slot 6 page (the redefine keys screen)
1017  AA08              menu_go:
1018  AA08 ED 91 56 08          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_MENU
1019  AA0C C3 00 C0             jp main_menu
1020  AA0F
1021  AA0F              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1022  AA0F              ; Paused (main_loop: the pause control, K_PAUSE, BREAK or a Mega Drive pad's
1023  AA0F              ; START by default): PAUSED over the view, white on an opaque black ULA box,
1024  AA0F              ; and under it S: SAVE AND QUIT, until the pause control is pressed again.
1025  AA0F              ; Nothing moves meanwhile and the screen keeps its last frame.  S (fixed:
1026  AA0F              ; only while paused) saves the game (save_game) and goes back to the main
1027  AA0F              ; menu; if it can't be saved, SAVE FAILED, and it stays paused.  (Not in a
1028  AA0F              ; wreck: nothing to save.)  Needs the input page in slot 7.
1029  AA0F              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1030  AA0F              PAUSE_ROW       equ 7               ; ULA character row and column of the box
1031  AA0F              PAUSE_COL       equ 12
1032  AA0F              PAUSE_ROW2      equ 9               ; and of the save line
1033  AA0F              PAUSE_COL2      equ 7
1034  AA0F              PAUSE_ATTR      equ 2<<3 | 7        ; paper 2 (opaque black), ink 7 (white)
1035  AA0F              VIEW_ATTR       equ %01000111       ; the viewport's own (ula_init): transparent
1036  AA0F              game_pause:
1037  AA0F 21 7C AA             ld hl,txt_paused
1038  AA12 11 0C 07             ld de,PAUSE_ROW<<8 | PAUSE_COL
1039  AA15 0E 17                ld c,PAUSE_ATTR
1040  AA17 CD 02 A1             call menu_print
1041  AA1A 3A 41 AE             ld a,(vtol_dead)
1042  AA1D B7                   or a
1043  AA1E 20 0B                jr nz,.w                        ; (a wreck: no saving)
1044  AA20 21 8E AA             ld hl,txt_pause_save
1045  AA23 11 07 09             ld de,PAUSE_ROW2<<8 | PAUSE_COL2
1046  AA26 0E 17                ld c,PAUSE_ATTR
1047  AA28 CD 02 A1             call menu_print
1048  AA2B CD 4C A1     .w:     call menu_frame
1049  AA2E CD 00 E0             call kbd_scan
1050  AA31 3A 41 AE             ld a,(vtol_dead)
1051  AA34 B7                   or a
1052  AA35 20 08                jr nz,.np
1053  AA37 21 02 01             ld hl,KB_S                      ; save and quit
1054  AA3A CD F1 E0             call key_code_pressed
1055  AA3D 20 1D                jr nz,.save
1056  AA3F 3E 12        .np:    ld a,K_PAUSE
1057  AA41 CD CF E0             call key_pressed
1058  AA44 28 E5                jr z,.w
1059  AA46 21 85 AA             ld hl,txt_unpaused              ; the box gone: the view again
1060  AA49 11 0C 07             ld de,PAUSE_ROW<<8 | PAUSE_COL
1061  AA4C 0E 47                ld c,VIEW_ATTR
1062  AA4E CD 02 A1             call menu_print
1063  AA51 21 B4 AA             ld hl,txt_unpaused2
1064  AA54 11 07 09             ld de,PAUSE_ROW2<<8 | PAUSE_COL2
1065  AA57 0E 47                ld c,VIEW_ATTR
1066  AA59 C3 02 A1             jp menu_print
1067  AA5C CD 11 AB     .save:  call save_game                  ; (pages the file handler in)
1068  AA5F 38 0A                jr c,.fail
1069  AA61 ED 91 56 08          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_MENU     ; saved: the main menu
1070  AA65 31 85 B5             ld sp,stack_top
1071  AA68 C3 00 C0             jp main_menu
1072  AA6B ED 91 57 0C  .fail:  nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_INPUT     ; couldn't: say so, still paused
1073  AA6F 21 A1 AA             ld hl,txt_save_fail
1074  AA72 11 07 09             ld de,PAUSE_ROW2<<8 | PAUSE_COL2
1075  AA75 0E 17                ld c,PAUSE_ATTR
1076  AA77 CD 02 A1             call menu_print
1077  AA7A 18 AF                jr .w
1078  AA7C
1079  AA7C 20 50 41 55  txt_paused:     db " PAUSED ", 0
1079  AA80 53 45 44 20
1079  AA84 00
1080  AA85 20 20 20 20  txt_unpaused:   db "        ", 0
1080  AA89 20 20 20 20
1080  AA8D 00
1081  AA8E                      ASSERT txt_unpaused - txt_paused == $ - txt_unpaused
1082  AA8E 20 53 3A 20  txt_pause_save: db " S: SAVE AND QUIT ", 0
1082  AA92 53 41 56 45
1082  AA96 20 41 4E 44
1082  AA9A 20 51 55 49
1082  AA9E 54 20 00
1083  AAA1 20 20 20 53  txt_save_fail:  db "   SAVE FAILED    ", 0
1083  AAA5 41 56 45 20
1083  AAA9 46 41 49 4C
1083  AAAD 45 44 20 20
1083  AAB1 20 20 00
1084  AAB4 20 20 20 20  txt_unpaused2:  db "                  ", 0
1084  AAB8 20 20 20 20
1084  AABC 20 20 20 20
1084  AAC0 20 20 20 20
1084  AAC4 20 20 00
1085  AAC7                      ASSERT txt_save_fail - txt_pause_save == txt_unpaused2 - txt_save_fail
1086  AAC7                      ASSERT txt_unpaused2 - txt_save_fail == $ - txt_unpaused2
1087  AAC7
1088  AAC7              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1089  AAC7              ; Save games: ZPDATA/SAVEGAME.ZPS (beside the level files), one game.  Written
1090  AAC7              ; by save and quit (game_pause: save_game), read by CONTINUE on the main menu
1091  AAC7              ; (load_save, continue_game).  "ZPSV", SG_VERSION, then the fields in
1092  AAC7              ; sg_fields, in order: the level and wave, the salvage, the VTOL's integrity
1093  AAC7              ; (vtol_hp), its weapon slots (type and ammo each) and the selected one, the
1094  AAC7              ; store (wpn_stock, wpn_unlocked), and the booster (fitted, fuel).
1095  AAC7              ; Continued, the level starts afresh at the saved wave: its zombies, the
1096  AAC7              ; humans (and this level's rescues so far) and the base are new.
1097  AAC7              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1098  AAC7              SG_VERSION      equ 1
1099  AAC7              sg_fields:      ; address, bytes
1100  AAC7 3D AE                dw level
1101  AAC9 01                   db 1
1102  AACA 46 AE                dw wave_number
1103  AACC 01                   db 1
1104  AACD 48 AE                dw salvage_points
1105  AACF 02                   db 2
1106  AAD0 40 AE                dw vtol_hp
1107  AAD2 01                   db 1
1108  AAD3 30 AE                dw wpn_slots
1109  AAD5 0A                   db WPN_SLOTS*2
1110  AAD6 3A AE                dw wpn_sel
1111  AAD8 01                   db 1
1112  AAD9 A7 AE                dw wpn_stock
1113  AADB 09                   db WPN_LAST+1
1114  AADC B0 AE                dw wpn_unlocked
1115  AADE 09                   db WPN_LAST+1
1116  AADF B9 AE                dw boost_fitted                 ; (and boost_fuel, after it)
1117  AAE1 02                   db 2
1118  AAE2 00 00                dw 0                            ; (the end)
1119  AAE4                      ASSERT boost_fuel == boost_fitted+1
1120  AAE4              SG_SIZE         equ 5+1+1+2+1+WPN_SLOTS*2+1+2*(WPN_LAST+1)+2
1121  AAE4
1122  AAE4              ; Copy the fields: A = 0 the game's into sg_buf, 1 sg_buf's into the game
1123  AAE4              sg_copy:
1124  AAE4 32 9F AB             ld (sg_dir),a
1125  AAE7 DD 21 C7 AA          ld ix,sg_fields
1126  AAEB 11 A6 AB             ld de,sg_buf+5
1127  AAEE DD 6E 00     .lp:    ld l,(ix+0)
1128  AAF1 DD 66 01             ld h,(ix+1)
1129  AAF4 7C                   ld a,h
1130  AAF5 B5                   or l
1131  AAF6 C8                   ret z
1132  AAF7 DD 4E 02             ld c,(ix+2)
1133  AAFA 06 00                ld b,0
1134  AAFC 3A 9F AB             ld a,(sg_dir)
1135  AAFF B7                   or a
1136  AB00 28 06                jr z,.out
1137  AB02 EB                   ex de,hl                        ; into the game: HL = sg_buf
1138  AB03 ED B0                ldir
1139  AB05 EB                   ex de,hl
1140  AB06 18 02                jr .nx
1141  AB08 ED B0        .out:   ldir                            ; out of it: HL = the field
1142  AB0A 01 03 00     .nx:    ld bc,3
1143  AB0D DD 09                add ix,bc
1144  AB0F 18 DD                jr .lp
1145  AB11
1146  AB11              ; Save the game.  Carry set (A = the esxDOS error) if it couldn't be
1147  AB11              ; written.  Leaves the file handler in slot 7.
1148  AB11              save_game:
1149  AB11 21 9B AB             ld hl,sg_magic
1150  AB14 11 A1 AB             ld de,sg_buf
1151  AB17 01 04 00             ld bc,4
1152  AB1A ED B0                ldir
1153  AB1C 3E 01                ld a,SG_VERSION
1154  AB1E 12                   ld (de),a
1155  AB1F AF                   xor a
1156  AB20 CD E4 AA             call sg_copy
1157  AB23 ED 91 57 10          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_FILE
1158  AB27 21 87 AB             ld hl,sg_name
1159  AB2A 11 A1 AB             ld de,sg_buf
1160  AB2D 01 29 00             ld bc,SG_SIZE
1161  AB30 C3 90 E0             jp fh_save
1162  AB33
1163  AB33              ; Read the save game into sg_buf: carry set if there's none, or it isn't a
1164  AB33              ; save game this version can use (its level out of range too).  Leaves the
1165  AB33              ; file handler in slot 7.
1166  AB33              load_save:
1167  AB33 ED 91 57 10          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_FILE
1168  AB37 21 87 AB             ld hl,sg_name
1169  AB3A 11 A1 AB             ld de,sg_buf
1170  AB3D 01 29 00             ld bc,SG_SIZE
1171  AB40 CD 81 E0             call fh_load
1172  AB43 D8                   ret c
1173  AB44 21 D7 FF             ld hl,-SG_SIZE                  ; all of it?
1174  AB47 09                   add hl,bc
1175  AB48 7C                   ld a,h
1176  AB49 B5                   or l
1177  AB4A 20 21                jr nz,.bad
1178  AB4C 21 A1 AB             ld hl,sg_buf
1179  AB4F 11 9B AB             ld de,sg_magic
1180  AB52 06 04                ld b,4
1181  AB54 1A           .m:     ld a,(de)
1182  AB55 BE                   cp (hl)
1183  AB56 20 15                jr nz,.bad
1184  AB58 23                   inc hl
1185  AB59 13                   inc de
1186  AB5A 10 F8                djnz .m
1187  AB5C 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1188  AB5D FE 01                cp SG_VERSION
1189  AB5F 20 0C                jr nz,.bad
1190  AB61 3A A6 AB             ld a,(sg_buf+5)                 ; its level (sg_fields' first)
1191  AB64 B7                   or a
1192  AB65 28 06                jr z,.bad
1193  AB67 FE 33                cp LEVEL_LAST+1
1194  AB69 30 02                jr nc,.bad
1195  AB6B B7                   or a                            ; usable: carry clear
1196  AB6C C9                   ret
1197  AB6D 37           .bad:   scf
1198  AB6E C9                   ret
1199  AB6F
1200  AB6F              ; CONTINUE (main menu, after load_save): a new game with the save game's
1201  AB6F              ; fields over it, into its level at its wave
1202  AB6F              continue_game:
1203  AB6F 31 85 B5             ld sp,stack_top
1204  AB72 CD BC A7             call game_reset
1205  AB75 3E 01                ld a,1
1206  AB77 CD E4 AA             call sg_copy
1207  AB7A 3A 46 AE             ld a,(wave_number)
1208  AB7D B7                   or a
1209  AB7E 20 01                jr nz,.w
1210  AB80 3C                   inc a
1211  AB81 32 A0 AB     .w:     ld (resume_wave),a
1212  AB84 C3 41 A8             jp next_level
1213  AB87
1214  AB87 5A 50 44 41  sg_name:    db "ZPDATA/SAVEGAME.ZPS", 0
1214  AB8B 54 41 2F 53
1214  AB8F 41 56 45 47
1214  AB93 41 4D 45 2E
1214  AB97 5A 50 53 00
1215  AB9B 5A 50 53 56  sg_magic:   db "ZPSV"
1216  AB9F 00           sg_dir:     db 0
1217  ABA0 01           resume_wave: db 1                       ; next_level's first wave (1, or a
1218  ABA1                                                      ; save game's: continue_game)
1219  ABA1 00 00 00...  sg_buf:     ds SG_SIZE
1220  ABCA
1221  ABCA              ; Z if the VTOL is landed: speed 0 and resting on the floor under it (the
1222  ABCA              ; road, the landing pad or a roof: floor_y), and not a wreck.  Landed, its
1223  ABCA              ; weapons don't fire (update_weapons) and humans can board or get off
1224  ABCA              ; (human_update).
1225  ABCA              vtol_landed:
1226  ABCA 3A 41 AE             ld a,(vtol_dead)
1227  ABCD B7                   or a
1228  ABCE C0                   ret nz                          ; a wreck
1229  ABCF 3A 2F AE             ld a,(speed)
1230  ABD2 B7                   or a
1231  ABD3 C0                   ret nz                          ; moving
1232  ABD4 CD B9 A9             call floor_y
1233  ABD7 ED 5B 29 AE          ld de,(cam_y)
1234  ABDB B7                   or a
1235  ABDC ED 52                sbc hl,de                       ; Z: resting on it
1236  ABDE C9                   ret
1237  ABDF
1238  ABDF              ; The VTOL's footprint: the corners and the long-side midpoints of its
1239  ABDF              ; VTOL_HL x VTOL_HW (half size) rectangle, turned to the heading, round
1240  ABDF              ; (cam_x, cam_z).  The rectangle is smaller than a tile, so these six points
1241  ABDF              ; find every cell it overlaps (to within a tile corner nicking a long side).
1242  ABDF              ; HL = vg_max = the highest ground under them; Z if all road (0).
1243  ABDF              vtol_ground:
1244  ABDF 3A 2C AE             ld a,(yaw)                      ; forward (sin, cos), right (cos, -sin)
1245  ABE2 CD 33 AD             call get_sin
1246  ABE5 22 BD AE             ld (vg_sin),hl
1247  ABE8 3A 2C AE             ld a,(yaw)
1248  ABEB C6 40                add a,64
1249  ABED CD 33 AD             call get_sin
1250  ABF0 22 BF AE             ld (vg_cos),hl
1251  ABF3 EB                   ex de,hl
1252  ABF4 21 40 00             ld hl,VTOL_HL
1253  ABF7 CD EA AD             call fmul
1254  ABFA 22 C3 AE             ld (vg_fz),hl                   ; nose offset z = HL cos
1255  ABFD ED 5B BF AE          ld de,(vg_cos)
1256  AC01 21 13 00             ld hl,VTOL_HW
1257  AC04 CD EA AD             call fmul
1258  AC07 22 C5 AE             ld (vg_rx),hl                   ; side offset x = HW cos
1259  AC0A ED 5B BD AE          ld de,(vg_sin)
1260  AC0E 21 40 00             ld hl,VTOL_HL
1261  AC11 CD EA AD             call fmul
1262  AC14 22 C1 AE             ld (vg_fx),hl                   ; nose offset x = HL sin
1263  AC17 ED 5B BD AE          ld de,(vg_sin)
1264  AC1B 21 13 00             ld hl,VTOL_HW
1265  AC1E CD EA AD             call fmul
1266  AC21 CD DE AD             call neg_hl
1267  AC24 22 C7 AE             ld (vg_rz),hl                   ; side offset z = -HW sin
1268  AC27 21 00 00             ld hl,0
1269  AC2A 22 C9 AE             ld (vg_max),hl
1270  AC2D 2A C1 AE             ld hl,(vg_fx)                   ; nose: the front corners
1271  AC30 ED 5B C3 AE          ld de,(vg_fz)
1272  AC34 CD 58 AC             call .side
1273  AC37 21 00 00             ld hl,0                         ; the long-side midpoints
1274  AC3A 11 00 00             ld de,0
1275  AC3D CD 58 AC             call .side
1276  AC40 2A C1 AE             ld hl,(vg_fx)                   ; tail: the back corners
1277  AC43 CD DE AD             call neg_hl
1278  AC46 E5                   push hl
1279  AC47 2A C3 AE             ld hl,(vg_fz)
1280  AC4A CD DE AD             call neg_hl
1281  AC4D EB                   ex de,hl
1282  AC4E E1                   pop hl
1283  AC4F CD 58 AC             call .side
1284  AC52 2A C9 AE             ld hl,(vg_max)
1285  AC55 7C                   ld a,h
1286  AC56 B5                   or l
1287  AC57 C9                   ret
1288  AC58              ; HL, DE = an offset along the length (x, z): the points it +- the side offset
1289  AC58 E5           .side:  push hl
1290  AC59 D5                   push de
1291  AC5A ED 4B C5 AE          ld bc,(vg_rx)
1292  AC5E 09                   add hl,bc
1293  AC5F EB                   ex de,hl
1294  AC60 ED 4B C7 AE          ld bc,(vg_rz)
1295  AC64 09                   add hl,bc
1296  AC65 EB                   ex de,hl
1297  AC66 CD 7B AC             call .pt
1298  AC69 D1                   pop de
1299  AC6A E1                   pop hl
1300  AC6B ED 4B C5 AE          ld bc,(vg_rx)
1301  AC6F B7                   or a
1302  AC70 ED 42                sbc hl,bc
1303  AC72 EB                   ex de,hl
1304  AC73 ED 4B C7 AE          ld bc,(vg_rz)
1305  AC77 B7                   or a
1306  AC78 ED 42                sbc hl,bc
1307  AC7A EB                   ex de,hl
1308  AC7B              ; HL, DE = offset (x, z) from the centre: vg_max = max(vg_max, its ground)
1309  AC7B ED 4B 24 AE  .pt:    ld bc,(cam_x+1)
1310  AC7F 09                   add hl,bc
1311  AC80 7C                   ld a,h                          ; column
1312  AC81 EB                   ex de,hl
1313  AC82 ED 4B 27 AE          ld bc,(cam_z+1)
1314  AC86 09                   add hl,bc
1315  AC87 6C                   ld l,h                          ; row
1316  AC88 26 00                ld h,0
1317  AC8A                      ROWOFS
1317  AC8A 29          >        add hl,hl
1317  AC8B 29          >        add hl,hl
1317  AC8C 29          >        add hl,hl
1317  AC8D 29          >        add hl,hl
1317  AC8E 29          >        add hl,hl
1317  AC8F 29          >        add hl,hl
1318  AC90 ED 31                add hl,a
1319  AC92 11 00 70             ld de,city_map
1320  AC95 19                   add hl,de
1321  AC96 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1322  AC97 E6 1F                and CELL_CODE
1323  AC99 C8                   ret z                           ; road
1324  AC9A CD AA AC             call bld_height
1325  AC9D ED 5B C9 AE          ld de,(vg_max)
1326  ACA1 B7                   or a
1327  ACA2 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1328  ACA4 D8                   ret c                           ; lower than the highest so far
1329  ACA5 19                   add hl,de
1330  ACA6 22 C9 AE             ld (vg_max),hl
1331  ACA9 C9                   ret
1332  ACAA
1333  ACAA              ; A = map byte -> HL = height in world units (levels 0-15, PAD_CODE = the
1334  ACAA              ; base landing pad, TERRACE_CODE = the terrace round it)
1335  ACAA              bld_height:
1336  ACAA E6 1F                and 31
1337  ACAC 87                   add a,a
1338  ACAD 21 31 A0             ld hl,htab
1339  ACB0 ED 31                add hl,a
1340  ACB2 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1341  ACB3 23                   inc hl
1342  ACB4 66                   ld h,(hl)
1343  ACB5 6F                   ld l,a
1344  ACB6 C9                   ret
1345  ACB7
1346  ACB7              ;==============================================================================
1347  ACB7              ; Per-frame view setup: trig, rotated building edge vectors, horizon
1348  ACB7              ;==============================================================================
1349  ACB7              setup_frame:
1350  ACB7 3A 2C AE             ld a,(yaw)
1351  ACBA CD 33 AD             call get_sin
1352  ACBD 22 59 B4             ld (sin_y),hl
1353  ACC0 3A 2C AE             ld a,(yaw)
1354  ACC3 C6 40                add a,64
1355  ACC5 CD 33 AD             call get_sin
1356  ACC8 22 5B B4             ld (cos_y),hl
1357  ACCB 3A 2E AE             ld a,(pitch)
1358  ACCE CD 33 AD             call get_sin
1359  ACD1 22 5D B4             ld (sin_p),hl
1360  ACD4 3A 2E AE             ld a,(pitch)
1361  ACD7 C6 40                add a,64
1362  ACD9 CD 33 AD             call get_sin
1363  ACDC 22 5F B4             ld (cos_p),hl
1364  ACDF
1365  ACDF                      ; ground plane relative to camera: dy = -camY
1366  ACDF 2A 29 AE             ld hl,(cam_y)
1367  ACE2 CD DE AD             call neg_hl
1368  ACE5 E5                   push hl
1369  ACE6 ED 5B 5F B4          ld de,(cos_p)
1370  ACEA CD EA AD             call fmul
1371  ACED 22 61 B4             ld (yb),hl                      ; Y contribution of ground
1372  ACF0 E1                   pop hl
1373  ACF1 ED 5B 5D B4          ld de,(sin_p)
1374  ACF5 CD EA AD             call fmul
1375  ACF8 22 63 B4             ld (zb),hl                      ; Z contribution of ground
1376  ACFB
1377  ACFB                      ; frustum cull factor c = cosP - 0.5|sinP|
1378  ACFB 2A 5D B4             ld hl,(sin_p)
1379  ACFE CD E5 AD             call abs_hl
1380  AD01 CD 1E AE             call zhalf
1381  AD04 EB                   ex de,hl
1382  AD05 2A 5F B4             ld hl,(cos_p)
1383  AD08 B7                   or a
1384  AD09 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1385  AD0B 22 75 B4             ld (cull_c),hl
1386  AD0E
1387  AD0E 21 00 01             ld hl,BLD_SIZE
1388  AD11 DD 21 65 B4          ld ix,vecs0
1389  AD15 CD 3F AD             call make_vecs
1390  AD18
1391  AD18 3A 25 AE             ld a,(cam_x+2)
1392  AD1B 32 7D B4             ld (cx),a
1393  AD1E 3A 28 AE             ld a,(cam_z+2)
1394  AD21 32 7E B4             ld (cz),a
1395  AD24
1396  AD24 3A 2E AE             ld a,(pitch)
1397  AD27 C6 18                add a,PMAX
1398  AD29 21 00 A0             ld hl,hztab
1399  AD2C ED 31                add hl,a
1400  AD2E 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1401  AD2F 32 58 B4             ld (horizon),a
1402  AD32 C9                   ret
1403  AD33
1404  AD33              ; A = angle -> HL = sin (S7.8).  Pages the sine table into slot 0 (PAGE_SIN):
1405  AD33              ; call it only outside the Layer 2 drawing (it is: setup_frame, the player,
1406  AD33              ; vtol_ground), never between a section's paging and its writes.
1407  AD33              get_sin:
1408  AD33 ED 91 50 0E          nextreg SIN_MMU,PAGE_SIN        ; the table at $0000 (slot 0)
1409  AD37 6F                   ld l,a
1410  AD38 26 00                ld h,high sin_lo
1411  AD3A 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1412  AD3B 24                   inc h
1413  AD3C 66                   ld h,(hl)
1414  AD3D 6F                   ld l,a
1415  AD3E C9                   ret
1416  AD3F
1417  AD3F              ; Rotate the building edge vectors (s,0,0) and (0,0,s) into camera space.
1418  AD3F              ; HL = s, IX -> 16 bytes: VX(X,Y,Z) VZ(X,Y,Z) corner offset (X,Z1)
1419  AD3F              make_vecs:
1420  AD3F 22 77 B4             ld (mv_s),hl
1421  AD42 ED 5B 5B B4          ld de,(cos_y)
1422  AD46 CD EA AD             call fmul
1423  AD49 22 79 B4             ld (mv_c),hl                    ; s*cosY
1424  AD4C 2A 77 B4             ld hl,(mv_s)
1425  AD4F ED 5B 59 B4          ld de,(sin_y)
1426  AD53 CD EA AD             call fmul
1427  AD56 22 7B B4             ld (mv_n),hl                    ; s*sinY
1428  AD59                      ; VX = ( s cosY, -s sinY sinP, s sinY cosP )
1429  AD59 2A 79 B4             ld hl,(mv_c)
1430  AD5C DD 75 00             ld (ix+0),l
1431  AD5F DD 74 01             ld (ix+1),h
1432  AD62 2A 7B B4             ld hl,(mv_n)
1433  AD65 ED 5B 5D B4          ld de,(sin_p)
1434  AD69 CD EA AD             call fmul
1435  AD6C CD DE AD             call neg_hl
1436  AD6F DD 75 02             ld (ix+2),l
1437  AD72 DD 74 03             ld (ix+3),h
1438  AD75 2A 7B B4             ld hl,(mv_n)
1439  AD78 ED 5B 5F B4          ld de,(cos_p)
1440  AD7C CD EA AD             call fmul
1441  AD7F DD 75 04             ld (ix+4),l
1442  AD82 DD 74 05             ld (ix+5),h
1443  AD85                      ; VZ = ( -s sinY, -s cosY sinP, s cosY cosP )
1444  AD85 2A 7B B4             ld hl,(mv_n)
1445  AD88 CD DE AD             call neg_hl
1446  AD8B DD 75 06             ld (ix+6),l
1447  AD8E DD 74 07             ld (ix+7),h
1448  AD91 2A 79 B4             ld hl,(mv_c)
1449  AD94 ED 5B 5D B4          ld de,(sin_p)
1450  AD98 CD EA AD             call fmul
1451  AD9B CD DE AD             call neg_hl
1452  AD9E DD 75 08             ld (ix+8),l
1453  ADA1 DD 74 09             ld (ix+9),h
1454  ADA4 2A 79 B4             ld hl,(mv_c)
1455  ADA7 ED 5B 5F B4          ld de,(cos_p)
1456  ADAB CD EA AD             call fmul
1457  ADAE DD 75 0A             ld (ix+10),l
1458  ADB1 DD 74 0B             ld (ix+11),h
1459  ADB4                      ; cell centre -> footprint corner (-s/2,-s/2) in yaw space
1460  ADB4 2A 7B B4             ld hl,(mv_n)
1461  ADB7 ED 5B 79 B4          ld de,(mv_c)
1462  ADBB B7                   or a
1463  ADBC ED 52                sbc hl,de
1464  ADBE CB 2C                sra h
1465  ADC0 CB 1D                rr l
1466  ADC2 DD 75 0C             ld (ix+12),l                    ; X: s/2 (sinY - cosY)
1467  ADC5 DD 74 0D             ld (ix+13),h
1468  ADC8 2A 7B B4             ld hl,(mv_n)
1469  ADCB ED 5B 79 B4          ld de,(mv_c)
1470  ADCF 19                   add hl,de
1471  ADD0 CB 2C                sra h
1472  ADD2 CB 1D                rr l
1473  ADD4 CD DE AD             call neg_hl
1474  ADD7 DD 75 0E             ld (ix+14),l                    ; Z1: -s/2 (sinY + cosY)
1475  ADDA DD 74 0F             ld (ix+15),h
1476  ADDD C9                   ret
1477  ADDE
1478  ADDE              ;==============================================================================
1479  ADDE              ; Fixed point maths
1480  ADDE              ;==============================================================================
1481  ADDE
1482  ADDE              ; HL = -HL
1483  ADDE              neg_hl:
1484  ADDE AF                   xor a
1485  ADDF 95                   sub l
1486  ADE0 6F                   ld l,a
1487  ADE1 9F                   sbc a,a
1488  ADE2 94                   sub h
1489  ADE3 67                   ld h,a
1490  ADE4 C9                   ret
1491  ADE5
1492  ADE5              ; HL = |HL|
1493  ADE5              abs_hl:
1494  ADE5 CB 7C                bit 7,h
1495  ADE7 C8                   ret z
1496  ADE8 18 F4                jr neg_hl
1497  ADEA
1498  ADEA              ; fmul: HL = HL * DE >> 8
1499  ADEA              ;   HL = signed 16-bit integer, DE = S7.8 factor in -1.0..+1.0
1500  ADEA              ;   Sign/magnitude using two 8x8 MUL D,E.  Preserves IX, IY.
1501  ADEA              fmul:
1502  ADEA 7C                   ld a,h
1503  ADEB AA                   xor d
1504  ADEC F5                   push af                         ; result sign in bit 7
1505  ADED CB 7C                bit 7,h
1506  ADEF 28 06                jr z,.ap
1507  ADF1 AF                   xor a
1508  ADF2 95                   sub l
1509  ADF3 6F                   ld l,a
1510  ADF4 9F                   sbc a,a
1511  ADF5 94                   sub h
1512  ADF6 67                   ld h,a
1513  ADF7 CB 7A        .ap:    bit 7,d
1514  ADF9 28 06                jr z,.bp
1515  ADFB AF                   xor a
1516  ADFC 93                   sub e
1517  ADFD 5F                   ld e,a
1518  ADFE 9F                   sbc a,a
1519  ADFF 92                   sub d
1520  AE00 57                   ld d,a
1521  AE01 7A           .bp:    ld a,d
1522  AE02 B7                   or a
1523  AE03 20 10                jr nz,.one                      ; |factor| == 1.0
1524  AE05 7B                   ld a,e
1525  AE06 55                   ld d,l
1526  AE07 ED 30                mul d,e                         ; lo * f
1527  AE09 4A                   ld c,d
1528  AE0A 54                   ld d,h
1529  AE0B 5F                   ld e,a
1530  AE0C ED 30                mul d,e                         ; hi * f
1531  AE0E 7B                   ld a,e
1532  AE0F 81                   add a,c
1533  AE10 6F                   ld l,a
1534  AE11 7A                   ld a,d
1535  AE12 CE 00                adc a,0
1536  AE14 67                   ld h,a
1537  AE15 F1           .one:   pop af
1538  AE16 F0                   ret p
1539  AE17 AF                   xor a
1540  AE18 95                   sub l
1541  AE19 6F                   ld l,a
1542  AE1A 9F                   sbc a,a
1543  AE1B 94                   sub h
1544  AE1C 67                   ld h,a
1545  AE1D C9                   ret
1546  AE1E
1547  AE1E              ; HL = 0.5 * HL (signed): the top/bottom frustum slope, VIEW_CY/128
1548  AE1E              zhalf:
1549  AE1E CB 2C                sra h
1550  AE20 CB 1D                rr l
1551  AE22 C9                   ret
1552  AE23
1553  AE23              ;==============================================================================
1554  AE23              ; Shared data (resident: used by the main code and by both pages)
1555  AE23              ;==============================================================================
1556  AE23              ; --- player state ---
1557  AE23              ; (cam_x .. wflash_t are set from start_state by newGame)
1558  AE23 00 00 00     cam_x:      ds 3                        ; frac, integer lo, hi
1559  AE26 00 00 00     cam_z:      ds 3
1560  AE29 00 00        cam_y:      ds 2
1561  AE2B 00           yaw_frac:   ds 1                        ; fraction below yaw (keep together:
1562  AE2C 00           yaw:        ds 1                        ; yaw_frac/yaw is one 8.8 word)
1563  AE2D 00           pitch_frac: ds 1                        ; fraction below pitch (keep together:
1564  AE2E 00           pitch:      ds 1                        ; pitch_frac/pitch is one signed 8.8
1565  AE2F                                                      ; word; drawing uses pitch only)
1566  AE2F 00           speed:      ds 1
1567  AE30              ; weapon slots: type, ammo (AMMO_INF = 255 = infinite).  Address of slot n
1568  AE30              ; is wpn_slots + n*2.
1569  AE30 00 00 00...  wpn_slots:  ds WPN_SLOTS*2
1570  AE3A 00           wpn_sel:    ds 1                        ; selected slot (keys 1-5), fires on SPACE
1571  AE3B 00           wflash_slot: ds 1                       ; empty slot requested: which, and
1572  AE3C 00           wflash_t:   ds 1                        ; frames of yellow/blue flash left
1573  AE3D 01           level:      db START_LEVEL              ; current level (newGame: START_LEVEL)
1574  AE3E 06           view_r:     db VIEW_R                   ; draw distance, tiles (load_level)
1575  AE3F 05           dot_r:      db VIEW_R-1                 ; street dots' radius (load_level)
1576  AE40 32           vtol_hp:    db VTOL_HP                  ; the VTOL's hit points (newGame)
1577  AE41 00           vtol_dead:  db 0                        ; 1: destroyed (hit points 0)
1578  AE42 00           dead_down:  db 0                        ; 1: the wreck has hit the ground
1579  AE43 00           dead_vy:    db 0                        ; its falling speed, units a frame
1580  AE44 64           dead_wait:  db DEAD_PAUSE               ; frames on the ground to GAME OVER
1581  AE45 00           level_done: db 0                        ; 1: the level's last wave is wiped out
1582  AE46 01           wave_number: db 1                       ; current zombie wave of the level,
1583  AE47                                                      ; 1..LV_WAVES (newGame: 1)
1584  AE47 00           game_over:  db 0                        ; 1: the landing pad was destroyed
1585  AE48 00 00        salvage_points: dw 0                    ; salvage points, 16-bit (newGame: 0)
1586  AE4A 00           gun_tilt:   db 0                        ; centre gun tilt, signed (weapons_reset)
1587  AE4B 00           barr_n:     db 0                        ; barricades in barr_tab (barricade_init)
1588  AE4C 00 00 00...  barr_tab:   ds NBARR*BARR_REC           ; barricades: x, z, hit points
1589  AEA6 40           sight_y:    db VIEW_CY                  ; the gunsight's row (gun_aim)
1590  AEA7              ; Weapons unlocked by salvage (salvage_award, menu.asm), not yet fitted: a
1591  AEA7              ; count per weapon type (WPN_*), for the load-out page (newGame: none)
1592  AEA7 00 00 00...  wpn_stock:  ds WPN_LAST+1
1593  AEB0              ; 1: the weapon type has been unlocked (fitted at the start, or by salvage):
1594  AEB0              ; the load-out screen lists it, even with none in store (newGame)
1595  AEB0 00 00 00...  wpn_unlocked: ds WPN_LAST+1
1596  AEB9 00           boost_fitted: db 0                      ; 1: the booster module (salvage)
1597  AEBA 00           boost_fuel: db 0                        ; its fuel, 0..BOOST_FUEL_MAX
1598  AEBB 00           boost_on:   db 0                        ; 1: boosting (speed BOOST_SPEED)
1599  AEBC 00           boost_spd:  db 0                        ; the speed before the boost
1600  AEBD                      ASSERT wpn_unlocked == wpn_stock + WPN_LAST + 1
1601  AEBD 00 00        vg_sin:     dw 0                        ; vtol_ground: heading sin, cos,
1602  AEBF 00 00        vg_cos:     dw 0
1603  AEC1 00 00        vg_fx:      dw 0                        ; nose offset (x, z),
1604  AEC3 00 00        vg_fz:      dw 0
1605  AEC5 00 00        vg_rx:      dw 0                        ; side offset (x, z),
1606  AEC7 00 00        vg_rz:      dw 0
1607  AEC9 00 00        vg_max:     dw 0                        ; and the highest ground found
1608  AECB
1609  AECB              ; The start of a game: copied over cam_x .. wflash_t by newGame
1610  AECB              start_state:
1611  AECB 00 80 20                 db 0, $80, PAD_X            ; cam_x: landed on the base pad
1612  AECE 00 80 20                 db 0, $80, PAD_Z            ; cam_z  (gentables.py)
1613  AED1 4D 00                    dw PAD_H+COLL_H+1           ; cam_y: resting on it
1614  AED3 00 00                    db 0, BASE_YAW              ; yaw: facing the city centre
1615  AED5 00 00                    db 0, 0                     ; pitch: level
1616  AED7 00                       db 0                        ; speed
1617  AED8 01 FF                    db WPN_GUN, AMMO_INF        ; slot 0: centre gun, infinite
1618  AEDA 00 00                    db WPN_NONE, 0              ; 1: left outer pylon: empty
1619  AEDC 00 00                    db WPN_NONE, 0              ; 2: left inner pylon: empty
1620  AEDE 00 00                    db WPN_NONE, 0              ; 3: right inner pylon: empty
1621  AEE0 00 00                    db WPN_NONE, 0              ; 4: right outer pylon: empty
1622  AEE2 00                       db WPN_CENTRE               ; wpn_sel
1623  AEE3 00 00                    db 0, 0                     ; wflash_slot, wflash_t
1624  AEE5              start_state_end:
1625  AEE5                          ASSERT start_state_end - start_state == wflash_t + 1 - cam_x
1626  AEE5 00 00 00...  bullets:    ds NBULLETS*BREC            ; machine gun rounds (weapons.asm)
1627  B16D 00 00 00...  laser_rec:  ds BREC                     ; laser beam, drawn like a round
1628  B188                                                      ; (state 3); must follow bullets
1629  B188 00 00 00...  particles:  ds NPART*PREC               ; zombie burst particles (must follow
1630  B458                                                      ; laser_rec: newGame clears them all)
1631  B458
1632  B458              ; --- view, set up each frame by setup_frame ---
1633  B458 40           horizon:    db VIEW_CY
1634  B459 00 00        sin_y:      dw 0
1635  B45B 00 00        cos_y:      dw 0
1636  B45D 00 00        sin_p:      dw 0
1637  B45F 00 00        cos_p:      dw 0
1638  B461 00 00        yb:         dw 0
1639  B463 00 00        zb:         dw 0
1640  B465 00 00 00...  vecs0:      ds 16
1641  B475 00 00        cull_c:     dw 0
1642  B477 00 00        mv_s:       dw 0
1643  B479 00 00        mv_c:       dw 0
1644  B47B 00 00        mv_n:       dw 0
1645  B47D 00           cx:         db 0
1646  B47E 00           cz:         db 0
1647  B47F 00           seg_base:   db 0                        ; 8K page of row 0 of the draw buffer
1648  B480
1649  B480              ; --- fps ---
1650  B480 00 00        fps_line:   dw 0
1651  B482 00           fps_vframes: db 0
1652  B483 00           fps_count:  db 0
1653  B484 00           fps_value:  db 0
1654  B485
1655  B485
1656  B485 00 00 00...              ds 256                      ; stack (must stay resident)
1657  B585              stack_top:

1660  B585                      ASSERT $ <= MAIN_ADDR+MEMBANK_SIZE

   1+ E000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ E000              ; Z-POLICE - city page (8K page PAGE_CITY, runs at $E000)
   3+ E000              ;
   4+ E000              ; All Layer 2 drawing: back-to-front city traversal, building transform,
   5+ E000              ; culling and shading, 3D clipping, projection, polygon fill, lines, the
   6+ E000              ; viewport clear, ground dots and the gunsight.  Paged in by main_loop with
   7+ E000              ; nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_CITY.  Shared variables and tables live in zpolice.asm.

  13+ E0C0
  14+ E0C0 00 00 00...          ALIGN 256
  15+ E100              ; Unrolled span writer: entry = push_end - 1 - pairs (pairs 1..128).  SP is
  16+ E100              ; borrowed (it points into the span), so an interrupt pushes its return
  17+ E100              ; address just below SP: harmless while PUSHes remain, as they overwrite it,
  18+ E100              ; but not after the last one.  So interrupts are off from just before the
  19+ E100              ; last PUSH until SP is back (a few instructions: one arriving meanwhile
  20+ E100              ; waits, as the interrupt line is held far longer).  The routine
  21+ E100              ; (im2_routine) also switches to its own stack at once.
  22+ E100              push_blk:
  23+ E100                      REPT 127
