 106+ E769 8F                   adc a,a
 107+ E76A D1                   pop de
 108+ E76B D5                   push de
 109+ E76C 50                   ld d,b
 110+ E76D ED 30                mul d,e                         ; ah*tl
 111+ E76F 19                   add hl,de
 112+ E770 CE 00                adc a,0                         ; A:HL = middle sum
 113+ E772 6C                   ld l,h
 114+ E773 67                   ld h,a
 115+ E774 D1                   pop de
 116+ E775 5A                   ld e,d
 117+ E776 50                   ld d,b
 118+ E777 ED 30                mul d,e                         ; ah*th
 119+ E779 19                   add hl,de
 120+ E77A 3A AE FC             ld a,(mf_sign)
 121+ E77D CB 7F                bit 7,a
 122+ E77F C8                   ret z
 123+ E780 C3 DE AD             jp neg_hl
 124+ E783
 125+ E783              ; IX = (HL << 16) / DE   with HL < DE (unsigned) -> 0.16 fraction
 126+ E783              div_frac:
 127+ E783 DD 21 00 00          ld ix,0
 128+ E787 3E 10                ld a,16
 129+ E789 DD 29        .lp:    add ix,ix
 130+ E78B 29                   add hl,hl
 131+ E78C 38 08                jr c,.sub
 132+ E78E B7                   or a
 133+ E78F ED 52                sbc hl,de
 134+ E791 30 06                jr nc,.set
 135+ E793 19                   add hl,de
 136+ E794 18 05                jr .nx
 137+ E796 B7           .sub:   or a
 138+ E797 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 139+ E799 DD 23        .set:   inc ix
 140+ E79B 3D           .nx:    dec a
 141+ E79C 20 EB                jr nz,.lp
 142+ E79E C9                   ret
 143+ E79F
 144+ E79F              ; E = A / C, A = A mod C  (8-bit unsigned)
 145+ E79F              div8:
 146+ E79F 5F                   ld e,a
 147+ E7A0 AF                   xor a
 148+ E7A1 06 08                ld b,8
 149+ E7A3 CB 23        .lp:    sla e
 150+ E7A5 17                   rla
 151+ E7A6 38 03                jr c,.s
 152+ E7A8 B9                   cp c
 153+ E7A9 38 02                jr c,.n
 154+ E7AB 91           .s:     sub c
 155+ E7AC 1C                   inc e
 156+ E7AD 10 F4        .n:     djnz .lp
 157+ E7AF C9                   ret
 158+ E7B0
 159+ E7B0              ;==============================================================================
 160+ E7B0              ; Perspective projection
 161+ E7B0              ;   in : HL -> X,Y,Z (camera space, inside the view frustum, Z > 0)
 162+ E7B0              ;   out: A = screen x (0..255), C = screen y (0..VIEW_H-1)
 163+ E7B0              ;   sx = 128 + 128*X/Z,  sy = 64 - 128*Y/Z   (90 x 53 degree field of view)
 164+ E7B0              ;   Z is normalised to 9 bits (256..511) with a barrel shift (count from a
 165+ E7B0              ;   log2 table) that is also applied to X and Y, which are then multiplied
 166+ E7B0              ;   by a 16-bit reciprocal from rtab.  |X| <= Z keeps Xn within 9 bits.
 167+ E7B0              ;==============================================================================
 168+ E7B0              project:
 169+ E7B0 4E                   ld c,(hl)
 170+ E7B1 23                   inc hl
 171+ E7B2 46                   ld b,(hl)
 172+ E7B3 23                   inc hl                          ; BC = X
 173+ E7B4 5E                   ld e,(hl)
 174+ E7B5 23                   inc hl
 175+ E7B6 56                   ld d,(hl)
 176+ E7B7 23                   inc hl                          ; DE = Y
 177+ E7B8 D5                   push de
 178+ E7B9 C5                   push bc
 179+ E7BA 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 180+ E7BB 23                   inc hl
 181+ E7BC 66                   ld h,(hl)
 182+ E7BD 6F                   ld l,a                          ; HL = Z
 183+ E7BE CD 01 E8             call zpatch
 184+ E7C1
 185+ E7C1 E1                   pop hl                          ; X
 186+ E7C2 7C                   ld a,h
 187+ E7C3 F5                   push af
 188+ E7C4 CD 45 E8             call scale_comp
 189+ E7C7 4F                   ld c,a
 190+ E7C8 F1                   pop af
 191+ E7C9 17                   rla
 192+ E7CA 38 09                jr c,.xn
 193+ E7CC 3E 80                ld a,128
 194+ E7CE 81                   add a,c
 195+ E7CF 30 0A                jr nc,.xd
 196+ E7D1 3E FF                ld a,255
 197+ E7D3 18 06                jr .xd
 198+ E7D5 3E 80        .xn:    ld a,128
 199+ E7D7 91                   sub c
 200+ E7D8 30 01                jr nc,.xd
 201+ E7DA AF                   xor a
 202+ E7DB 32 AD FC     .xd:    ld (pj_sx),a
 203+ E7DE
 204+ E7DE E1                   pop hl                          ; Y
 205+ E7DF                      ; fall through
 206+ E7DF
 207+ E7DF              ; HL = Y -> C = screen y, A = screen x of the last project.  Uses the Z
 208+ E7DF              ; patched into scale_comp by the last project, so on its own it projects a
 209+ E7DF              ; point straight above or below that one when pitch is 0 (same X and Z).
 210+ E7DF              proj_y:
 211+ E7DF 7C                   ld a,h
 212+ E7E0 F5                   push af
 213+ E7E1 CD 45 E8             call scale_comp
 214+ E7E4 4F                   ld c,a
 215+ E7E5 F1                   pop af
 216+ E7E6 17                   rla
 217+ E7E7 38 08                jr c,.yn
 218+ E7E9 3E 40                ld a,VIEW_CY
 219+ E7EB 91                   sub c
 220+ E7EC 30 0E                jr nc,.yd
 221+ E7EE AF                   xor a
 222+ E7EF 18 0B                jr .yd
 223+ E7F1 3E 40        .yn:    ld a,VIEW_CY
 224+ E7F3 81                   add a,c
 225+ E7F4 38 04                jr c,.yc
 226+ E7F6 FE 80                cp VIEW_H
 227+ E7F8 38 02                jr c,.yd
 228+ E7FA 3E 7F        .yc:    ld a,VIEW_H-1
 229+ E7FC 4F           .yd:    ld c,a
 230+ E7FD 3A AD FC             ld a,(pj_sx)
 231+ E800 C9                   ret
 232+ E801
 233+ E801              ; HL = Z (camera space): normalise it and patch its shift and reciprocal
 234+ E801              ; into scale_comp, which then gives A = |component| * 128 / Z
 235+ E801              zpatch:
 236+ E801 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 237+ E803 20 03                jr nz,.z1
 238+ E805 B4                   or h
 239+ E806 20 03                jr nz,.zok
 240+ E808 21 01 00     .z1:    ld hl,1
 241+ E80B 7C           .zok:   ld a,h
 242+ E80C FE 02                cp 2
 243+ E80E 38 0B                jr c,.lt512
 244+ E810 5F                   ld e,a                          ; Z >= 512: shift right by log2(H)
 245+ E811 16 E6                ld d,high lgtab
 246+ E813 1A                   ld a,(de)
 247+ E814 47                   ld b,a
 248+ E815 EB                   ex de,hl
 249+ E816 ED 2A                bsrl de,b
 250+ E818 EB                   ex de,hl
 251+ E819 18 14                jr .nrm
 252+ E81B 06 00        .lt512: ld b,0
 253+ E81D B7                   or a
 254+ E81E 20 0F                jr nz,.nrm                      ; 256..511 already normalised
 255+ E820 5D                   ld e,l                          ; Z < 256: shift left by 8-log2(L)
 256+ E821 16 E6                ld d,high lgtab
 257+ E823 1A                   ld a,(de)
 258+ E824 D6 08                sub 8
 259+ E826 4F                   ld c,a                          ; negative shift count
 260+ E827 ED 44                neg
 261+ E829 47                   ld b,a
 262+ E82A EB                   ex de,hl
 263+ E82B ED 28                bsla de,b
 264+ E82D EB                   ex de,hl
 265+ E82E 41                   ld b,c
 266+ E82F 78           .nrm:   ld a,b                          ; patch shift and reciprocal into
 267+ E830 32 50 E8             ld (scale_comp.sh+1),a                  ; scale_comp's immediates
 268+ E833 26 E4                ld h,high rtab_lo               ; L = Zn - 256
 269+ E835 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 270+ E836 32 6C E8             ld (scale_comp.rl+1),a
 271+ E839 32 82 E8             ld (scale_comp.r16+1),a
 272+ E83C 24                   inc h
 273+ E83D 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 274+ E83E 32 72 E8             ld (scale_comp.rh+1),a
 275+ E841 32 83 E8             ld (scale_comp.r16+2),a
 276+ E844 C9                   ret
 277+ E845
 278+ E845              ; HL = signed component -> A = round(|HL| * 128 / Z)
 279+ E845              ; The shift and reciprocal for this Z are patched in by project.
 280+ E845              scale_comp:
 281+ E845 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 282+ E847 28 06                jr z,.ap
 283+ E849 AF                   xor a
 284+ E84A 95                   sub l
 285+ E84B 6F                   ld l,a
 286+ E84C 9F                   sbc a,a
 287+ E84D 94                   sub h
 288+ E84E 67                   ld h,a
 289+ E84F              .ap:
 290+ E84F 3E 00        .sh:    ld a,0
 291+ E851 B7                   or a
 292+ E852 28 10                jr z,.sd
 293+ E854 EB                   ex de,hl
 294+ E855 FA 5E E8             jp m,.l
 295+ E858 47                   ld b,a
 296+ E859 ED 2A                bsrl de,b
 297+ E85B EB                   ex de,hl
 298+ E85C 18 06                jr .sd
 299+ E85E ED 44        .l:     neg
 300+ E860 47                   ld b,a
 301+ E861 ED 28                bsla de,b
 302+ E863 EB                   ex de,hl
 303+ E864 7C           .sd:    ld a,h
 304+ E865 FE 02                cp 2
 305+ E867 30 27                jr nc,.big
 306+ E869 44           .ok:    ld b,h                          ; bit 8 of Xn
 307+ E86A 55                   ld d,l
 308+ E86B 1E 00        .rl:    ld e,0
 309+ E86D ED 30                mul d,e                         ; Xl*Rl
 310+ E86F 4A                   ld c,d
 311+ E870 55                   ld d,l
 312+ E871 1E 00        .rh:    ld e,0
 313+ E873 ED 30                mul d,e                         ; Xl*Rh
 314+ E875 79                   ld a,c
 315+ E876 83                   add a,e
 316+ E877 6F                   ld l,a
 317+ E878 7A                   ld a,d
 318+ E879 CE 00                adc a,0
 319+ E87B 67                   ld h,a                          ; HL = (Xl*R) >> 8
 320+ E87C 0E 00                ld c,0
 321+ E87E 05                   dec b
 322+ E87F 20 06                jr nz,.nh
 323+ E881 11 00 00     .r16:   ld de,0
 324+ E884 19                   add hl,de                       ; + (256*R) >> 8
 325+ E885 CB 11                rl c
 326+ E887 24           .nh:    inc h                           ; + rounding (0.5 after >>9)
 327+ E888 20 01                jr nz,.nr
 328+ E88A 0C                   inc c
 329+ E88B 79           .nr:    ld a,c
 330+ E88C 1F                   rra
 331+ E88D 7C                   ld a,h
 332+ E88E 1F                   rra                             ; A = (C:H) >> 1  = P >> 9
 333+ E88F C9                   ret
 334+ E890 21 FF 01     .big:   ld hl,511                       ; clamp (rounding at frustum edge)
 335+ E893 18 D4                jr .ok
 336+ E895
 337+ E895              ;==============================================================================
 338+ E895              ; Outcode: HL -> vertex (X,Y,Z) -> A = bits
 339+ E895              ;   1 near (Z<NEAR)  2 left (X<-Z)  4 right (X>Z)
 340+ E895              ;   8 top (Y>0.5Z)   16 bottom (Y<-0.5Z)
 341+ E895              ;==============================================================================
 342+ E895              calc_oc:
 343+ E895 5E                   ld e,(hl)
 344+ E896 23                   inc hl
 345+ E897 56                   ld d,(hl)                       ; DE = X
 346+ E898 23                   inc hl
 347+ E899 4E                   ld c,(hl)
 348+ E89A 23                   inc hl
 349+ E89B 46                   ld b,(hl)                       ; BC = Y
 350+ E89C 23                   inc hl
 351+ E89D 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 352+ E89E 23                   inc hl
 353+ E89F 66                   ld h,(hl)
 354+ E8A0 6F                   ld l,a                          ; HL = Z
 355+ E8A1 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 356+ E8A3 20 0D                jr nz,.nr
 357+ E8A5 7C                   ld a,h
 358+ E8A6 B7                   or a
 359+ E8A7 3E 00                ld a,0
 360+ E8A9 20 09                jr nz,.n1
 361+ E8AB 7D                   ld a,l
 362+ E8AC FE 10                cp NEAR
 363+ E8AE 3E 00                ld a,0
 364+ E8B0 30 02                jr nc,.n1
 365+ E8B2 3E 01        .nr:    ld a,1
 366+ E8B4 E5           .n1:    push hl
 367+ E8B5 19                   add hl,de                       ; Z+X
 368+ E8B6 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 369+ E8B8 28 02                jr z,.n2
 370+ E8BA F6 02                or 2
 371+ E8BC E1           .n2:    pop hl
 372+ E8BD E5                   push hl
 373+ E8BE B7                   or a
 374+ E8BF ED 52                sbc hl,de                       ; Z-X
 375+ E8C1 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 376+ E8C3 28 02                jr z,.n3
 377+ E8C5 F6 04                or 4
 378+ E8C7 E1           .n3:    pop hl
 379+ E8C8 CB 2C                sra h
 380+ E8CA CB 1D                rr l                            ; 0.5Z
 381+ E8CC E5                   push hl
 382+ E8CD B7                   or a
 383+ E8CE ED 42                sbc hl,bc                       ; 0.5Z-Y
 384+ E8D0 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 385+ E8D2 28 02                jr z,.n4
 386+ E8D4 F6 08                or 8
 387+ E8D6 E1           .n4:    pop hl
 388+ E8D7 09                   add hl,bc                       ; 0.5Z+Y
 389+ E8D8 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 390+ E8DA C8                   ret z
 391+ E8DB F6 10                or 16
 392+ E8DD C9                   ret
 393+ E8DE
 394+ E8DE              ; Signed distance of vertex IX to clip plane (clip_plane bit). >= 0 is inside.
 395+ E8DE              plane_dist:
 396+ E8DE DD 6E 04             ld l,(ix+4)
 397+ E8E1 DD 66 05             ld h,(ix+5)
 398+ E8E4 3A BE FC             ld a,(clip_plane)
 399+ E8E7 FE 01                cp 1
 400+ E8E9 20 05                jr nz,.p2
 401+ E8EB 11 F0 FF             ld de,-NEAR
 402+ E8EE 19                   add hl,de
 403+ E8EF C9                   ret
 404+ E8F0 DD 5E 00     .p2:    ld e,(ix+0)
 405+ E8F3 DD 56 01             ld d,(ix+1)
 406+ E8F6 FE 02                cp 2
 407+ E8F8 20 02                jr nz,.p3
 408+ E8FA 19                   add hl,de
 409+ E8FB C9                   ret
 410+ E8FC FE 04        .p3:    cp 4
 411+ E8FE 20 04                jr nz,.p4
 412+ E900 B7                   or a
 413+ E901 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 414+ E903 C9                   ret
 415+ E904 F5           .p4:    push af
 416+ E905 CD 1E AE             call zhalf
 417+ E908 F1                   pop af
 418+ E909 DD 5E 02             ld e,(ix+2)
 419+ E90C DD 56 03             ld d,(ix+3)
 420+ E90F FE 08                cp 8
 421+ E911 20 04                jr nz,.p5
 422+ E913 B7                   or a
 423+ E914 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 424+ E916 C9                   ret
 425+ E917 19           .p5:    add hl,de
 426+ E918 C9                   ret
 427+ E919
 428+ E919              ;==============================================================================
 429+ E919              ; Sutherland-Hodgman clip of clip_src (clip_n verts) against clip_plane into
 430+ E919              ; clip_dst.  Vertex = X,Y,Z,flag,corner (8 bytes).  flag=1 means the edge from
 431+ E919              ; this vertex to the next is a real building edge (gets outlined); edges
 432+ E919              ; created along a clip plane get flag=0 so the screen border isn't outlined.
 433+ E919              ; corner = vtx offset of an unclipped building corner that is on screen, so
 434+ E919              ; its cached projection can be reused; $80 + plane bit for a vertex the
 435+ E919              ; clipper put on that plane; $FF for any other vertex.
 436+ E919              ;==============================================================================
 437+ E919              clip_poly:
 438+ E919 3A BB FC             ld a,(clip_n)
 439+ E91C 3D                   dec a
 440+ E91D 87                   add a,a
 441+ E91E 87                   add a,a
 442+ E91F 87                   add a,a
 443+ E920 2A BF FC             ld hl,(clip_src)
 444+ E923 ED 31                add hl,a
 445+ E925 22 C3 FC             ld (clip_s),hl
 446+ E928 E5                   push hl
 447+ E929 DD E1                pop ix
 448+ E92B CD DE E8             call plane_dist
 449+ E92E 22 C7 FC             ld (clip_ds),hl
 450+ E931 2A BF FC             ld hl,(clip_src)
 451+ E934 22 C5 FC             ld (clip_e),hl
 452+ E937 FD 2A C1 FC          ld iy,(clip_dst)
 453+ E93B AF                   xor a
 454+ E93C 32 BC FC             ld (clip_on),a
 455+ E93F 3A BB FC             ld a,(clip_n)
 456+ E942 32 BD FC             ld (clip_cnt),a
 457+ E945 DD 2A C5 FC  .loop:  ld ix,(clip_e)
 458+ E949 CD DE E8             call plane_dist
 459+ E94C 22 C9 FC             ld (clip_de),hl
 460+ E94F CB 7C                bit 7,h
 461+ E951 20 2C                jr nz,.eout
 462+ E953 3A C8 FC             ld a,(clip_ds+1)
 463+ E956 CB 7F                bit 7,a
 464+ E958 28 0D                jr z,.ein
 465+ E95A                      ; S out, E in: intersection carries S's edge flag
 466+ E95A DD 2A C3 FC          ld ix,(clip_s)
 467+ E95E DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
 468+ E961 32 CB FC             ld (clip_if),a
 469+ E964 CD AD E9             call clip_isect
 470+ E967 DD 2A C5 FC  .ein:   ld ix,(clip_e)
 471+ E96B 06 08                ld b,8                          ; whole vertex, with its corner byte
 472+ E96D DD 7E 00     .cpy:   ld a,(ix+0)
 473+ E970 FD 77 00             ld (iy+0),a
 474+ E973 DD 23                inc ix
 475+ E975 FD 23                inc iy
 476+ E977 10 F4                djnz .cpy
 477+ E979 21 BC FC             ld hl,clip_on
 478+ E97C 34                   inc (hl)
 479+ E97D 18 0E                jr .next
 480+ E97F 3A C8 FC     .eout:  ld a,(clip_ds+1)
 481+ E982 CB 7F                bit 7,a
 482+ E984 20 07                jr nz,.next
 483+ E986                      ; S in, E out: intersection starts a clip-plane edge
 484+ E986 AF                   xor a
 485+ E987 32 CB FC             ld (clip_if),a
 486+ E98A CD AD E9             call clip_isect
 487+ E98D 2A C5 FC     .next:  ld hl,(clip_e)
 488+ E990 22 C3 FC             ld (clip_s),hl
 489+ E993 11 08 00             ld de,8
 490+ E996 19                   add hl,de
 491+ E997 22 C5 FC             ld (clip_e),hl
 492+ E99A 2A C9 FC             ld hl,(clip_de)
 493+ E99D 22 C7 FC             ld (clip_ds),hl
 494+ E9A0 21 BD FC             ld hl,clip_cnt
 495+ E9A3 35                   dec (hl)
 496+ E9A4 20 9F                jr nz,.loop
 497+ E9A6 3A BC FC             ld a,(clip_on)
 498+ E9A9 32 BB FC             ld (clip_n),a
 499+ E9AC C9                   ret
 500+ E9AD
 501+ E9AD              ; Output S + t*(E-S) at IY, t = dS/(dS-dE)
 502+ E9AD              clip_isect:
 503+ E9AD                  IFDEF LEVEL_FAST_PATH
 504+ E9AD                      ; Level flight: a vertical building edge (S and E share X and Z)
 505+ E9AD                      ; meets the top or bottom plane at (X, +-0.5Z, Z) - no divide or
 506+ E9AD                      ; interpolation.  At pitch 0 vertical edges can't cross the near or
 507+ E9AD                      ; side planes, so this covers every plane they do cross.
 508+ E9AD 3A 2E AE             ld a,(pitch)
 509+ E9B0 B7                   or a
 510+ E9B1 20 49                jr nz,.gen
 511+ E9B3 3A BE FC             ld a,(clip_plane)
 512+ E9B6 FE 08                cp 8
 513+ E9B8 38 42                jr c,.gen                       ; not top or bottom
 514+ E9BA 2A C3 FC             ld hl,(clip_s)
 515+ E9BD ED 5B C5 FC          ld de,(clip_e)
 516+ E9C1 1A                   ld a,(de)
 517+ E9C2 BE                   cp (hl)
 518+ E9C3 20 37                jr nz,.gen
 519+ E9C5 23                   inc hl
 520+ E9C6 13                   inc de
 521+ E9C7 1A                   ld a,(de)
 522+ E9C8 BE                   cp (hl)
 523+ E9C9 20 31                jr nz,.gen                      ; X differs
 524+ E9CB 23                   inc hl
 525+ E9CC 23                   inc hl
 526+ E9CD 23                   inc hl
 527+ E9CE 13                   inc de
 528+ E9CF 13                   inc de
 529+ E9D0 13                   inc de
 530+ E9D1 1A                   ld a,(de)
 531+ E9D2 BE                   cp (hl)
 532+ E9D3 20 27                jr nz,.gen
 533+ E9D5 23                   inc hl
 534+ E9D6 13                   inc de
 535+ E9D7 1A                   ld a,(de)
 536+ E9D8 BE                   cp (hl)
 537+ E9D9 20 21                jr nz,.gen                      ; Z differs
 538+ E9DB DD 2A C3 FC          ld ix,(clip_s)
 539+ E9DF DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+0)                     ; X and Z from S (= E)
 540+ E9E2 FD 77 00             ld (iy+0),a
 541+ E9E5 DD 7E 01             ld a,(ix+1)
 542+ E9E8 FD 77 01             ld (iy+1),a
 543+ E9EB DD 6E 04             ld l,(ix+4)
 544+ E9EE DD 66 05             ld h,(ix+5)
 545+ E9F1 FD 75 04             ld (iy+4),l
 546+ E9F4 FD 74 05             ld (iy+5),h
 547+ E9F7 3A BE FC             ld a,(clip_plane)
 548+ E9FA 18 6D                jr .ty                          ; Y = +-0.5Z
 549+ E9FC              .gen:
 550+ E9FC                  ENDIF
 551+ E9FC 2A C7 FC             ld hl,(clip_ds)
 552+ E9FF CD E5 AD             call abs_hl
 553+ EA02 22 CC FC             ld (ci_a),hl
 554+ EA05 2A C9 FC             ld hl,(clip_de)
 555+ EA08 CD E5 AD             call abs_hl
 556+ EA0B ED 5B CC FC          ld de,(ci_a)
 557+ EA0F 19                   add hl,de
 558+ EA10 EB                   ex de,hl                        ; DE = |dS|+|dE|, HL = |dS|
 559+ EA11 E5                   push hl
 560+ EA12 B7                   or a
 561+ EA13 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 562+ EA15 E1                   pop hl
 563+ EA16 38 06                jr c,.dv
 564+ EA18 DD 21 FF FF          ld ix,$FFFF
 565+ EA1C 18 03                jr .t
 566+ EA1E CD 83 E7     .dv:    call div_frac
 567+ EA21 DD 22 CE FC  .t:     ld (ci_t),ix
 568+ EA25 AF                   xor a
 569+ EA26 CD 9B EA             call lerp_c
 570+ EA29 FD 75 00             ld (iy+0),l
 571+ EA2C FD 74 01             ld (iy+1),h
 572+ EA2F 3E 02                ld a,2
 573+ EA31 CD 9B EA             call lerp_c
 574+ EA34 FD 75 02             ld (iy+2),l
 575+ EA37 FD 74 03             ld (iy+3),h
 576+ EA3A 3E 04                ld a,4
 577+ EA3C CD 9B EA             call lerp_c
 578+ EA3F FD 75 04             ld (iy+4),l
 579+ EA42 FD 74 05             ld (iy+5),h
 580+ EA45                      ; snap the point exactly onto the plane so rounding can't leave a
 581+ EA45                      ; gap between the polygon and the screen edge
 582+ EA45 FD 6E 04             ld l,(iy+4)
 583+ EA48 FD 66 05             ld h,(iy+5)                     ; Z
 584+ EA4B 3A BE FC             ld a,(clip_plane)
 585+ EA4E FE 01                cp 1
 586+ EA50 20 0A                jr nz,.s2
 587+ EA52 FD 36 04 10          ld (iy+4),NEAR                  ; near:   Z = NEAR
 588+ EA56 FD 36 05 00          ld (iy+5),0
 589+ EA5A 18 27                jr .nz
 590+ EA5C FE 02        .s2:    cp 2
 591+ EA5E 20 05                jr nz,.s4
 592+ EA60 CD DE AD             call neg_hl                     ; left:   X = -Z
 593+ EA63 18 18                jr .sx
 594+ EA65 FE 04        .s4:    cp 4
 595+ EA67 28 14                jr z,.sx                        ; right:  X = Z
 596+ EA69 F5           .ty:    push af
 597+ EA6A CD 1E AE             call zhalf
 598+ EA6D F1                   pop af
 599+ EA6E FE 08                cp 8
 600+ EA70 28 03                jr z,.sy                        ; top:    Y = 0.5Z
 601+ EA72 CD DE AD             call neg_hl                     ; bottom: Y = -0.5Z
 602+ EA75 FD 75 02     .sy:    ld (iy+2),l
 603+ EA78 FD 74 03             ld (iy+3),h
 604+ EA7B 18 06                jr .nz
 605+ EA7D FD 75 00     .sx:    ld (iy+0),l
 606+ EA80 FD 74 01             ld (iy+1),h
 607+ EA83 3A CB FC     .nz:    ld a,(clip_if)
 608+ EA86 FD 77 06             ld (iy+6),a
 609+ EA89 3A BE FC             ld a,(clip_plane)               ; new vertex: no cached projection,
 610+ EA8C F6 80                or $80                          ; but remember the plane it is on
 611+ EA8E FD 77 07             ld (iy+7),a
 612+ EA91 01 08 00             ld bc,8
 613+ EA94 FD 09                add iy,bc
 614+ EA96 21 BC FC             ld hl,clip_on
 615+ EA99 34                   inc (hl)
 616+ EA9A C9                   ret
 617+ EA9B
 618+ EA9B              ; A = coordinate offset -> HL = S.c + (E.c - S.c) * t
 619+ EA9B              lerp_c:
 620+ EA9B 2A C5 FC             ld hl,(clip_e)
 621+ EA9E ED 31                add hl,a
 622+ EAA0 5E                   ld e,(hl)
 623+ EAA1 23                   inc hl
 624+ EAA2 56                   ld d,(hl)
 625+ EAA3 2A C3 FC             ld hl,(clip_s)
 626+ EAA6 ED 31                add hl,a
 627+ EAA8 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 628+ EAA9 23                   inc hl
 629+ EAAA 66                   ld h,(hl)
 630+ EAAB 6F                   ld l,a
 631+ EAAC E5                   push hl
 632+ EAAD EB                   ex de,hl
 633+ EAAE B7                   or a
 634+ EAAF ED 52                sbc hl,de
 635+ EAB1 ED 5B CE FC          ld de,(ci_t)
 636+ EAB5 CD 50 E7             call mul_frac
 637+ EAB8 D1                   pop de
 638+ EAB9 19                   add hl,de
 639+ EABA C9                   ret
 640+ EABB
 641+ EABB              ;==============================================================================
 642+ EABB              ; Draw one face of the current building
 643+ EABB              ;   HL -> 4 vertex offsets into vtx[] (index*16), df_mask = edges to outline
 644+ EABB              ;   (bit k = edge from corner k to corner k+1)
 645+ EABB              ;==============================================================================
 646+ EABB              draw_face:
 647+ EABB 22 AF FC             ld (df_ptr),hl
 648+ EABE 01 00 FF             ld bc,$FF00                     ; B = AND of outcodes, C = OR
 649+ EAC1 3A 6C FC             ld a,(vtx_hi)
 650+ EAC4 57                   ld d,a
 651+ EAC5                      REPT 4
 652+ EAC5 7E          >        ld a,(hl)
 653+ EAC6 23          >        inc hl
 654+ EAC7 C6 06       >        add a,6
 655+ EAC9 5F          >        ld e,a
 656+ EACA 1A          >        ld a,(de)
 657+ EACB 5F          >        ld e,a
 658+ EACC A0          >        and b
 659+ EACD 47          >        ld b,a
 660+ EACE 7B          >        ld a,e
 661+ EACF B1          >        or c
 662+ EAD0 4F          >        ld c,a
 652+ EAD1 7E          >        ld a,(hl)
 653+ EAD2 23          >        inc hl
 654+ EAD3 C6 06       >        add a,6
 655+ EAD5 5F          >        ld e,a
 656+ EAD6 1A          >        ld a,(de)
 657+ EAD7 5F          >        ld e,a
 658+ EAD8 A0          >        and b
 659+ EAD9 47          >        ld b,a
 660+ EADA 7B          >        ld a,e
 661+ EADB B1          >        or c
 662+ EADC 4F          >        ld c,a
 652+ EADD 7E          >        ld a,(hl)
 653+ EADE 23          >        inc hl
 654+ EADF C6 06       >        add a,6
 655+ EAE1 5F          >        ld e,a
 656+ EAE2 1A          >        ld a,(de)
 657+ EAE3 5F          >        ld e,a
 658+ EAE4 A0          >        and b
 659+ EAE5 47          >        ld b,a
 660+ EAE6 7B          >        ld a,e
 661+ EAE7 B1          >        or c
 662+ EAE8 4F          >        ld c,a
 652+ EAE9 7E          >        ld a,(hl)
 653+ EAEA 23          >        inc hl
 654+ EAEB C6 06       >        add a,6
 655+ EAED 5F          >        ld e,a
 656+ EAEE 1A          >        ld a,(de)
 657+ EAEF 5F          >        ld e,a
 658+ EAF0 A0          >        and b
 659+ EAF1 47          >        ld b,a
 660+ EAF2 7B          >        ld a,e
 661+ EAF3 B1          >        or c
 662+ EAF4 4F          >        ld c,a
 663+ EAF5                      ENDR
 664+ EAF5 78                   ld a,b
 665+ EAF6 B7                   or a
 666+ EAF7 C0                   ret nz                          ; wholly outside one plane
 667+ EAF8 79                   ld a,c
 668+ EAF9 B7                   or a
 669+ EAFA 20 62                jr nz,.clip
 670+ EAFC
 671+ EAFC                      ; fast path - every corner on screen, use cached projections
 672+ EAFC 2A AF FC             ld hl,(df_ptr)

 674+ EB02 3A 6C FC             ld a,(vtx_hi)
 675+ EB05 47                   ld b,a
 676+ EB06                      FACEVTX 1
 676+ EB06 7E          >        ld a,(hl)
 676+ EB07 23          >        inc hl
 676+ EB08 C6 07       >        add a,7
 676+ EB0A 4F          >        ld c,a
 676+ EB0B 0A          >        ld a,(bc)
 676+ EB0C 12          >        ld (de),a
 676+ EB0D 1C          >        inc e
 676+ EB0E 0C          >        inc c
 676+ EB0F 0A          >        ld a,(bc)
 676+ EB10 12          >        ld (de),a

 676+ EB15 E6 01       >        and 1
 676+ EB17 12          >        ld (de),a
 676+ EB18 1C          >        inc e
 676+ EB19 1C          >        inc e
 677+ EB1A                      FACEVTX 2
 677+ EB1A 7E          >        ld a,(hl)
 677+ EB1B 23          >        inc hl
 677+ EB1C C6 07       >        add a,7
 677+ EB1E 4F          >        ld c,a
 677+ EB1F 0A          >        ld a,(bc)
 677+ EB20 12          >        ld (de),a
 677+ EB21 1C          >        inc e
 677+ EB22 0C          >        inc c
 677+ EB23 0A          >        ld a,(bc)
 677+ EB24 12          >        ld (de),a
 677+ EB25 1C          >        inc e
 677+ EB26 3A B2 FC    >        ld a,(df_mask)
 677+ EB29 E6 02       >        and 2
 677+ EB2B 12          >        ld (de),a
 677+ EB2C 1C          >        inc e
 677+ EB2D 1C          >        inc e
 678+ EB2E                      FACEVTX 4
 678+ EB2E 7E          >        ld a,(hl)
 678+ EB2F 23          >        inc hl
 678+ EB30 C6 07       >        add a,7
 678+ EB32 4F          >        ld c,a
 678+ EB33 0A          >        ld a,(bc)
 678+ EB34 12          >        ld (de),a
 678+ EB35 1C          >        inc e
 678+ EB36 0C          >        inc c
 678+ EB37 0A          >        ld a,(bc)
 678+ EB38 12          >        ld (de),a
 678+ EB39 1C          >        inc e
 678+ EB3A 3A B2 FC    >        ld a,(df_mask)
 678+ EB3D E6 04       >        and 4
 678+ EB3F 12          >        ld (de),a
 678+ EB40 1C          >        inc e
 678+ EB41 1C          >        inc e
 679+ EB42                      FACEVTX 8
 679+ EB42 7E          >        ld a,(hl)
 679+ EB43 23          >        inc hl
 679+ EB44 C6 07       >        add a,7
 679+ EB46 4F          >        ld c,a
 679+ EB47 0A          >        ld a,(bc)
 679+ EB48 12          >        ld (de),a
 679+ EB49 1C          >        inc e
 679+ EB4A 0C          >        inc c
 679+ EB4B 0A          >        ld a,(bc)
 679+ EB4C 12          >        ld (de),a
 679+ EB4D 1C          >        inc e
 679+ EB4E 3A B2 FC    >        ld a,(df_mask)
 679+ EB51 E6 08       >        and 8
 679+ EB53 12          >        ld (de),a
 679+ EB54 1C          >        inc e
 679+ EB55 1C          >        inc e
 680+ EB56 3E 04                ld a,4
 681+ EB58 32 90 FD             ld (p2n),a
 682+ EB5B C3 48 EC             jp .render
 683+ EB5E
 684+ EB5E 32 B1 FC     .clip:  ld (df_or),a
 685+ EB61 3A B2 FC             ld a,(df_mask)
 686+ EB64 32 B3 FC             ld (df_m),a
 687+ EB67 2A AF FC             ld hl,(df_ptr)
 688+ EB6A FD 21 D0 FC          ld iy,clipA
 689+ EB6E 06 04                ld b,4
 690+ EB70 7E           .cp:    ld a,(hl)
 691+ EB71 23                   inc hl
 692+ EB72 32 B4 FC             ld (df_cur),a
 693+ EB75 DD 6F                ld ixl,a
 694+ EB77 3A 6C FC             ld a,(vtx_hi)
 695+ EB7A DD 67                ld ixh,a
 696+ EB7C 0E 06                ld c,6
 697+ EB7E DD 7E 00     .cpb:   ld a,(ix+0)
 698+ EB81 FD 77 00             ld (iy+0),a
 699+ EB84 DD 23                inc ix
 700+ EB86 FD 23                inc iy
 701+ EB88 0D                   dec c
 702+ EB89 20 F3                jr nz,.cpb
 703+ EB8B 3A B3 FC             ld a,(df_m)
 704+ EB8E 5F                   ld e,a
 705+ EB8F E6 01                and 1
 706+ EB91 FD 77 00             ld (iy+0),a                     ; edge flag from mask
 707+ EB94 7B                   ld a,e
 708+ EB95 0F                   rrca
 709+ EB96 32 B3 FC             ld (df_m),a
 710+ EB99 DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+0)                     ; the corner's outcode (IX at +6)
 711+ EB9C B7                   or a
 712+ EB9D 3E FF                ld a,$FF
 713+ EB9F 20 03                jr nz,.cn
 714+ EBA1 3A B4 FC             ld a,(df_cur)                   ; on screen: projection is cached
 715+ EBA4 FD 77 01     .cn:    ld (iy+1),a
 716+ EBA7 FD 23                inc iy
 717+ EBA9 FD 23                inc iy
 718+ EBAB 10 C3                djnz .cp
 719+ EBAD 3E 04                ld a,4
 720+ EBAF 32 BB FC             ld (clip_n),a
 721+ EBB2 21 D0 FC             ld hl,clipA
 722+ EBB5 22 BF FC             ld (clip_src),hl
 723+ EBB8 21 30 FD             ld hl,clipB
 724+ EBBB 22 C1 FC             ld (clip_dst),hl
 725+ EBBE 3E 01                ld a,1
 726+ EBC0 32 BE FC     .pl:    ld (clip_plane),a
 727+ EBC3 21 B1 FC             ld hl,df_or
 728+ EBC6 A6                   and (hl)
 729+ EBC7 28 17                jr z,.skip
 730+ EBC9 CD 19 E9             call clip_poly
 731+ EBCC 3A BB FC             ld a,(clip_n)
 732+ EBCF FE 03                cp 3
 733+ EBD1 D8                   ret c
 734+ EBD2 2A BF FC             ld hl,(clip_src)
 735+ EBD5 ED 5B C1 FC          ld de,(clip_dst)
 736+ EBD9 ED 53 BF FC          ld (clip_src),de
 737+ EBDD 22 C1 FC             ld (clip_dst),hl
 738+ EBE0 3A BE FC     .skip:  ld a,(clip_plane)
 739+ EBE3 87                   add a,a
 740+ EBE4 FE 20                cp 32
 741+ EBE6 20 D8                jr nz,.pl
 742+ EBE8
 743+ EBE8 DD 2A BF FC          ld ix,(clip_src)
 744+ EBEC FD 21 80 E0          ld iy,p2buf
 745+ EBF0 3A BB FC             ld a,(clip_n)
 746+ EBF3 32 90 FD             ld (p2n),a
 747+ EBF6 47                   ld b,a
 748+ EBF7 C5           .pj:    push bc
 749+ EBF8 DD 7E 07             ld a,(ix+7)
 750+ EBFB FE 80                cp $80
 751+ EBFD 30 0C                jr nc,.pf
 752+ EBFF C6 07                add a,7                         ; unclipped on-screen corner:
 753+ EC01 6F                   ld l,a                          ; reuse its cached sx, sy
 754+ EC02 3A 6C FC             ld a,(vtx_hi)
 755+ EC05 67                   ld h,a
 756+ EC06 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 757+ EC07 2C                   inc l
 758+ EC08 4E                   ld c,(hl)
 759+ EC09 18 24                jr .ps
 760+ EC0B DD E5        .pf:    push ix
 761+ EC0D E1                   pop hl
 762+ EC0E CD B0 E7             call project
 763+ EC11 5F                   ld e,a
 764+ EC12 DD 7E 07             ld a,(ix+7)
 765+ EC15 3C                   inc a
 766+ EC16 28 16                jr z,.p0                        ; $FF: an ordinary vertex
 767+ EC18 3D                   dec a
 768+ EC19                      ; On a clip plane: put it exactly on that screen edge (projection
 769+ EC19                      ; rounding could leave it a pixel inside, opening a 1-pixel gap).
 770+ EC19 1F                   rra                             ; near: nothing
 771+ EC1A 1F                   rra
 772+ EC1B 30 02                jr nc,.e1
 773+ EC1D 1E 00                ld e,0                          ; left
 774+ EC1F 1F           .e1:    rra
 775+ EC20 30 02                jr nc,.e2
 776+ EC22 1E FF                ld e,255                        ; right
 777+ EC24 1F           .e2:    rra
 778+ EC25 30 02                jr nc,.e3
 779+ EC27 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; top
 780+ EC29 1F           .e3:    rra
 781+ EC2A 30 02                jr nc,.p0
 782+ EC2C 0E 7F                ld c,VIEW_H-1                   ; bottom
 783+ EC2E 7B           .p0:    ld a,e
 784+ EC2F FD 77 00     .ps:    ld (iy+0),a
 785+ EC32 FD 71 01             ld (iy+1),c
 786+ EC35 DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
 787+ EC38 FD 77 02             ld (iy+2),a
 788+ EC3B 01 08 00             ld bc,8
 789+ EC3E DD 09                add ix,bc
 790+ EC40 01 04 00             ld bc,4
 791+ EC43 FD 09                add iy,bc
 792+ EC45 C1                   pop bc
 793+ EC46 10 AF                djnz .pj
 794+ EC48
 795+ EC48              .render:
 796+ EC48                      ; close the polygon: copy vertex 0 after the last
 797+ EC48 3A 90 FD             ld a,(p2n)
 798+ EC4B 87                   add a,a
 799+ EC4C 87                   add a,a
 800+ EC4D 21 80 E0             ld hl,p2buf
 801+ EC50 ED 31                add hl,a
 802+ EC52 3A 80 E0             ld a,(p2buf)
 803+ EC55 77                   ld (hl),a
 804+ EC56 23                   inc hl
 805+ EC57 3A 81 E0             ld a,(p2buf+1)
 806+ EC5A 77                   ld (hl),a
 807+ EC5B                  IFDEF DRAW_LINES
 808+ EC5B ~                    call fill_poly
 809+ EC5B ~                    ld a,EDGE_COL
 810+ EC5B ~                    ld (line_col),a
 811+ EC5B ~                    jp outline_poly
 812+ EC5B                  ELSE
 813+ EC5B C3 5E EC             jp fill_poly                    ; solid faces only
 814+ EC5E                  ENDIF
 815+ EC5E
 816+ EC5E              ;==============================================================================
 817+ EC5E              ; Convex polygon fill from p2buf (8-bit screen coords).
 818+ EC5E              ;   Each edge is walked with an exact integer DDA into one of two x tables:
 819+ EC5E              ;   downward edges -> TA, upward edges -> TB.  For a convex polygon every row
 820+ EC5E              ;   then has one entry in each, and the span between them is filled.
 821+ EC5E              ;   Winding doesn't matter.
 822+ EC5E              ;==============================================================================
 823+ EC5E              fill_poly:
 824+ EC5E 3A 90 FD             ld a,(p2n)
 825+ EC61 47                   ld b,a
 826+ EC62 21 81 E0             ld hl,p2buf+1
 827+ EC65 0E FF                ld c,255
 828+ EC67 16 00                ld d,0
 829+ EC69 7E           .mm:    ld a,(hl)
 830+ EC6A B9                   cp c
 831+ EC6B 30 01                jr nc,.m1
 832+ EC6D 4F                   ld c,a
 833+ EC6E BA           .m1:    cp d
 834+ EC6F 38 01                jr c,.m2
 835+ EC71 57                   ld d,a
 836+ EC72 23           .m2:    inc hl
 837+ EC73 23                   inc hl
 838+ EC74 23                   inc hl
 839+ EC75 23                   inc hl
 840+ EC76 10 F1                djnz .mm
 841+ EC78 79                   ld a,c
 842+ EC79 BA                   cp d
 843+ EC7A C8                   ret z                           ; zero height - outline only
 844+ EC7B 32 91 FD             ld (fy0),a
 845+ EC7E 7A                   ld a,d
 846+ EC7F 32 92 FD             ld (fy1),a
 847+ EC82
 848+ EC82 DD 21 80 E0          ld ix,p2buf
 849+ EC86 3A 90 FD             ld a,(p2n)
 850+ EC89 32 93 FD             ld (fe_cnt),a
 851+ EC8C DD 56 00     .ed:    ld d,(ix+0)
 852+ EC8F DD 5E 01             ld e,(ix+1)
 853+ EC92 DD 46 04             ld b,(ix+4)
 854+ EC95 DD 4E 05             ld c,(ix+5)
 855+ EC98 7B                   ld a,e
 856+ EC99 B9                   cp c
 857+ EC9A 28 15                jr z,.nx                        ; horizontal edge
 858+ EC9C 38 0A                jr c,.down
 859+ EC9E 7A                   ld a,d                          ; going up: swap ends, use TB
 860+ EC9F 50                   ld d,b
 861+ ECA0 47                   ld b,a
 862+ ECA1 7B                   ld a,e
 863+ ECA2 59                   ld e,c
 864+ ECA3 4F                   ld c,a
 865+ ECA4 3E E3                ld a,high TB
 866+ ECA6 18 02                jr .walk
 867+ ECA8 3E E2        .down:  ld a,high TA
 868+ ECAA DD E5        .walk:  push ix
 869+ ECAC CD C7 EC             call walk_edge
 870+ ECAF DD E1                pop ix
 871+ ECB1 01 04 00     .nx:    ld bc,4
 872+ ECB4 DD 09                add ix,bc
 873+ ECB6 21 93 FD             ld hl,fe_cnt
 874+ ECB9 35                   dec (hl)
 875+ ECBA 20 D0                jr nz,.ed
 876+ ECBC
 877+ ECBC 3A 54 FC             ld a,(face_col)                 ; shaded colour of this face
 878+ ECBF 32 97 FD             ld (l2_cls_byte),a              ; DMA source byte for long spans
 879+ ECC2 57                   ld d,a
 880+ ECC3 5F                   ld e,a                          ; PUSH pair for short spans
 881+ ECC4 C3 55 ED             jp fill_spans
 882+ ECC7
 883+ ECC7              ; Walk an edge (D=xa, E=ya) -> (B=xb, C=yb), yb > ya, into table page A.
 884+ ECC7              ; Bresenham style: x += q each row, fractional r/dy accumulated in a biased
 885+ ECC7              ; byte (carry == wrap), so the end point is hit exactly.
 886+ ECC7              walk_edge:
 887+ ECC7 67                   ld h,a
 888+ ECC8 6B                   ld l,e
 889+ ECC9 79                   ld a,c
 890+ ECCA 93                   sub e
 891+ ECCB 4F                   ld c,a                          ; C = dy (1..191)
 892+ ECCC 78                   ld a,b
 893+ ECCD 92                   sub d                           ; dx
 894+ ECCE 28 72                jr z,.vert                      ; vertical (every wall in level flight)
 895+ ECD0 38 37                jr c,.neg
 896+ ECD2 B9                   cp c
 897+ ECD3 30 07                jr nc,.pdv
 898+ ECD5 DD 6F                ld ixl,a                        ; steep: q = 0, r = dx
 899+ ECD7 CD 49 ED             call .setup
 900+ ECDA 18 20                jr .p0
 901+ ECDC CD 9F E7     .pdv:   call div8                       ; E = q, A = r
 902+ ECDF DD 63                ld ixh,e
 903+ ECE1 DD 6F                ld ixl,a
 904+ ECE3 CD 49 ED             call .setup
 905+ ECE6 DD 7C                ld a,ixh
 906+ ECE8 B7                   or a
 907+ ECE9 28 11                jr z,.p0
 908+ ECEB 72           .pl:    ld (hl),d
 909+ ECEC 2C                   inc l
 910+ ECED 7A                   ld a,d
 911+ ECEE DD 84                add a,ixh
 912+ ECF0 57                   ld d,a
 913+ ECF1 7B                   ld a,e
 914+ ECF2 DD 85                add a,ixl
 915+ ECF4 30 02                jr nc,.pl1
 916+ ECF6 81                   add a,c
 917+ ECF7 14                   inc d
 918+ ECF8 5F           .pl1:   ld e,a
 919+ ECF9 10 F0                djnz .pl
 920+ ECFB C9                   ret
 921+ ECFC 72           .p0:    ld (hl),d
 922+ ECFD 2C                   inc l
 923+ ECFE 7B                   ld a,e
 924+ ECFF DD 85                add a,ixl
 925+ ED01 30 02                jr nc,.p01
 926+ ED03 81                   add a,c
 927+ ED04 14                   inc d
 928+ ED05 5F           .p01:   ld e,a
 929+ ED06 10 F4                djnz .p0
 930+ ED08 C9                   ret
 931+ ED09
 932+ ED09 ED 44        .neg:   neg
 933+ ED0B B9                   cp c
 934+ ED0C 30 07                jr nc,.ndv
 935+ ED0E DD 6F                ld ixl,a
 936+ ED10 CD 49 ED             call .setup
 937+ ED13 18 20                jr .n0
 938+ ED15 CD 9F E7     .ndv:   call div8
 939+ ED18 DD 63                ld ixh,e
 940+ ED1A DD 6F                ld ixl,a
 941+ ED1C CD 49 ED             call .setup
 942+ ED1F DD 7C                ld a,ixh
 943+ ED21 B7                   or a
 944+ ED22 28 11                jr z,.n0
 945+ ED24 72           .nl:    ld (hl),d
 946+ ED25 2C                   inc l
 947+ ED26 7A                   ld a,d
 948+ ED27 DD 94                sub ixh
 949+ ED29 57                   ld d,a
 950+ ED2A 7B                   ld a,e
 951+ ED2B DD 85                add a,ixl
 952+ ED2D 30 02                jr nc,.nl1
 953+ ED2F 81                   add a,c
 954+ ED30 15                   dec d
 955+ ED31 5F           .nl1:   ld e,a
 956+ ED32 10 F0                djnz .nl
 957+ ED34 C9                   ret
 958+ ED35 72           .n0:    ld (hl),d
 959+ ED36 2C                   inc l
 960+ ED37 7B                   ld a,e
 961+ ED38 DD 85                add a,ixl
 962+ ED3A 30 02                jr nc,.n01
 963+ ED3C 81                   add a,c
 964+ ED3D 15                   dec d
 965+ ED3E 5F           .n01:   ld e,a
 966+ ED3F 10 F4                djnz .n0
 967+ ED41 C9                   ret
 968+ ED42
 969+ ED42              ; A vertical edge: the same x on every row (as the steep walk gives it with
 970+ ED42              ; no x step: exactly)
 971+ ED42 41           .vert:  ld b,c
 972+ ED43 04                   inc b                           ; dy+1 rows
 973+ ED44 72           .vl:    ld (hl),d
 974+ ED45 2C                   inc l
 975+ ED46 10 FC                djnz .vl
 976+ ED48 C9                   ret
 977+ ED49
 978+ ED49              ; B = dy+1 rows, E = biased accumulator (dy/2 - dy), C = -dy
 979+ ED49 41           .setup: ld b,c
 980+ ED4A 04                   inc b
 981+ ED4B 79                   ld a,c
 982+ ED4C CB 3F                srl a
 983+ ED4E 91                   sub c
 984+ ED4F 5F                   ld e,a
 985+ ED50 79                   ld a,c
 986+ ED51 ED 44                neg
 987+ ED53 4F                   ld c,a
 988+ ED54 C9                   ret
 989+ ED55
 990+ ED55              ;==============================================================================
 991+ ED55              ; Span filler.  Rows fy0..fy1, x from TA/TB.  DE = colour pair.
 992+ ED55              ; Short spans use the stack pointer as a 2-byte write pointer into the frame
 993+ ED55              ; buffer (see push_blk), jumping into an unrolled block of PUSH DE.  Spans of
 994+ ED55              ; DMA_SPAN_MIN pixels or more are written by the zxnDMA, which only needs its
 995+ ED55              ; length and destination reloaded (see clear_screen).
 996+ ED55              ; For speed: the stack pointer is kept in IY (ld sp,iy), the rows are
 997+ ED55              ; counted down in IXH, and the table that is the left edge at the polygon's
 998+ ED55              ; middle row is read first, so the left / right swap (still made whenever
 999+ ED55              ; it's needed: a sliver's edges can cross) is mostly skipped.
1000+ ED55              ;==============================================================================
1001+ ED55              fill_spans:
1002+ ED55 3A 91 FD             ld a,(fy0)
1003+ ED58 CD 20 E7             call seg_map                    ; first row's section into slot 0
1004+ ED5B FD 21 00 00          ld iy,0
1005+ ED5F FD 39                add iy,sp                       ; the stack, for push_end
1006+ ED61 3A 91 FD             ld a,(fy0)
1007+ ED64 47                   ld b,a
1008+ ED65 3A 92 FD             ld a,(fy1)
1009+ ED68 90                   sub b
1010+ ED69 3C                   inc a
1011+ ED6A DD 67                ld ixh,a                        ; rows to fill
1012+ ED6C 3A 92 FD             ld a,(fy1)                      ; the middle row: which table is left?
1013+ ED6F 80                   add a,b
1014+ ED70 1F                   rra
1015+ ED71 6F                   ld l,a
1016+ ED72 26 E2                ld h,high TA
1017+ ED74 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1018+ ED75 24                   inc h
1019+ ED76 BE                   cp (hl)
1020+ ED77 3E E2                ld a,high TA                    ; TA left (or level): TA first
1021+ ED79 0E 24                ld c,$24                        ;   (inc h)
1022+ ED7B 38 04                jr c,.ta
1023+ ED7D 3E E3                ld a,high TB                    ; TB left: TB first
1024+ ED7F 0E 25                ld c,$25                        ;   (dec h)
1025+ ED81 32 89 ED     .ta:    ld (fs_loop+1),a
1026+ ED84 79                   ld a,c
1027+ ED85 32 8C ED             ld (fs_loop.tb),a
1028+ ED88
1029+ ED88              fs_loop:
1030+ ED88 26 E2                ld h,high TA                    ; (patched: the left table first)
1031+ ED8A 68                   ld l,b
1032+ ED8B 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1033+ ED8C 24           .tb:    inc h                           ; (patched: inc h, or dec h, the other)
1034+ ED8D 4E                   ld c,(hl)
1035+ ED8E B9                   cp c
1036+ ED8F 38 03                jr c,.ord
1037+ ED91 69                   ld l,c
1038+ ED92 4F                   ld c,a
1039+ ED93 7D                   ld a,l
1040+ ED94 6F           .ord:   ld l,a                          ; A = left x, C = right x
1041+ ED95 78                   ld a,b
1042+ ED96 E6 1F                and 31
1043+ ED98 67                   ld h,a                          ; row within the paged section
1044+ ED99 79                   ld a,c
1045+ ED9A 95                   sub l                           ; width-1
1046+ ED9B FE 13                cp DMA_SPAN_MIN-1
1047+ ED9D 30 17                jr nc,.dma
1048+ ED9F CB 3F        .push:  srl a
1049+ EDA1 38 07                jr c,.even
1050+ EDA3 73                   ld (hl),e                       ; odd width: single pixel first
1051+ EDA4 B7                   or a
1052+ EDA5 CA 84 E1             jp z,push_next                  ; (1 pixel: no PUSHes, so SP isn't
1053+ EDA8 18 01                jr .go                          ; borrowed: see push_blk)
1054+ EDAA 3C           .even:  inc a
1055+ EDAB 69           .go:    ld l,c
1056+ EDAC 23                   inc hl                          ; one past right edge (may be $C000)
1057+ EDAD F9                   ld sp,hl
1058+ EDAE 6F                   ld l,a
1059+ EDAF 3E 80                ld a,low push_end - 1           ; (the DI before the last PUSH)
1060+ EDB1 95                   sub l
1061+ EDB2 6F                   ld l,a
1062+ EDB3 26 E1                ld h,high push_end
1063+ EDB5 E9                   jp (hl)
1064+ EDB6                      ASSERT high push_blk == high push_end && low push_end - 1 - 128 == low push_blk
1065+ EDB6
1066+ EDB6                      ; Long span: zxnDMA from the fixed colour byte (port A) to HL.
1067+ EDB6                      ; Everything but length and destination was set up by clear_screen.
1068+ EDB6 3C           .dma:   inc a
1069+ EDB7 20 03                jr nz,.dmal
1070+ EDB9 3D                   dec a                           ; 256 wide: length needs the high
1071+ EDBA 18 E3                jr .push                        ; byte, so PUSH it (A = width-1)
1072+ EDBC              .dmal:
1073+ EDBC 4F                   ld c,a
1074+ EDBD 3E 25                ld a,DMA_WR0_LEN_LO
1075+ EDBF D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a
1076+ EDC1 79                   ld a,c
1077+ EDC2 D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a    ; length = width
1078+ EDC4 3E AD                ld a,DMA_WR4_CONT_MODE
1079+ EDC6 D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a
1080+ EDC8 7D                   ld a,l
1081+ EDC9 D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a    ; port B = y*256 + x
1082+ EDCB 7C                   ld a,h
1083+ EDCC D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a
1084+ EDCE 3E CF                ld a,DMA_LOAD
1085+ EDD0 D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a
1086+ EDD2 3E 87                ld a,DMA_ENABLE
1087+ EDD4 D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a    ; CPU waits until the span is written
1088+ EDD6 04                   inc b                           ; the next row (as push_next: SP was
1089+ EDD7 DD 25                dec ixh                         ; never borrowed here)
1090+ EDD9 C8                   ret z
1091+ EDDA 78                   ld a,b
1092+ EDDB E6 1F                and 31
1093+ EDDD C2 88 ED             jp nz,fs_loop
1094+ EDE0 C3 8E E1             jp push_next.sect
1095+ EDE3                      ASSERT high TB == high TA + 1
1096+ EDE3
1097+ EDE3              ;==============================================================================
1098+ EDE3              ; Outline the polygon in p2buf, skipping edges flagged 0 (clip edges)
1099+ EDE3              ;==============================================================================
1100+ EDE3              outline_poly:
1101+ EDE3 DD 21 80 E0          ld ix,p2buf
1102+ EDE7 3A 90 FD             ld a,(p2n)
1103+ EDEA 47                   ld b,a
1104+ EDEB C5           .lp:    push bc
1105+ EDEC DD 7E 02             ld a,(ix+2)
1106+ EDEF B7                   or a
1107+ EDF0 28 0F                jr z,.sk
1108+ EDF2 DD 6E 00             ld l,(ix+0)
1109+ EDF5 DD 66 01             ld h,(ix+1)
1110+ EDF8 DD 5E 04             ld e,(ix+4)
1111+ EDFB DD 56 05             ld d,(ix+5)
1112+ EDFE CD 0A EE             call draw_line
1113+ EE01 01 04 00     .sk:    ld bc,4
1114+ EE04 DD 09                add ix,bc
1115+ EE06 C1                   pop bc
1116+ EE07 10 E2                djnz .lp
1117+ EE09 C9                   ret
1118+ EE0A
1119+ EE0A              ;==============================================================================
1120+ EE0A              ; Bresenham line, on-screen 8-bit coordinates.
1121+ EE0A              ;   L = x0, H = y0, E = x1, D = y1, colour in line_col.  Preserves IX, IY.
1122+ EE0A              ;   Pages Layer 2 sections into slot 0 as the line crosses them.
1123+ EE0A              ;==============================================================================
1124+ EE0A              draw_line:
1125+ EE0A 3A 94 FD             ld a,(line_col)
1126+ EE0D 4F                   ld c,a
1127+ EE0E 7B                   ld a,e
1128+ EE0F 95                   sub l
1129+ EE10 30 06                jr nc,.xp
1130+ EE12 ED 44                neg
1131+ EE14 06 2D                ld b,OP_DEC_L
1132+ EE16 18 02                jr .xd
1133+ EE18 06 2C        .xp:    ld b,OP_INC_L
1134+ EE1A 5F           .xd:    ld e,a                          ; |dx|
1135+ EE1B 78                   ld a,b
1136+ EE1C 32 44 EE             ld (.smx1),a
1137+ EE1F 32 61 EE             ld (.smx2),a
1138+ EE22 7A                   ld a,d
1139+ EE23 94                   sub h
1140+ EE24 30 06                jr nc,.yp
1141+ EE26 ED 44                neg
1142+ EE28 06 25                ld b,OP_DEC_H
1143+ EE2A 18 02                jr .yd
1144+ EE2C 06 24        .yp:    ld b,OP_INC_H
1145+ EE2E 57           .yd:    ld d,a                          ; |dy|
1146+ EE2F 78                   ld a,b
1147+ EE30 32 48 EE             ld (.smy1),a
1148+ EE33 32 58 EE             ld (.smy2),a
1149+ EE36 7C                   ld a,h
1150+ EE37 CD 20 E7             call seg_map                    ; page in the start row's section
1151+ EE3A 67                   ld h,a                          ; y within the section
1152+ EE3B 7B                   ld a,e
1153+ EE3C BA                   cp d
1154+ EE3D 38 13                jr c,.ymaj
1155+ EE3F 43                   ld b,e
1156+ EE40 04                   inc b
1157+ EE41 CB 3F                srl a
1158+ EE43 71           .xl:    ld (hl),c
1159+ EE44 2C           .smx1:  inc l
1160+ EE45 92                   sub d
1161+ EE46 30 07                jr nc,.xs
1162+ EE48 24           .smy1:  inc h
1163+ EE49 CB 6C                bit 5,h                         ; left the 32-row section?
1164+ EE4B C4 37 E7             call nz,line_seg
1165+ EE4E 83                   add a,e
1166+ EE4F 10 F2        .xs:    djnz .xl
1167+ EE51 C9                   ret
1168+ EE52 42           .ymaj:  ld b,d
1169+ EE53 04                   inc b
1170+ EE54 7A                   ld a,d
1171+ EE55 CB 3F                srl a
1172+ EE57 71           .yl:    ld (hl),c
1173+ EE58 24           .smy2:  inc h
1174+ EE59 CB 6C                bit 5,h
1175+ EE5B C4 37 E7             call nz,line_seg
1176+ EE5E 93                   sub e
1177+ EE5F 30 02                jr nc,.ys
1178+ EE61 2C           .smx2:  inc l
1179+ EE62 82                   add a,d
1180+ EE63 10 F2        .ys:    djnz .yl
1181+ EE65 C9                   ret
1182+ EE66
1183+ EE66              ;==============================================================================
1184+ EE66              ; Clear the viewport (rows 0..VIEW_H-1) of the back buffer with the zxnDMA,
1185+ EE66              ; one 8K section (32 rows) at a time through slot 0: sky above the horizon
1186+ EE66              ; row, ground below.  The fills reuse the l2_fill command block from
1187+ EE66              ; layer2_cls.asm, patching its destination (port B) and length; the source
1188+ EE66              ; (port A) is the fixed byte l2_cls_byte.  Zero-length fills are skipped.
1189+ EE66              ;==============================================================================
1190+ EE66              clear_screen:
1191+ EE66 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; C = first row of the section
1192+ EE68 79           .sec:   ld a,c
1193+ EE69 CD 20 E7             call seg_map
1194+ EE6C 3A 58 B4             ld a,(horizon)                  ; sky rows here = clamp(hz - C, 0, 32)
1195+ EE6F                  IFDEF SKY_IS_WALL
1196+ EE6F 3C                   inc a                           ; the horizon row too: at the horizon
1197+ EE70                                                      ; it is all distant wall, and it hides
1198+ EE70                                                      ; a 1-row rounding gap under the
1199+ EE70                                                      ; wall's top edge when pitched
1200+ EE70                  ENDIF
1201+ EE70 91                   sub c
1202+ EE71 30 01                jr nc,.s1
1203+ EE73 AF                   xor a
1204+ EE74 FE 21        .s1:    cp 33
1205+ EE76 38 02                jr c,.s2
1206+ EE78 3E 20                ld a,32
1207+ EE7A 32 96 FD     .s2:    ld (cs_sky),a
1208+ EE7D B7                   or a
1209+ EE7E 28 0D                jr z,.gnd
1210+ EE80 C5                   push bc
1211+ EE81 47                   ld b,a                          ; sky: section start, rows*256 bytes
1212+ EE82 0E 00                ld c,0
1213+ EE84 21 00 00             ld hl,0
1214+ EE87                  IFDEF SKY_IS_WALL
1215+ EE87 3E 01                ld a,WALL_FAMILY*4+SHADE_FACE   ; "sky" is the perimeter wall
1216+ EE89                  ELSE
1217+ EE89 ~                    ld a,SKY_COL
1218+ EE89                  ENDIF
1219+ EE89 CD B5 EE             call dma_fill
1220+ EE8C C1                   pop bc
1221+ EE8D 3A 96 FD     .gnd:   ld a,(cs_sky)
1222+ EE90 FE 20                cp 32
1223+ EE92 28 11                jr z,.nx
1224+ EE94 C5                   push bc
1225+ EE95 67                   ld h,a                          ; ground: after the sky rows
1226+ EE96 2E 00                ld l,0
1227+ EE98 ED 44                neg
1228+ EE9A C6 20                add a,32
1229+ EE9C 47                   ld b,a
1230+ EE9D 0E 00                ld c,0
1231+ EE9F 3E 07                ld a,GROUND_COL
1232+ EEA1 CD B5 EE             call dma_fill
1233+ EEA4 C1                   pop bc
1234+ EEA5 79           .nx:    ld a,c
1235+ EEA6 C6 20                add a,32
1236+ EEA8 4F                   ld c,a
1237+ EEA9 FE 80                cp VIEW_H
1238+ EEAB 20 BB                jr nz,.sec
1239+ EEAD                      ; Leave the DMA ready for fill_spans: length high byte 0, so each span
1240+ EEAD                      ; only reloads length low, destination, LOAD and ENABLE.  fill_poly
1241+ EEAD                      ; puts each building's colour in the source byte l2_cls_byte.
1242+ EEAD 3E 45        .spans: ld a,DMA_WR0_LEN_HI
1243+ EEAF D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a
1244+ EEB1 AF                   xor a
1245+ EEB2 D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a
1246+ EEB4 C9                   ret
1247+ EEB5
1248+ EEB5              ; DMA fill: HL = destination, BC = length (non-zero), A = colour
1249+ EEB5              dma_fill:
1250+ EEB5 32 97 FD             ld (l2_cls_byte),a
1251+ EEB8 22 A4 FD             ld (l2_fill_bstrt),hl
1252+ EEBB ED 43 9F FD          ld (l2_fill_length),bc
1253+ EEBF 21 98 FD             ld hl,l2_fill
1254+ EEC2 06 12                ld b,l2_fill_cmd_len
1255+ EEC4 0E 6B                ld c,IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT
1256+ EEC6 ED B3                otir                            ; CPU waits while the DMA runs
1257+ EEC8 C9                   ret
1258+ EEC9
1259+ EEC9              ;==============================================================================
1260+ EEC9              ; City traversal - back to front without sorting.
1261+ EEC9              ;   For grid-aligned boxes in separate cells, a box can only hide boxes whose
1262+ EEC9              ;   cell offsets from the camera cell are larger (or equal) on both axes in
1263+ EEC9              ;   the same quadrant.  So: rows from the far edges inwards, and within each
1264+ EEC9              ;   row columns from the far edges inwards, the camera row/column last.
1265+ EEC9              ;==============================================================================
1266+ EEC9              draw_city:
1267+ EEC9                  IFDEF OCCLUSION_CULL
1268+ EEC9 CD F9 F7             call occ_begin
1269+ EECC 3A 6E FC             ld a,(occ_on)
1270+ EECF B7                   or a
1271+ EED0 28 0E                jr z,draw_cells                 ; pitched: the painter's order as is
1272+ EED2 CD 2F F8             call occ_cells                  ; front to back: keep what isn't hidden
1273+ EED5 3A 6F FC             ld a,(occ_full)
1274+ EED8 B7                   or a
1275+ EED9 CA EB FB             jp z,occ_draw_list              ; back to front: draw it
1276+ EEDC AF                   xor a                           ; too many to keep: draw this frame
1277+ EEDD 32 6E FC             ld (occ_on),a                   ; the plain way
1278+ EEE0                  ENDIF
1279+ EEE0              draw_cells:
1280+ EEE0 3A 7E B4             ld a,(cz)
1281+ EEE3 21 3E AE             ld hl,view_r
1282+ EEE6 96                   sub (hl)
1283+ EEE7 32 4F FC             ld (cell_j),a
1284+ EEEA 3A 4F FC     .b:     ld a,(cell_j)
1285+ EEED 21 7E B4             ld hl,cz
1286+ EEF0 BE                   cp (hl)
1287+ EEF1 28 09                jr z,.f
1288+ EEF3 CD 18 EF             call draw_row
1289+ EEF6 21 4F FC             ld hl,cell_j
1290+ EEF9 34                   inc (hl)
1291+ EEFA 18 EE                jr .b
1292+ EEFC 3A 7E B4     .f:     ld a,(cz)
1293+ EEFF 21 3E AE             ld hl,view_r
1294+ EF02 86                   add a,(hl)
1295+ EF03 32 4F FC             ld (cell_j),a
1296+ EF06 3A 4F FC     .f2:    ld a,(cell_j)
1297+ EF09 21 7E B4             ld hl,cz
1298+ EF0C BE                   cp (hl)
1299+ EF0D 28 09                jr z,draw_row                   ; camera row last
1300+ EF0F CD 18 EF             call draw_row
1301+ EF12 21 4F FC             ld hl,cell_j
1302+ EF15 35                   dec (hl)
1303+ EF16 18 EE                jr .f2
1304+ EF18
1305+ EF18              draw_row:
1306+ EF18 3A 4F FC             ld a,(cell_j)
1307+ EF1B FE 40                cp GRID
1308+ EF1D D0                   ret nc
1309+ EF1E 3A 7D B4             ld a,(cx)
1310+ EF21 21 3E AE             ld hl,view_r
1311+ EF24 96                   sub (hl)
1312+ EF25 32 4E FC             ld (cell_i),a
1313+ EF28 CD 8C EF             call row_start
1314+ EF2B 3A 4E FC     .l:     ld a,(cell_i)
1315+ EF2E 21 7D B4             ld hl,cx
1316+ EF31 BE                   cp (hl)
1317+ EF32 28 1F                jr z,.r
1318+ EF34 CD B9 EF             call try_cell
1319+ EF37 21 4E FC             ld hl,cell_i
1320+ EF3A 34                   inc (hl)
1321+ EF3B 2A 67 FC             ld hl,(row_x)                   ; one cell +x: (X,Z1) += (cosY,sinY)
1322+ EF3E ED 5B 5B B4          ld de,(cos_y)
1323+ EF42 19                   add hl,de
1324+ EF43 22 67 FC             ld (row_x),hl
1325+ EF46 2A 69 FC             ld hl,(row_z1)
1326+ EF49 ED 5B 59 B4          ld de,(sin_y)
1327+ EF4D 19                   add hl,de
1328+ EF4E 22 69 FC             ld (row_z1),hl
1329+ EF51 18 D8                jr .l
1330+ EF53 3A 7D B4     .r:     ld a,(cx)
1331+ EF56 21 3E AE             ld hl,view_r
1332+ EF59 86                   add a,(hl)
1333+ EF5A 32 4E FC             ld (cell_i),a
1334+ EF5D CD 8C EF             call row_start
1335+ EF60 3A 4E FC     .r2:    ld a,(cell_i)
1336+ EF63 21 7D B4             ld hl,cx
1337+ EF66 BE                   cp (hl)
1338+ EF67 28 50                jr z,try_cell                   ; camera column last
1339+ EF69 CD B9 EF             call try_cell
1340+ EF6C 21 4E FC             ld hl,cell_i
1341+ EF6F 35                   dec (hl)
1342+ EF70 2A 67 FC             ld hl,(row_x)                   ; one cell -x
1343+ EF73 ED 5B 5B B4          ld de,(cos_y)
1344+ EF77 B7                   or a
1345+ EF78 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1346+ EF7A 22 67 FC             ld (row_x),hl
1347+ EF7D 2A 69 FC             ld hl,(row_z1)
1348+ EF80 ED 5B 59 B4          ld de,(sin_y)
1349+ EF84 B7                   or a
1350+ EF85 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1351+ EF87 22 69 FC             ld (row_z1),hl
1352+ EF8A 18 D4                jr .r2
1353+ EF8C
1354+ EF8C              ; Yaw-rotated centre of cell (cell_i, cell_j) -> row_x, row_z1.  Moving one
1355+ EF8C              ; cell (256 units) in x then changes (X, Z1) by exactly the raw (cosY, sinY).
1356+ EF8C              row_start:
1357+ EF8C 3A 4E FC             ld a,(cell_i)
1358+ EF8F 67                   ld h,a
1359+ EF90 2E 80                ld l,128
1360+ EF92 ED 5B 24 AE          ld de,(cam_x+1)
1361+ EF96 B7                   or a
1362+ EF97 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1363+ EF99 22 5F FC             ld (t_dx),hl
1364+ EF9C 3A 4F FC             ld a,(cell_j)
1365+ EF9F 67                   ld h,a
1366+ EFA0 2E 80                ld l,128
1367+ EFA2 ED 5B 27 AE          ld de,(cam_z+1)
1368+ EFA6 B7                   or a
1369+ EFA7 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1370+ EFA9 22 61 FC             ld (t_dz),hl
1371+ EFAC CD 4E F4             call rot_yaw
1372+ EFAF 22 67 FC             ld (row_x),hl
1373+ EFB2 2A 63 FC             ld hl,(t_z1)
1374+ EFB5 22 69 FC             ld (row_z1),hl
1375+ EFB8 C9                   ret
1376+ EFB9
1377+ EFB9              try_cell:
1378+ EFB9 3A 4E FC             ld a,(cell_i)
1379+ EFBC FE 40                cp GRID
1380+ EFBE D0                   ret nc
1381+ EFBF                  IFDEF DRAW_FPS
1382+ EFBF ~                    call vtick                      ; keep the FPS clock sampling
1383+ EFBF                  ENDIF
1384+ EFBF                      ; fall through
1385+ EFBF
1386+ EFBF              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1387+ EFBF              ; Transform and draw the building in (cell_i, cell_j)
1388+ EFBF              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1389+ EFBF              draw_cell:
1390+ EFBF 3A 4F FC             ld a,(cell_j)
1391+ EFC2 6F                   ld l,a
1392+ EFC3 26 00                ld h,0
1393+ EFC5                      ROWOFS
1393+ EFC5 29          >        add hl,hl
1393+ EFC6 29          >        add hl,hl
1393+ EFC7 29          >        add hl,hl
1393+ EFC8 29          >        add hl,hl
1393+ EFC9 29          >        add hl,hl
1393+ EFCA 29          >        add hl,hl
1394+ EFCB 3A 4E FC             ld a,(cell_i)
1395+ EFCE ED 31                add hl,a
1396+ EFD0 11 00 70             ld de,city_map
1397+ EFD3 19                   add hl,de
1398+ EFD4 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1399+ EFD5 4F                   ld c,a
1400+ EFD6 E6 1F                and CELL_CODE
1401+ EFD8 20 1A                jr nz,.nst
1402+ EFDA                      ; street: the zombies and humans standing there (bits 7, 6)
1403+ EFDA 79                   ld a,c
1404+ EFDB E6 C0                and CELL_ZOMBIE|CELL_HUMAN
1405+ EFDD C8                   ret z                           ; nobody
1406+ EFDE                  IFDEF OCCLUSION_CULL
1407+ EFDE 3A 6E FC             ld a,(occ_on)
1408+ EFE1 B7                   or a
1409+ EFE2 28 08                jr z,.sdraw
1410+ EFE4 E5                   push hl
1411+ EFE5 CD 0E F9             call occ_zombie                 ; level flight: list them
1412+ EFE8 E1                   pop hl
1413+ EFE9 C3 FB F8             jp occ_human
1414+ EFEC              .sdraw:
1415+ EFEC                  ENDIF
1416+ EFEC E5                   push hl
1417+ EFED CD CA 2C             call zombie_cell
1418+ EFF0 E1                   pop hl
1419+ EFF1 C3 09 37             jp human_cell
1420+ EFF4 FE 12        .nst:   cp VOID_CODE
1421+ EFF6 C8                   ret z                           ; out of bounds: never drawn
1422+ EFF7 79           .bld:   ld a,c                          ; humans on the roof, monsters
1423+ EFF8 E6 C0                and CELL_ZOMBIE|CELL_HUMAN      ; (ghosts) above it?
1424+ EFFA 28 21                jr z,draw_bld                   ; nobody
1425+ EFFC                  IFDEF OCCLUSION_CULL
1426+ EFFC 3A 6E FC             ld a,(occ_on)
1427+ EFFF B7                   or a
1428+ F000 28 0E                jr z,.hdraw
1429+ F002 C5                   push bc
1430+ F003 E5                   push hl
1431+ F004 CD FB F8             call occ_human                  ; listed before the building, so
1432+ F007 E1                   pop hl                          ; drawn after it
1433+ F008 E5                   push hl
1434+ F009 CD 0E F9             call occ_zombie
1435+ F00C E1                   pop hl
1436+ F00D C1                   pop bc
1437+ F00E 18 0D                jr draw_bld
1438+ F010              .hdraw:
1439+ F010                  ENDIF
1440+ F010 E5                   push hl
1441+ F011 CD 1D F0             call draw_bld                   ; the building, then the ghosts
1442+ F014 E1                   pop hl                          ; above it and the humans on its
1443+ F015 E5                   push hl                         ; roof
1444+ F016 CD CA 2C             call zombie_cell
1445+ F019 E1                   pop hl
1446+ F01A C3 09 37             jp human_cell
1447+ F01D
1448+ F01D              ; The building in the cell at HL (C = its map byte)
1449+ F01D              BLD_R           equ 256             ; cull radius: 181 (half diagonal) * sqrt2
1450+ F01D              draw_bld:
1451+ F01D                      ; Horizontal frustum cull, valid at any pitch: a visible point has
1452+ F01D                      ; |X|*c <= Z1 with c = cosP - 0.5|sinP|.  Reject the bounding circle
1453+ F01D                      ; (radius BLD_R) round the cell's yaw-space centre (row_x, row_z1,
1454+ F01D                      ; kept up to date by draw_row).  First, as it needs nothing else:
1455+ F01D                      ; most cells it rejects then cost no more.  (Level flight: c is
1456+ F01D                      ; exactly 1.0, so no multiply.)
1457+ F01D E5                   push hl
1458+ F01E C5                   push bc
1459+ F01F 2A 69 FC             ld hl,(row_z1)
1460+ F022 22 63 FC             ld (t_z1),hl
1461+ F025 2A 67 FC             ld hl,(row_x)
1462+ F028 22 65 FC             ld (t_x),hl
1463+ F02B CD E5 AD             call abs_hl

1465+ F031 B7                   or a
1466+ F032 28 07                jr z,.cl
1467+ F034 ED 5B 75 B4          ld de,(cull_c)
1468+ F038 CD EA AD             call fmul
1469+ F03B ED 5B 63 FC  .cl:    ld de,(t_z1)
1470+ F03F B7                   or a
1471+ F040 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1472+ F042 11 00 01             ld de,BLD_R
1473+ F045 B7                   or a
1474+ F046 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1475+ F048 C1                   pop bc
1476+ F049 E1                   pop hl
1477+ F04A F0                   ret p                           ; wholly outside: nothing to draw
1478+ F04B 11 00 F0             ld de,city_col-city_map         ; same index in city_col (city bank)
1479+ F04E 19                   add hl,de
1480+ F04F 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1481+ F050 E6 FC                and %11111100
1482+ F052 32 53 FC             ld (bld_col),a                  ; colour family (shade bits clear)
1483+ F055 79                   ld a,c
1484+ F056 CD AA AC             call bld_height
1485+ F059 22 50 FC             ld (bld_h),hl
1486+ F05C                  IFDEF SKY_IS_WALL
1487+ F05C                      ; Perimeter wall (outer row/column): everything above the horizon is
1488+ F05C                      ; already wall-coloured, so draw it only up to camera height - its
1489+ F05C                      ; top edge then lies exactly on the horizon line, at any pitch.
1490+ F05C AF                   xor a

1493+ F063 3C                   inc a
1494+ F064 E6 3E                and GRID-2                      ; zero for column 0 or GRID-1
1495+ F066 28 08                jr z,.wall
1496+ F068 3A 4F FC             ld a,(cell_j)
1497+ F06B 3C                   inc a
1498+ F06C E6 3E                and GRID-2
1499+ F06E 20 0B                jr nz,.nwall
1500+ F070 2A 29 AE     .wall:  ld hl,(cam_y)
1501+ F073 22 50 FC             ld (bld_h),hl
1502+ F076 3E 01                ld a,1
1503+ F078 32 52 FC             ld (bld_wall),a
1504+ F07B              .nwall:
1505+ F07B                  ENDIF
1506+ F07B                      ; world corners: the building fills its cell
1507+ F07B 3A 4E FC             ld a,(cell_i)
1508+ F07E 67                   ld h,a
1509+ F07F 2E 00                ld l,0
1510+ F081 22 57 FC             ld (bld_x0),hl

1512+ F085 22 59 FC             ld (bld_x1),hl

1516+ F08F 24                   inc h
1517+ F090 22 5D FC             ld (bld_z1),hl
1518+ F093
1519+ F093 21 65 B4             ld hl,vecs0
1520+ F096 22 55 FC             ld (bld_vecs),hl
1521+ F099                      ; (the frustum test was first: t_x, t_z1 hold the cell's centre)
1522+ F099                  IFDEF OCCLUSION_CULL
1523+ F099 3A 6E FC             ld a,(occ_on)
1524+ F09C B7                   or a                            ; (carry clear)
1525+ F09D C4 83 FA             call nz,occ_pretest
1526+ F0A0 D8                   ret c                           ; surely hidden: skip the transform
1527+ F0A1                  ENDIF
1528+ F0A1                      ; centre -> corner (x0,z0), still in yaw space
1529+ F0A1 DD 2A 55 FC          ld ix,(bld_vecs)

1577+ F1E8                      ; C = visible faces: bit0 -X, bit1 +X, bit2 -Z, bit3 +Z, bit4 top
