1758+ F35A 28 0B                jr z,.nws
1759+ F35C 3A 53 FC             ld a,(bld_col)                  ; wall: every side in the base colour,
1760+ F35F F6 01                or SHADE_FACE                   ; to match the wall-coloured sky
1761+ F361 32 B8 FC             ld (col_x),a
1762+ F364 32 B9 FC             ld (col_z),a
1763+ F367              .nws:
1764+ F367                  ENDIF
1765+ F367 3A B5 FC             ld a,(bld_vis)
1766+ F36A 4F                   ld c,a
1767+ F36B                      ; An edge shared with a face drawn later is left to that face:
1768+ F36B                      ; X faces skip their top edge if the roof shows and the vertical
1769+ F36B                      ; edge shared with a visible Z face; Z faces skip the top edge.
1770+ F36B 06 0F                ld b,$0F
1771+ F36D CB 61                bit 4,c
1772+ F36F 28 02                jr z,.m1
1773+ F371 CB 90                res 2,b
1774+ F373 78           .m1:    ld a,b
1775+ F374 32 B7 FC             ld (bld_mz),a
1776+ F377 CB 59                bit 3,c
1777+ F379 28 02                jr z,.m2
1778+ F37B CB 88                res 1,b
1779+ F37D CB 51        .m2:    bit 2,c
1780+ F37F 28 02                jr z,.m3
1781+ F381 CB 98                res 3,b
1782+ F383 78           .m3:    ld a,b
1783+ F384 32 B6 FC             ld (bld_mx),a
1784+ F387                  IFDEF OCCLUSION_CULL
1785+ F387 3A 6E FC             ld a,(occ_on)
1786+ F38A B7                   or a
1787+ F38B C2 1D F9             jp nz,occ_record                ; level flight: draw it later
1788+ F38E                  ENDIF
1789+ F38E
1790+ F38E              ; Draw the visible faces of the building whose corners are at vtx_hi:00,
1791+ F38E              ; from bld_vis, bld_mx, bld_mz, col_x, col_z and col_top
1792+ F38E              draw_faces:
1793+ F38E 3A B5 FC             ld a,(bld_vis)
1794+ F391 CB 47                bit 0,a
1795+ F393 28 12                jr z,.f1
1796+ F395 3A B6 FC             ld a,(bld_mx)
1797+ F398 32 B2 FC             ld (df_mask),a
1798+ F39B 3A B8 FC             ld a,(col_x)
1799+ F39E 32 54 FC             ld (face_col),a
1800+ F3A1 21 28 FC             ld hl,face_nx
1801+ F3A4 CD BB EA             call draw_face
1802+ F3A7 3A B5 FC     .f1:    ld a,(bld_vis)
1803+ F3AA CB 4F                bit 1,a
1804+ F3AC 28 12                jr z,.f2
1805+ F3AE 3A B6 FC             ld a,(bld_mx)
1806+ F3B1 32 B2 FC             ld (df_mask),a
1807+ F3B4 3A B8 FC             ld a,(col_x)
1808+ F3B7 32 54 FC             ld (face_col),a
1809+ F3BA 21 2C FC             ld hl,face_px
1810+ F3BD CD BB EA             call draw_face
1811+ F3C0 3A B5 FC     .f2:    ld a,(bld_vis)
1812+ F3C3 CB 57                bit 2,a
1813+ F3C5 28 12                jr z,.f3
1814+ F3C7 3A B7 FC             ld a,(bld_mz)
1815+ F3CA 32 B2 FC             ld (df_mask),a
1816+ F3CD 3A B9 FC             ld a,(col_z)
1817+ F3D0 32 54 FC             ld (face_col),a
1818+ F3D3 21 30 FC             ld hl,face_nz
1819+ F3D6 CD BB EA             call draw_face
1820+ F3D9 3A B5 FC     .f3:    ld a,(bld_vis)
1821+ F3DC CB 5F                bit 3,a
1822+ F3DE 28 12                jr z,.f4
1823+ F3E0 3A B7 FC             ld a,(bld_mz)
1824+ F3E3 32 B2 FC             ld (df_mask),a
1825+ F3E6 3A B9 FC             ld a,(col_z)
1826+ F3E9 32 54 FC             ld (face_col),a
1827+ F3EC 21 34 FC             ld hl,face_pz
1828+ F3EF CD BB EA             call draw_face
1829+ F3F2 3A B5 FC     .f4:    ld a,(bld_vis)
1830+ F3F5 CB 67                bit 4,a
1831+ F3F7 C8                   ret z
1832+ F3F8 3E 0F                ld a,$0F
1833+ F3FA 32 B2 FC             ld (df_mask),a
1834+ F3FD 3A BA FC             ld a,(col_top)
1835+ F400 32 54 FC             ld (face_col),a
1836+ F403 21 38 FC             ld hl,face_top
1837+ F406 C3 BB EA             jp draw_face
1838+ F409
1839+ F409              ; IX -> vertex: outcode into (ix+6), ANDed into and_oc.  A = outcode.
1840+ F409              vtx_oc:
1841+ F409 DD E5                push ix
1842+ F40B E1                   pop hl
1843+ F40C CD 95 E8             call calc_oc
1844+ F40F DD 77 06             ld (ix+6),a
1845+ F412 21 7D FC             ld hl,and_oc
1846+ F415 A6                   and (hl)
1847+ F416 77                   ld (hl),a
1848+ F417 DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
1849+ F41A C9                   ret
1850+ F41B
1851+ F41B              ; IX -> vertex inside the frustum: project it into (ix+7) sx, (ix+8) sy
1852+ F41B              vtx_proj:
1853+ F41B DD E5                push ix
1854+ F41D E1                   pop hl
1855+ F41E CD B0 E7             call project
1856+ F421 DD 77 07             ld (ix+7),a
1857+ F424 DD 71 08             ld (ix+8),c
1858+ F427 C9                   ret
1859+ F428
1860+ F428              ; B = i, A = j of a neighbouring cell (may be off the map) -> carry set if
1861+ F428              ; it holds a building at least as tall as this one (bld_h).  Keeps C.
1862+ F428              nb_cover:
1863+ F428 FE 40                cp GRID
1864+ F42A D0                   ret nc                          ; off the map (carry clear)
1865+ F42B 6F                   ld l,a
1866+ F42C 78                   ld a,b
1867+ F42D FE 40                cp GRID
1868+ F42F D0                   ret nc
1869+ F430 26 00                ld h,0
1870+ F432                      ROWOFS
1870+ F432 29          >        add hl,hl
1870+ F433 29          >        add hl,hl
1870+ F434 29          >        add hl,hl
1870+ F435 29          >        add hl,hl
1870+ F436 29          >        add hl,hl
1870+ F437 29          >        add hl,hl
1871+ F438 ED 31                add hl,a
1872+ F43A 11 00 70             ld de,city_map
1873+ F43D 19                   add hl,de
1874+ F43E 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1875+ F43F E6 1F                and CELL_CODE
1876+ F441 C8                   ret z                           ; street (carry clear)
1877+ F442 CD AA AC             call bld_height
1878+ F445 ED 5B 50 FC          ld de,(bld_h)
1879+ F449 B7                   or a
1880+ F44A ED 52                sbc hl,de
1881+ F44C 3F                   ccf                             ; carry = neighbour >= this building
1882+ F44D C9                   ret
1883+ F44E
1884+ F44E              ; Yaw rotation of (t_dx, t_dz):  Z1 = dx sinY + dz cosY -> t_z1
1885+ F44E              ;                               X  = dx cosY - dz sinY -> HL
1886+ F44E              rot_yaw:
1887+ F44E 2A 5F FC             ld hl,(t_dx)
1888+ F451 ED 5B 59 B4          ld de,(sin_y)
1889+ F455 CD EA AD             call fmul
1890+ F458 E5                   push hl
1891+ F459 2A 61 FC             ld hl,(t_dz)
1892+ F45C ED 5B 5B B4          ld de,(cos_y)
1893+ F460 CD EA AD             call fmul
1894+ F463 D1                   pop de
1895+ F464 19                   add hl,de
1896+ F465 22 63 FC             ld (t_z1),hl
1897+ F468 2A 61 FC             ld hl,(t_dz)
1898+ F46B ED 5B 59 B4          ld de,(sin_y)
1899+ F46F CD EA AD             call fmul
1900+ F472 E5                   push hl
1901+ F473 2A 5F FC             ld hl,(t_dx)
1902+ F476 ED 5B 5B B4          ld de,(cos_y)
1903+ F47A CD EA AD             call fmul
1904+ F47D D1                   pop de
1905+ F47E B7                   or a
1906+ F47F ED 52                sbc hl,de
1907+ F481 C9                   ret
1908+ F482
1909+ F482              ; Pitch rotation of a ground point at t_z1:
1910+ F482              ;   HL = Y = yb - Z1 sinP     DE = Z = zb + Z1 cosP
1911+ F482              ; (Level flight: sinP 0 and cosP 1.0 exactly, so Y = yb, Z = zb + Z1.)
1912+ F482              rot_pitch:
1913+ F482 3A 2E AE             ld a,(pitch)
1914+ F485 B7                   or a
1915+ F486 20 0D                jr nz,.p
1916+ F488 2A 63 FC             ld hl,(t_z1)
1917+ F48B ED 5B 63 B4          ld de,(zb)
1918+ F48F 19                   add hl,de
1919+ F490 EB                   ex de,hl
1920+ F491 2A 61 B4             ld hl,(yb)
1921+ F494 C9                   ret
1922+ F495 2A 63 FC     .p:     ld hl,(t_z1)
1923+ F498 ED 5B 5D B4          ld de,(sin_p)
1924+ F49C CD EA AD             call fmul
1925+ F49F EB                   ex de,hl
1926+ F4A0 2A 61 B4             ld hl,(yb)
1927+ F4A3 B7                   or a
1928+ F4A4 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1929+ F4A6 E5                   push hl
1930+ F4A7 2A 63 FC             ld hl,(t_z1)
1931+ F4AA ED 5B 5F B4          ld de,(cos_p)
1932+ F4AE CD EA AD             call fmul
1933+ F4B1 ED 5B 63 B4          ld de,(zb)
1934+ F4B5 19                   add hl,de
1935+ F4B6 EB                   ex de,hl
1936+ F4B7 E1                   pop hl
1937+ F4B8 C9                   ret
1938+ F4B9
1939+ F4B9              ;==============================================================================
1940+ F4B9              ; Street intersection markers - one dot per grid corner near the camera
1941+ F4B9              ;==============================================================================
1942+ F4B9              draw_dots:
1943+ F4B9 3A 7E B4             ld a,(cz)
1944+ F4BC 21 3F AE             ld hl,dot_r
1945+ F4BF 96                   sub (hl)
1946+ F4C0 32 9B FC             ld (dot_j),a
1947+ F4C3 3A 3F AE             ld a,(dot_r)
1948+ F4C6 87                   add a,a
1949+ F4C7 C6 02                add a,2
1950+ F4C9 32 9C FC             ld (dot_rows),a
1951+ F4CC 3A 9B FC     .row:   ld a,(dot_j)
1952+ F4CF FE 41                cp GRID+1
1953+ F4D1 30 5E                jr nc,.nrow
1954+ F4D3                      ; first corner of the row by full rotation, then step along x:
1955+ F4D3                      ; +256 world units in x adds exactly (cosY, sinY) to (X, Z1)
1956+ F4D3 3A 7D B4             ld a,(cx)
1957+ F4D6 21 3F AE             ld hl,dot_r
1958+ F4D9 96                   sub (hl)
1959+ F4DA 32 9A FC             ld (dot_i),a
1960+ F4DD 67                   ld h,a
1961+ F4DE 2E 00                ld l,0
1962+ F4E0 ED 5B 24 AE          ld de,(cam_x+1)
1963+ F4E4 B7                   or a
1964+ F4E5 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1965+ F4E7 22 5F FC             ld (t_dx),hl
1966+ F4EA 3A 9B FC             ld a,(dot_j)
1967+ F4ED 67                   ld h,a
1968+ F4EE 2E 00                ld l,0
1969+ F4F0 ED 5B 27 AE          ld de,(cam_z+1)
1970+ F4F4 B7                   or a
1971+ F4F5 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1972+ F4F7 22 61 FC             ld (t_dz),hl
1973+ F4FA CD 4E F4             call rot_yaw
1974+ F4FD 22 65 FC             ld (t_x),hl
1975+ F500 3A 3F AE             ld a,(dot_r)
1976+ F503 87                   add a,a
1977+ F504 C6 02                add a,2
1978+ F506 32 9D FC             ld (dot_cols),a
1979+ F509 3A 9A FC     .col:   ld a,(dot_i)
1980+ F50C FE 41                cp GRID+1
1981+ F50E DC 3C F5             call c,plot_dot
1982+ F511 2A 65 FC             ld hl,(t_x)
1983+ F514 ED 5B 5B B4          ld de,(cos_y)
1984+ F518 19                   add hl,de
1985+ F519 22 65 FC             ld (t_x),hl
1986+ F51C 2A 63 FC             ld hl,(t_z1)
1987+ F51F ED 5B 59 B4          ld de,(sin_y)
1988+ F523 19                   add hl,de
1989+ F524 22 63 FC             ld (t_z1),hl
1990+ F527 21 9A FC             ld hl,dot_i
1991+ F52A 34                   inc (hl)
1992+ F52B 21 9D FC             ld hl,dot_cols
1993+ F52E 35                   dec (hl)
1994+ F52F 20 D8                jr nz,.col
1995+ F531 21 9B FC     .nrow:  ld hl,dot_j
1996+ F534 34                   inc (hl)
1997+ F535 21 9C FC             ld hl,dot_rows
1998+ F538 35                   dec (hl)
1999+ F539 20 91                jr nz,.row
2000+ F53B C9                   ret
2001+ F53C
2002+ F53C              ; Plot the ground point (t_x, t_z1)
2003+ F53C              plot_dot:

2005+ F53F 22 9E FC             ld (tmpv+0),hl
2006+ F542 CD E5 AD             call abs_hl

2008+ F549 CD EA AD             call fmul
2009+ F54C ED 5B 63 FC          ld de,(t_z1)

2011+ F551 ED 52                sbc hl,de
2012+ F553 F0                   ret p                           ; outside horizontal frustum / behind
2013+ F554 CD 82 F4             call rot_pitch

2015+ F55A ED 53 A2 FC          ld (tmpv+4),de
2016+ F55E 21 9E FC             ld hl,tmpv
2017+ F561 CD 95 E8             call calc_oc
2018+ F564 B7                   or a
2019+ F565 C0                   ret nz
2020+ F566 21 9E FC             ld hl,tmpv

2023+ F56D 79                   ld a,c

2026+ F572 36 17                ld (hl),DOT_COL
2027+ F574 C9                   ret
2028+ F575
2029+ F575              ;==============================================================================
2030+ F575              ; Rounds and the laser beam (`bullets`, laser_rec; moved by weapons.asm): a
2031+ F575              ; line along the path each covered this frame, from where it started to
2032+ F575              ; where it is.  A fresh round's path starts at the muzzle, below and behind
2033+ F575              ; the view, so its start is moved along the line to where it enters the
2034+ F575              ; view and pinned to that screen edge: the shot rises from the bottom row.
2035+ F575              ; Drawn over the city; a line whose end is outside the view is skipped.
2036+ F575              ;==============================================================================
2037+ F575              draw_bullets:
2038+ F575 DD 21 E5 AE          ld ix,bullets                   ; the rounds, then the laser beam
2039+ F579 06 19                ld b,NBULLETS+1
2040+ F57B C5           .lp:    push bc
2041+ F57C DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+B_STATE)
2042+ F57F B7                   or a
2043+ F580 28 53                jr z,.nx
2044+ F582 DD 7E 1A             ld a,(ix+B_KIND)
2045+ F585 FE 01                cp BK_ROCKET
2046+ F587 38 09                jr c,.rnd                       ; a round (or a Bofors shell)
2047+ F589 FE 03                cp BK_MISSILE+1
2048+ F58B 30 05                jr nc,.rnd
2049+ F58D CD DE F5             call draw_rocket                ; a rocket or a missile
2050+ F590 18 43                jr .nx
2051+ F592 DD 7E 00     .rnd:   ld a,(ix+B_STATE)
2052+ F595 FE 03                cp 3                            ; 3: laser beam

2054+ F599 20 02                jr nz,.col
2055+ F59B 3E 23                ld a,LASER_COL
2056+ F59D 32 94 FD     .col:   ld (line_col),a
2057+ F5A0 DD E5                push ix                         ; end: where it is now
2058+ F5A2 E1                   pop hl
2059+ F5A3 23                   inc hl                          ; -> B_X, B_Y, B_Z
2060+ F5A4 CD B7 F6             call bul_cam
2061+ F5A7 20 2C                jr nz,.nx                       ; not in view
2062+ F5A9 32 8E FC             ld (bl_x),a
2063+ F5AC 79                   ld a,c
2064+ F5AD 32 8F FC             ld (bl_y),a
2065+ F5B0 21 9E FC             ld hl,tmpv
2066+ F5B3 11 90 FC             ld de,bh_cam
2067+ F5B6 01 06 00             ld bc,6
2068+ F5B9 ED B0                ldir

2073+ F5C2 CD B7 F6             call bul_cam
2074+ F5C5 C4 1D F7             call nz,bul_clip                ; outside: where the line enters
2075+ F5C8 5F                   ld e,a
2076+ F5C9 51                   ld d,c
2077+ F5CA 3A 8E FC             ld a,(bl_x)
2078+ F5CD 6F                   ld l,a
2079+ F5CE 3A 8F FC             ld a,(bl_y)

2082+ F5D5 11 1B 00     .nx:    ld de,BREC

2084+ F5DA C1                   pop bc
2085+ F5DB 10 9E                djnz .lp
2086+ F5DD C9                   ret
2087+ F5DE
2088+ F5DE              ; Rocket IX: a white streak ROCKET_LEN (1 m) long from its head back along
2089+ F5DE              ; its path, then a yellow trail to ROCKET_TRAIL (1.5 m) behind the head.
2090+ F5DE              ; Drawn only when every point drawn is in view (it's tiny: no clipping).
2091+ F5DE              draw_rocket:

2107+ F5FE 3E 13                ld a,ROCKET_TRAIL               ; the end of the trail

2110+ F606 20 12                jr nz,.white
2111+ F608 5F                   ld e,a                          ; yellow trail first, so the white
2112+ F609 51                   ld d,c                          ; streak stays on top
2113+ F60A 3A 86 FC             ld a,(rk_sx)
2114+ F60D 6F                   ld l,a
2115+ F60E 3A 87 FC             ld a,(rk_sy)
2116+ F611 67                   ld h,a
2117+ F612 3E 1F                ld a,TRAIL_COL

2121+ F61D 5F                   ld e,a
2122+ F61E 3A 87 FC             ld a,(rk_sy)
2123+ F621 57                   ld d,a
2124+ F622 18 08                jr .line
2125+ F624 3A 8E FC     .head:  ld a,(bl_x)
2126+ F627 5F                   ld e,a
2127+ F628 3A 8F FC             ld a,(bl_y)
2128+ F62B 57                   ld d,a
2129+ F62C 3A 8E FC     .line:  ld a,(bl_x)
2130+ F62F 6F                   ld l,a
2131+ F630 3A 8F FC             ld a,(bl_y)
2132+ F633 67                   ld h,a
2133+ F634 3E 0B                ld a,ROCKET_COL
2134+ F636 32 94 FD             ld (line_col),a
2135+ F639 C3 0A EE             jp draw_line
2136+ F63C
2137+ F63C              ; A = distance d (units) -> HL -> rk_pt = rocket IX's head - d * (sin, slope,
2138+ F63C              ; cos): d back along its path
2139+ F63C              rk_back:

2141+ F63F DD 5E 07             ld e,(ix+B_SIN)

2145+ F64B 22 7F FC             ld (rk_pt),hl

2147+ F651 DD 56 0C             ld d,(ix+B_SLOPE+1)

2149+ F657 CD 70 F6             call .ax
2150+ F65A 22 81 FC             ld (rk_pt+2),hl

2161+ F674 6F                   ld l,a

2171+ F682 66                   ld h,(hl)

2173+ F684 B7                   or a
2174+ F685 ED 52                sbc hl,de

2177+ F688              ;==============================================================================
2178+ F688              ; Zombie burst particles: one pixel each, coloured by age (PART_COL0 + 4 per
2179+ F688              ; frame of age, bright to dark green), drawn over the city
2180+ F688              ;==============================================================================
2181+ F688              draw_particles:

2193+ F69E 6F                   ld l,a                          ; x

2196+ F6A3 67                   ld h,a

2198+ F6A7 3D                   dec a                           ; + (age-1)*4
2199+ F6A8 87                   add a,a
2200+ F6A9 87                   add a,a

2202+ F6AD 77                   ld (hl),a
2203+ F6AE 11 0C 00     .nx:    ld de,PREC
2204+ F6B1 DD 19                add ix,de
2205+ F6B3 C1                   pop bc
2206+ F6B4 10 D8                djnz .lp
2207+ F6B6 C9                   ret
2208+ F6B7
2209+ F6B7              ; HL -> world x, y, z: camera space into tmpv; A = outcode, Z set if in view
2210+ F6B7              ; (then A = screen x, C = screen y)
2211+ F6B7              bul_cam:
2212+ F6B7 11 88 FC             ld de,bp_x
2213+ F6BA 01 06 00             ld bc,6
2214+ F6BD ED B0                ldir
2215+ F6BF 2A 88 FC             ld hl,(bp_x)
2216+ F6C2 ED 5B 24 AE          ld de,(cam_x+1)
2217+ F6C6 B7                   or a
2218+ F6C7 ED 52                sbc hl,de
2219+ F6C9 22 5F FC             ld (t_dx),hl
2220+ F6CC 2A 8C FC             ld hl,(bp_z)
2221+ F6CF ED 5B 27 AE          ld de,(cam_z+1)
2222+ F6D3 B7                   or a
2223+ F6D4 ED 52                sbc hl,de
2224+ F6D6 22 61 FC             ld (t_dz),hl
2225+ F6D9 CD 4E F4             call rot_yaw
2226+ F6DC 22 9E FC             ld (tmpv+0),hl
2227+ F6DF CD 82 F4             call rot_pitch                  ; at ground level
2228+ F6E2 22 A0 FC             ld (tmpv+2),hl
2229+ F6E5 ED 53 A2 FC          ld (tmpv+4),de
2230+ F6E9 2A 8A FC             ld hl,(bp_y)                    ; + height * (cosP, sinP)
2231+ F6EC ED 5B 5F B4          ld de,(cos_p)
2232+ F6F0 CD EA AD             call fmul
2233+ F6F3 ED 5B A0 FC          ld de,(tmpv+2)
2234+ F6F7 19                   add hl,de
2235+ F6F8 22 A0 FC             ld (tmpv+2),hl
2236+ F6FB 2A 8A FC             ld hl,(bp_y)
2237+ F6FE ED 5B 5D B4          ld de,(sin_p)
2238+ F702 CD EA AD             call fmul
2239+ F705 ED 5B A2 FC          ld de,(tmpv+4)
2240+ F709 19                   add hl,de
2241+ F70A 22 A2 FC             ld (tmpv+4),hl
2242+ F70D 21 9E FC             ld hl,tmpv
2243+ F710 CD 95 E8             call calc_oc
2244+ F713 B7                   or a
2245+ F714 C0                   ret nz
2246+ F715 21 9E FC             ld hl,tmpv
2247+ F718 CD B0 E7             call project
2248+ F71B BF                   cp a                            ; Z set
2249+ F71C C9                   ret
2250+ F71D
2251+ F71D              ; tmpv = the start, outside the planes in A; bh_cam = the end, in view.
2252+ F71D              ; -> A = screen x, C = screen y where the line enters the view, pinned to
2253+ F71D              ; the edge it crosses.
2254+ F71D              bul_clip:
2255+ F71D DD E5                push ix
2256+ F71F 32 96 FC             ld (bc_oc),a
2257+ F722 21 00 00             ld hl,0
2258+ F725 22 97 FC             ld (bc_t),hl
2259+ F728 0E 01                ld c,1                          ; each plane the start is outside
2260+ F72A 3A 96 FC     .pl:    ld a,(bc_oc)
2261+ F72D A1                   and c
2262+ F72E 28 34                jr z,.np
2263+ F730 79                   ld a,c
2264+ F731 32 BE FC             ld (clip_plane),a
2265+ F734 C5                   push bc
2266+ F735 DD 21 9E FC          ld ix,tmpv
2267+ F739 CD DE E8             call plane_dist                 ; start: < 0
2268+ F73C E5                   push hl
2269+ F73D DD 21 90 FC          ld ix,bh_cam
2270+ F741 CD DE E8             call plane_dist                 ; end: >= 0
2271+ F744 D1                   pop de
2272+ F745 B7                   or a
2273+ F746 ED 52                sbc hl,de                       ; fe - fs
2274+ F748 EB                   ex de,hl
2275+ F749 CD DE AD             call neg_hl                     ; -fs
2276+ F74C CD 83 E7             call div_frac                   ; t = -fs / (fe - fs)
2277+ F74F DD E5                push ix
2278+ F751 E1                   pop hl
2279+ F752 ED 5B 97 FC          ld de,(bc_t)                    ; the latest crossing counts
2280+ F756 B7                   or a
2281+ F757 ED 52                sbc hl,de
2282+ F759 C1                   pop bc
2283+ F75A 38 08                jr c,.np
2284+ F75C 19                   add hl,de
2285+ F75D 22 97 FC             ld (bc_t),hl
2286+ F760 79                   ld a,c
2287+ F761 32 99 FC             ld (bc_pl),a
2288+ F764 CB 21        .np:    sla c
2289+ F766 79                   ld a,c
2290+ F767 FE 20                cp 32
2291+ F769 20 BF                jr nz,.pl
2292+ F76B DD 21 9E FC          ld ix,tmpv                      ; start += t * (end - start)
2293+ F76F 06 03                ld b,3
2294+ F771 C5           .lc:    push bc
2295+ F772 DD 6E F2             ld l,(ix+bh_cam-tmpv)           ; end - start
2296+ F775 DD 66 F3             ld h,(ix+bh_cam-tmpv+1)
2297+ F778 DD 5E 00             ld e,(ix+0)
2298+ F77B DD 56 01             ld d,(ix+1)
2299+ F77E B7                   or a
2300+ F77F ED 52                sbc hl,de
2301+ F781 D5                   push de
2302+ F782 ED 5B 97 FC          ld de,(bc_t)
2303+ F786 CD 50 E7             call mul_frac
2304+ F789 D1                   pop de
2305+ F78A 19                   add hl,de
2306+ F78B DD 75 00             ld (ix+0),l
2307+ F78E DD 74 01             ld (ix+1),h
2308+ F791 DD 23                inc ix
2309+ F793 DD 23                inc ix
2310+ F795 C1                   pop bc
2311+ F796 10 D9                djnz .lc
2312+ F798 DD E1                pop ix
2313+ F79A 21 9E FC             ld hl,tmpv
2314+ F79D CD B0 E7             call project
2315+ F7A0 5F                   ld e,a
2316+ F7A1 3A 99 FC             ld a,(bc_pl)                    ; pin to the edge it crossed
2317+ F7A4 FE 10                cp 16
2318+ F7A6 20 02                jr nz,.t
2319+ F7A8 0E 7F                ld c,VIEW_H-1                   ; bottom
2320+ F7AA FE 08        .t:     cp 8
2321+ F7AC 20 02                jr nz,.l
2322+ F7AE 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; top
2323+ F7B0 FE 02        .l:     cp 2
2324+ F7B2 20 02                jr nz,.r
2325+ F7B4 1E 00                ld e,0                          ; left
2326+ F7B6 FE 04        .r:     cp 4
2327+ F7B8 20 02                jr nz,.d
2328+ F7BA 1E FF                ld e,255                        ; right
2329+ F7BC 7B           .d:     ld a,e
2330+ F7BD C9                   ret
2331+ F7BE
2332+ F7BE              ;==============================================================================
2333+ F7BE              ; Gunsight in the centre of the Layer 2 viewport
2334+ F7BE              ;==============================================================================
2335+ F7BE              draw_gunsight:
2336+ F7BE 3E 0F                ld a,HUD_COL                    ; centred on row sight_y (the centre
2337+ F7C0 32 94 FD             ld (line_col),a                 ; gun's tilt: gun_aim, weapons.asm)
2338+ F7C3 3A A6 AE             ld a,(sight_y)
2339+ F7C6 67                   ld h,a
2340+ F7C7 57                   ld d,a
2341+ F7C8 2E 7A                ld l,122
2342+ F7CA 1E 7D                ld e,125
2343+ F7CC CD 0A EE             call draw_line
2344+ F7CF 3A A6 AE             ld a,(sight_y)
2345+ F7D2 67                   ld h,a
2346+ F7D3 57                   ld d,a
2347+ F7D4 2E 83                ld l,131
2348+ F7D6 1E 86                ld e,134
2349+ F7D8 CD 0A EE             call draw_line
2350+ F7DB 3A A6 AE             ld a,(sight_y)
2351+ F7DE D6 06                sub 6
2352+ F7E0 67                   ld h,a
2353+ F7E1 C6 03                add a,3
2354+ F7E3 57                   ld d,a
2355+ F7E4 2E 80                ld l,128
2356+ F7E6 5D                   ld e,l
2357+ F7E7 CD 0A EE             call draw_line
2358+ F7EA 3A A6 AE             ld a,(sight_y)
2359+ F7ED C6 03                add a,3
2360+ F7EF 67                   ld h,a
2361+ F7F0 C6 03                add a,3
2362+ F7F2 57                   ld d,a
2363+ F7F3 2E 80                ld l,128
2364+ F7F5 5D                   ld e,l
2365+ F7F6 C3 0A EE             jp draw_line
2366+ F7F9
2367+ F7F9                  IFDEF OCCLUSION_CULL
2368+ F7F9              ;==============================================================================
2369+ F7F9              ; Occlusion culling (OCCLUSION_CULL, level flight only)
2370+ F7F9              ;
2371+ F7F9              ; In level flight every screen column is a vertical slice of the world through
2372+ F7F9              ; the camera.  The cells are walked in exactly the reverse of the painter's
2373+ F7F9              ; order, i.e. front to back.  Each building is transformed as usual, then
2374+ F7F9              ; tested against OCC_YBUF, which holds per column the row from which the
2375+ F7F9              ; screen is known to be covered down to the ground:
2376+ F7F9              ;   * hidden - in every column it spans, its highest point is no higher than
2377+ F7F9              ;     that row - it is dropped;
2378+ F7F9              ;   * otherwise it lowers the row across its columns to its lowest top
2379+ F7F9              ;     corner, and its corners and face settings are kept in a record.
2380+ F7F9              ; The kept buildings and the zombie cells are then drawn back to front.
2381+ F7F9              ; This is safe because buildings that share a column meet the walk front to
2382+ F7F9              ; back (the grid order guarantees it), the camera is above the ground (what
2383+ F7F9              ; is behind sits higher on screen), the cover row is the lowest top corner
2384+ F7F9              ; (an underestimate) and bounds are clamped inwards to the screen.  A
2385+ F7F9              ; building with a corner behind the camera is never dropped and never covers.
2386+ F7F9              ;==============================================================================
2387+ F7F9              OCC_YBUF        equ $4000       ; per column: covered from this row down
2388+ F7F9              OCC_YBUF8       equ $42E0       ; per 8 columns: at least the most of
2389+ F7F9                                              ; OCC_YBUF's 8 (in record 0's unused tail:
2390+ F7F9                                              ; records use 0-133), so a hidden test can
2391+ F7F9                                              ; pass 8 columns at once
2392+ F7F9                      ASSERT low OCC_YBUF8 + 32 <= 256 && OR_VIS + 6 <= low OCC_YBUF8
2393+ F7F9              OCC_LIST        equ $4100       ; kept buildings and zombie cells, 2 bytes each
2394+ F7F9              OCC_REC0        equ $42         ; first record (high byte); 256 bytes each,
2395+ F7F9              OCC_RECN        equ $60         ; up to $5F: 30 records ($6000 on: city_col)
2396+ F7F9              ; Record: copy of vtx (bytes 0-8 of each 16), then
2397+ F7F9              OR_VIS          equ 128         ; bld_vis, bld_mx, bld_mz, col_x, col_z, col_top
2398+ F7F9              ; A list entry is a record's high byte (< $60), a cell with humans as its
2399+ F7F9              ; city_col address ($6000-$6FFF) or a cell with zombies as its city_map
2400+ F7F9              ; address ($7000-$7FFF).
2401+ F7F9
2402+ F7F9              ; Start of the city: occlusion on only in level flight
2403+ F7F9              occ_begin:
2404+ F7F9 AF                   xor a
2405+ F7FA 32 6E FC             ld (occ_on),a
2406+ F7FD 32 6F FC             ld (occ_full),a
2407+ F800 3A 2E AE             ld a,(pitch)
2408+ F803 B7                   or a
2409+ F804 C0                   ret nz
2410+ F805 3C                   inc a
2411+ F806 32 6E FC             ld (occ_on),a
2412+ F809 21 00 41             ld hl,OCC_LIST
2413+ F80C 22 70 FC             ld (occ_lp),hl
2414+ F80F 3E 42                ld a,OCC_REC0
2415+ F811 32 72 FC             ld (occ_rec),a
2416+ F814 21 00 40             ld hl,OCC_YBUF                  ; nothing covered yet
2417+ F817 36 80                ld (hl),VIEW_H
2418+ F819 11 01 40             ld de,OCC_YBUF+1
2419+ F81C 01 FF 00             ld bc,255
2420+ F81F ED B0                ldir
2421+ F821 21 E0 42             ld hl,OCC_YBUF8                 ; (and so in every group of 8)
2422+ F824 36 80                ld (hl),VIEW_H
2423+ F826 11 E1 42             ld de,OCC_YBUF8+1
2424+ F829 01 1F 00             ld bc,31
2425+ F82C ED B0                ldir
2426+ F82E C9                   ret
2427+ F82F
2428+ F82F              ; The cells of draw_cells in exactly the reverse order.  Each half row
2429+ F82F              ; starts from the same cell as draw_row and steps the same way, so every
2430+ F82F              ; cell gets bit-identical yaw-space coordinates.
2431+ F82F              occ_cells:
2432+ F82F 3A 7E B4             ld a,(cz)
2433+ F832 32 4F FC             ld (cell_j),a
2434+ F835 CD 5D F8             call occ_row                    ; camera row first
2435+ F838 3A 3E AE             ld a,(view_r)
2436+ F83B 47                   ld b,a
2437+ F83C C5           .f:     push bc
2438+ F83D 21 4F FC             ld hl,cell_j
2439+ F840 34                   inc (hl)
2440+ F841 CD 5D F8             call occ_row                    ; then cz+1 .. cz+R
2441+ F844 C1                   pop bc
2442+ F845 10 F5                djnz .f
2443+ F847 3A 7E B4             ld a,(cz)
2444+ F84A 32 4F FC             ld (cell_j),a
2445+ F84D 3A 3E AE             ld a,(view_r)
2446+ F850 47                   ld b,a
2447+ F851 C5           .n:     push bc
2448+ F852 21 4F FC             ld hl,cell_j
2449+ F855 35                   dec (hl)
2450+ F856 CD 5D F8             call occ_row                    ; then cz-1 .. cz-R
2451+ F859 C1                   pop bc
2452+ F85A 10 F5                djnz .n
2453+ F85C C9                   ret
2454+ F85D
2455+ F85D              occ_row:
2456+ F85D 3A 4F FC             ld a,(cell_j)
2457+ F860 FE 40                cp GRID
2458+ F862 D0                   ret nc
2459+ F863                      ; right half, as draw_row: start at cx+R, step down to cx
2460+ F863 3A 7D B4             ld a,(cx)
2461+ F866 21 3E AE             ld hl,view_r
2462+ F869 86                   add a,(hl)
2463+ F86A 32 4E FC             ld (cell_i),a
2464+ F86D CD 8C EF             call row_start
2465+ F870 3A 3E AE             ld a,(view_r)
2466+ F873 47                   ld b,a
2467+ F874 CD E0 F8     .d:     call occ_stepm
2468+ F877 10 FB                djnz .d
2469+ F879 3A 7D B4             ld a,(cx)
2470+ F87C 32 4E FC             ld (cell_i),a
2471+ F87F CD B9 EF             call try_cell                   ; camera column first
2472+ F882 3A 3E AE             ld a,(view_r)
2473+ F885 47                   ld b,a
2474+ F886 C5           .r:     push bc
2475+ F887 21 4E FC             ld hl,cell_i
2476+ F88A 34                   inc (hl)
2477+ F88B CD C9 F8             call occ_stepp
2478+ F88E CD B9 EF             call try_cell                   ; then cx+1 .. cx+R
2479+ F891 C1                   pop bc
2480+ F892 10 F2                djnz .r
2481+ F894                      ; left half, as draw_row: start at cx-R, step up to cx-1
2482+ F894 3A 7D B4             ld a,(cx)
2483+ F897 21 3E AE             ld hl,view_r
2484+ F89A 96                   sub (hl)
2485+ F89B 32 4E FC             ld (cell_i),a
2486+ F89E CD 8C EF             call row_start
2487+ F8A1 3A 3E AE             ld a,(view_r)
2488+ F8A4 3D                   dec a
2489+ F8A5 47                   ld b,a
2490+ F8A6 CD C9 F8     .u:     call occ_stepp
2491+ F8A9 10 FB                djnz .u
2492+ F8AB 3A 7D B4             ld a,(cx)
2493+ F8AE 3D                   dec a
2494+ F8AF 32 4E FC             ld (cell_i),a
2495+ F8B2 CD B9 EF             call try_cell                   ; then cx-1 .. cx-R
2496+ F8B5 3A 3E AE             ld a,(view_r)
2497+ F8B8 3D                   dec a
2498+ F8B9 47                   ld b,a
2499+ F8BA C5           .l:     push bc
2500+ F8BB 21 4E FC             ld hl,cell_i
2501+ F8BE 35                   dec (hl)
2502+ F8BF CD E0 F8             call occ_stepm
2503+ F8C2 CD B9 EF             call try_cell
2504+ F8C5 C1                   pop bc
2505+ F8C6 10 F2                djnz .l
2506+ F8C8 C9                   ret
2507+ F8C9
2508+ F8C9              ; One cell +x / -x in yaw space: (X, Z1) +/-= (cosY, sinY).  Keeps B.
2509+ F8C9              occ_stepp:
2510+ F8C9 2A 67 FC             ld hl,(row_x)
2511+ F8CC ED 5B 5B B4          ld de,(cos_y)
2512+ F8D0 19                   add hl,de
2513+ F8D1 22 67 FC             ld (row_x),hl
2514+ F8D4 2A 69 FC             ld hl,(row_z1)
2515+ F8D7 ED 5B 59 B4          ld de,(sin_y)
2516+ F8DB 19                   add hl,de
2517+ F8DC 22 69 FC             ld (row_z1),hl
2518+ F8DF C9                   ret
2519+ F8E0              occ_stepm:
2520+ F8E0 2A 67 FC             ld hl,(row_x)
2521+ F8E3 ED 5B 5B B4          ld de,(cos_y)
2522+ F8E7 B7                   or a
2523+ F8E8 ED 52                sbc hl,de
2524+ F8EA 22 67 FC             ld (row_x),hl
2525+ F8ED 2A 69 FC             ld hl,(row_z1)
2526+ F8F0 ED 5B 59 B4          ld de,(sin_y)
2527+ F8F4 B7                   or a
2528+ F8F5 ED 52                sbc hl,de
2529+ F8F7 22 69 FC             ld (row_z1),hl
2530+ F8FA C9                   ret
2531+ F8FB
2532+ F8FB              ; HL -> a city_map byte: list the cell (as its city_col address) if humans
2533+ F8FB              ; stand there (bit 6)
2534+ F8FB              occ_human:
2535+ F8FB CB 76                bit CELL_HUMAN_B,(hl)
2536+ F8FD C8                   ret z
2537+ F8FE 11 00 F0             ld de,city_col-city_map
2538+ F901 19                   add hl,de
2539+ F902 EB                   ex de,hl
2540+ F903 2A 70 FC             ld hl,(occ_lp)
2541+ F906 73                   ld (hl),e
2542+ F907 2C                   inc l
2543+ F908 72                   ld (hl),d
2544+ F909 2C                   inc l
2545+ F90A 22 70 FC             ld (occ_lp),hl
2546+ F90D C9                   ret
2547+ F90E
2548+ F90E              ; HL -> a city_map byte: list the cell if zombies stand there, or special
2549+ F90E              ; monsters fly over it (bit 7)
2550+ F90E              occ_zombie:
2551+ F90E CB 7E                bit CELL_ZOMBIE_B,(hl)
2552+ F910 C8                   ret z
2553+ F911 EB                   ex de,hl
2554+ F912 2A 70 FC             ld hl,(occ_lp)
2555+ F915 73                   ld (hl),e
2556+ F916 2C                   inc l
2557+ F917 72                   ld (hl),d
2558+ F918 2C                   inc l
2559+ F919 22 70 FC             ld (occ_lp),hl
2560+ F91C C9                   ret
2561+ F91D
2562+ F91D              ; draw_cell tail in level flight: drop the building if it is hidden,
2563+ F91D              ; otherwise make it a cover and keep it (corners in vtx, face settings)
2564+ F91D              occ_record:
2565+ F91D 3A 6F FC             ld a,(occ_full)
2566+ F920 B7                   or a
2567+ F921 C0                   ret nz
2568+ F922 3A 72 FC             ld a,(occ_rec)
2569+ F925 FE 60                cp OCC_RECN
2570+ F927 38 06                jr c,.room
2571+ F929 3E 01                ld a,1                          ; out of records: give up this frame
2572+ F92B 32 6F FC             ld (occ_full),a
2573+ F92E C9                   ret
2574+ F92F 32 73 FC     .room:  ld (occ_cur),a
2575+ F932                      ; screen bounds of the corners of the drawn faces
2576+ F932 AF                   xor a
2577+ F933 32 76 FC             ld (o_xmax),a
2578+ F936 32 78 FC             ld (o_tcov),a
2579+ F939 3D                   dec a
2580+ F93A 32 75 FC             ld (o_xmin),a
2581+ F93D 32 77 FC             ld (o_ymin),a
2582+ F940 3A 6D FC             ld a,(bld_used0)
2583+ F943 32 74 FC             ld (o_used),a
2584+ F946 DD 21 00 E0          ld ix,vtx
2585+ F94A 06 08                ld b,8
2586+ F94C C5           .cl:    push bc
2587+ F94D 3A 74 FC             ld a,(o_used)
2588+ F950 0F                   rrca
2589+ F951 32 74 FC             ld (o_used),a
2590+ F954 30 40                jr nc,.nx                       ; corner not on a drawn face
2591+ F956 DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
2592+ F959 CB 47                bit 0,a
2593+ F95B 20 47                jr nz,.keep                     ; behind the near plane: no bounds
2594+ F95D B7                   or a
2595+ F95E C4 1B F4             call nz,vtx_proj                ; off screen: clamped projection
2596+ F961 DD 7E 07             ld a,(ix+7)                     ; sx
2597+ F964 21 75 FC             ld hl,o_xmin
2598+ F967 BE                   cp (hl)
2599+ F968 30 01                jr nc,.x1
2600+ F96A 77                   ld (hl),a
2601+ F96B 21 76 FC     .x1:    ld hl,o_xmax
2602+ F96E BE                   cp (hl)
2603+ F96F 38 01                jr c,.x2
2604+ F971 77                   ld (hl),a
2605+ F972 DD 7E 08     .x2:    ld a,(ix+8)                     ; sy
2606+ F975 21 77 FC             ld hl,o_ymin
2607+ F978 BE                   cp (hl)
2608+ F979 30 01                jr nc,.y1
2609+ F97B 77                   ld (hl),a
2610+ F97C C1           .y1:    pop bc
2611+ F97D C5                   push bc
2612+ F97E 78                   ld a,b
2613+ F97F FE 05                cp 5
2614+ F981 30 13                jr nc,.nx                       ; corners 0-3 are the bottom ones
2615+ F983 DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
2616+ F986 E6 10                and 16
2617+ F988 3E 80                ld a,VIEW_H                     ; top corner below the screen:
2618+ F98A 20 03                jr nz,.tc                       ; covers nothing
2619+ F98C DD 7E 08             ld a,(ix+8)
2620+ F98F 21 78 FC     .tc:    ld hl,o_tcov
2621+ F992 BE                   cp (hl)
2622+ F993 38 01                jr c,.nx
2623+ F995 77                   ld (hl),a                       ; lowest top corner
2624+ F996 11 10 00     .nx:    ld de,16
2625+ F999 DD 19                add ix,de
2626+ F99B C1                   pop bc
2627+ F99C 10 AE                djnz .cl
2628+ F99E CD 38 FB             call occ_test
2629+ F9A1 D8                   ret c                           ; hidden: dropped
2630+ F9A2 18 01                jr .store
2631+ F9A4 C1           .keep:  pop bc                          ; no bounds: keep, but no cover
2632+ F9A5 3A 73 FC     .store: ld a,(occ_cur)
2633+ F9A8 57                   ld d,a
2634+ F9A9 1E 00                ld e,0
2635+ F9AB 21 00 E0             ld hl,vtx
2636+ F9AE                      ; corners: only bytes 0-8 of each 16 are used (X,Y,Z,oc,sx,sy);
2637+ F9AE                      ; vtx and the record are both page aligned, so L = E throughout
2638+ F9AE                      REPT 8
2639+ F9AE ED A0       >        ldi
2640+ F9B0 ED A0       >        ldi
2641+ F9B2 ED A0       >        ldi
2642+ F9B4 ED A0       >        ldi

2650+ FA64 6F          >        ld l,a
2651+ FA65 5F          >        ld e,a
2652+ FA66                      ENDR
2653+ FA66 1E 80                ld e,OR_VIS
2654+ FA68 21 B5 FC             ld hl,bld_vis                   ; bld_vis .. col_top: record order
2655+ FA6B 01 06 00             ld bc,6
2656+ FA6E ED B0                ldir
2657+ FA70 2A 70 FC             ld hl,(occ_lp)                  ; keep it on the list
2658+ FA73 36 00                ld (hl),0
2659+ FA75 2C                   inc l
2660+ FA76 3A 73 FC             ld a,(occ_cur)
2661+ FA79 77                   ld (hl),a
2662+ FA7A 2C                   inc l
2663+ FA7B 22 70 FC             ld (occ_lp),hl
2664+ FA7E 21 72 FC             ld hl,occ_rec
2665+ FA81 34                   inc (hl)
2666+ FA82 C9                   ret
2667+ FA83
2668+ FA83              ; Before the transform: rough screen bounds of the building from its cell
2669+ FA83              ; centre (t_x, t_z1; at pitch 0 camera Z = Z1), a circle of radius OCC_R
2670+ FA83              ; around it and its height, each widened by a pixel for rounding.
2671+ FA83              ; Carry set if those bounds are already hidden.
2672+ FA83              OCC_R           equ 188             ; half the cell diagonal (181) + rounding
2673+ FA83              occ_pretest:
2674+ FA83 3A 6F FC             ld a,(occ_full)
2675+ FA86 B7                   or a
2676+ FA87 C0                   ret nz                          ; not keeping any more: no point
2677+ FA88 2A 63 FC             ld hl,(t_z1)
2678+ FA8B 11 34 FF             ld de,-OCC_R-NEAR
2679+ FA8E 19                   add hl,de
2680+ FA8F CB 7C                bit 7,h
2681+ FA91 C2 19 FB             jp nz,.no                       ; may reach the near plane
2682+ FA94 2A 63 FC             ld hl,(t_z1)
2683+ FA97 11 44 FF             ld de,-OCC_R
2684+ FA9A 19                   add hl,de
2685+ FA9B 22 79 FC             ld (o_zmin),hl
2686+ FA9E 11 78 01             ld de,2*OCC_R
2687+ FAA1 19                   add hl,de
2688+ FAA2 22 7B FC             ld (o_zmax),hl
2689+ FAA5                      ; left edge: X-R, over the far Z if positive, the near Z if negative
2690+ FAA5 2A 65 FC             ld hl,(t_x)
2691+ FAA8 11 44 FF             ld de,-OCC_R
2692+ FAAB 19                   add hl,de
2693+ FAAC ED 5B 7B FC          ld de,(o_zmax)
2694+ FAB0 CB 7C                bit 7,h
2695+ FAB2 28 04                jr z,.l1
2696+ FAB4 ED 5B 79 FC          ld de,(o_zmin)
2697+ FAB8 CD 1B FB     .l1:    call occ_sx
2698+ FABB B7                   or a
2699+ FABC 28 01                jr z,.l2
2700+ FABE 3D                   dec a
2701+ FABF 32 75 FC     .l2:    ld (o_xmin),a
2702+ FAC2                      ; right edge: X+R, over the near Z if positive, the far Z if negative
2703+ FAC2 2A 65 FC             ld hl,(t_x)
2704+ FAC5 11 BC 00             ld de,OCC_R
2705+ FAC8 19                   add hl,de
2706+ FAC9 ED 5B 79 FC          ld de,(o_zmin)
2707+ FACD CB 7C                bit 7,h
2708+ FACF 28 04                jr z,.r1
2709+ FAD1 ED 5B 7B FC          ld de,(o_zmax)
2710+ FAD5 CD 1B FB     .r1:    call occ_sx
2711+ FAD8 3C                   inc a
2712+ FAD9 20 01                jr nz,.r2
2713+ FADB 3D                   dec a
2714+ FADC 32 76 FC     .r2:    ld (o_xmax),a
2715+ FADF                      ; top: height above the eye over the near Z, or below it over the far Z
2716+ FADF 2A 50 FC             ld hl,(bld_h)
2717+ FAE2 ED 5B 29 AE          ld de,(cam_y)
2718+ FAE6 B7                   or a
2719+ FAE7 ED 52                sbc hl,de
2720+ FAE9 38 14                jr c,.below
2721+ FAEB E5                   push hl                         ; (zpatch uses DE)
2722+ FAEC 2A 79 FC             ld hl,(o_zmin)
2723+ FAEF CD 01 E8             call zpatch
2724+ FAF2 E1                   pop hl
2725+ FAF3 CD 45 E8             call scale_comp                 ; A = 128 * (h - camY) / Zmin
2726+ FAF6 4F                   ld c,a
2727+ FAF7 3E 3F                ld a,VIEW_CY-1
2728+ FAF9 91                   sub c
2729+ FAFA 30 17                jr nc,.ty
2730+ FAFC AF                   xor a
2731+ FAFD 18 14                jr .ty
2732+ FAFF CD DE AD     .below: call neg_hl                     ; camY - h
2733+ FB02 E5                   push hl
2734+ FB03 2A 7B FC             ld hl,(o_zmax)
2735+ FB06 CD 01 E8             call zpatch
2736+ FB09 E1                   pop hl
2737+ FB0A CD 45 E8             call scale_comp                 ; A = 128 * (camY - h) / Zmax
2738+ FB0D C6 3F                add a,VIEW_CY-1
2739+ FB0F 30 02                jr nc,.ty
2740+ FB11 3E FF                ld a,255
2741+ FB13 32 77 FC     .ty:    ld (o_ymin),a
2742+ FB16 C3 86 FB             jp occ_hidden
2743+ FB19 B7           .no:    or a
2744+ FB1A C9                   ret
2745+ FB1B
2746+ FB1B              ; HL = X, DE = Z -> A = screen x, clamped to 0..255
2747+ FB1B              occ_sx:
2748+ FB1B 7C                   ld a,h
2749+ FB1C F5                   push af
2750+ FB1D E5                   push hl
2751+ FB1E EB                   ex de,hl
2752+ FB1F CD 01 E8             call zpatch
2753+ FB22 E1                   pop hl
2754+ FB23 CD 45 E8             call scale_comp
2755+ FB26 4F                   ld c,a
2756+ FB27 F1                   pop af
2757+ FB28 17                   rla
2758+ FB29 38 07                jr c,.n
2759+ FB2B 3E 80                ld a,128
2760+ FB2D 81                   add a,c
2761+ FB2E D0                   ret nc
2762+ FB2F 3E FF                ld a,255
2763+ FB31 C9                   ret
2764+ FB32 3E 80        .n:     ld a,128
2765+ FB34 91                   sub c
2766+ FB35 D0                   ret nc
2767+ FB36 AF                   xor a
2768+ FB37 C9                   ret
2769+ FB38
2770+ FB38              ; Bounds o_xmin/o_xmax/o_ymin against the cover buffer: carry set if hidden
2771+ FB38              ; in every column; otherwise lower the cover to o_tcov over those columns.
2772+ FB38              occ_test:
2773+ FB38 CD 86 FB             call occ_hidden
2774+ FB3B D8                   ret c
2775+ FB3C 3A 78 FC             ld a,(o_tcov)
2776+ FB3F FE 80                cp VIEW_H
2777+ FB41 30 41                jr nc,.none                     ; covers nothing
2778+ FB43 4F                   ld c,a
2779+ FB44 6B                   ld l,e
2780+ FB45 7E           .u:     ld a,(hl)
2781+ FB46 B9                   cp c
2782+ FB47 38 01                jr c,.un                        ; already covered higher up
2783+ FB49 71                   ld (hl),c
2784+ FB4A 2C           .un:    inc l
2785+ FB4B 10 F8                djnz .u
2786+ FB4D                      ; and OCC_YBUF8 over those columns' groups: a group wholly inside
2787+ FB4D                      ; them now has at most C (its 8, each lowered to C), so its bound
2788+ FB4D                      ; becomes C if that is less.  A group at either end keeps its bound:
2789+ FB4D                      ; still at least its most, as cover only ever goes down.
2790+ FB4D 3A 75 FC             ld a,(o_xmin)
2791+ FB50 5F                   ld e,a
2792+ FB51 3A 76 FC             ld a,(o_xmax)
2793+ FB54 57                   ld d,a
2794+ FB55 0F                   rrca
2795+ FB56 0F                   rrca
2796+ FB57 0F                   rrca
2797+ FB58 E6 1F                and 31
2798+ FB5A 47                   ld b,a                          ; last group
2799+ FB5B 7B                   ld a,e
2800+ FB5C 0F                   rrca
2801+ FB5D 0F                   rrca
2802+ FB5E 0F                   rrca
2803+ FB5F E6 1F                and 31                          ; first group
2804+ FB61 6F                   ld l,a
2805+ FB62 78                   ld a,b
2806+ FB63 95                   sub l
2807+ FB64 3C                   inc a
2808+ FB65 47                   ld b,a                          ; groups
2809+ FB66 7D                   ld a,l
2810+ FB67 C6 E0                add a,low OCC_YBUF8
2811+ FB69 6F                   ld l,a
2812+ FB6A 26 42                ld h,high OCC_YBUF8
2813+ FB6C 7D           .g:     ld a,l
2814+ FB6D D6 E0                sub low OCC_YBUF8
2815+ FB6F 87                   add a,a
2816+ FB70 87                   add a,a
2817+ FB71 87                   add a,a                         ; the group's first column
2818+ FB72 BB                   cp e
2819+ FB73 38 0C                jr c,.edge                      ; starts before the span
2820+ FB75 C6 07                add a,7
2821+ FB77 BA                   cp d
2822+ FB78 28 02                jr z,.full
2823+ FB7A 30 05                jr nc,.gn                       ; ends after it
2824+ FB7C 7E           .full:  ld a,(hl)
2825+ FB7D B9                   cp c
2826+ FB7E 38 01                jr c,.gn                        ; already less
2827+ FB80 71                   ld (hl),c
2828+ FB81              .edge:
2829+ FB81 2C           .gn:    inc l
2830+ FB82 10 E8                djnz .g
2831+ FB84 B7           .none:  or a                            ; visible (carry clear)
2832+ FB85 C9                   ret
2833+ FB86
2834+ FB86
2835+ FB86              ; Carry set if o_xmin..o_xmax are all covered from o_ymin or higher.
2836+ FB86              ; Leaves E = o_xmin, B = column count, H = high OCC_YBUF.  Whole groups of 8
2837+ FB86              ; columns are passed at once if their bound (OCC_YBUF8, at least the most of
2838+ FB86              ; the 8) is covered; if it isn't, the 8 are looked at one by one, so the
2839+ FB86              ; answer is exactly the column-by-column one.
2840+ FB86              occ_hidden:
2841+ FB86 3A 75 FC             ld a,(o_xmin)
2842+ FB89 5F                   ld e,a
2843+ FB8A 3A 76 FC             ld a,(o_xmax)
2844+ FB8D 93                   sub e
2845+ FB8E 3C                   inc a
2846+ FB8F 47                   ld b,a                          ; columns (0 = all 256)
2847+ FB90 3A 77 FC             ld a,(o_ymin)
2848+ FB93 3C                   inc a
2849+ FB94 4F                   ld c,a                          ; hidden where cover <= ymin
2850+ FB95 26 40                ld h,high OCC_YBUF
2851+ FB97 6B                   ld l,e
2852+ FB98 16 42                ld d,high OCC_YBUF8
2853+ FB9A C5                   push bc
2854+ FB9B 7D                   ld a,l
2855+ FB9C E6 07                and 7
2856+ FB9E 20 2E                jr nz,.one                      ; not at a group's start
2857+ FBA0 78           .grp:   ld a,b
2858+ FBA1 3D                   dec a                           ; (B = 0: all 256 left)
2859+ FBA2 FE 07                cp 7
2860+ FBA4 38 28                jr c,.one                       ; fewer than 8 columns left
2861+ FBA6 7D                   ld a,l
2862+ FBA7 0F                   rrca
2863+ FBA8 0F                   rrca
2864+ FBA9 0F                   rrca                            ; the group (L's low 3 bits are 0)
2865+ FBAA C6 E0                add a,low OCC_YBUF8
2866+ FBAC 5F                   ld e,a
2867+ FBAD 1A                   ld a,(de)
2868+ FBAE B9                   cp c
2869+ FBAF 30 0C                jr nc,.g8                       ; maybe not: look at the 8
2870+ FBB1 7D                   ld a,l
2871+ FBB2 C6 08                add a,8
2872+ FBB4 6F                   ld l,a
2873+ FBB5 78                   ld a,b
2874+ FBB6 D6 08                sub 8
2875+ FBB8 47                   ld b,a
2876+ FBB9 20 E5                jr nz,.grp
2877+ FBBB 18 20                jr .hid
2878+ FBBD 1E 08        .g8:    ld e,8                          ; the group's 8, one by one
2879+ FBBF 7E           .g8l:   ld a,(hl)
2880+ FBC0 B9                   cp c
2881+ FBC1 30 21                jr nc,.vis
2882+ FBC3 2C                   inc l
2883+ FBC4 05                   dec b
2884+ FBC5 1D                   dec e
2885+ FBC6 20 F7                jr nz,.g8l
2886+ FBC8 78                   ld a,b
2887+ FBC9 B7                   or a
2888+ FBCA 20 D4                jr nz,.grp
2889+ FBCC 18 0F                jr .hid
2890+ FBCE 7E           .one:   ld a,(hl)                       ; one column
2891+ FBCF B9                   cp c
2892+ FBD0 30 12                jr nc,.vis
2893+ FBD2 2C                   inc l
2894+ FBD3 05                   dec b
2895+ FBD4 28 07                jr z,.hid
2896+ FBD6 7D                   ld a,l
2897+ FBD7 E6 07                and 7
2898+ FBD9 20 F3                jr nz,.one
2899+ FBDB 18 C3                jr .grp                         ; at a group's start
2900+ FBDD C1           .hid:   pop bc
2901+ FBDE 3A 75 FC             ld a,(o_xmin)
2902+ FBE1 5F                   ld e,a
2903+ FBE2 37                   scf
2904+ FBE3 C9                   ret
2905+ FBE4 C1           .vis:   pop bc
2906+ FBE5 3A 75 FC             ld a,(o_xmin)
2907+ FBE8 5F                   ld e,a
2908+ FBE9 B7                   or a
2909+ FBEA C9                   ret
2910+ FBEB
2911+ FBEB              ; Draw the kept buildings and zombie cells, back to front (list backwards)
2912+ FBEB              occ_draw_list:
2913+ FBEB 2A 70 FC             ld hl,(occ_lp)
2914+ FBEE 7D           .l:     ld a,l
2915+ FBEF B7                   or a
2916+ FBF0 C8                   ret z
2917+ FBF1 2D                   dec l
2918+ FBF2 56                   ld d,(hl)
2919+ FBF3 2D                   dec l
2920+ FBF4 5E                   ld e,(hl)
2921+ FBF5 E5                   push hl
2922+ FBF6 7A                   ld a,d
2923+ FBF7 FE 60                cp $60
2924+ FBF9 38 13                jr c,.bld                       ; < $60: a building record

2926+ FBFC FE 70                cp high city_map
2927+ FBFE 30 09                jr nc,.zom
2928+ FC00 11 00 10             ld de,city_map-city_col         ; $60-$6F: humans (city_col address)
2929+ FC03 19                   add hl,de
2930+ FC04 CD 09 37             call human_cell
2931+ FC07 18 1C                jr .nx
2932+ FC09 CD CA 2C     .zom:   call zombie_cell                ; $70-$7F: zombies (city_map address)
2933+ FC0C 18 17                jr .nx
2934+ FC0E 62           .bld:   ld h,d                          ; record D
2935+ FC0F 2E 80                ld l,OR_VIS
2936+ FC11 11 B5 FC             ld de,bld_vis
2937+ FC14 01 06 00             ld bc,6
2938+ FC17 ED B0                ldir
2939+ FC19 7C                   ld a,h
2940+ FC1A 32 6C FC             ld (vtx_hi),a
2941+ FC1D CD 8E F3             call draw_faces
2942+ FC20 3E E0                ld a,high vtx
2943+ FC22 32 6C FC             ld (vtx_hi),a
2944+ FC25 E1           .nx:    pop hl
2945+ FC26 18 C6                jr .l
2946+ FC28                  ENDIF

2949+ FC28              ; City page data
2950+ FC28              ;==============================================================================
2951+ FC28              ; Faces as vertex offsets (index*16); vertex index = x + 2*z + 4*top.
2952+ FC28              ; Side faces list bottom edge first, then the z1/x1 vertical, top, z0/x0.
2953+ FC28 00 20 60 40  face_nx:  db 0*16,2*16,6*16,4*16
2954+ FC2C 10 30 70 50  face_px:  db 1*16,3*16,7*16,5*16
2955+ FC30 00 10 50 40  face_nz:  db 0*16,1*16,5*16,4*16
2956+ FC34 20 30 70 60  face_pz:  db 2*16,3*16,7*16,6*16
2957+ FC38 40 50 70 60  face_top: db 4*16,5*16,7*16,6*16

  68+ E015              ; ULA (Layer 1) setup.  Rows 0-127: paper 0 is made transparent, so only the
  69+ E015              ; FPS text shows over the Layer 2 viewport.  Rows 128-191: opaque instrument
  70+ E015              ; panel with a green divider, gauge labels and the gauge tracks.

  82+ E031 ED 91 41 1C          nextreg $41,$1C                 ;   green

 166+ E0F7              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 167+ E0F7              ; Weapon slots (placeholders): an 8x16 box for each, 2 character cells on
 168+ E0F7              ; rows WPN_ROW, WPN_ROW+1, at columns WPN_COL0, +2, ... in the order
 169+ E0F7              ; wpn_order (as numbered on keys 1-5: 2 4 1 5 3).  A box is green if its
 170+ E0F7              ; slot holds a weapon, red if it is empty; the selected slot has a white
 171+ E0F7              ; outline.  An empty slot that was asked for flashes amber / red (WFLASH
 172+ E0F7              ; frames each, 3 times, ending red).  Under an occupied slot, on row
 173+ E0F7              ; WPN_ROW+2, the letter of its weapon (wpn_glyph): M, L, C or R.
 174+ E0F7              ; Redrawn every frame.
 175+ E0F7              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 176+ E0F7              WPN_ATTR_ON     equ C_OK<<3 | 7         ; paper green, ink white (outline)
 177+ E0F7              WPN_ATTR_OFF    equ C_WARN<<3 | 7       ; paper red
 178+ E0F7              WPN_ATTR_FLASH  equ C_AMBER<<3 | 7      ; paper amber
 179+ E0F7              WPN_BMP         equ ULA_BMP + (WPN_ROW/8)*$800 + (WPN_ROW&7)*32 + WPN_COL0
 180+ E0F7                      ASSERT WPN_ROW/8 == (WPN_ROW+2)/8       ; the 3 rows in one third
 181+ E0F7                      ASSERT WPN_COL0 + 2*(WPN_SLOTS-1) < MAP_COL
 182+ E0F7
 183+ E0F7              draw_wpn:
 184+ E0F7 0E FF                ld c,$FF                        ; the slot flashing amber this frame
 185+ E0F9 3A 3C AE             ld a,(wflash_t)
 186+ E0FC B7                   or a
 187+ E0FD 28 10                jr z,.nf
 188+ E0FF 3D                   dec a                           ; phase (t-1)/WFLASH: odd = amber
 189+ E100 06 FF                ld b,-1
 190+ E102 04           .dv:    inc b
 191+ E103 D6 05                sub WFLASH
 192+ E105 30 FB                jr nc,.dv
 193+ E107 CB 40                bit 0,b
 194+ E109 28 04                jr z,.nf
 195+ E10B 3A 3B AE             ld a,(wflash_slot)
 196+ E10E 4F                   ld c,a
 197+ E10F 79           .nf:    ld a,c
 198+ E110 32 FD E2             ld (wd_flash),a
 199+ E113 21 3C AE             ld hl,wflash_t                  ; count the flash down
 200+ E116 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 201+ E117 B7                   or a
 202+ E118 28 01                jr z,.go
 203+ E11A 35                   dec (hl)
 204+ E11B 06 00        .go:    ld b,0                          ; box position, left to right
 205+ E11D C5           .lp:    push bc
 206+ E11E 78                   ld a,b
 207+ E11F 87                   add a,a
 208+ E120 32 FE E2             ld (wd_col),a                   ; its column offset
 209+ E123 21 00 E3             ld hl,wpn_order
 210+ E126 78                   ld a,b
 211+ E127 ED 31                add hl,a
 212+ E129 4E                   ld c,(hl)                       ; C = its slot (0 = the centre gun)
 213+ E12A 79                   ld a,c
 214+ E12B 87                   add a,a
 215+ E12C 21 30 AE             ld hl,wpn_slots+WPN_TYPE
 216+ E12F ED 31                add hl,a
 217+ E131 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 218+ E132 32 FF E2             ld (wd_type),a
 219+ E135 1E 27                ld e,WPN_ATTR_ON                ; fitted: green
 220+ E137 B7                   or a
 221+ E138 20 0A                jr nz,.pc
 222+ E13A 1E 1F                ld e,WPN_ATTR_OFF               ; empty: red, or amber while flashing
 223+ E13C 3A FD E2             ld a,(wd_flash)
 224+ E13F B9                   cp c
 225+ E140 20 02                jr nz,.pc
 226+ E142 1E 37                ld e,WPN_ATTR_FLASH
 227+ E144 3A FE E2     .pc:    ld a,(wd_col)                   ; the box's two cells
 228+ E147 21 2B 9A             ld hl,ULA_ATTR + WPN_ROW*32 + WPN_COL0
 229+ E14A ED 31                add hl,a
 230+ E14C 73                   ld (hl),e
 231+ E14D 3E 20                ld a,32
 232+ E14F ED 31                add hl,a
 233+ E151 73                   ld (hl),e
 234+ E152 3A 3A AE             ld a,(wpn_sel)                  ; outline if selected, else clear
 235+ E155 B9                   cp c
 236+ E156 11 81 FF             ld de,$FF81                     ; D: top / bottom line, E: sides
 237+ E159 28 03                jr z,.bx
 238+ E15B 11 00 00             ld de,0
 239+ E15E 3A FE E2     .bx:    ld a,(wd_col)
 240+ E161 21 2B 90             ld hl,WPN_BMP
 241+ E164 ED 31                add hl,a
 242+ E166 E5                   push hl
 243+ E167 72                   ld (hl),d                       ; upper cell: top line, then sides
 244+ E168 06 07                ld b,7
 245+ E16A 24           .b1:    inc h
 246+ E16B 73                   ld (hl),e
 247+ E16C 10 FC                djnz .b1
 248+ E16E E1                   pop hl
 249+ E16F 3E 20                ld a,32
 250+ E171 ED 31                add hl,a
 251+ E173 E5                   push hl
 252+ E174 06 07                ld b,7                          ; lower cell: sides, then bottom line
 253+ E176 73           .b2:    ld (hl),e
 254+ E177 24                   inc h
 255+ E178 10 FC                djnz .b2
 256+ E17A 72                   ld (hl),d
 257+ E17B E1                   pop hl
 258+ E17C 3E 20                ld a,32                         ; the cell below: its weapon's letter
 259+ E17E ED 31                add hl,a
 260+ E180 EB                   ex de,hl
 261+ E181 3A FF E2             ld a,(wd_type)
 262+ E184 21 05 E3             ld hl,wpn_glyph
 263+ E187 ED 31                add hl,a
 264+ E189 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 265+ E18A CD 02 E0             call print_glyph_at
 266+ E18D C1                   pop bc
 267+ E18E 04                   inc b
 268+ E18F 78                   ld a,b
 269+ E190 FE 05                cp WPN_SLOTS
 270+ E192 20 89                jr nz,.lp
 271+ E194 C9                   ret
 272+ E195
 273+ E195              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 274+ E195              ; Integrity: the VTOL's hit points as a percentage of VTOL_HP, "nnn%" on row
 275+ E195              ; INT_ROW+1 under the INTEGRITY label.  VTOL_HP is 50, so the percentage is
 276+ E195              ; just vtol_hp * 2 (a shift left).  Green, amber below INT_WARN, red below
 277+ E195              ; INT_DANGER.  Redrawn only when it changes (int_shown).
 278+ E195              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 279+ E195              draw_integrity:
 280+ E195 3A 40 AE             ld a,(vtol_hp)
 281+ E198 87                   add a,a                         ; percent = hp * 2
 282+ E199 21 EF E2             ld hl,int_shown
 283+ E19C BE                   cp (hl)
 284+ E19D C8                   ret z                           ; unchanged
 285+ E19E 77                   ld (hl),a
 286+ E19F 0E 0C                ld c,P_PANEL<<3 | C_OK          ; its colour
 287+ E1A1 FE 32                cp INT_WARN
 288+ E1A3 30 08                jr nc,.col
 289+ E1A5 0E 0E                ld c,P_PANEL<<3 | C_AMBER
 290+ E1A7 FE 19                cp INT_DANGER
 291+ E1A9 30 02                jr nc,.col
 292+ E1AB 0E 0B                ld c,P_PANEL<<3 | C_WARN
 293+ E1AD 21 CC 9A     .col:   ld hl,ULA_ATTR + (INT_ROW+1)*32 + INT_PCT_COL
 294+ E1B0 06 04                ld b,4
 295+ E1B2 71           .at:    ld (hl),c
 296+ E1B3 23                   inc hl
 297+ E1B4 10 FC                djnz .at
 298+ E1B6 21 F0 E2             ld hl,int_txt                   ; the digits, no leading zeros
 299+ E1B9 06 64                ld b,100
 300+ E1BB CD E4 E1             call .dig
 301+ E1BE 06 0A                ld b,10
 302+ E1C0 CD E4 E1             call .dig
 303+ E1C3 77                   ld (hl),a                       ; units
 304+ E1C4 3A F0 E2             ld a,(int_txt)
 305+ E1C7 B7                   or a
 306+ E1C8 20 10                jr nz,.pr
 307+ E1CA 3E 0D                ld a,GLYPH_SPACE
 308+ E1CC 32 F0 E2             ld (int_txt),a
 309+ E1CF 3A F1 E2             ld a,(int_txt+1)
 310+ E1D2 B7                   or a
 311+ E1D3 20 05                jr nz,.pr
 312+ E1D5 3E 0D                ld a,GLYPH_SPACE
 313+ E1D7 32 F1 E2             ld (int_txt+1),a
 314+ E1DA 21 F0 E2     .pr:    ld hl,int_txt
 315+ E1DD 11 CC 90             ld de,ULA_ROW_INT+32+INT_PCT_COL
 316+ E1E0 06 04                ld b,4
 317+ E1E2 18 0B                jr print_glyphs
 318+ E1E4 36 00        .dig:   ld (hl),0                       ; A = A mod B, (HL) = A / B
 319+ E1E6 B8           .ds:    cp b
 320+ E1E7 38 04                jr c,.dd
 321+ E1E9 90                   sub b
 322+ E1EA 34                   inc (hl)
 323+ E1EB 18 F9                jr .ds
 324+ E1ED 23           .dd:    inc hl
 325+ E1EE C9                   ret
 326+ E1EF                      ASSERT VTOL_HP == 50            ; (percent = hp * 2)
 327+ E1EF                      ASSERT INT_PCT_COL+3 < MAP_COL && INT_COL+8 < MAP_COL
 328+ E1EF
 329+ E1EF              ; HL -> B glyph codes, DE = first ULA cell: print them along the row
 330+ E1EF              print_glyphs:
 331+ E1EF C5           .l:     push bc
 332+ E1F0 D5                   push de
 333+ E1F1 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 334+ E1F2 23                   inc hl
 335+ E1F3 E5                   push hl
 336+ E1F4 CD 02 E0             call print_glyph_at
 337+ E1F7 E1                   pop hl
 338+ E1F8 D1                   pop de
 339+ E1F9 1C                   inc e
 340+ E1FA C1                   pop bc
 341+ E1FB 10 F2                djnz .l
 342+ E1FD C9                   ret
 343+ E1FE
 344+ E1FE              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 345+ E1FE              ; Layer 2 double buffering with the hardware shadow register.
 346+ E1FE              ;   NextReg $12 = active bank (on screen), NextReg $13 = shadow bank.
 347+ E1FE              ; The game always draws into the shadow bank; l2_flip swaps the two during
 348+ E1FE              ; vertical blank, so the registers themselves record which buffer is which.
 349+ E1FE              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 350+ E1FE
 351+ E1FE              ;==============================================================================
 352+ E1FE              ; HUD: the weapon slots, and the speed, altitude and ground height gauges
 353+ E1FE              ; (the humans and the compass are hardware sprites: hud_sprites.asm).  The
 354+ E1FE              ; gauges are vertical bars on Layer 2 (in the back buffer, every frame)
 355+ E1FE              ; behind transparent ULA cells: the
 356+ E1FE              ; altitude and ground bars share one 8-pixel cell column, which ULA
 357+ E1FE              ; attributes couldn't colour (green, amber and black in one cell).
 358+ E1FE              ;==============================================================================
 359+ E1FE              draw_hud:
 360+ E1FE CD F7 E0             call draw_wpn
 361+ E201 CD 95 E1             call draw_integrity
 362+ E204                      ; (the humans' states: hardware sprites, hum_hud in human.asm)
 363+ E204
 364+ E204 CD 0A E2             call draw_gauges
 365+ E207 C3 AE 3A             jp map_player
 366+ E20A
 367+ E20A              ; The gauges, GAUGE_H (48) pixels tall:
 368+ E20A              ;   S  speed, 8 wide: (speed * 55) / 32 pixels, so SPEED_MAX 28 fills it;
 369+ E20A              ;      green, red when reversing, amber while boosting (full: BOOST_SPEED is
 370+ E20A              ;      past the top)
 371+ E20A              ;   A  left 4 pixels: altitude, green; right 4: the height of the city tile
 372+ E20A              ;      under the craft (0 on a road), amber.  Both 1 pixel per 4 units, so
 373+ E20A              ;      the ceiling (MAX_Y 1.5 height units, 192) fills the bar; the ground
 374+ E20A              ;      under the craft is always lower, so the gap between them is the
 375+ E20A              ;      clearance.
 376+ E20A              ;   B  the booster's fuel, 8 wide: fuel * 62 / 256 pixels, so BOOST_FUEL_MAX
 377+ E20A              ;      (200) fills it; green.  Drawn always, but its cells are opaque panel
 378+ E20A              ;      until the booster is fitted (ula_init).
 379+ E20A              draw_gauges:
 380+ E20A 3A BA AE             ld a,(boost_fuel)
 381+ E20D 57                   ld d,a
 382+ E20E 1E 3E                ld e,62
 383+ E210 ED 30                mul d,e
 384+ E212 7A                   ld a,d
 385+ E213 CD E9 E2             call g_clamp

 387+ E219                      ASSERT BOOST_FUEL_MAX*62/256 >= GAUGE_H

 391+ E220 28 04                jr z,.sp
 392+ E222 ED 44                neg

 395+ E227 3A BB AE             ld a,(boost_on)
 396+ E22A B7                   or a

 398+ E22C 28 02                jr z,.nbo
 399+ E22E 0E 5F                ld c,GAUGE_AMBER                ; boosting

 401+ E231 1E 37                ld e,55
 402+ E233 ED 30                mul d,e

 404+ E237 ED 2A                bsrl de,b
 405+ E239 79                   ld a,c
 406+ E23A 32 0F E3             ld (g_spdc),a

 416+ E254 CD DB E2             call g_px
 417+ E257 32 11 E3             ld (g_gnd),a
 418+ E25A 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; row from the top
 419+ E25C 79           .row:   ld a,c
 420+ E25D C6 88                add a,GAUGE_TOP

 423+ E261 07                   rlca
 424+ E262 07                   rlca

 436+ E276 47                   ld b,a                          ; this row's level: GAUGE_H..1
