1677  E000                      INCLUDE "input.asm"
# file opened: input.asm
   1+ E000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ E000              ; Z-POLICE - input: keyboard and joysticks (8K page PAGE_INPUT, runs at
   3+ E000              ; $E000 in slot 7: main_loop and the menu page it in, and the game logic
   4+ E000              ; pages in slot 6 call it)
   5+ E000              ;
   6+ E000              ; Redefinable controls.  An input (a key, or a joystick direction or button)
   7+ E000              ; is a 16-bit code: high byte = input bank, low byte = bit mask within it.
   8+ E000              ;   banks 0-7: the ZX Spectrum keyboard half-rows (port $xxFE, address line
   9+ E000              ;              A8+bank low): read 0 = pressed
  10+ E000              ;   bank 8:    NextReg $B0, Extended Keys 0: read 1 = pressed
  11+ E000              ;   bank 9:    NextReg $B1, Extended Keys 1: read 1 = pressed
  12+ E000              ;   bank 10:   port $1F, Kempston 1 / Mega Drive 1 (J1_*): read 1 = pressed
  13+ E000              ;   bank 11:   port $37, Kempston 2 / Mega Drive 2 (J2_*): read 1 = pressed
  14+ E000              ;   bank 12:   NextReg $B2, the 6-button Mega Drive pads' X, Y, Z and MODE
  15+ E000              ;              (left connector J1_*, right J2_*): read 1 = pressed
  16+ E000              ; A joystick bank is read only while a joystick is set (NextReg $05, the
  17+ E000              ; Next's joystick modes) to the port it reads, else it is 0: so an unused
  18+ E000              ; port can't read as everything pressed.  Joysticks in Sinclair or Cursor
  19+ E000              ; mode enter keys (6-0, 1-5, 5-8) and are read as keys.
  20+ E000              ; kbd_scan, once per frame at the top of the main loop, reads all the banks
  21+ E000              ; into key_now, inverting the ZX rows so that every bank holds 1 = pressed,
  22+ E000              ; and keeps the previous scan in key_prev.  The game asks about actions
  23+ E000              ; (K_FIRE, K_LEFT, ...), not inputs: each action has a key (key_defs) and a
  24+ E000              ; joystick input (joy_defs; 0 = none), either of which works, so both can
  25+ E000              ; be redefined (key_define) at any time.
  26+ E000              ;   key_held    A = action -> NZ if its key or joystick input is down
  27+ E000              ;   key_pressed A = action -> NZ if its key or joystick input went down
  28+ E000              ;               since the last scan
  29+ E000              ;   key_find    -> NZ and HL = code of an input that is down (for a
  30+ E000              ;               redefine screen); Z if none
  31+ E000              ;   key_code_pressed  HL = key code -> NZ if that key went down
  32+ E000              ; The Next normally also enters the extended keys into the 8x5 matrix (e.g.
  33+ E000              ; cursor LEFT as CAPS SHIFT + 5).  NextReg $68 bit 4 stops that: set it with
  34+ E000              ; kbd_double_on (KBD_STOP_DOUBLING = TRUE), clear it with kbd_double_off
  35+ E000              ; (FALSE, the default).
  36+ E000              ;==============================================================================
  37+ E000
  38+ E000              ; --- key codes: bank << 8 | mask ---------------------------------------------
  39+ E000              KB_SHIFT    equ $0001                   ; bank 0: $FEFE
  40+ E000              KB_Z        equ $0002
  41+ E000              KB_X        equ $0004
  42+ E000              KB_C        equ $0008
  43+ E000              KB_V        equ $0010
  44+ E000              KB_A        equ $0101                   ; bank 1: $FDFE
  45+ E000              KB_S        equ $0102
  46+ E000              KB_D        equ $0104
  47+ E000              KB_F        equ $0108
  48+ E000              KB_G        equ $0110
  49+ E000              KB_Q        equ $0201                   ; bank 2: $FBFE
  50+ E000              KB_W        equ $0202
  51+ E000              KB_E        equ $0204
  52+ E000              KB_R        equ $0208
  53+ E000              KB_T        equ $0210
  54+ E000              KB_1        equ $0301                   ; bank 3: $F7FE
  55+ E000              KB_2        equ $0302
  56+ E000              KB_3        equ $0304
  57+ E000              KB_4        equ $0308
  58+ E000              KB_5        equ $0310
  59+ E000              KB_0        equ $0401                   ; bank 4: $EFFE
  60+ E000              KB_9        equ $0402
  61+ E000              KB_8        equ $0404
  62+ E000              KB_7        equ $0408
  63+ E000              KB_6        equ $0410
  64+ E000              KB_P        equ $0501                   ; bank 5: $DFFE
  65+ E000              KB_O        equ $0502
  66+ E000              KB_I        equ $0504
  67+ E000              KB_U        equ $0508
  68+ E000              KB_Y        equ $0510
  69+ E000              KB_ENTER    equ $0601                   ; bank 6: $BFFE
  70+ E000              KB_L        equ $0602
  71+ E000              KB_K        equ $0604
  72+ E000              KB_J        equ $0608
  73+ E000              KB_H        equ $0610
  74+ E000              KB_SPACE    equ $0701                   ; bank 7: $7FFE
  75+ E000              KB_SYM      equ $0702
  76+ E000              KB_M        equ $0704
  77+ E000              KB_N        equ $0708
  78+ E000              KB_B        equ $0710
  79+ E000              KB_RIGHT    equ $0801                   ; bank 8: NextReg $B0
  80+ E000              KB_LEFT     equ $0802
  81+ E000              KB_DOWN     equ $0804
  82+ E000              KB_UP       equ $0808
  83+ E000              KB_PERIOD   equ $0810
  84+ E000              KB_COMMA    equ $0820
  85+ E000              KB_QUOTE    equ $0840
  86+ E000              KB_SEMI     equ $0880
  87+ E000              KB_EXTEND   equ $0901                   ; bank 9: NextReg $B1
  88+ E000              KB_CAPSLOCK equ $0902
  89+ E000              KB_GRAPH    equ $0904
  90+ E000              KB_TRUEVID  equ $0908
  91+ E000              KB_INVVID   equ $0910
  92+ E000              KB_BREAK    equ $0920
  93+ E000              KB_EDIT     equ $0940
  94+ E000              KB_DELETE   equ $0980
  95+ E000              JOY_BANK    equ 10                      ; the first joystick bank
  96+ E000              J1_RIGHT    equ $0A01                   ; bank 10: port $1F (Kempston 1 /
  97+ E000              J1_LEFT     equ $0A02                   ;   Mega Drive 1)
  98+ E000              J1_DOWN     equ $0A04
  99+ E000              J1_UP       equ $0A08
 100+ E000              J1_FIRE     equ $0A10                   ; (Mega Drive: B)
 101+ E000              J1_C        equ $0A20                   ; Mega Drive C, A, START
 102+ E000              J1_A        equ $0A40
 103+ E000              J1_START    equ $0A80
 104+ E000              J2_RIGHT    equ $0B01                   ; bank 11: port $37 (Kempston 2 /
 105+ E000              J2_LEFT     equ $0B02                   ;   Mega Drive 2)
 106+ E000              J2_DOWN     equ $0B04
 107+ E000              J2_UP       equ $0B08
 108+ E000              J2_FIRE     equ $0B10
 109+ E000              J2_C        equ $0B20
 110+ E000              J2_A        equ $0B40
 111+ E000              J2_START    equ $0B80
 112+ E000              J1_MODE     equ $0C01                   ; bank 12: NextReg $B2, 6-button
 113+ E000              J1_Y        equ $0C02                   ;   pads (left connector)
 114+ E000              J1_Z        equ $0C04
 115+ E000              J1_X        equ $0C08
 116+ E000              J2_MODE     equ $0C10                   ;   (right connector)
 117+ E000              J2_Y        equ $0C20
 118+ E000              J2_Z        equ $0C40
 119+ E000              J2_X        equ $0C80
 120+ E000              KEY_BANKS   equ 13
 121+ E000              JP_1F_B     equ 0                       ; joystick ports in use (joy_ports):
 122+ E000              JP_37_B     equ 1                       ;   $1F, $37, the 6-button extras
 123+ E000              JP_MD_B     equ 2
 124+ E000              FALSE       equ 0
 125+ E000              TRUE        equ 1
 126+ E000
 127+ E000              ; --- actions: index into key_defs --------------------------------------------
 128+ E000              K_SPEED_UP  equ 0
 129+ E000              K_SPEED_DN  equ 1
 130+ E000              K_AIRBRAKE  equ 2                       ; air brake: rapid stop
 131+ E000              K_LEFT      equ 3
 132+ E000              K_RIGHT     equ 4
 133+ E000              K_CLIMB     equ 5
 134+ E000              K_DESCEND   equ 6
 135+ E000              K_NOSE_UP   equ 7
 136+ E000              K_NOSE_DN   equ 8
 137+ E000              K_FIRE      equ 9
 138+ E000              K_GUN_UP    equ 10                      ; tilt the centre gun up,
 139+ E000              K_SLOT1     equ 11                      ; select weapon slots 1-5
 140+ E000              K_GUN_DN    equ 16                      ; down,
 141+ E000              K_GUN_CTR   equ 17                      ; and back to the centre
 142+ E000              K_PAUSE     equ 18                      ; pause / resume (main_loop)
 143+ E000              K_BOOST     equ 19                      ; the booster on / off (player.asm)
 144+ E000              K_ACTIONS   equ 20
 145+ E000
 146+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 147+ E000              ; Read all the input banks: key_prev = last scan, key_now = this one, every
 148+ E000              ; bit 1 = pressed
 149+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 150+ E000              kbd_scan:
 151+ E000 21 56 E1             ld hl,key_now
 152+ E003 11 63 E1             ld de,key_prev
 153+ E006 01 0D 00             ld bc,KEY_BANKS
 154+ E009 ED B0                ldir
 155+ E00B 21 56 E1             ld hl,key_now
 156+ E00E 01 FE FE             ld bc,$FEFE                     ; banks 0-7: half-rows $FE..$7F
 157+ E011 ED 78        .row:   in a,(c)
 158+ E013 2F                   cpl                             ; 0 = pressed -> 1 = pressed
 159+ E014 E6 1F                and %00011111
 160+ E016 77                   ld (hl),a
 161+ E017 23                   inc hl
 162+ E018 CB 00                rlc b                           ; next half-row
 163+ E01A 38 F5                jr c,.row                       ; (stops after $7F)
 164+ E01C 01 3B 24             ld bc,$243B                     ; banks 8, 9: NextRegs $B0, $B1
 165+ E01F 3E B0                ld a,$B0
 166+ E021 ED 79                out (c),a
 167+ E023 04                   inc b
 168+ E024 ED 78                in a,(c)                        ; already 1 = pressed
 169+ E026 77                   ld (hl),a
 170+ E027 23                   inc hl
 171+ E028 05                   dec b
 172+ E029 3E B1                ld a,$B1
 173+ E02B ED 79                out (c),a
 174+ E02D 04                   inc b
 175+ E02E ED 78                in a,(c)
 176+ E030 77                   ld (hl),a
 177+ E031 23                   inc hl
 178+ E032                      ; banks 10-12, the joysticks: only the ports a joystick is set to
 179+ E032                      ; (NextReg $05), so an unused port can't read as all pressed
 180+ E032 05                   dec b                           ; (BC = $243B)
 181+ E033 3E 05                ld a,$05
 182+ E035 ED 79                out (c),a
 183+ E037 04                   inc b
 184+ E038 ED 58                in e,(c)                        ; the joystick modes
 185+ E03A 7B                   ld a,e                          ; joystick 1: bits 7-6, and bit 3
 186+ E03B 07                   rlca
 187+ E03C 07                   rlca
 188+ E03D E6 03                and 3
 189+ E03F CB 5B                bit 3,e
 190+ E041 28 02                jr z,.j1
 191+ E043 F6 04                or 4
 192+ E045 CD 84 E0     .j1:    call joy_ports
 193+ E048 57                   ld d,a                          ; D: the ports in use (JP_*)
 194+ E049 7B                   ld a,e                          ; joystick 2: bits 5-4, and bit 1
 195+ E04A 0F                   rrca
 196+ E04B 0F                   rrca
 197+ E04C 0F                   rrca
 198+ E04D 0F                   rrca
 199+ E04E E6 03                and 3
 200+ E050 CB 4B                bit 1,e
 201+ E052 28 02                jr z,.j2
 202+ E054 F6 04                or 4
 203+ E056 CD 84 E0     .j2:    call joy_ports
 204+ E059 B2                   or d
 205+ E05A 57                   ld d,a
 206+ E05B AF                   xor a                           ; bank 10: port $1F
 207+ E05C CB 42                bit JP_1F_B,d
 208+ E05E 28 05                jr z,.p1
 209+ E060 DB 1F                in a,($1F)
 210+ E062 CD 95 E0             call joy_sane
 211+ E065 77           .p1:    ld (hl),a
 212+ E066 23                   inc hl
 213+ E067 AF                   xor a                           ; bank 11: port $37
 214+ E068 CB 4A                bit JP_37_B,d
 215+ E06A 28 05                jr z,.p2
 216+ E06C DB 37                in a,($37)
 217+ E06E CD 95 E0             call joy_sane
 218+ E071 77           .p2:    ld (hl),a
 219+ E072 23                   inc hl
 220+ E073 AF                   xor a                           ; bank 12: NextReg $B2, the 6-button
 221+ E074 CB 52                bit JP_MD_B,d                   ; pads' extra buttons
 222+ E076 28 0A                jr z,.p3
 223+ E078 01 3B 24             ld bc,$243B
 224+ E07B 3E B2                ld a,$B2
 225+ E07D ED 79                out (c),a
 226+ E07F 04                   inc b
 227+ E080 ED 78                in a,(c)
 228+ E082 77           .p3:    ld (hl),a
 229+ E083 C9                   ret
 230+ E084                      ASSERT KEY_BANKS == 13 && JOY_BANK == 10
 231+ E084
 232+ E084              ; A = a joystick mode (NextReg $05: 0 Sinclair 2, 1 Kempston 1, 2 Cursor,
 233+ E084              ; 3 Sinclair 1, 4 Kempston 2, 5 Mega Drive 1, 6 Mega Drive 2, 7 I/O) -> A =
 234+ E084              ; the ports it is read through (JP_* bits).  Keeps DE, HL.
 235+ E084              joy_ports:
 236+ E084 E5                   push hl
 237+ E085 21 8D E0             ld hl,joy_mode_ports
 238+ E088 ED 31                add hl,a
 239+ E08A 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 240+ E08B E1                   pop hl
 241+ E08C C9                   ret
 242+ E08D
 243+ E08D              joy_mode_ports:
 244+ E08D 00 01 00 00          db 0, 1<<JP_1F_B, 0, 0, 1<<JP_37_B
 244+ E091 02
 245+ E092 05 06 00             db 1<<JP_1F_B | 1<<JP_MD_B, 1<<JP_37_B | 1<<JP_MD_B, 0
 246+ E095
 247+ E095              ; A = a Kempston / Mega Drive port read -> A, or 0 if it reads left and
 248+ E095              ; right at once (no stick can: nothing there).  Uses C.
 249+ E095              joy_sane:
 250+ E095 4F                   ld c,a
 251+ E096 E6 03                and %00000011
 252+ E098 FE 03                cp %00000011
 253+ E09A 79                   ld a,c
 254+ E09B C0                   ret nz
 255+ E09C AF                   xor a
 256+ E09D C9                   ret
 257+ E09E
 258+ E09E              ; Stop the extended keys also appearing in the 8x5 matrix:
 259+ E09E              ; KBD_STOP_DOUBLING = TRUE, NextReg $68 bit 4 = 1
 260+ E09E              kbd_double_on:
 261+ E09E 3E 01                ld a,TRUE
 262+ E0A0 18 01                jr kbd_double
 263+ E0A2
 264+ E0A2              ; Let the extended keys also appear in the 8x5 matrix (the default):
 265+ E0A2              ; KBD_STOP_DOUBLING = FALSE, NextReg $68 bit 4 = 0
 266+ E0A2              kbd_double_off:
 267+ E0A2              kdb_double_off:                         ; (alias: the name as first asked for)
 268+ E0A2 AF                   xor a                           ; FALSE
 269+ E0A3
 270+ E0A3              ; A = TRUE / FALSE: store it, and set NextReg $68 bit 4 to match, keeping
 271+ E0A3              ; the register's other bits
 272+ E0A3              kbd_double:
 273+ E0A3 32 55 E1             ld (KBD_STOP_DOUBLING),a
 274+ E0A6 01 3B 24             ld bc,$243B
 275+ E0A9 3E 68                ld a,$68
 276+ E0AB ED 79                out (c),a
 277+ E0AD 04                   inc b
 278+ E0AE ED 78                in a,(c)                        ; current NextReg $68
 279+ E0B0 CB A7                res 4,a
 280+ E0B2 21 55 E1             ld hl,KBD_STOP_DOUBLING
 281+ E0B5 CB 46                bit 0,(hl)
 282+ E0B7 28 02                jr z,.w
 283+ E0B9 CB E7                set 4,a
 284+ E0BB ED 79        .w:     out (c),a                       ; ($253B: still selects $68)
 285+ E0BD C9                   ret
 286+ E0BE
 287+ E0BE              ; A = action -> NZ if its key or its joystick input is down.  Preserves BC,
 288+ E0BE              ; DE, HL.
 289+ E0BE              key_held:
 290+ E0BE E5                   push hl
 291+ E0BF C5                   push bc
 292+ E0C0 4F                   ld c,a
 293+ E0C1 CD 09 E1             call key_def
 294+ E0C4 A6                   and (hl)
 295+ E0C5 20 05                jr nz,.x
 296+ E0C7 79                   ld a,c
 297+ E0C8 CD 1A E1             call joy_def
 298+ E0CB A6                   and (hl)
 299+ E0CC C1           .x:     pop bc
 300+ E0CD E1                   pop hl
 301+ E0CE C9                   ret
 302+ E0CF
 303+ E0CF              ; A = action -> NZ if its key or its joystick input is down now but wasn't
 304+ E0CF              ; at the last scan.  Preserves BC, DE, HL.
 305+ E0CF              key_pressed:
 306+ E0CF E5                   push hl
 307+ E0D0 C5                   push bc
 308+ E0D1 47                   ld b,a
 309+ E0D2 CD 09 E1             call key_def
 310+ E0D5 CD E4 E0             call def_new
 311+ E0D8 20 07                jr nz,.x
 312+ E0DA 78                   ld a,b
 313+ E0DB CD 1A E1             call joy_def
 314+ E0DE CD E4 E0             call def_new
 315+ E0E1 C1           .x:     pop bc
 316+ E0E2 E1                   pop hl
 317+ E0E3 C9                   ret
 318+ E0E4
 319+ E0E4              ; HL -> key_now[bank], A = mask -> NZ if down now but not at the last scan
 320+ E0E4              ; (a mask of 0, no input: never).  Uses C.
 321+ E0E4              def_new:
 322+ E0E4 4F                   ld c,a
 323+ E0E5 A6                   and (hl)
 324+ E0E6 C8                   ret z                           ; not down
 325+ E0E7 79                   ld a,c
 326+ E0E8 D5                   push de
 327+ E0E9 11 0D 00             ld de,KEY_BANKS
 328+ E0EC 19                   add hl,de                       ; key_prev, same bank
 329+ E0ED A6                   and (hl)
 330+ E0EE D1                   pop de
 331+ E0EF A9                   xor c                           ; down before: Z; new: NZ
 332+ E0F0 C9                   ret
 333+ E0F1
 334+ E0F1              ; HL = a key code (KB_...) -> NZ if that key went down since the last scan
 335+ E0F1              ; (for fixed keys, such as the menu's).  Preserves BC, DE, HL.
 336+ E0F1              key_code_pressed:
 337+ E0F1 E5                   push hl
 338+ E0F2 C5                   push bc
 339+ E0F3 4D                   ld c,l                          ; mask
 340+ E0F4 7C                   ld a,h                          ; bank
 341+ E0F5 21 56 E1             ld hl,key_now
 342+ E0F8 ED 31                add hl,a
 343+ E0FA 79                   ld a,c
 344+ E0FB A6                   and (hl)
 345+ E0FC 28 08                jr z,.x                         ; not down
 346+ E0FE D5                   push de
 347+ E0FF 11 0D 00             ld de,KEY_BANKS
 348+ E102 19                   add hl,de                       ; key_prev, same bank
 349+ E103 A6                   and (hl)
 350+ E104 D1                   pop de
 351+ E105 A9                   xor c                           ; down before: Z; new: NZ
 352+ E106 C1           .x:     pop bc
 353+ E107 E1                   pop hl
 354+ E108 C9                   ret
 355+ E109
 356+ E109              ; A = action -> HL -> key_now[its key's bank], A = its key's mask
 357+ E109              key_def:
 358+ E109 21 70 E1             ld hl,key_defs
 359+ E10C                      ; fall through
 360+ E10C
 361+ E10C              ; HL = key_defs or joy_defs, A = action -> HL -> key_now[the bank], A = mask
 362+ E10C              def_at:
 363+ E10C 87                   add a,a
 364+ E10D ED 31                add hl,a
 365+ E10F 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; mask
 366+ E110 23                   inc hl
 367+ E111 F5                   push af
 368+ E112 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; bank
 369+ E113 21 56 E1             ld hl,key_now
 370+ E116 ED 31                add hl,a
 371+ E118 F1                   pop af
 372+ E119 C9                   ret
 373+ E11A
 374+ E11A              ; A = action -> HL -> key_now[its joystick input's bank], A = its mask (0:
 375+ E11A              ; none)
 376+ E11A              joy_def:
 377+ E11A 21 98 E1             ld hl,joy_defs
 378+ E11D 18 ED                jr def_at
 379+ E11F
 380+ E11F              ; A = action, HL = an input's code: redefine the action's key, or its
 381+ E11F              ; joystick input if the code is a joystick's (bank JOY_BANK on).  Uses B.
 382+ E11F              key_define:
 383+ E11F EB                   ex de,hl
 384+ E120 87                   add a,a
 385+ E121 21 70 E1             ld hl,key_defs
 386+ E124 47                   ld b,a
 387+ E125 7A                   ld a,d
 388+ E126 FE 0A                cp JOY_BANK
 389+ E128 78                   ld a,b
 390+ E129 38 03                jr c,.k
 391+ E12B 21 98 E1             ld hl,joy_defs
 392+ E12E ED 31        .k:     add hl,a
 393+ E130 73                   ld (hl),e
 394+ E131 23                   inc hl
 395+ E132 72                   ld (hl),d
 396+ E133 EB                   ex de,hl
 397+ E134 C9                   ret
 398+ E135
 399+ E135              ; -> NZ and HL = code of an input that is down (lowest bank, lowest bit); Z
 400+ E135              ; if none.  For a redefine screen.
 401+ E135              key_find:
 402+ E135 21 56 E1             ld hl,key_now
 403+ E138 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; bank
 404+ E13A 7E           .b:     ld a,(hl)
 405+ E13B B7                   or a
 406+ E13C 20 09                jr nz,.got
 407+ E13E 23                   inc hl
 408+ E13F 0C                   inc c
 409+ E140 79                   ld a,c
 410+ E141 FE 0D                cp KEY_BANKS
 411+ E143 20 F5                jr nz,.b
 412+ E145 AF                   xor a                           ; Z: nothing down
 413+ E146 C9                   ret
 414+ E147 06 01        .got:   ld b,1                          ; lowest bit set
 415+ E149 1F           .m:     rra
 416+ E14A 38 04                jr c,.f
 417+ E14C CB 20                sla b
 418+ E14E 18 F9                jr .m
 419+ E150 61           .f:     ld h,c
 420+ E151 68                   ld l,b
 421+ E152 F6 01                or 1                            ; NZ
 422+ E154 C9                   ret
 423+ E155
 424+ E155              ;==============================================================================
 425+ E155              ; Input data
 426+ E155              ;==============================================================================
 427+ E155              KBD_STOP_DOUBLING:
 428+ E155 00                       db FALSE                    ; TRUE: NextReg $68 bit 4 set, extended
 429+ E156                                                      ; keys kept out of the 8x5 matrix
 430+ E156 00 00 00...  key_now:    ds KEY_BANKS                ; this scan, 1 = pressed
 431+ E163 00 00 00...  key_prev:   ds KEY_BANKS                ; the scan before (must follow key_now)
 432+ E170              ; The key and the joystick input of each action, by K_*: the defaults
 433+ E170              ; (key_defaults, joy_defaults), and the ones in use (key_defs, joy_defs:
 434+ E170              ; redefinable on the redefine keys screen, keydef.asm, which can put the
 435+ E170              ; defaults back).  Each set in that order: key_defs, joy_defs, key_defaults,
 436+ E170              ; joy_defaults, one after another (keydef.asm copies all four as one).
 437+ E170                  MACRO KEY_DEFAULTS
 438+ E170 ~                        dw KB_Q                     ; K_SPEED_UP
 439+ E170 ~                        dw KB_A                     ; K_SPEED_DN
 440+ E170 ~                        dw KB_Z                     ; K_AIRBRAKE
 441+ E170 ~                        dw KB_O                     ; K_LEFT
 442+ E170 ~                        dw KB_P                     ; K_RIGHT
 443+ E170 ~                        dw KB_W                     ; K_CLIMB
 444+ E170 ~                        dw KB_S                     ; K_DESCEND
 445+ E170 ~                        dw KB_E                     ; K_NOSE_UP
 446+ E170 ~                        dw KB_D                     ; K_NOSE_DN
 447+ E170 ~                        dw KB_SPACE                 ; K_FIRE
 448+ E170 ~                        dw KB_R                     ; K_GUN_UP
 449+ E170 ~                        dw KB_1, KB_2, KB_3, KB_4, KB_5     ; K_SLOT1..5
 450+ E170 ~                        dw KB_F                     ; K_GUN_DN
 451+ E170 ~                        dw KB_V                     ; K_GUN_CTR
 452+ E170 ~                        dw KB_BREAK                 ; K_PAUSE
 453+ E170 ~                        dw KB_X                     ; K_BOOST
 454+ E170                  ENDM
 455+ E170              ; The joystick defaults: the stick flies (left / right turn, up / down climb
 456+ E170              ; and descend), FIRE (Mega Drive B) fires; a Mega Drive pad's C / A are
 457+ E170              ; speed up / down, START pauses, and a 6-button pad's X is the air brake,
 458+ E170              ; Y / Z nose up / down, MODE the boost.  0: none.
 459+ E170                  MACRO JOY_DEFAULTS
 460+ E170 ~                        dw J1_C                     ; K_SPEED_UP
 461+ E170 ~                        dw J1_A                     ; K_SPEED_DN
 462+ E170 ~                        dw J1_X                     ; K_AIRBRAKE
 463+ E170 ~                        dw J1_LEFT                  ; K_LEFT
 464+ E170 ~                        dw J1_RIGHT                 ; K_RIGHT
 465+ E170 ~                        dw J1_UP                    ; K_CLIMB
 466+ E170 ~                        dw J1_DOWN                  ; K_DESCEND
 467+ E170 ~                        dw J1_Y                     ; K_NOSE_UP
 468+ E170 ~                        dw J1_Z                     ; K_NOSE_DN
 469+ E170 ~                        dw J1_FIRE                  ; K_FIRE
 470+ E170 ~                        dw 0                        ; K_GUN_UP
 471+ E170 ~                        dw 0, 0, 0, 0, 0            ; K_SLOT1..5
 472+ E170 ~                        dw 0                        ; K_GUN_DN
 473+ E170 ~                        dw 0                        ; K_GUN_CTR
 474+ E170 ~                        dw J1_START                 ; K_PAUSE
 475+ E170 ~                        dw J1_MODE                  ; K_BOOST (a 6-button pad's MODE)
 476+ E170                  ENDM
 477+ E170              key_defs:   KEY_DEFAULTS
 477+ E170 01 02       >            dw KB_Q                     ; K_SPEED_UP
 477+ E172 01 01       >            dw KB_A                     ; K_SPEED_DN
 477+ E174 02 00       >            dw KB_Z                     ; K_AIRBRAKE
 477+ E176 02 05       >            dw KB_O                     ; K_LEFT
 477+ E178 01 05       >            dw KB_P                     ; K_RIGHT
 477+ E17A 02 02       >            dw KB_W                     ; K_CLIMB
 477+ E17C 02 01       >            dw KB_S                     ; K_DESCEND
 477+ E17E 04 02       >            dw KB_E                     ; K_NOSE_UP
 477+ E180 04 01       >            dw KB_D                     ; K_NOSE_DN
 477+ E182 01 07       >            dw KB_SPACE                 ; K_FIRE
 477+ E184 08 02       >            dw KB_R                     ; K_GUN_UP
 477+ E186 01 03 02 03 >            dw KB_1, KB_2, KB_3, KB_4, KB_5     ; K_SLOT1..5
 477+ E18A 04 03 08 03 >
 477+ E18E 10 03       >
 477+ E190 08 01       >            dw KB_F                     ; K_GUN_DN
 477+ E192 10 00       >            dw KB_V                     ; K_GUN_CTR
 477+ E194 20 09       >            dw KB_BREAK                 ; K_PAUSE
 477+ E196 04 00       >            dw KB_X                     ; K_BOOST
 478+ E198                          ASSERT $ - key_defs == K_ACTIONS*2
 479+ E198              joy_defs:   JOY_DEFAULTS
 479+ E198 20 0A       >            dw J1_C                     ; K_SPEED_UP
 479+ E19A 40 0A       >            dw J1_A                     ; K_SPEED_DN
 479+ E19C 08 0C       >            dw J1_X                     ; K_AIRBRAKE
 479+ E19E 02 0A       >            dw J1_LEFT                  ; K_LEFT
 479+ E1A0 01 0A       >            dw J1_RIGHT                 ; K_RIGHT
 479+ E1A2 08 0A       >            dw J1_UP                    ; K_CLIMB
 479+ E1A4 04 0A       >            dw J1_DOWN                  ; K_DESCEND
 479+ E1A6 02 0C       >            dw J1_Y                     ; K_NOSE_UP
 479+ E1A8 04 0C       >            dw J1_Z                     ; K_NOSE_DN
 479+ E1AA 10 0A       >            dw J1_FIRE                  ; K_FIRE
 479+ E1AC 00 00       >            dw 0                        ; K_GUN_UP
 479+ E1AE 00 00 00 00 >            dw 0, 0, 0, 0, 0            ; K_SLOT1..5
 479+ E1B2 00 00 00 00 >
 479+ E1B6 00 00       >
 479+ E1B8 00 00       >            dw 0                        ; K_GUN_DN
 479+ E1BA 00 00       >            dw 0                        ; K_GUN_CTR
 479+ E1BC 80 0A       >            dw J1_START                 ; K_PAUSE
 479+ E1BE 01 0C       >            dw J1_MODE                  ; K_BOOST (a 6-button pad's MODE)
 480+ E1C0                          ASSERT $ - joy_defs == K_ACTIONS*2
 481+ E1C0              key_defaults: KEY_DEFAULTS
 481+ E1C0 01 02       >            dw KB_Q                     ; K_SPEED_UP
 481+ E1C2 01 01       >            dw KB_A                     ; K_SPEED_DN
 481+ E1C4 02 00       >            dw KB_Z                     ; K_AIRBRAKE
 481+ E1C6 02 05       >            dw KB_O                     ; K_LEFT
 481+ E1C8 01 05       >            dw KB_P                     ; K_RIGHT
 481+ E1CA 02 02       >            dw KB_W                     ; K_CLIMB
 481+ E1CC 02 01       >            dw KB_S                     ; K_DESCEND
 481+ E1CE 04 02       >            dw KB_E                     ; K_NOSE_UP
 481+ E1D0 04 01       >            dw KB_D                     ; K_NOSE_DN
 481+ E1D2 01 07       >            dw KB_SPACE                 ; K_FIRE
 481+ E1D4 08 02       >            dw KB_R                     ; K_GUN_UP
 481+ E1D6 01 03 02 03 >            dw KB_1, KB_2, KB_3, KB_4, KB_5     ; K_SLOT1..5
 481+ E1DA 04 03 08 03 >
 481+ E1DE 10 03       >
 481+ E1E0 08 01       >            dw KB_F                     ; K_GUN_DN
 481+ E1E2 10 00       >            dw KB_V                     ; K_GUN_CTR

1680  E210                      MMU CODE_SLOT e, PAGE_SPRITES, CODE_ADDR
1681  E000                      ORG CODE_ADDR
1682  E000                      INCLUDE "sprites.asm"
# file opened: sprites.asm
   1+ E000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ E000              ; Z-POLICE - hardware sprites, Layer 3 (8K page PAGE_SPRITES, runs at $E000
   3+ E000              ; in slot 7: page it in with nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_SPRITES before calling)
   4+ E000              ;
   5+ E000              ; The Next has 128 hardware sprites, each 16x16 pixels from a pattern in the
   6+ E000              ; sprite pattern memory (16K, separate from the Z80's: 64 8-bit patterns of
   7+ E000              ; 256 bytes, one byte per pixel, row by row).  A pattern byte is an index
   8+ E000              ; into the sprite palette; SPR_TRANS ($E3, NextReg $4B) is transparent.  The
   9+ E000              ; sprite palette starts as the identity, so until spr_palette changes it a
  10+ E000              ; pattern byte is simply its RRRGGGBB colour.
  11+ E000              ;
  12+ E000              ; Sprites are drawn above Layer 2 and the ULA (priority S U L) and, with
  13+ E000              ; NextReg $15 bit 1 set, over the border as well, so the sprite screen is
  14+ E000              ; 320 x 256 pixels:
  15+ E000              ;
  16+ E000              ;     x 0..319, y 0..255;  (SPR_SCREEN_X, SPR_SCREEN_Y) = (32, 32) is the top
  17+ E000              ;     left of the 256x192 Layer 2 / ULA screen, (288, 224) its bottom right
  18+ E000              ;
  19+ E000              ; A sprite's position is its top left pixel.  Coordinates are 9 bits and
  20+ E000              ; wrap round at 512, so they may be negative (two's complement): a sprite at
  21+ E000              ; x = -8 shows its right half at the left edge.  With overlapping sprites,
  22+ E000              ; the higher numbered one is drawn on top.
  23+ E000              ;
  24+ E000              ; Use: sprites_init once (start-up), load patterns with spr_patterns or
  25+ E000              ; from a sprite bank (sprite_data.asm: page the bank into slot 7 and call
  26+ E000              ; its spr_load_bank, from outside slot 7), and a palette with spr_palette
  27+ E000              ; if wanted, then set up sprites in the shadow
  28+ E000              ; table with spr_set / spr_pos / spr_pattern / spr_flags / spr_scale /
  29+ E000              ; spr_hide / spr_show / spr_hide_all, and copy the table to the hardware
  30+ E000              ; once a frame with spr_update.  The table holds the first SPR_COUNT sprites;
  31+ E000              ; the rest stay hidden.
  32+ E000              ;
  33+ E000              ; Attributes, SPR_ATTR (5) bytes a sprite, kept in the table as they are
  34+ E000              ; sent (the 5-byte form):
  35+ E000              ;   +0  x bits 0-7
  36+ E000              ;   +1  y bits 0-7
  37+ E000              ;   +2  bits 7-4 palette offset (added to the pattern byte's top nibble),
  38+ E000              ;       bit 3 x mirror, 2 y mirror, 1 rotate 90 degrees clockwise, 0 x bit 8
  39+ E000              ;   +3  bit 7 visible, bit 6 = 1 (byte +4 follows), bits 5-0 pattern
  40+ E000              ;   +4  bits 4-3 x scale, bits 2-1 y scale (0..3 = x1, x2, x4, x8), 0 y bit 8
  41+ E000              ;==============================================================================
  42+ E000
  43+ E000              SPR_COUNT       equ 32          ; sprites in the shadow table (max 128)
  44+ E000              SPR_HW          equ 128         ; hardware sprites
  45+ E000              SPR_ATTR        equ 5           ; attribute bytes per sprite
  46+ E000              SPR_PATTERNS    equ 64          ; 8-bit 16x16 patterns in pattern memory
  47+ E000              SPR_SIZE        equ 16          ; pixels, each way (at scale x1)
  48+ E000              SPR_SCREEN_X    equ 32          ; sprite coordinates of the 256x192
  49+ E000              SPR_SCREEN_Y    equ 32          ; screen's top left pixel
  50+ E000              SPR_TRANS       equ $E3         ; transparent pattern byte (NextReg $4B)
  51+ E000
  52+ E000              SPRF_PAL        equ $10         ; flags (attribute +2): palette offset x 16,
  53+ E000              SPRF_XMIRROR    equ $08         ; mirror left-right,
  54+ E000              SPRF_YMIRROR    equ $04         ; mirror top-bottom,
  55+ E000              SPRF_ROT        equ $02         ; rotate 90 degrees clockwise
  56+ E000
  57+ E000              SPR_SLOT_PORT   equ $303B       ; write: select sprite / pattern slot
  58+ E000              SPR_ATTR_PORT   equ $57         ; write: attributes, from the selected sprite
  59+ E000              SPR_PAT_PORT    equ $5B         ; write: pattern bytes, from the selected slot
  60+ E000
  61+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  62+ E000              ; Start-up: sprites on, shown over the border too (the full 320 x 256),
  63+ E000              ; priority S U L; the transparent index; every hardware sprite hidden and the shadow table cleared (all hidden,
  64+ E000              ; pattern 0, at 0,0).
  65+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  66+ E000              sprites_init:
  67+ E000 ED 91 15 0B          nextreg $15,%00001011           ; priority S U L, sprites over the
  68+ E004                                                      ; border (bit 1), visible (bit 0)
  69+ E004 ED 91 4B E3          nextreg $4B,SPR_TRANS
  70+ E008 01 3B 30             ld bc,SPR_SLOT_PORT             ; from sprite 0, the 4-byte form
  71+ E00B AF                   xor a                           ; (+3 bit 6 clear): all hidden
  72+ E00C ED 79                out (c),a
  73+ E00E 0E 57                ld c,SPR_ATTR_PORT
  74+ E010 11 00 02             ld de,SPR_HW*4
  75+ E013 ED 79        .hw:    out (c),a
  76+ E015 1B                   dec de
  77+ E016 7A                   ld a,d
  78+ E017 B3                   or e
  79+ E018 3E 00                ld a,0
  80+ E01A 20 F7                jr nz,.hw
  81+ E01C 21 00 E2             ld hl,spr_shadow
  82+ E01F 06 20                ld b,SPR_COUNT
  83+ E021 77           .sh:    ld (hl),a                       ; x, y, flags 0
  84+ E022 23                   inc hl
  85+ E023 77                   ld (hl),a
  86+ E024 23                   inc hl
  87+ E025 77                   ld (hl),a
  88+ E026 23                   inc hl
  89+ E027 36 40                ld (hl),$40                     ; hidden, 5-byte form, pattern 0
  90+ E029 23                   inc hl
  91+ E02A 77                   ld (hl),a                       ; scale x1
  92+ E02B 23                   inc hl
  93+ E02C 10 F3                djnz .sh
  94+ E02E                      ; fall through: send it
  95+ E02E
  96+ E02E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  97+ E02E              ; Copy the shadow table to sprites 0..SPR_COUNT-1.  Once a frame, after the
  98+ E02E              ; changes (best just after the frame flip, so a move doesn't show half done).
  99+ E02E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 100+ E02E              spr_update:
 101+ E02E 01 3B 30             ld bc,SPR_SLOT_PORT
 102+ E031 AF                   xor a
 103+ E032 ED 79                out (c),a                       ; from sprite 0
 104+ E034 21 00 E2             ld hl,spr_shadow
 105+ E037 0E 57                ld c,SPR_ATTR_PORT
 106+ E039 16 20                ld d,SPR_COUNT
 107+ E03B 06 05        .lp:    ld b,SPR_ATTR
 108+ E03D ED B3                otir
 109+ E03F 15                   dec d
 110+ E040 20 F9                jr nz,.lp
 111+ E042 C9                   ret
 112+ E043
 113+ E043              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 114+ E043              ; Load B 8-bit patterns (256 bytes each) from HL into pattern memory, from
 115+ E043              ; pattern A (0..63).  HL ends past them.
 116+ E043              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 117+ E043              spr_patterns:
 118+ E043 50                   ld d,b
 119+ E044 01 3B 30             ld bc,SPR_SLOT_PORT
 120+ E047 ED 79                out (c),a
 121+ E049 0E 5B                ld c,SPR_PAT_PORT
 122+ E04B 06 00        .lp:    ld b,0                          ; 256 bytes
 123+ E04D ED B3                otir
 124+ E04F 15                   dec d
 125+ E050 20 F9                jr nz,.lp
 126+ E052 C9                   ret
 127+ E053
 128+ E053              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 129+ E053              ; Load B colours (8-bit RRRGGGBB) from HL into the sprite palette, from
 130+ E053              ; index A.  (It starts as the identity: only needed for other colours.)
 131+ E053              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 132+ E053              spr_palette:
 133+ E053 ED 91 43 20          nextreg $43,%00100000           ; edit the first sprite palette
 134+ E057 ED 92 40             nextreg $40,a
 135+ E05A 7E           .lp:    ld a,(hl)
 136+ E05B ED 92 41             nextreg $41,a
 137+ E05E 23                   inc hl
 138+ E05F 10 F9                djnz .lp
 139+ E061 C9                   ret
 140+ E062
 141+ E062              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 142+ E062              ; Sprite A (0..SPR_COUNT-1) in the shadow table.  BC = x, DE = y (sprite
 143+ E062              ; screen, signed 9 bits), H = pattern (0..63), L = flags (SPRF_*).  Shows it.
 144+ E062              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 145+ E062              spr_set:
 146+ E062 22 04 E1             ld (spr_pf),hl
 147+ E065 F5                   push af
 148+ E066 CD 84 E0             call spr_pos
 149+ E069 F1                   pop af
 150+ E06A CD FA E0             call spr_addr
 151+ E06D 23                   inc hl
 152+ E06E 23                   inc hl                          ; +2: the flags, keeping x bit 8
 153+ E06F 3A 04 E1             ld a,(spr_pf)
 154+ E072 E6 FE                and $FE
 155+ E074 5F                   ld e,a
 156+ E075 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 157+ E076 E6 01                and 1
 158+ E078 B3                   or e
 159+ E079 77                   ld (hl),a
 160+ E07A 23                   inc hl                          ; +3: visible, 5-byte form, pattern
 161+ E07B 3A 05 E1             ld a,(spr_pf+1)
 162+ E07E E6 3F                and $3F
 163+ E080 F6 C0                or $C0
 164+ E082 77                   ld (hl),a
 165+ E083 C9                   ret
 166+ E084
 167+ E084              ; Sprite A: move to BC = x, DE = y (signed 9 bits)
 168+ E084              spr_pos:
 169+ E084 D5                   push de
 170+ E085 CD FA E0             call spr_addr
 171+ E088 D1                   pop de
 172+ E089 71                   ld (hl),c                       ; +0 x bits 0-7
 173+ E08A 23                   inc hl
 174+ E08B 73                   ld (hl),e                       ; +1 y bits 0-7
 175+ E08C 23                   inc hl
 176+ E08D 78                   ld a,b                          ; +2 bit 0: x bit 8
 177+ E08E E6 01                and 1
 178+ E090 47                   ld b,a
 179+ E091 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 180+ E092 E6 FE                and $FE
 181+ E094 B0                   or b
 182+ E095 77                   ld (hl),a
 183+ E096 23                   inc hl
 184+ E097 23                   inc hl
 185+ E098 7A                   ld a,d                          ; +4 bit 0: y bit 8
 186+ E099 E6 01                and 1
 187+ E09B 57                   ld d,a
 188+ E09C 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 189+ E09D E6 FE                and $FE
 190+ E09F B2                   or d
 191+ E0A0 77                   ld (hl),a
 192+ E0A1 C9                   ret
 193+ E0A2
 194+ E0A2              ; Sprite A: pattern E (0..63), keeping whether it is shown
 195+ E0A2              spr_pattern:
 196+ E0A2 CD FA E0             call spr_addr
 197+ E0A5 7D                   ld a,l
 198+ E0A6 C6 03                add a,3
 199+ E0A8 6F                   ld l,a
 200+ E0A9 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 201+ E0AA E6 C0                and $C0
 202+ E0AC B3                   or e
 203+ E0AD 77                   ld (hl),a
 204+ E0AE C9                   ret
 205+ E0AF
 206+ E0AF              ; Sprite A: flags E (SPRF_*: palette offset, mirrors, rotation)
 207+ E0AF              spr_flags:
 208+ E0AF CD FA E0             call spr_addr
 209+ E0B2 23                   inc hl
 210+ E0B3 23                   inc hl
 211+ E0B4 7B                   ld a,e
 212+ E0B5 E6 FE                and $FE
 213+ E0B7 5F                   ld e,a
 214+ E0B8 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 215+ E0B9 E6 01                and 1
 216+ E0BB B3                   or e
 217+ E0BC 77                   ld (hl),a
 218+ E0BD C9                   ret
 219+ E0BE
 220+ E0BE              ; Sprite A: scale E across, D down (0..3 = x1, x2, x4, x8)
 221+ E0BE              spr_scale:
 222+ E0BE D5                   push de
 223+ E0BF CD FA E0             call spr_addr
 224+ E0C2 D1                   pop de
 225+ E0C3 7D                   ld a,l
 226+ E0C4 C6 04                add a,4
 227+ E0C6 6F                   ld l,a
 228+ E0C7 7B                   ld a,e
 229+ E0C8 E6 03                and 3
 230+ E0CA 07                   rlca
 231+ E0CB 07                   rlca
 232+ E0CC 5F                   ld e,a
 233+ E0CD 7A                   ld a,d
 234+ E0CE E6 03                and 3
 235+ E0D0 B3                   or e
 236+ E0D1 07                   rlca                            ; x scale bits 4-3, y scale 2-1
 237+ E0D2 5F                   ld e,a
 238+ E0D3 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 239+ E0D4 E6 01                and 1                           ; (keep y bit 8)
 240+ E0D6 B3                   or e
 241+ E0D7 77                   ld (hl),a
 242+ E0D8 C9                   ret
 243+ E0D9
 244+ E0D9              ; Sprite A: hide / show
 245+ E0D9              spr_hide:
 246+ E0D9 CD F3 E0             call spr_addr3
 247+ E0DC CB BE                res 7,(hl)
 248+ E0DE C9                   ret
 249+ E0DF              spr_show:
 250+ E0DF CD F3 E0             call spr_addr3
 251+ E0E2 CB FE                set 7,(hl)
 252+ E0E4 C9                   ret
 253+ E0E5
 254+ E0E5              ; Hide every sprite in the table
 255+ E0E5              spr_hide_all:
 256+ E0E5 21 03 E2             ld hl,spr_shadow+3
 257+ E0E8 11 05 00             ld de,SPR_ATTR
 258+ E0EB 06 20                ld b,SPR_COUNT
 259+ E0ED CB BE        .lp:    res 7,(hl)
 260+ E0EF 19                   add hl,de
 261+ E0F0 10 FB                djnz .lp
 262+ E0F2 C9                   ret
 263+ E0F3
 264+ E0F3              ; A = sprite -> HL -> its attributes in the table, +3 for spr_addr3.
 265+ E0F3              ; (The table doesn't cross a 256-byte boundary, so +n is just L + n.)
 266+ E0F3              spr_addr3:
 267+ E0F3 CD FA E0             call spr_addr
 268+ E0F6 23                   inc hl
 269+ E0F7 23                   inc hl
 270+ E0F8 23                   inc hl
 271+ E0F9 C9                   ret
 272+ E0FA              spr_addr:
 273+ E0FA 57                   ld d,a
 274+ E0FB 1E 05                ld e,SPR_ATTR
 275+ E0FD ED 30                mul d,e
 276+ E0FF 21 00 E2             ld hl,spr_shadow
 277+ E102 19                   add hl,de
 278+ E103 C9                   ret
 279+ E104
 280+ E104              ;==============================================================================
 281+ E104              ; Sprite data
 282+ E104              ;==============================================================================
 283+ E104              ; The patterns themselves are in the sprite banks (sprite_data.asm).
 284+ E104              ; Bank 0 has the human status sprites, loaded as patterns 0-3 by
 285+ E104              ; sprites_init, after the HUD's passenger markers:
 286+ E104              ;   HSPR_FIELD   0  waiting in the field, not yet rescued: an empty square
 287+ E104              ;   HSPR_ABOARD  1  aboard the VTOL: a person sitting in a chair
 288+ E104              ;   HSPR_SAVED   2  rescued: a green person standing
 289+ E104              ;   HSPR_DEAD    3  dead: a white person with a red cross over them
 290+ E104
 291+ E104 00 00        spr_pf:     dw 0                        ; spr_set: flags, pattern
 292+ E106 00 00 00...          ALIGN 256
 293+ E200 00 00 00...  spr_shadow: ds SPR_COUNT*SPR_ATTR       ; attributes, as sent (5-byte form)
 294+ E2A0                      ASSERT SPR_COUNT*SPR_ATTR <= 256
 295+ E2A0

1683  E2A0                      INCLUDE "hud_sprites.asm"       ; the human column, the compass bar
# file opened: hud_sprites.asm
   1+ E2A0              ;==============================================================================
   2+ E2A0              ; Z-POLICE - HUD sprites (in the sprites page, PAGE_SPRITES, at $E000 in slot
   3+ E2A0              ; 7): the human status column and the compass bar, as hardware sprites on
   4+ E2A0              ; the 320 x 256 sprite screen.  hud_sprites sets both and sends the sprite
   5+ E2A0              ; table to the hardware; main_loop calls it once a frame, after the flip.
   6+ E2A0              ;
   7+ E2A0              ; Sprites used: 0..HUMAN_MAX-1 the humans, CSPR_SPR0.. the compass bar's
   8+ E2A0              ; marks, CSPR_PTR_SPR its pointer.
   9+ E2A0              ;==============================================================================
  10+ E2A0
  11+ E2A0              hud_sprites:
  12+ E2A0 CD 14 E3             call hum_hud
  13+ E2A3 CD A9 E2             call compass_spr
  14+ E2A6 C3 2E E0             jp spr_update
  15+ E2A9
  16+ E2A9              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  17+ E2A9              ; The compass bar, across the top of the screen in the top border (sprite
  18+ E2A9              ; row COMP_SPR_Y): a tape of marks every 16 yaw units (22.5 degrees), at 2
  19+ E2A9              ; pixels a yaw unit, centred on the heading at sprite x COMP_SPR_CX, across
  20+ E2A9              ; the full 320-pixel width of the sprite screen: 225 degrees (+-COMP_HALF,
  21+ E2A9              ; 80 yaw units).  Marks at the very edges are half off the screen (their x
  22+ E2A9              ; wraps, as sprite x does).  The letters N,
  23+ E2A9              ; E, S, W (red) at 0, 64, 128, 192; a yellow dot at the 45 degree points;
  24+ E2A9              ; a small white dot between.  A white pointer under the bar's middle marks
  25+ E2A9              ; the heading.  Turning right (yaw up) moves the marks left.
  26+ E2A9              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  27+ E2A9              CSPR_SPR0       equ HUMAN_MAX       ; first compass mark sprite (20)
  28+ E2A9              COMP_HALF       equ 80              ; yaw units shown either side: 160 px
  29+ E2A9              CSPR_MARKS      equ 2*COMP_HALF/16+1    ; most marks in view at once (11)
  30+ E2A9              CSPR_PTR_SPR    equ CSPR_SPR0+CSPR_MARKS   ; the pointer's sprite
  31+ E2A9              COMP_SPR_Y      equ 8               ; the bar's sprite row
  32+ E2A9              COMP_PTR_Y      equ COMP_SPR_Y+SPR_SIZE    ; the pointer, under it
  33+ E2A9              COMP_SPR_CX     equ 160             ; the heading's x: the screen's middle
  34+ E2A9              COMP_SPR_X0     equ COMP_SPR_CX-2*COMP_HALF-SPR_SIZE/2  ; x of a mark =
  35+ E2A9                                                  ; this + 2 * (COMP_HALF + mark - yaw)
  36+ E2A9                      ASSERT CSPR_PTR_SPR < SPR_COUNT
  37+ E2A9                      ASSERT CSPR_E == CSPR_N+1 && CSPR_S == CSPR_N+2 && CSPR_W == CSPR_N+3
  38+ E2A9
  39+ E2A9              compass_spr:
  40+ E2A9 3E 14                ld a,CSPR_SPR0
  41+ E2AB 32 52 E3             ld (cs_spr),a
  42+ E2AE 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; mark k, at yaw k * 16
  43+ E2B0 C5           .lp:    push bc
  44+ E2B1 79                   ld a,c
  45+ E2B2 87                   add a,a
  46+ E2B3 87                   add a,a
  47+ E2B4 87                   add a,a
  48+ E2B5 87                   add a,a
  49+ E2B6 21 2C AE             ld hl,yaw
  50+ E2B9 96                   sub (hl)                        ; mark - yaw, signed
  51+ E2BA C6 50                add a,COMP_HALF
  52+ E2BC FE A1                cp 2*COMP_HALF+1
  53+ E2BE 30 30                jr nc,.nx                       ; off the bar: not shown
  54+ E2C0 6F                   ld l,a                          ; x = COMP_SPR_X0 + 2 * that
  55+ E2C1 26 00                ld h,0
  56+ E2C3 29                   add hl,hl
  57+ E2C4 11 F8 FF             ld de,COMP_SPR_X0
  58+ E2C7 19                   add hl,de
  59+ E2C8 E5                   push hl
  60+ E2C9 79                   ld a,c                          ; which mark: a letter every 64,
  61+ E2CA E6 03                and 3                           ; a dot every 32, else a tick
  62+ E2CC 79                   ld a,c
  63+ E2CD 20 08                jr nz,.nl
  64+ E2CF 0F                   rrca
  65+ E2D0 0F                   rrca
  66+ E2D1 E6 03                and 3
  67+ E2D3 C6 04                add a,CSPR_N
  68+ E2D5 18 08                jr .pat
  69+ E2D7 E6 01        .nl:    and 1
  70+ E2D9 3E 08                ld a,CSPR_DOT
  71+ E2DB 28 02                jr z,.pat
  72+ E2DD 3E 09                ld a,CSPR_TICK
  73+ E2DF 67           .pat:   ld h,a
  74+ E2E0 2E 00                ld l,0
  75+ E2E2 C1                   pop bc                          ; BC = x
  76+ E2E3 11 08 00             ld de,COMP_SPR_Y
  77+ E2E6 3A 52 E3             ld a,(cs_spr)
  78+ E2E9 CD 62 E0             call spr_set
  79+ E2EC 21 52 E3             ld hl,cs_spr
  80+ E2EF 34                   inc (hl)
  81+ E2F0 C1           .nx:    pop bc
  82+ E2F1 0C                   inc c
  83+ E2F2 79                   ld a,c
  84+ E2F3 FE 10                cp 16
  85+ E2F5 20 B9                jr nz,.lp
  86+ E2F7 3A 52 E3             ld a,(cs_spr)                   ; hide the mark sprites not used
  87+ E2FA FE 1F        .hd:    cp CSPR_SPR0+CSPR_MARKS
  88+ E2FC 30 08                jr nc,.ptr
  89+ E2FE F5                   push af
  90+ E2FF CD D9 E0             call spr_hide
  91+ E302 F1                   pop af
  92+ E303 3C                   inc a
  93+ E304 18 F4                jr .hd
  94+ E306 3E 1F        .ptr:   ld a,CSPR_PTR_SPR               ; the pointer
  95+ E308 01 98 00             ld bc,COMP_SPR_CX-SPR_SIZE/2
  96+ E30B 11 18 00             ld de,COMP_PTR_Y
  97+ E30E 21 00 0A             ld hl,CSPR_PTR<<8
  98+ E311 C3 62 E0             jp spr_set
  99+ E314
 100+ E314              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 101+ E314              ; The human status HUD: a column of hardware sprites, sprite k for human k,
 102+ E314              ; HUD_SPR_X pixels in from the left edge of the 320 x 256 sprite screen,
 103+ E314              ; human 0 at the bottom (HUD_SPR_Y) and each next one HUD_SPR_STEP higher.
 104+ E314              ; Each shows its human's state with the human status patterns
 105+ E314              ; (sprite_data.asm): waiting in the city (HSPR_FIELD), aboard the VTOL
 106+ E314              ; (HSPR_ABOARD), rescued (HSPR_SAVED) or dead (HSPR_DEAD).  Unused humans'
 107+ E314              ; sprites are hidden.
 108+ E314              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 109+ E314              HUD_SPR_X       equ 8
 110+ E314              HUD_SPR_Y       equ 256-8-SPR_SIZE  ; the bottom one: 8 pixels above the edge
 111+ E314              HUD_SPR_STEP    equ SPR_SIZE
 112+ E314              HUD_SPR_MAX     equ (HUD_SPR_Y+HUD_SPR_STEP)/HUD_SPR_STEP   ; that fit (15)
 113+ E314                      ASSERT HSPR_FIELD == HS_WAIT-1 && HSPR_ABOARD == HS_ABOARD-1
 114+ E314                      ASSERT HSPR_SAVED == HS_SAVED-1 && HSPR_DEAD == HS_DEAD-1
 115+ E314                      ASSERT HUMAN_MAX <= SPR_COUNT
 116+ E314              hum_hud:
 117+ E314 DD 21 31 39          ld ix,humans
 118+ E318 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; human = sprite number
 119+ E31A C5           .lp:    push bc
 120+ E31B DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
 121+ E31E B7                   or a
 122+ E31F 28 20                jr z,.hide                      ; unused
 123+ E321 79                   ld a,c
 124+ E322 FE 0F                cp HUD_SPR_MAX
 125+ E324 30 1B                jr nc,.hide                     ; no room on the screen
 126+ E326 87                   add a,a                         ; y = HUD_SPR_Y - human * 16
 127+ E327 87                   add a,a
 128+ E328 87                   add a,a
 129+ E329 87                   add a,a
 130+ E32A 5F                   ld e,a
 131+ E32B 3E E8                ld a,HUD_SPR_Y
 132+ E32D 93                   sub e
 133+ E32E 5F                   ld e,a
 134+ E32F 16 00                ld d,0
 135+ E331 DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
 136+ E334 3D                   dec a
 137+ E335 67                   ld h,a                          ; the state's pattern
 138+ E336 2E 00                ld l,0                          ; no mirror, rotation
 139+ E338 79                   ld a,c

# file opened: initialise.asm
   1+ E000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ E000              ; Z-POLICE - start-up initialisation (8K page PAGE_INIT, runs at $E000 in
   3+ E000              ; slot 7)
   4+ E000              ;
   5+ E000              ; Code needed only once, at start-up, kept out of the resident page: start
   6+ E000              ; (zpolice.asm) pages this in with nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_INIT and calls
   7+ E000              ; initgame, then slot 7 is free for the other core pages.
   8+ E000              ;==============================================================================
   9+ E000
  10+ E000              ; Hardware set-up: 28MHz, Layer 2 mode, transparency, layer priority, Layer
  11+ E000              ; 2 scroll and clip window, the memory map of the always-mapped slots, the
  12+ E000              ; peripheral settings (AY sound, turbo sound, internal speaker, no
  13+ E000              ; contention), and a black border.
  14+ E000              initgame:
  15+ E000 ED 91 07 03          nextreg $07,3                   ; 28MHz
  16+ E004 ED 91 70 00          nextreg $70,0                   ; Layer 2 256x192 mode
  17+ E008 ED 91 14 E3          nextreg $14,$E3                 ; global transparency colour
  18+ E00C ED 91 15 08          nextreg $15,%00001000           ; priority S U L (ULA over L2); sprites
  19+ E010                                                      ; off until sprites_init
  20+ E010 ED 91 16 00          nextreg $16,0                   ; Layer 2 scroll X
  21+ E014 ED 91 17 00          nextreg $17,0                   ; Layer 2 scroll Y
  22+ E018 ED 91 1C 01          nextreg $1C,%00000001           ; reset L2 clip index
  23+ E01C ED 91 18 00          nextreg $18,0                   ; Layer 2 clip: x 0..255,
  24+ E020 ED 91 18 FF          nextreg $18,255
  25+ E024 ED 91 18 00          nextreg $18,0                   ; y 0..191: the viewport, and the
  26+ E028 ED 91 18 BF          nextreg $18,191                 ; city map through the ULA panel
  27+ E02C ED 91 54 0A          nextreg ULA_MMU,PAGE_ULA0       ; ULA screen (bank 5) at $8000
  28+ E030 ED 91 51 05          nextreg ZOMBIE_MMU,PAGE_ZOMBIE  ; zombie code/data stays at $2000
  29+ E034 ED 91 52 06          nextreg OCC_MMU,PAGE_OCC0       ; occlusion data at $4000-$5FFF,
  30+ E038 ED 91 53 07          nextreg TABLES_MMU,PAGE_OCC1    ; city_col at $6000-$6FFF
  31+ E03C                      ; Peripheral settings.  $06 and $08 are read, changed and written
  32+ E03C                      ; back, so the bits not named here keep their values (in $06, bit 4
  33+ E03C                      ; is the divMMC automap, which the file handler's RST $08 needs).
  34+ E03C 3E 06                ld a,$06                        ; Peripheral 2: audio chip mode
  35+ E03E CD 4F A9             call read_nextreg               ; (bits 1-0) = %01, AY
  36+ E041 E6 FC                and %11111100
  37+ E043 F6 01                or %00000001
  38+ E045 ED 92 06             nextreg $06,a
  39+ E048 3E 08                ld a,$08                        ; Peripheral 3:
  40+ E04A CD 4F A9             call read_nextreg
  41+ E04D F6 52                or %01010010                    ; bit 6: no RAM / I/O contention,
  42+ E04F ED 92 08             nextreg $08,a                   ; bit 4: internal speaker on,
  43+ E052                                                      ; bit 1: turbo sound on
  44+ E052 ED 91 09 00          nextreg $09,0                   ; Peripheral 4: AY stereo (not
  45+ E056                                                      ; mono), no sprite id lockstep, HDMI
  46+ E056                                                      ; audio on, no scanlines
  47+ E056 AF                   xor a
  48+ E057 D3 FE                out ($FE),a                     ; black border
  49+ E059 C9                   ret
  50+ E05A
