# file opened: file_handler.asm
   1+ E000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ E000              ; Z-POLICE - file handling: loading and saving on the SD card (8K page
   3+ E000              ; PAGE_FILE, runs at $E000 in slot 7: page it in with nextreg
   4+ E000              ; CODE_MMU,PAGE_FILE before calling)
   5+ E000              ;
   6+ E000              ; General-purpose routines over the esxDOS / NextZXOS file API (RST $08 with
   7+ E000              ; a function code after it).  Game-specific loading and saving (save games,
   8+ E000              ; high scores, settings) go on top of these.
   9+ E000              ;
  10+ E000              ; The ROM: the divMMC only traps RST $08 while the ROM is paged in at
  11+ E000              ; $0000-$3FFF, but the game keeps other pages there (slot 0: a Layer 2
  12+ E000              ; section or the sine table; slot 1: the zombie page).  So every call pages
  13+ E000              ; the ROM into slots 0 and 1 (fh_rom_in), makes the RST $08, then puts the
  14+ E000              ; game's pages back (fh_rom_out).  Interrupts are off over the call (the
  15+ E000              ; game's IM 2 routine is in slot 1, covered by the ROM, and IY is in use and
  16+ E000              ; slot 2 holds game data, so the ROM's routine must not run either), and
  17+ E000              ; fh_rom_out turns them back on if they were on.
  18+ E000              ;
  19+ E000              ; Rules for callers:
  20+ E000              ;   * page this in at $E000 (slot 7) first, and call from outside slot 7:
  21+ E000              ;     resident code or a game logic page in slot 6;
  22+ E000              ;   * file names and data buffers must be in $4000-$DFFF: not in slots 0-1
  23+ E000              ;     (the ROM and esxDOS are there during a call) nor slot 7 (this page);
  24+ E000              ;   * file names end with a 0 byte.
  25+ E000              ;
  26+ E000              ; Every routine returns carry clear on success; carry set on failure with
  27+ E000              ; A = the esxDOS error code.  A file that was opened is closed again on
  28+ E000              ; any error in fh_load / fh_save.
  29+ E000              ;
  30+ E000              ; Routines:
  31+ E000              ;   fh_init      get the default drive (call once, before the others)
  32+ E000              ;   fh_open      HL = name, B = mode (FA_*)      -> A = handle
  33+ E000              ;   fh_close     A = handle
  34+ E000              ;   fh_read      A = handle, HL = address, BC = length -> BC = bytes read
  35+ E000              ;   fh_write     A = handle, HL = address, BC = length -> BC = bytes written
  36+ E000              ;   fh_seek      A = handle, BCDE = offset, L = SEEK_* -> BCDE = position
  37+ E000              ;   fh_size      A = handle                      -> BCDE = file size
  38+ E000              ;   fh_load      HL = name, DE = address, BC = most bytes -> BC = bytes read
  39+ E000              ;   fh_save      HL = name, DE = address, BC = length (created / replaced)
  40+ E000              ;   fh_exists    HL = name -> carry clear if the file exists
  41+ E000              ;   fh_delete    HL = name
  42+ E000              ;==============================================================================
  43+ E000
  44+ E000              ; esxDOS function codes (the byte after RST $08)
  45+ E000              M_GETSETDRV     equ $89             ; A = 0: get the default drive
  46+ E000              F_OPEN          equ $9A
  47+ E000              F_CLOSE         equ $9B
  48+ E000              F_READ          equ $9D
  49+ E000              F_WRITE         equ $9E
  50+ E000              F_SEEK          equ $9F
  51+ E000              F_FSTAT         equ $A1
  52+ E000              F_UNLINK        equ $AD
  53+ E000
  54+ E000              ; F_OPEN modes (B)
  55+ E000              FA_READ         equ $01             ; read
  56+ E000              FA_WRITE        equ $02             ; write
  57+ E000              FA_OPEN_EX      equ $00             ; open an existing file only
  58+ E000              FA_OPEN_AL      equ $08             ; open, or create if it doesn't exist
  59+ E000              FA_CREATE_NEW   equ $04             ; create, fail if it exists
  60+ E000              FA_CREATE_AL    equ $0C             ; create, replacing any existing file
  61+ E000              FA_OVERWRITE    equ FA_WRITE|FA_CREATE_AL   ; for saving a whole file
  62+ E000
  63+ E000              ; F_SEEK modes (L)
  64+ E000              SEEK_START      equ 0               ; from the start of the file
  65+ E000              SEEK_FWD        equ 1               ; forwards from the current position
  66+ E000              SEEK_BACK       equ 2               ; backwards from the current position
  67+ E000
  68+ E000              FSTAT_SIZE      equ 7               ; F_FSTAT buffer: file size, 4 bytes
  69+ E000              FSTAT_LEN       equ 11              ; F_FSTAT buffer length
  70+ E000
  71+ E000              ESX_DEFAULT     equ '*'             ; drive: the default drive
  72+ E000
  73+ E000              ; An esxDOS call: the ROM in at $0000-$3FFF for the RST $08, the game's
  74+ E000              ; pages back after.  Registers in and out are the function's own; flags
  75+ E000              ; and A come back from esxDOS (carry set = error, A = code).
  76+ E000                      MACRO ESXDOS fn
  77+ E000 ~                    call fh_rom_in
  78+ E000 ~                    rst $08
  79+ E000 ~                    db fn
  80+ E000 ~                    call fh_rom_out
  81+ E000                      ENDM
  82+ E000
  83+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  84+ E000              ; Get the default drive into fh_drive (else the '*' default is used)
  85+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  86+ E000              fh_init:
  87+ E000 AF                   xor a                           ; A = 0: get
  88+ E001                      ESXDOS M_GETSETDRV
  88+ E001 CD CC E0    >        call fh_rom_in
  88+ E004 CF          >        rst $08
  88+ E005 89          >        db M_GETSETDRV
  88+ E006 CD FD E0    >        call fh_rom_out
  89+ E009 D8                   ret c
  90+ E00A 32 16 E1             ld (fh_drive),a
  91+ E00D C9                   ret
  92+ E00E
  93+ E00E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  94+ E00E              ; HL -> file name, B = mode (FA_*) -> A = handle (also in fh_handle)
  95+ E00E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  96+ E00E              fh_open:
  97+ E00E DD E5                push ix
  98+ E010 E5                   push hl
  99+ E011 DD E1                pop ix                          ; IX = HL = the name (both
 100+ E013 3A 16 E1             ld a,(fh_drive)                 ; conventions: programs, dot
 101+ E016                      ESXDOS F_OPEN                   ; commands)
 101+ E016 CD CC E0    >        call fh_rom_in
 101+ E019 CF          >        rst $08
 101+ E01A 9A          >        db F_OPEN
 101+ E01B CD FD E0    >        call fh_rom_out
 102+ E01E DD E1                pop ix
 103+ E020 D8                   ret c
 104+ E021 32 17 E1             ld (fh_handle),a
 105+ E024 C9                   ret
 106+ E025
 107+ E025              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 108+ E025              ; A = handle: close it
 109+ E025              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 110+ E025              fh_close:
 111+ E025                      ESXDOS F_CLOSE
 111+ E025 CD CC E0    >        call fh_rom_in
 111+ E028 CF          >        rst $08
 111+ E029 9B          >        db F_CLOSE
 111+ E02A CD FD E0    >        call fh_rom_out
 112+ E02D C9                   ret
 113+ E02E
 114+ E02E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 115+ E02E              ; A = handle, HL = address, BC = length -> BC = bytes read (fewer at the end
 116+ E02E              ; of the file)
 117+ E02E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 118+ E02E              fh_read:
 119+ E02E DD E5                push ix
 120+ E030 E5                   push hl
 121+ E031 DD E1                pop ix
 122+ E033                      ESXDOS F_READ
 122+ E033 CD CC E0    >        call fh_rom_in
 122+ E036 CF          >        rst $08
 122+ E037 9D          >        db F_READ
 122+ E038 CD FD E0    >        call fh_rom_out
 123+ E03B DD E1                pop ix
 124+ E03D C9                   ret
 125+ E03E
 126+ E03E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 127+ E03E              ; A = handle, HL = address, BC = length -> BC = bytes written
 128+ E03E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 129+ E03E              fh_write:
 130+ E03E DD E5                push ix
 131+ E040 E5                   push hl
 132+ E041 DD E1                pop ix
 133+ E043                      ESXDOS F_WRITE
 133+ E043 CD CC E0    >        call fh_rom_in
 133+ E046 CF          >        rst $08
 133+ E047 9E          >        db F_WRITE
 133+ E048 CD FD E0    >        call fh_rom_out
 134+ E04B DD E1                pop ix
 135+ E04D C9                   ret
 136+ E04E
 137+ E04E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 138+ E04E              ; A = handle, BCDE = offset (32-bit), L = mode (SEEK_*) -> BCDE = position
 139+ E04E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 140+ E04E              fh_seek:
 141+ E04E DD E5                push ix
 142+ E050 E5                   push hl
 143+ E051 E5                   push hl
 144+ E052 DD E1                pop ix                          ; mode in IXL (programs) and L
 145+ E054                      ESXDOS F_SEEK
 145+ E054 CD CC E0    >        call fh_rom_in
 145+ E057 CF          >        rst $08
 145+ E058 9F          >        db F_SEEK
 145+ E059 CD FD E0    >        call fh_rom_out
 146+ E05C E1                   pop hl
 147+ E05D DD E1                pop ix
 148+ E05F C9                   ret
 149+ E060
 150+ E060              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 151+ E060              ; A = handle -> BCDE = file size (32-bit)
 152+ E060              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 153+ E060              fh_size:
 154+ E060 DD E5                push ix
 155+ E062 E5                   push hl
 156+ E063 21 1B E1             ld hl,fh_stat
 157+ E066 E5                   push hl
 158+ E067 DD E1                pop ix
 159+ E069                      ESXDOS F_FSTAT
 159+ E069 CD CC E0    >        call fh_rom_in
 159+ E06C CF          >        rst $08
 159+ E06D A1          >        db F_FSTAT
 159+ E06E CD FD E0    >        call fh_rom_out
 160+ E071 38 0A                jr c,.x
 161+ E073 2A 22 E1             ld hl,(fh_stat+FSTAT_SIZE)
 162+ E076 EB                   ex de,hl                        ; DE = low word
 163+ E077 2A 24 E1             ld hl,(fh_stat+FSTAT_SIZE+2)
 164+ E07A 44                   ld b,h                          ; BC = high word
 165+ E07B 4D                   ld c,l
 166+ E07C B7                   or a
 167+ E07D E1           .x:     pop hl
 168+ E07E DD E1                pop ix
 169+ E080 C9                   ret
 170+ E081
 171+ E081              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 172+ E081              ; Load a file: HL -> name, DE = address, BC = most bytes to read
 173+ E081              ; -> BC = bytes read.  The file is opened, read and closed.
 174+ E081              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 175+ E081              fh_load:
 176+ E081 D5                   push de
 177+ E082 C5                   push bc
 178+ E083 06 01                ld b,FA_READ|FA_OPEN_EX
 179+ E085 CD 0E E0             call fh_open
 180+ E088 C1                   pop bc
 181+ E089 E1                   pop hl                          ; HL = address
 182+ E08A D8                   ret c                           ; couldn't open (A = error)
 183+ E08B CD 2E E0             call fh_read
 184+ E08E 18 0D                jr fh_done                      ; close it, keeping any error
 185+ E090
 186+ E090              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 187+ E090              ; Save a file: HL -> name, DE = address, BC = length.  The file is created,
 188+ E090              ; replacing any file of that name.
 189+ E090              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 190+ E090              fh_save:
 191+ E090 D5                   push de
 192+ E091 C5                   push bc
 193+ E092 06 0E                ld b,FA_OVERWRITE
 194+ E094 CD 0E E0             call fh_open
 195+ E097 C1                   pop bc
 196+ E098 E1                   pop hl
 197+ E099 D8                   ret c
 198+ E09A CD 3E E0             call fh_write
 199+ E09D                      ; fall through
 200+ E09D
 201+ E09D              ; After a read / write on fh_handle: close the file.  Carry and A as from
 202+ E09D              ; that read / write if it failed, else as from the close.
 203+ E09D              fh_done:
 204+ E09D F5                   push af
 205+ E09E 3A 17 E1             ld a,(fh_handle)
 206+ E0A1 CD 25 E0             call fh_close
 207+ E0A4 38 02                jr c,.cerr
 208+ E0A6 F1                   pop af                          ; the read / write's result
 209+ E0A7 C9                   ret
 210+ E0A8 E1           .cerr:  pop hl                          ; the close failed: if the read /
 211+ E0A9 CB 45                bit 0,l                         ; write had already failed, report
 212+ E0AB 28 01                jr z,.ce                        ; that, else the close's error
 213+ E0AD 7C                   ld a,h
 214+ E0AE 37           .ce:    scf
 215+ E0AF C9                   ret
 216+ E0B0
 217+ E0B0              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 218+ E0B0              ; HL -> name: carry clear if the file exists (it can be opened to read)
 219+ E0B0              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 220+ E0B0              fh_exists:
 221+ E0B0 06 01                ld b,FA_READ|FA_OPEN_EX
 222+ E0B2 CD 0E E0             call fh_open
 223+ E0B5 D8                   ret c
 224+ E0B6 C3 25 E0             jp fh_close
 225+ E0B9
 226+ E0B9              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 227+ E0B9              ; HL -> name: delete the file
 228+ E0B9              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 229+ E0B9              fh_delete:
 230+ E0B9 DD E5                push ix
 231+ E0BB E5                   push hl
 232+ E0BC DD E1                pop ix
 233+ E0BE 3A 16 E1             ld a,(fh_drive)
 234+ E0C1                      ESXDOS F_UNLINK
 234+ E0C1 CD CC E0    >        call fh_rom_in
 234+ E0C4 CF          >        rst $08
 234+ E0C5 AD          >        db F_UNLINK
 234+ E0C6 CD FD E0    >        call fh_rom_out
 235+ E0C9 DD E1                pop ix
 236+ E0CB C9                   ret
 237+ E0CC
 238+ E0CC              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 239+ E0CC              ; Page the ROM into slots 0 and 1 for RST $08, remembering the game's pages
 240+ E0CC              ; (fh_rom_in), and put them back (fh_rom_out).  Interrupts are off in
 241+ E0CC              ; between (the ROM covers the interrupt routine, slot 1), and back on after
 242+ E0CC              ; if they were on before.  Both keep every register and flag.
 243+ E0CC              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 244+ E0CC              fh_rom_in:
 245+ E0CC F5                   push af
 246+ E0CD ED 57                ld a,i                          ; P/V: interrupts on (IFF2)
 247+ E0CF F3                   di
 248+ E0D0 3E 00                ld a,0
 249+ E0D2 E2 D6 E0             jp po,.off
 250+ E0D5 3C                   inc a
 251+ E0D6 32 1A E1     .off:   ld (fh_ints),a
 252+ E0D9 C5                   push bc
 253+ E0DA 01 3B 24             ld bc,$243B
 254+ E0DD 3E 50                ld a,MMU_SLOT_0_REGISTER        ; slot 0's page now
 255+ E0DF ED 79                out (c),a
 256+ E0E1 04                   inc b
 257+ E0E2 ED 78                in a,(c)
 258+ E0E4 32 18 E1             ld (fh_mmu0),a
 259+ E0E7 05                   dec b
 260+ E0E8 3E 51                ld a,MMU_SLOT_1_REGISTER        ; slot 1's
 261+ E0EA ED 79                out (c),a
 262+ E0EC 04                   inc b
 263+ E0ED ED 78                in a,(c)
 264+ E0EF 32 19 E1             ld (fh_mmu1),a
 265+ E0F2 ED 91 50 FF          nextreg MMU_SLOT_0_REGISTER,PAGE_ROM
 266+ E0F6 ED 91 51 FF          nextreg MMU_SLOT_1_REGISTER,PAGE_ROM
 267+ E0FA C1                   pop bc
 268+ E0FB F1                   pop af
 269+ E0FC C9                   ret
 270+ E0FD
 271+ E0FD              fh_rom_out:
 272+ E0FD F3                   di                              ; (in case esxDOS enabled them)
 273+ E0FE F5                   push af
 274+ E0FF 3A 18 E1             ld a,(fh_mmu0)
 275+ E102 ED 92 50             nextreg MMU_SLOT_0_REGISTER,a
 276+ E105 3A 19 E1             ld a,(fh_mmu1)
 277+ E108 ED 92 51             nextreg MMU_SLOT_1_REGISTER,a
 278+ E10B 3A 1A E1             ld a,(fh_ints)
 279+ E10E B7                   or a
 280+ E10F 28 03                jr z,.off
 281+ E111 F1                   pop af
 282+ E112 FB                   ei                              ; on again, as they were
 283+ E113 C9                   ret
 284+ E114 F1           .off:   pop af
 285+ E115 C9                   ret
 286+ E116
 287+ E116              ;==============================================================================
 288+ E116              ; File handler data
 289+ E116              ;==============================================================================
 290+ E116 2A           fh_drive:   db ESX_DEFAULT              ; drive for F_OPEN (fh_init)
 291+ E117 00           fh_handle:  db 0                        ; the last file opened
 292+ E118 00           fh_mmu0:    db 0                        ; the game's slot 0 / 1 pages during
 293+ E119 00           fh_mmu1:    db 0                        ; an esxDOS call
 294+ E11A 00           fh_ints:    db 0                        ; 1: interrupts were on before it
 295+ E11B 00 00 00...  fh_stat:    ds FSTAT_LEN                ; F_FSTAT buffer (fh_size)
 296+ E126
# file closed: file_handler.asm

# file opened: audio.asm
   1+ E000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ E000              ; Z-POLICE - audio (8K page PAGE_AUDIO, runs at $E000 in slot 7: page it in
   3+ E000              ; with nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_AUDIO before calling)
   4+ E000              ;
   5+ E000              ; Sound devices: the Next's TurboSound, three AY-3-8912 chips.  initgame
   6+ E000              ; (initialise.asm) has already set the chip mode to AY (NextReg $06),
   7+ E000              ; turned TurboSound and the internal speaker on (NextReg $08) and the AY
   8+ E000              ; outputs to stereo (NextReg $09).  init_audio then sets every chip to a
   9+ E000              ; known, silent state (at start-up, and on every menu screen: menu_clear).
  10+ E000              ; AY 1 is the VTOL's engines: engine_start here, then the interrupt routine
  11+ E000              ; plays them (engine_tick, interrupts.asm).  AY 2 is the weapons' sound
  12+ E000              ; effects, AY 3 explosions: the game asks for one with ld a,SFX_n / ld
  13+ E000              ; (sfx_req),a (AY 2), ld (sfx2_req),a (AY 3's A and B) or ld (sfx3_req),a
  14+ E000              ; (AY 3's C), and the interrupt plays it (sfx_tick).  (Music would need AY 3
  15+ E000              ; back.)  The interrupt writes the AY ports while eng_on is 1,
  16+ E000              ; so code here writes them only with eng_on 0 (or interrupts off).
  17+ E000              ;
  18+ E000              ; TurboSound ports:
  19+ E000              ;   $FFFD write %1LR111cc: select AY chip cc (AY_CHIP_1..3) with its left
  20+ E000              ;         (L) and right (R) outputs on;
  21+ E000              ;         any value 0..15: select that register of the selected chip
  22+ E000              ;   $BFFD write: the value of the selected register
  23+ E000              ;==============================================================================
  24+ E000
  25+ E000              AY_CTRL_PORT    equ $FFFD           ; chip / register select
  26+ E000              AY_DATA_PORT    equ $BFFD           ; register value
  27+ E000
  28+ E000              ; Chip select ($FFFD): AY_SELECT | AY_LEFT | AY_RIGHT | AY_CHIP_n
  29+ E000              AY_SELECT       equ %10011100       ; bit 7 and bits 4-2 must be set
  30+ E000              AY_LEFT         equ %01000000       ; left output on
  31+ E000              AY_RIGHT        equ %00100000       ; right output on
  32+ E000              AY_CHIP_1       equ %11
  33+ E000              AY_CHIP_2       equ %10
  34+ E000              AY_CHIP_3       equ %01
  35+ E000
  36+ E000              ; AY registers
  37+ E000              AY_TONE_A       equ 0               ; channel A tone period: fine, coarse (4)
  38+ E000              AY_TONE_B       equ 2
  39+ E000              AY_TONE_C       equ 4
  40+ E000              AY_NOISE        equ 6               ; noise period (5 bits)
  41+ E000              AY_MIXER        equ 7               ; tone / noise enable, 0 = on:
  42+ E000              AY_NO_TONE_A    equ %00000001       ;   bits 0-2 tone A-C off,
  43+ E000              AY_NO_TONE_B    equ %00000010
  44+ E000              AY_NO_TONE_C    equ %00000100
  45+ E000              AY_NO_NOISE_A   equ %00001000       ;   bits 3-5 noise A-C off
  46+ E000              AY_NO_NOISE_B   equ %00010000
  47+ E000              AY_NO_NOISE_C   equ %00100000
  48+ E000              AY_VOL_A        equ 8               ; amplitude 0-15 (bit 4: use envelope)
  49+ E000              AY_VOL_B        equ 9
  50+ E000              AY_VOL_C        equ 10
  51+ E000              AY_ENV_FINE     equ 11              ; envelope period
  52+ E000              AY_ENV_COARSE   equ 12
  53+ E000              AY_ENV_SHAPE    equ 13              ; envelope shape
  54+ E000              AY_REGS         equ 14
  55+ E000
  56+ E000              AY_ENV_ATTACK_HOLD equ %1101        ; envelope: rise once, then hold
  57+ E000              AY_MIXER_INIT   equ AY_NO_NOISE_A|AY_NO_NOISE_B|AY_NO_NOISE_C
  58+ E000                                                  ; tones on, noise off
  59+ E000
  60+ E000              ; The engines (AY 1): jet turbines, from n = |speed| (at least 1: the idle,
  61+ E000              ; at zero throttle, sounds as speed 1), 1..SPEED_MAX
  62+ E000              ENG_MIXER       equ AY_NO_TONE_A|AY_NO_NOISE_B|AY_NO_NOISE_C
  63+ E000                                                  ; A noise only, B and C tone only
  64+ E000              ENG_NOISE0      equ 29              ; A, the roar: noise period 29 - n*13/16
  65+ E000              ENG_NOISE_MUL   equ 13              ;   (29 at idle, a rumble; 7 flat out, a hiss)
  66+ E000              ENG_ROAR_VOL0   equ 4               ;   volume 4 + n*7/32 (4..10)
  67+ E000              ENG_WHINE0      equ 222             ; B, the turbine whine: period 222 - 3n
  68+ E000              ENG_WHINE_STEP  equ 3               ;   (~500 Hz at idle, ~790 Hz flat out)
  69+ E000              ENG_WHINE_VOL0  equ 2               ;   volume 2 + n*7/32 (2..8)
  70+ E000              ENG_VOL_MUL     equ 7               ; the volumes rise by n*7/32 (0..6)
  71+ E000              ENG_DETUNE      equ 2               ; C, the same whine 2 periods lower: the
  72+ E000              ENG_SHIMMER_VOL equ 2               ;   two beat (the turbines' shimmer), 2 quieter
  73+ E000              ; Sound effects (sfx_tick, interrupts.asm): their numbers, and the step codes
  74+ E000              SFX_MG          equ 1               ; the machine gun: one round
  75+ E000              SFX_LASER       equ 2               ; the pulse laser: one pulse (a zap)
  76+ E000              SFX_CHAIN       equ 3               ; the chain gun: one round
  77+ E000              SFX_ROCKET      equ 4               ; a rocket fired: a short woosh
  78+ E000              SFX_MISSILE     equ 5               ; a missile fired: a longer woosh
  79+ E000              SFX_BOFORS      equ 6               ; the Bofors / proximity cannon: one shell
  80+ E000              SFX_BOOM_ROCKET equ 7               ; explosions (AY 3, sfx2_req): a rocket's,
  81+ E000              SFX_BOOM_MISSILE equ 8              ;   a missile's,
  82+ E000              SFX_BOOM_BOFORS equ 9               ;   a Bofors shell's,
  83+ E000              SFX_BOOM_PROX   equ 10              ;   a proximity shell's
  84+ E000              SFX_SPLAT       equ 11              ; a zombie hit (AY 3 channel C, sfx3_req)
  85+ E000              SFX_LAST        equ 11              ; (sfx_table, sfx_data.asm)
  86+ E000              SFX_STEP        equ $FE             ; end of a step: the next, next interrupt
  87+ E000              SFX_END         equ $FF             ; end of the sound
  88+ E000              SFX_MIX_NOISE_A_TONE_B equ AY_NO_TONE_A|AY_NO_TONE_C|AY_NO_NOISE_B|AY_NO_NOISE_C
  89+ E000                                                  ; mixer: A noise only, B tone only
  90+ E000              SFX_MIX_TONE_AB equ AY_NO_TONE_C|AY_NO_NOISE_A|AY_NO_NOISE_B|AY_NO_NOISE_C
  91+ E000                                                  ; mixer: A and B tones only
  92+ E000              SFX_MIX_NOISE_A equ AY_NO_TONE_A|AY_NO_TONE_B|AY_NO_TONE_C|AY_NO_NOISE_B|AY_NO_NOISE_C
  93+ E000                                                  ; mixer: A noise only
  94+ E000              SFX_MIX_BOOM    equ AY_NO_TONE_A|AY_NO_NOISE_B|AY_NO_NOISE_C
  95+ E000                                                  ; AY 3's mixer: A noise, B tone (the
  96+ E000                                                  ; explosions), C tone (the splats)
  97+ E000                      ASSERT AY_REGS < SFX_STEP
  98+ E000
  99+ E000              ENG_FRAC        equ 16              ; the engines' speed (eng_cur) in 1/16 steps:
 100+ E000              ENG_SPIN_UP     equ 4               ;   up by at most 4/16 an interrupt (12.5 steps
 101+ E000              ENG_SPIN_DOWN   equ 2               ;   a second), down by 2/16 (6.25 a second)
 102+ E000                      ASSERT ENG_SPIN_UP > 0 && ENG_SPIN_DOWN > 0
 103+ E000                      ASSERT -SPEED_MIN <= SPEED_MAX
 104+ E000                      ASSERT ENG_WHINE0 - SPEED_MAX*ENG_WHINE_STEP > 0 && ENG_WHINE0 + ENG_DETUNE < 256
 105+ E000                      ASSERT ENG_NOISE0 < 32 && ENG_NOISE0 - (SPEED_MAX*ENG_NOISE_MUL)/16 >= 1
 106+ E000                      ASSERT ENG_ROAR_VOL0 + SPEED_MAX*ENG_VOL_MUL/32 <= 15 && ENG_WHINE_VOL0 + SPEED_MAX*ENG_VOL_MUL/32 <= 15
 107+ E000                      ASSERT SPEED_MAX*ENG_VOL_MUL < 256
 108+ E000                      ASSERT ENG_WHINE_VOL0 >= ENG_SHIMMER_VOL
 109+ E000
 110+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 111+ E000              ; Set up the sound devices: each AY chip selected with both outputs, every
 112+ E000              ; register 0 (silent: all volumes 0), then the mixer to tones only and the
 113+ E000              ; envelope to attack-and-hold.  AY 1 is left selected.
 114+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 115+ E000              init_audio:
 116+ E000 AF                   xor a                           ; the engines off first: the interrupt
 117+ E001 32 6B 3F             ld (eng_on),a                   ; leaves the AYs alone (interrupts.asm)
 118+ E004 32 6C 3F             ld (sfx_req),a                  ; and no sound effect asked for or
 119+ E007 32 6F 3F             ld (sfx2_req),a                 ; playing, on any voice
 120+ E00A 32 72 3F             ld (sfx3_req),a
 121+ E00D 67                   ld h,a
 122+ E00E 6F                   ld l,a
 123+ E00F 22 6D 3F             ld (sfx_ptr),hl
 124+ E012 22 70 3F             ld (sfx2_ptr),hl
 125+ E015 22 73 3F             ld (sfx3_ptr),hl
 126+ E018 3E 01                ld a,AY_CHIP_3
 127+ E01A CD 24 E0             call ay_init_chip
 128+ E01D 3E 02                ld a,AY_CHIP_2
 129+ E01F CD 24 E0             call ay_init_chip
 130+ E022 3E 03                ld a,AY_CHIP_1
 131+ E024                      ; fall through
 132+ E024
 133+ E024              ; A = chip (AY_CHIP_n): select it in stereo, clear it, mixer and envelope
 134+ E024              ay_init_chip:
 135+ E024 CD 5D E0             call ay_select
 136+ E027 16 0D                ld d,AY_REGS-1                  ; registers 13..0 = 0
 137+ E029 7A           .clr:   ld a,d
 138+ E02A 1E 00                ld e,0
 139+ E02C CD 65 E0             call ay_write
 140+ E02F 15                   dec d
 141+ E030 F2 29 E0             jp p,.clr
 142+ E033 3E 07                ld a,AY_MIXER
 143+ E035 1E 38                ld e,AY_MIXER_INIT
 144+ E037 CD 65 E0             call ay_write
 145+ E03A 3E 0D                ld a,AY_ENV_SHAPE
 146+ E03C 1E 0D                ld e,AY_ENV_ATTACK_HOLD
 147+ E03E C3 65 E0             jp ay_write
 148+ E041
 149+ E041              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 150+ E041              ; The VTOL's engines: jet turbines, not rotors, so a steady roar and whine
 151+ E041              ; rather than a chop.  AY 1, all three channels, kept for them.  They never
 152+ E041              ; stop in play: at zero throttle they idle, as at speed 1.  The interrupt
 153+ E041              ; routine plays them, 50 times a second (engine_tick, interrupts.asm): the
 154+ E041              ; sound's speed spools towards the VTOL's, so pitch and volume rise and fall
 155+ E041              ; smoothly.  (The Next's AYs run at 1.75 MHz: a tone of period P is
 156+ E041              ; 109375/P Hz.)
 157+ E041              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 158+ E041              ; Start of each level (next_level): AY 1's mixer for the engines, the sound's
 159+ E041              ; speed 0 (they spool up to the idle), and the interrupt playing them
 160+ E041              engine_start:
 161+ E041 AF                   xor a
 162+ E042 32 6B 3F             ld (eng_on),a                   ; (the interrupt off AY 1 meanwhile)
 163+ E045 21 00 00             ld hl,0
 164+ E048 22 76 3F             ld (eng_cur),hl
 165+ E04B 3E 03                ld a,AY_CHIP_1
 166+ E04D CD 5D E0             call ay_select
 167+ E050 3E 07                ld a,AY_MIXER
 168+ E052 1E 31                ld e,ENG_MIXER
 169+ E054 CD 65 E0             call ay_write
 170+ E057 3E 01                ld a,1
 171+ E059 32 6B 3F             ld (eng_on),a                   ; the interrupt plays them now
 172+ E05C C9                   ret
 173+ E05D
 174+ E05D              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 175+ E05D              ; A = chip (AY_CHIP_n): select it, with both outputs on
 176+ E05D              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 177+ E05D              ay_select:
 178+ E05D F6 FC                or AY_SELECT|AY_LEFT|AY_RIGHT
 179+ E05F 01 FD FF             ld bc,AY_CTRL_PORT
 180+ E062 ED 79                out (c),a
 181+ E064 C9                   ret
 182+ E065
 183+ E065              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 184+ E065              ; A = register, E = value: write it to the selected chip.  Keeps D, E, HL.
 185+ E065              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 186+ E065              ay_write:
 187+ E065 01 FD FF             ld bc,AY_CTRL_PORT
 188+ E068 ED 79                out (c),a
 189+ E06A 06 BF                ld b,high AY_DATA_PORT
 190+ E06C ED 59                out (c),e
 191+ E06E C9                   ret
 192+ E06F
# file closed: audio.asm
1699  E06F                      DISPLAY "Audio end: ",/A,$
1700  E06F
1701  E06F              ;==============================================================================
1702  E06F              ; Game logic pages: assembled at $C000, swapped into slot 6.  They may call
1703  E06F              ; the core page in slot 7 (main_loop has the keys there while they run).
1704  E06F              ;==============================================================================
1705  E06F                      MMU LOGIC_SLOT e, PAGE_PLAYER, LOGIC_ADDR
1706  C000                      ORG LOGIC_ADDR
1707  C000                      INCLUDE "player.asm"
# file opened: player.asm
   1+ C000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ C000              ; Z-POLICE - player page (8K page PAGE_PLAYER, runs at $C000 in slot 6)
   3+ C000              ;
   4+ C000              ; Keyboard input, movement along the heading, world-edge clamping and
   5+ C000              ; building collision.  Paged in by main_loop with nextreg LOGIC_MMU,
   6+ C000              ; PAGE_PLAYER, with the keys page (key_held) in slot 7.
   7+ C000              ; Uses the resident helpers get_sin, fmul and bld_height; camera state (cam_x/z/y, yaw, pitch, speed) is shared and
   8+ C000              ; stays in zpolice.asm.
   9+ C000              ;==============================================================================
  10+ C000
  11+ C000              ;==============================================================================
  12+ C000              ; Player input and movement
  13+ C000              ;==============================================================================
  14+ C000              update_player:
  15+ C000 AF                   xor a
  16+ C001 D3 FE                out ($FE),a                     ; border back to black
  17+ C003                      ; remember position in case we fly into something
  18+ C003 21 23 AE             ld hl,cam_x                     ; (and the heading: turning swings
  19+ C006 11 E7 C2             ld de,old_pos                   ; the VTOL's nose and tail)
  20+ C009 01 0A 00             ld bc,10                        ; cam_x(3) cam_z(3) cam_y(2) yaw(2)
  21+ C00C ED B0                ldir
  22+ C00E 3A 41 AE             ld a,(vtol_dead)
  23+ C011 B7                   or a
  24+ C012 28 07                jr z,.alive
  25+ C014 AF                   xor a
  26+ C015 32 BB AE             ld (boost_on),a                 ; (no boost in a wreck)
  27+ C018 C3 6C C2             jp vtol_wreck                   ; destroyed: out of control
  28+ C01B              .alive:
  29+ C01B                      ; (keys: kbd_scan has read them; actions from input.asm)
  30+ C01B                      ; --- boost (zpolice.asm: BOOST_*): while on, the speed keys and the
  31+ C01B                      ; air brake don't change the speed, and each frame burns a fuel
  32+ C01B 3A BB AE             ld a,(boost_on)
  33+ C01E B7                   or a
  34+ C01F 28 25                jr z,.nb
  35+ C021 3A 2F AE             ld a,(speed)
  36+ C024 FE 38                cp BOOST_SPEED
  37+ C026 20 13                jr nz,.bend                     ; stopped meanwhile (a collision)
  38+ C028 3E 13                ld a,K_BOOST
  39+ C02A CD CF E0             call key_pressed
  40+ C02D 20 06                jr nz,.boff                     ; pressed again: off
  41+ C02F 21 BA AE             ld hl,boost_fuel
  42+ C032 35                   dec (hl)
  43+ C033 20 0A                jr nz,.bkeep                    ; still burning
  44+ C035 3A BC AE     .boff:  ld a,(boost_spd)                ; off (or out of fuel): the speed it
  45+ C038 32 2F AE             ld (speed),a                    ; had before
  46+ C03B AF           .bend:  xor a
  47+ C03C 32 BB AE             ld (boost_on),a
  48+ C03F AF           .bkeep: xor a                           ; (no air brake this frame)
  49+ C040 32 F2 C2             ld (ab_on),a
  50+ C043 C3 DE C0             jp .nw
  51+ C046 3E 13        .nb:    ld a,K_BOOST                    ; off: pressed to start it?
  52+ C048 CD CF E0             call key_pressed
  53+ C04B 28 26                jr z,.nbs
  54+ C04D 3A B9 AE             ld a,(boost_fitted)
  55+ C050 B7                   or a
  56+ C051 28 20                jr z,.nbs                       ; no booster
  57+ C053 3A BA AE             ld a,(boost_fuel)
  58+ C056 B7                   or a
  59+ C057 28 1A                jr z,.nbs                       ; no fuel
  60+ C059 3A 2F AE             ld a,(speed)
  61+ C05C CB 7F                bit 7,a
  62+ C05E 20 13                jr nz,.nbs                      ; reversing
  63+ C060 FE 0E                cp BOOST_MIN
  64+ C062 38 0F                jr c,.nbs                       ; under half speed
  65+ C064 32 BC AE             ld (boost_spd),a
  66+ C067 3E 38                ld a,BOOST_SPEED                ; on: twice the top speed at once
  67+ C069 32 2F AE             ld (speed),a
  68+ C06C 3E 01                ld a,1
  69+ C06E 32 BB AE             ld (boost_on),a
  70+ C071 18 CC                jr .bkeep
  71+ C073              .nbs:
  72+ C073                      ; --- speed ---
  73+ C073 3E 00                ld a,K_SPEED_UP                 ; (by SPEED_STEP, 1)
  74+ C075 CD BE E0             call key_held
  75+ C078 28 0B                jr z,.nq
  76+ C07A 3A 2F AE             ld a,(speed)
  77+ C07D FE 1C                cp SPEED_MAX
  78+ C07F 28 04                jr z,.nq
  79+ C081 3C                   inc a
  80+ C082 32 2F AE             ld (speed),a
  81+ C085 3E 01        .nq:    ld a,K_SPEED_DN
  82+ C087 CD BE E0             call key_held
  83+ C08A 28 0B                jr z,.na
  84+ C08C 3A 2F AE             ld a,(speed)
  85+ C08F FE FA                cp (SPEED_MIN) & $FF
  86+ C091 28 04                jr z,.na
  87+ C093 3D                   dec a
  88+ C094 32 2F AE             ld (speed),a
  89+ C097              .na:    ; --- air brake: while held, the speed falls AIRBRAKE_STEP a frame
  90+ C097                      ; towards 0, going forwards or backwards.  Each braking frame tips the
  91+ C097                      ; nose one pitch step, as a nose key press does: up when braking
  92+ C097                      ; forward motion, down when braking reverse.  The pitch damper waits
  93+ C097                      ; while it brakes (ab_on), as it does while a nose key is held.
  94+ C097 AF                   xor a
  95+ C098 32 F2 C2             ld (ab_on),a
  96+ C09B 3E 02                ld a,K_AIRBRAKE
  97+ C09D CD BE E0             call key_held
  98+ C0A0 28 3C                jr z,.nw
  99+ C0A2 3A 2F AE             ld a,(speed)
 100+ C0A5 B7                   or a
 101+ C0A6 28 36                jr z,.nw                        ; stopped: nothing to brake
 102+ C0A8 32 F2 C2             ld (ab_on),a                    ; (non-zero: braking this frame)
 103+ C0AB FA C7 C0             jp m,.rev
 104+ C0AE D6 02                sub AIRBRAKE_STEP               ; forwards: slower, not past 0
 105+ C0B0 30 01                jr nc,.fwd
 106+ C0B2 AF                   xor a
 107+ C0B3 32 2F AE     .fwd:   ld (speed),a
 108+ C0B6 AF                   xor a
 109+ C0B7 32 2D AE             ld (pitch_frac),a               ; whole steps, as the keys
 110+ C0BA 3A 2E AE             ld a,(pitch)
 111+ C0BD FE 18                cp PMAX
 112+ C0BF 28 1D                jr z,.nw
 113+ C0C1 3C                   inc a                           ; nose up
 114+ C0C2 32 2E AE             ld (pitch),a
 115+ C0C5 18 17                jr .nw
 116+ C0C7 C6 02        .rev:   add a,AIRBRAKE_STEP             ; backwards: towards 0, not past it
 117+ C0C9 30 01                jr nc,.bk
 118+ C0CB AF                   xor a
 119+ C0CC 32 2F AE     .bk:    ld (speed),a
 120+ C0CF AF                   xor a
 121+ C0D0 32 2D AE             ld (pitch_frac),a
 122+ C0D3 3A 2E AE             ld a,(pitch)
 123+ C0D6 FE E8                cp (-PMAX) & $FF
 124+ C0D8 28 04                jr z,.nw
 125+ C0DA 3D                   dec a                           ; nose down
 126+ C0DB 32 2E AE             ld (pitch),a
 127+ C0DE              .nw:
 128+ C0DE                      ; --- yaw: YAW_RATE a frame, but YAW_FINE on the frame a turn key is
 129+ C0DE                      ; first pressed (a tap turns 1 degree: fine control)
 130+ C0DE 3E 03                ld a,K_LEFT
 131+ C0E0 CD BE E0             call key_held
 132+ C0E3 28 16                jr z,.ns
 133+ C0E5 11 80 01             ld de,YAW_RATE
 134+ C0E8 3E 03                ld a,K_LEFT
 135+ C0EA CD CF E0             call key_pressed
 136+ C0ED 28 03                jr z,.lr
 137+ C0EF 11 B6 00             ld de,YAW_FINE
 138+ C0F2 2A 2B AE     .lr:    ld hl,(yaw_frac)                ; 8.8 heading
 139+ C0F5 B7                   or a
 140+ C0F6 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 141+ C0F8 22 2B AE             ld (yaw_frac),hl
 142+ C0FB 3E 04        .ns:    ld a,K_RIGHT
 143+ C0FD CD BE E0             call key_held
 144+ C100 28 14                jr z,.nf
 145+ C102 11 80 01             ld de,YAW_RATE
 146+ C105 3E 04                ld a,K_RIGHT
 147+ C107 CD CF E0             call key_pressed
 148+ C10A 28 03                jr z,.rr
 149+ C10C 11 B6 00             ld de,YAW_FINE
 150+ C10F 2A 2B AE     .rr:    ld hl,(yaw_frac)
 151+ C112 19                   add hl,de
 152+ C113 22 2B AE             ld (yaw_frac),hl
 153+ C116              .nf:
 154+ C116                      ; --- pitch ---
 155+ C116 3E 08                ld a,K_NOSE_DN
 156+ C118 CD BE E0             call key_held
 157+ C11B 28 0F                jr z,.ng
 158+ C11D AF                   xor a
 159+ C11E 32 2D AE             ld (pitch_frac),a               ; keys move in whole steps
 160+ C121 3A 2E AE             ld a,(pitch)
 161+ C124 FE E8                cp (-PMAX) & $FF
 162+ C126 28 04                jr z,.ng
 163+ C128 3D                   dec a
 164+ C129 32 2E AE             ld (pitch),a
 165+ C12C 3E 07        .ng:    ld a,K_NOSE_UP
 166+ C12E CD BE E0             call key_held
 167+ C131 28 0F                jr z,.nt
 168+ C133 AF                   xor a
 169+ C134 32 2D AE             ld (pitch_frac),a
 170+ C137 3A 2E AE             ld a,(pitch)
 171+ C13A FE 18                cp PMAX
 172+ C13C 28 04                jr z,.nt
 173+ C13E 3C                   inc a
 174+ C13F 32 2E AE             ld (pitch),a
 175+ C142              .nt:    ; --- pitch damper: ease back to level when the pitch keys are released
 176+ C142                      ; (and the air brake isn't tipping the nose)
 177+ C142 3A F2 C2             ld a,(ab_on)
 178+ C145 B7                   or a
 179+ C146 20 1B                jr nz,.held
 180+ C148 3E 07                ld a,K_NOSE_UP
 181+ C14A CD BE E0             call key_held
 182+ C14D 20 14                jr nz,.held
 183+ C14F 3E 08                ld a,K_NOSE_DN
 184+ C151 CD BE E0             call key_held
 185+ C154 20 0D                jr nz,.held
 186+ C156 21 F1 C2             ld hl,pd_count
 187+ C159 35                   dec (hl)
 188+ C15A 20 0C                jr nz,.pdx
 189+ C15C 36 01                ld (hl),PDAMP_FRAMES
 190+ C15E CD BA C2             call pitch_damp
 191+ C161 18 05                jr .pdx
 192+ C163 3E 01        .held:  ld a,PDAMP_FRAMES               ; a full interval after letting go
 193+ C165 32 F1 C2             ld (pd_count),a
 194+ C168              .pdx:
 195+ C168                      ; --- height ---
 196+ C168 3E 05                ld a,K_CLIMB
 197+ C16A CD BE E0             call key_held
 198+ C16D 28 10                jr z,.ne
 199+ C16F 2A 29 AE             ld hl,(cam_y)
 200+ C172 11 04 00             ld de,CLIMB_RATE
 201+ C175 19                   add hl,de
 202+ C176 11 C0 00             ld de,MAX_Y
 203+ C179 CD 44 C2             call min_hl_de
 204+ C17C 22 29 AE             ld (cam_y),hl
 205+ C17F 3E 06        .ne:    ld a,K_DESCEND
 206+ C181 CD BE E0             call key_held
 207+ C184 28 19                jr z,.nd
 208+ C186 2A 29 AE             ld hl,(cam_y)
 209+ C189 11 FC FF             ld de,-CLIMB_RATE
 210+ C18C 19                   add hl,de
 211+ C18D 11 08 00             ld de,MIN_Y
 212+ C190 CD 4C C2             call max_hl_de
 213+ C193 E5                   push hl                         ; and not below the floor under the
 214+ C194 CD B9 A9             call floor_y                    ; VTOL: it lands on the road or the
 215+ C197 EB                   ex de,hl                        ; highest roof under it (no collision)
 216+ C198 E1                   pop hl
 217+ C199 CD 4C C2             call max_hl_de
 218+ C19C 22 29 AE             ld (cam_y),hl
 219+ C19F              .nd:
 220+ C19F                      ; --- move along heading: pos += speed * (sin, cos) ---
 221+ C19F 3A 2F AE             ld a,(speed)
 222+ C1A2 B7                   or a
 223+ C1A3 28 30                jr z,.moved
 224+ C1A5 3A 2C AE             ld a,(yaw)
 225+ C1A8 CD 33 AD             call get_sin
 226+ C1AB EB                   ex de,hl
 227+ C1AC 3A 2F AE             ld a,(speed)
 228+ C1AF 67                   ld h,a
 229+ C1B0 2E 00                ld l,0
 230+ C1B2 CD EA AD             call fmul                       ; HL = speed*sin in 1/256 units
 231+ C1B5 DD 21 23 AE          ld ix,cam_x
 232+ C1B9 CD FD C1             call add_pos
 233+ C1BC 3A 2C AE             ld a,(yaw)
 234+ C1BF C6 40                add a,64
 235+ C1C1 CD 33 AD             call get_sin
 236+ C1C4 EB                   ex de,hl
 237+ C1C5 3A 2F AE             ld a,(speed)
 238+ C1C8 67                   ld h,a
 239+ C1C9 2E 00                ld l,0
 240+ C1CB CD EA AD             call fmul
 241+ C1CE DD 21 26 AE          ld ix,cam_z
 242+ C1D2 CD FD C1             call add_pos
 243+ C1D5              .moved:
 244+ C1D5 CD 54 C2             call check_collision
 245+ C1D8 C8                   ret z
 246+ C1D9                      ; crashed - restore and stop, taking |speed| / 4 (a 2-bit shift
 247+ C1D9                      ; right, rounded down) as damage
 248+ C1D9 21 E7 C2             ld hl,old_pos
 249+ C1DC 11 23 AE             ld de,cam_x
 250+ C1DF 01 0A 00             ld bc,10
 251+ C1E2 ED B0                ldir
 252+ C1E4 3A 2F AE             ld a,(speed)
 253+ C1E7 CB 7F                bit 7,a
 254+ C1E9 28 02                jr z,.dmg
 255+ C1EB ED 44                neg                             ; (reversing into it)
 256+ C1ED CB 3F        .dmg:   srl a
 257+ C1EF CB 3F                srl a
 258+ C1F1 CD A5 A9             call vtol_damage                ; (zpolice.asm)
 259+ C1F4 AF                   xor a
 260+ C1F5 32 2F AE             ld (speed),a
 261+ C1F8 3E 03                ld a,C_WARN
 262+ C1FA D3 FE                out ($FE),a                     ; red border flash
 263+ C1FC C9                   ret
 264+ C1FD
 265+ C1FD              ; IX -> 24-bit position (frac, lo, hi). HL = signed delta in 1/256 units.
 266+ C1FD              ; Clamps the integer part to WORLD_MIN..WORLD_MAX.
 267+ C1FD              add_pos:
 268+ C1FD 7C                   ld a,h
 269+ C1FE 17                   rla
 270+ C1FF 9F                   sbc a,a
 271+ C200 4F                   ld c,a                          ; sign extension
 272+ C201 DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+0)
 273+ C204 85                   add a,l
 274+ C205 DD 77 00             ld (ix+0),a
 275+ C208 DD 7E 01             ld a,(ix+1)
 276+ C20B 8C                   adc a,h
 277+ C20C DD 77 01             ld (ix+1),a
 278+ C20F DD 7E 02             ld a,(ix+2)
 279+ C212 89                   adc a,c
 280+ C213 DD 77 02             ld (ix+2),a
 281+ C216 DD 6E 01             ld l,(ix+1)
 282+ C219 DD 66 02             ld h,(ix+2)
 283+ C21C CB 7C                bit 7,h
 284+ C21E 20 1B                jr nz,.lo
 285+ C220 11 40 00             ld de,WORLD_MIN
 286+ C223 B7                   or a
 287+ C224 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 288+ C226 38 13                jr c,.lo
 289+ C228 DD 6E 01             ld l,(ix+1)
 290+ C22B DD 66 02             ld h,(ix+2)
 291+ C22E 11 C0 3F             ld de,WORLD_MAX
 292+ C231 CD 44 C2             call min_hl_de
 293+ C234 DD 75 01             ld (ix+1),l
 294+ C237 DD 74 02             ld (ix+2),h
 295+ C23A C9                   ret
 296+ C23B DD 36 01 40  .lo:    ld (ix+1),WORLD_MIN & $FF
 297+ C23F DD 36 02 00          ld (ix+2),WORLD_MIN >> 8
 298+ C243 C9                   ret
 299+ C244
 300+ C244              ; Unsigned 16-bit clamps
 301+ C244              min_hl_de:
 302+ C244 E5                   push hl
 303+ C245 B7                   or a
 304+ C246 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 305+ C248 E1                   pop hl
 306+ C249 D8                   ret c
 307+ C24A EB                   ex de,hl
 308+ C24B C9                   ret
 309+ C24C
 310+ C24C              max_hl_de:
 311+ C24C E5                   push hl
 312+ C24D B7                   or a
 313+ C24E ED 52                sbc hl,de
 314+ C250 E1                   pop hl
 315+ C251 D0                   ret nc
 316+ C252 EB                   ex de,hl
 317+ C253 C9                   ret
 318+ C254
 319+ C254              ; Returns NZ if the VTOL hits a building: its footprint (10 m x 3 m, turned
 320+ C254              ; to the heading; vtol_ground) is over a roof less than COLL_H below it.
 321+ C254              check_collision:
 322+ C254 CD DF AB             call vtol_ground                ; HL = highest ground under it
 323+ C257 C8                   ret z                           ; all road
 324+ C258 11 0C 00             ld de,COLL_H
 325+ C25B 19                   add hl,de
 326+ C25C ED 5B 29 AE          ld de,(cam_y)
 327+ C260 B7                   or a
 328+ C261 ED 52                sbc hl,de                       ; height+M - camY
 329+ C263 38 05                jr c,.no
 330+ C265 28 03                jr z,.no
 331+ C267 F6 01                or 1                            ; NZ: collision
 332+ C269 C9                   ret
 333+ C26A AF           .no:    xor a
 334+ C26B C9                   ret
 335+ C26C
 336+ C26C              ; The VTOL has been destroyed (vtol_dead): the controls do nothing.  It
 337+ C26C              ; spins DEAD_SPIN a frame and falls, a unit a frame faster each frame (up
 338+ C26C              ; to DEAD_FALL_MAX), until it hits the ground under it (floor_y: the road,
 339+ C26C              ; or the highest roof under its footprint).  Once down it lies still for
 340+ C26C              ; DEAD_PAUSE frames, then sets game_over (main_loop: the GAME OVER screen,
 341+ C26C              ; then the main menu).
 342+ C26C              vtol_wreck:
 343+ C26C AF                   xor a
 344+ C26D 32 2F AE             ld (speed),a
 345+ C270 3A 42 AE             ld a,(dead_down)
 346+ C273 B7                   or a
 347+ C274 20 39                jr nz,.down
 348+ C276 2A 2B AE             ld hl,(yaw_frac)                ; spin
 349+ C279 11 00 08             ld de,DEAD_SPIN
 350+ C27C 19                   add hl,de
 351+ C27D 22 2B AE             ld (yaw_frac),hl
 352+ C280 3A 43 AE             ld a,(dead_vy)                  ; fall, faster each frame
 353+ C283 FE 08                cp DEAD_FALL_MAX
 354+ C285 30 04                jr nc,.vy
 355+ C287 3C                   inc a
 356+ C288 32 43 AE             ld (dead_vy),a
 357+ C28B 5F           .vy:    ld e,a
 358+ C28C 16 00                ld d,0
 359+ C28E 2A 29 AE             ld hl,(cam_y)
 360+ C291 B7                   or a
 361+ C292 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 362+ C294 30 03                jr nc,.y
 363+ C296 21 00 00             ld hl,0
 364+ C299 E5           .y:     push hl
 365+ C29A CD B9 A9             call floor_y                    ; HL = the ground under it
 366+ C29D EB                   ex de,hl
 367+ C29E E1                   pop hl
 368+ C29F B7                   or a
 369+ C2A0 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 370+ C2A2 19                   add hl,de
 371+ C2A3 30 06                jr nc,.set                      ; still above it
 372+ C2A5 EB                   ex de,hl                        ; hit the ground: on it
 373+ C2A6 3E 01                ld a,1
 374+ C2A8 32 42 AE             ld (dead_down),a
 375+ C2AB 22 29 AE     .set:   ld (cam_y),hl
 376+ C2AE C9                   ret
 377+ C2AF 21 44 AE     .down:  ld hl,dead_wait                 ; on the ground: the pause, then
 378+ C2B2 35                   dec (hl)                        ; GAME OVER
 379+ C2B3 C0                   ret nz
 380+ C2B4 3E 01                ld a,1
 381+ C2B6 32 47 AE             ld (game_over),a
 382+ C2B9 C9                   ret
 383+ C2BA
 384+ C2BA              ; One damping step: move the signed 8.8 pitch (pitch_frac/pitch) towards 0
 385+ C2BA              ; by max(PDAMP_MIN, |whole pitch| * PDAMP_K), stopping exactly at 0.
 386+ C2BA              ; Calculated rather than looked up: the Z80N MUL makes it as quick as a
 387+ C2BA              ; table, and the curve stays editable through the two equates.
 388+ C2BA              pitch_damp:
 389+ C2BA 2A 2D AE             ld hl,(pitch_frac)
 390+ C2BD 7C                   ld a,h
 391+ C2BE B5                   or l
 392+ C2BF C8                   ret z                           ; already level
 393+ C2C0 7C                   ld a,h
 394+ C2C1 F5                   push af                         ; sign
 395+ C2C2 CD E5 AD             call abs_hl
 396+ C2C5 54                   ld d,h                          ; whole part of |pitch|
 397+ C2C6 1E 00                ld e,PDAMP_K
 398+ C2C8 ED 30                mul d,e                         ; DE = step
 399+ C2CA 7A                   ld a,d
 400+ C2CB B7                   or a
 401+ C2CC 20 08                jr nz,.sub
 402+ C2CE 7B                   ld a,e
 403+ C2CF FE 80                cp PDAMP_MIN
 404+ C2D1 30 03                jr nc,.sub
 405+ C2D3 11 80 00             ld de,PDAMP_MIN
 406+ C2D6 B7           .sub:   or a
 407+ C2D7 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 408+ C2D9 30 03                jr nc,.sgn
 409+ C2DB 21 00 00             ld hl,0                         ; would pass level: stop at 0
 410+ C2DE F1           .sgn:   pop af
 411+ C2DF 17                   rla                             ; carry = was nose down
 412+ C2E0 DC DE AD             call c,neg_hl
 413+ C2E3 22 2D AE             ld (pitch_frac),hl
 414+ C2E6 C9                   ret
 415+ C2E7
 416+ C2E7              ;==============================================================================
 417+ C2E7              ; Player page data
 418+ C2E7              ;==============================================================================
 419+ C2E7 00 00 00...  old_pos:    ds 10                       ; cam_x, cam_z, cam_y, yaw before the move
 420+ C2F1 01           pd_count:   db PDAMP_FRAMES             ; frames to the next damping step
 421+ C2F2 00           ab_on:      db 0                        ; non-zero: the air brake acted this frame
 422+ C2F3

1710  C2F3                      MMU LOGIC_SLOT e, PAGE_WEAPONS, LOGIC_ADDR

# file opened: weapons.asm
   1+ C000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ C000              ; Z-POLICE - weapons page (8K page PAGE_WEAPONS, runs at $C000 in slot 6)
   3+ C000              ;
   4+ C000              ; Player weapons.  Paged in by main_loop with nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_WEAPONS
   5+ C000              ; (the keys page in slot 7) after
   6+ C000              ; the frame is set up (it uses sin_y/cos_y/sin_p/cos_p) and the zombies have
   7+ C000              ; moved.  Bullet state lives in the resident `bullets` table so that the city
   8+ C000              ; page can draw the tracers (draw_bullets).
   9+ C000              ;
  10+ C000              ; Keys 1-5 select the weapon slot that SPACE fires (wpn_sel: 1 = the centre
  11+ C000              ; slot, 2-5 = the wing pylons).  An empty slot isn't selected: the HUD
  12+ C000              ; flashes it instead (wflash_slot / wflash_t).  What is fitted where is set
  13+ C000              ; on the load-out screen between levels (loadout.asm).
  14+ C000              ;
  15+ C000              ; Machine gun (WPN_GUN, the centre slot): hold SPACE to fire one round every
  16+ C000              ; GUN_INTERVAL frames.  Rounds leave the ship's centre line 0.5 m below the
  17+ C000              ; camera and fly straight along the view direction at 500 m/s until they hit
  18+ C000              ; something.
  19+ C000              ;
  20+ C000              ; Collision, every frame for every round in flight: the path from its last
  21+ C000              ; position to its new one (BULLET_STEP long) is walked cell by cell through
  22+ C000              ; the city grid, in order (a 2D DDA on the horizontal distance s along the
  23+ C000              ; path).  The first thing met ends the path:
  24+ C000              ;   * a building cell: hit where the path enters the cell if it is below the
  25+ C000              ;     roof there (a wall), otherwise where it drops below the roof (the roof);
  26+ C000              ;   * a street cell marked as holding zombies (CELL_ZOMBIE): those in it, each a box
  27+ C000              ;     0.5 m wide and 2 m tall, nearest along the path first;
  28+ C000              ;   * the ground, where the path reaches height 0;
  29+ C000              ;   * leaving the GRID x GRID map: the round is gone.
  30+ C000              ; A round that hits is drawn once at the impact point, then freed.  A zombie
  31+ C000              ; that is hit loses B_DMG hit points; at 0 it is killed (its map blip
  32+ C000              ; removed, its record made inactive).  A rocket also bursts (rocket_blast).
  33+ C000              ;
  34+ C000              ; Rocket pod (WPN_ROCKETS, a wing pylon, ROCKET_SHOTS rockets): rockets are
  35+ C000              ; rounds too (B_KIND = BK_ROCKET), at 150 m/s and drawn as a short white
  36+ C000              ; streak with a yellow trail.  Wherever one stops (a building, the ground,
  37+ C000              ; a zombie) rocket_blast does BLAST_DMG to every zombie, human and base tile
  38+ C000              ; within BLAST_R on each axis, and sprays BLAST_PARTS orange particles.
  39+ C000              ;
  40+ C000              ; Bofors cannon (WPN_BOFORS, a wing pylon, BOFORS_SHOTS shells): the machine
  41+ C000              ; gun's rounds (B_KIND = BK_BOFORS, drawn as tracers) one every
  42+ C000              ; BOFORS_INTERVAL frames; each bursts where it stops: BOFORS_DMG within
  43+ C000              ; BOFORS_R (3 m) on each axis, BOFORS_PARTS particles.
  44+ C000              ;
  45+ C000              ; Proximity cannon (WPN_PROX, a wing pylon, PROX_SHOTS shells): the Bofors
  46+ C000              ; cannon (B_KIND = BK_PROX), its shells also bursting when they pass within
  47+ C000              ; PROX_R (2 m) of a monster (b_pr widens the monster hit boxes,
  48+ C000              ; cell_zombies); the burst does PROX_DMG within PROX_R on each axis.
  49+ C000              ;
  50+ C000              ; Missile pod (WPN_MISSILE, a wing pylon, MISSILE_SHOTS missiles): as the
  51+ C000              ; rockets (B_KIND = BK_MISSILE, drawn the same) at half the speed; the burst
  52+ C000              ; does MISSILE_DMG within MISSILE_R (1.0 tile) on each axis, and sprays
  53+ C000              ; MISSILE_PARTS particles.
  54+ C000              ;
  55+ C000              ; Damage: each round carries its damage (B_DMG): machine gun GUN_DMG, chain
  56+ C000              ; gun CHAIN_DMG, the laser LASER_DMG a beam frame, a rocket none directly
  57+ C000              ; (only its blast).  A zombie dies when its hit points (+18) run out.
  58+ C000              ;
  59+ C000              ; Positions are world units (1 tile = 20 m = 256 units, 1 m = 12.8).  Along the path,
  60+ C000              ; x = x0 + s*sinY, z = z0 + s*cosY and y = y0 + s*slope, with sinY/cosY the
  61+ C000              ; S7.8 heading at firing and slope = tan(pitch) in S7.8.
  62+ C000              ;==============================================================================
  63+ C000
  64+ C000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  65+ C000              ; Per frame: fire, then move every round in flight
  66+ C000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  67+ C000              update_weapons:
  68+ C000 CD AD C5             call part_update                ; age and move the burst particles
  69+ C003 3A 41 AE             ld a,(vtol_dead)
  70+ C006 B7                   or a
  71+ C007 28 05                jr z,.alive
  72+ C009 CD F4 C3             call las_reset                  ; destroyed: no firing, no beam
  73+ C00C 18 23                jr .move
  74+ C00E              .alive:
  75+ C00E                  IF DEBUG_KILLALL
  76+ C00E CD 6F C3             call debug_killall              ; (debug: keys 0 and 9)
  77+ C011 CD 4B C3             call debug_gun_cycle            ; (debug: key 8)
  78+ C014                  ENDIF
  79+ C014 CD 19 C3             call wpn_select                 ; (slots can be chosen when landed)
  80+ C017 CD 76 C2             call gun_aim                    ; and the centre gun tilted
  81+ C01A CD CA AB             call vtol_landed
  82+ C01D 20 08                jr nz,.fire
  83+ C01F CD F4 C3             call las_reset                  ; landed: no firing, no beam, until
  84+ C022 CD 09 C4             call pad_service                ; it takes off; on the pad: repairs
  85+ C025 18 0A                jr .move                        ; and reloads
  86+ C027 AF           .fire:  xor a
  87+ C028 32 99 CC             ld (svc_on),a                   ; (service starts afresh next landing)
  88+ C02B CD 54 C0             call gun_fire
  89+ C02E CD 8D C4             call laser_update
  90+ C031 DD 21 E5 AE  .move:  ld ix,bullets
  91+ C035 06 18                ld b,NBULLETS
  92+ C037 C5           .lp:    push bc
  93+ C038 DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+B_STATE)
  94+ C03B B7                   or a
  95+ C03C 28 0D                jr z,.nx
  96+ C03E FE 02                cp 2
  97+ C040 20 06                jr nz,.fly
  98+ C042 DD 36 00 00          ld (ix+B_STATE),0               ; its impact was shown last frame
  99+ C046 18 03                jr .nx
 100+ C048 CD 1C C6     .fly:   call bullet_step
 101+ C04B 11 1B 00     .nx:    ld de,BREC
 102+ C04E DD 19                add ix,de
 103+ C050 C1                   pop bc
 104+ C051 10 E4                djnz .lp
 105+ C053 C9                   ret
 106+ C054
 107+ C054              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 108+ C054              ; Machine gun / chain gun: SPACE held with one of them in the centre slot,
 109+ C054              ; ammo, and a free round
 110+ C054              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 111+ C054              gun_fire:
 112+ C054 21 98 CC             ld hl,gun_wait
 113+ C057 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 114+ C058 B7                   or a
 115+ C059 28 02                jr z,.ready
 116+ C05B 35                   dec (hl)                        ; still cycling
 117+ C05C C9                   ret
 118+ C05D 3E 09        .ready: ld a,K_FIRE
 119+ C05F CD BE E0             call key_held
 120+ C062 C8                   ret z                           ; fire not pressed
 121+ C063 CD FF C3             call sel_slot
 122+ C066 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; the selected slot's weapon
 123+ C067 FE 03                cp WPN_CHAIN
 124+ C069 CA FE C0             jp z,.chain
 125+ C06C FE 04                cp WPN_ROCKETS
 126+ C06E 28 2C                jr z,.rocket
 127+ C070 FE 05                cp WPN_MISSILE
 128+ C072 28 45                jr z,.missile
 129+ C074 FE 07                cp WPN_BOFORS
 130+ C076 28 62                jr z,.bofors
 131+ C078 FE 08                cp WPN_PROX
 132+ C07A 28 5A                jr z,.prox
 133+ C07C FE 01                cp WPN_GUN
 134+ C07E C0                   ret nz
 135+ C07F CD A3 C1             call free_round                 ; machine gun: one round
 136+ C082 D8                   ret c
 137+ C083 CD 95 C1             call take_ammo
 138+ C086 D8                   ret c
 139+ C087 3E 03                ld a,GUN_INTERVAL-1
 140+ C089 32 98 CC             ld (gun_wait),a
 141+ C08C 21 C9 01             ld hl,BULLET_STEP
 142+ C08F CD B7 C1             call spawn_round
 143+ C092 DD 36 19 04          ld (ix+B_DMG),GUN_DMG
 144+ C096 3E 01                ld a,SFX_MG                     ; its sound (the interrupt plays it)
 145+ C098 32 6C 3F             ld (sfx_req),a
 146+ C09B C9                   ret
 147+ C09C              .rocket:
 148+ C09C CD A3 C1             call free_round                 ; rocket pod: one rocket
 149+ C09F D8                   ret c
 150+ C0A0 CD 95 C1             call take_ammo
 151+ C0A3 D8                   ret c
 152+ C0A4 3E 06                ld a,ROCKET_INTERVAL-1
 153+ C0A6 32 98 CC             ld (gun_wait),a
 154+ C0A9 21 89 00             ld hl,ROCKET_STEP
 155+ C0AC CD B7 C1             call spawn_round
 156+ C0AF DD 36 1A 01          ld (ix+B_KIND),BK_ROCKET        ; no direct damage: its blast
 157+ C0B3 3E 04                ld a,SFX_ROCKET                 ; its woosh (the interrupt plays it)
 158+ C0B5 32 6C 3F             ld (sfx_req),a
 159+ C0B8 C9                   ret
 160+ C0B9              .missile:
 161+ C0B9 CD A3 C1             call free_round                 ; missile pod: one missile
 162+ C0BC D8                   ret c
 163+ C0BD CD 95 C1             call take_ammo
 164+ C0C0 D8                   ret c
 165+ C0C1 3E 06                ld a,MISSILE_INTERVAL-1
 166+ C0C3 32 98 CC             ld (gun_wait),a
 167+ C0C6 21 44 00             ld hl,MISSILE_STEP
 168+ C0C9 CD B7 C1             call spawn_round
 169+ C0CC DD 36 1A 02          ld (ix+B_KIND),BK_MISSILE       ; no direct damage: its blast
 170+ C0D0 3E 05                ld a,SFX_MISSILE                ; its woosh
 171+ C0D2 32 6C 3F             ld (sfx_req),a
 172+ C0D5 C9                   ret

 175+ C0DA              .bofors:

 188+ C0F8 3E 06                ld a,SFX_BOFORS                 ; its sound (the proximity cannon's

 191+ C0FE                      ASSERT PROX_INTERVAL == BOFORS_INTERVAL
 192+ C0FE              .chain: ; chain gun: one round every CHAIN_INTERVAL frames

 200+ C10D 32 6C 3F             ld (sfx_req),a
 201+ C110                      ; fall through
 202+ C110
 203+ C110              ; A chain gun round from IX: the view direction turned by a random angle
 204+ C110              ; of -CHAIN_DEV..+CHAIN_DEV steps (1/256 rad) sideways and up/down
 205+ C110              chain_round:
