 208+ C116 DD 36 19 02          ld (ix+B_DMG),CHAIN_DMG
 209+ C11A CD B5 2C             call zrand                      ; (zombie page)
 210+ C11D E5                   push hl
 211+ C11E 7D                   ld a,l
 212+ C11F CD 86 C1             call dev_n                      ; sideways: rotate (sin, cos) by d/256
 213+ C122 B7                   or a
 214+ C123 28 3F                jr z,.v
 215+ C125 67                   ld h,a
 216+ C126 2E 00                ld l,0
 217+ C128 22 A8 CC             ld (cg_d),hl                    ; d * 256
 218+ C12B DD 5E 09             ld e,(ix+B_COS)                 ; sin += round(d * cos / 256)
 219+ C12E DD 56 0A             ld d,(ix+B_COS+1)
 220+ C131 CD 79 C1             call dmul
 221+ C134 22 AA CC             ld (cg_s),hl
 222+ C137 2A A8 CC             ld hl,(cg_d)                    ; cos -= round(d * sin / 256)
 223+ C13A DD 5E 07             ld e,(ix+B_SIN)
 224+ C13D DD 56 08             ld d,(ix+B_SIN+1)
 225+ C140 CD 79 C1             call dmul
 226+ C143 EB                   ex de,hl
 227+ C144 DD 6E 09             ld l,(ix+B_COS)
 228+ C147 DD 66 0A             ld h,(ix+B_COS+1)
 229+ C14A B7                   or a
 230+ C14B ED 52                sbc hl,de
 231+ C14D DD 75 09             ld (ix+B_COS),l
 232+ C150 DD 74 0A             ld (ix+B_COS+1),h
 233+ C153 DD 6E 07             ld l,(ix+B_SIN)
 234+ C156 DD 66 08             ld h,(ix+B_SIN+1)
 235+ C159 ED 5B AA CC          ld de,(cg_s)
 236+ C15D 19                   add hl,de
 237+ C15E DD 75 07             ld (ix+B_SIN),l
 238+ C161 DD 74 08             ld (ix+B_SIN+1),h
 239+ C164 E1           .v:     pop hl                          ; up/down: slope (tan) +/- d/256
 240+ C165 7C                   ld a,h
 241+ C166 CD 86 C1             call dev_n
 242+ C169 DD 6E 0B             ld l,(ix+B_SLOPE)
 243+ C16C DD 66 0C             ld h,(ix+B_SLOPE+1)
 244+ C16F CD 8F C1             call add_sa
 245+ C172 DD 75 0B             ld (ix+B_SLOPE),l
 246+ C175 DD 74 0C             ld (ix+B_SLOPE+1),h
 247+ C178 C9                   ret
 248+ C179
 249+ C179              ; HL = d * 256, DE = S7.8 value -> HL = d * value / 256, rounded
 250+ C179              dmul:
 251+ C179 CD EA AD             call fmul                       ; HL = d * value (exact)
 252+ C17C 11 80 00             ld de,128
 253+ C17F 19                   add hl,de
 254+ C180 6C                   ld l,h                          ; >> 8, keeping the sign
 255+ C181 7C                   ld a,h
 256+ C182 17                   rla
 257+ C183 9F                   sbc a,a
 258+ C184 67                   ld h,a
 259+ C185 C9                   ret
 260+ C186
 261+ C186              ; A = random byte -> A = -CHAIN_DEV..+CHAIN_DEV, evenly spread
 262+ C186              dev_n:
 263+ C186 57                   ld d,a
 264+ C187 1E 13                ld e,2*CHAIN_DEV+1
 265+ C189 ED 30                mul d,e
 266+ C18B 7A                   ld a,d
 267+ C18C D6 09                sub CHAIN_DEV
 268+ C18E C9                   ret
 269+ C18F
 270+ C18F              ; HL = HL + A (signed)
 271+ C18F              add_sa:
 272+ C18F 5F                   ld e,a
 273+ C190 17                   rla
 274+ C191 9F                   sbc a,a
 275+ C192 57                   ld d,a
 276+ C193 19                   add hl,de
 277+ C194 C9                   ret
 278+ C195
 279+ C195              ; One round from the selected slot's ammo: carry set if it is empty.
 280+ C195              ; AMMO_INF (255) is never counted down.
 281+ C195              take_ammo:
 282+ C195 CD FF C3             call sel_slot
 283+ C198 23                   inc hl                          ; its ammo counter
 284+ C199 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 285+ C19A FE FF                cp AMMO_INF
 286+ C19C C8                   ret z                           ; infinite (carry clear)
 287+ C19D B7                   or a
 288+ C19E 37                   scf
 289+ C19F C8                   ret z                           ; empty
 290+ C1A0 35                   dec (hl)
 291+ C1A1 B7                   or a
 292+ C1A2 C9                   ret
 293+ C1A3
 294+ C1A3              ; IX = a free round, carry clear; carry set if all are in flight
 295+ C1A3              free_round:
 296+ C1A3 DD 21 E5 AE          ld ix,bullets
 297+ C1A7 06 18                ld b,NBULLETS
 298+ C1A9 11 1B 00             ld de,BREC
 299+ C1AC DD 7E 00     .f:     ld a,(ix+B_STATE)
 300+ C1AF B7                   or a
 301+ C1B0 C8                   ret z                           ; (carry clear)
 302+ C1B1 DD 19                add ix,de
 303+ C1B3 10 F7                djnz .f
 304+ C1B5 37                   scf
 305+ C1B6 C9                   ret
 306+ C1B7              ; Start round IX at the muzzle along the view direction, travelling HL
 307+ C1B7              ; units (along its path) per frame
 308+ C1B7              spawn_round:
 309+ C1B7 22 A2 CC             ld (sr_len),hl
 310+ C1BA DD 36 00 81          ld (ix+B_STATE),$81             ; in flight, fresh: its first path
 311+ C1BE                                                      ; starts at the muzzle (B_PX..)
 312+ C1BE AF                   xor a                           ; cell intervals: on its first step
 313+ C1BF DD 77 15             ld (ix+B_TDX),a
 314+ C1C2 DD 77 16             ld (ix+B_TDX+1),a
 315+ C1C5 DD 77 1A             ld (ix+B_KIND),a                ; a round, no damage (the caller
 316+ C1C8 DD 77 19             ld (ix+B_DMG),a                 ; sets them)
 317+ C1CB                      ; muzzle: the ship's centre line, GUN_DY below the camera
 318+ C1CB 2A 24 AE             ld hl,(cam_x+1)
 319+ C1CE DD 75 01             ld (ix+B_X),l
 320+ C1D1 DD 74 02             ld (ix+B_X+1),h
 321+ C1D4 2A 29 AE             ld hl,(cam_y)
 322+ C1D7 11 FA FF             ld de,GUN_DY
 323+ C1DA 19                   add hl,de
 324+ C1DB DD 75 03             ld (ix+B_Y),l
 325+ C1DE DD 74 04             ld (ix+B_Y+1),h
 326+ C1E1 2A 27 AE             ld hl,(cam_z+1)
 327+ C1E4 DD 75 05             ld (ix+B_Z),l
 328+ C1E7 DD 74 06             ld (ix+B_Z+1),h
 329+ C1EA DD 75 13             ld (ix+B_PZ),l
 330+ C1ED DD 74 14             ld (ix+B_PZ+1),h
 331+ C1F0 DD 6E 01             ld l,(ix+B_X)
 332+ C1F3 DD 66 02             ld h,(ix+B_X+1)
 333+ C1F6 DD 75 0F             ld (ix+B_PX),l
 334+ C1F9 DD 74 10             ld (ix+B_PX+1),h
 335+ C1FC DD 6E 03             ld l,(ix+B_Y)
 336+ C1FF DD 66 04             ld h,(ix+B_Y+1)
 337+ C202 DD 75 11             ld (ix+B_PY),l
 338+ C205 DD 74 12             ld (ix+B_PY+1),h
 339+ C208                      ; direction: the view direction this frame
 340+ C208 2A 59 B4             ld hl,(sin_y)
 341+ C20B DD 75 07             ld (ix+B_SIN),l
 342+ C20E DD 74 08             ld (ix+B_SIN+1),h
 343+ C211 2A 5B B4             ld hl,(cos_y)
 344+ C214 DD 75 09             ld (ix+B_COS),l
 345+ C217 DD 74 0A             ld (ix+B_COS+1),h
 346+ C21A 2A 5D B4             ld hl,(sin_p)                   ; its climb: the view's pitch, or from
 347+ C21D 22 A4 CC             ld (sr_sin),hl                  ; the centre slot pitch + gun_tilt
 348+ C220 2A 5F B4             ld hl,(cos_p)
 349+ C223 22 A6 CC             ld (sr_cos),hl
 350+ C226 3A 3A AE             ld a,(wpn_sel)
 351+ C229 FE 00                cp WPN_CENTRE
 352+ C22B 20 1A                jr nz,.pt
 353+ C22D 3A 4A AE             ld a,(gun_tilt)
 354+ C230 B7                   or a
 355+ C231 28 14                jr z,.pt
 356+ C233 21 2E AE             ld hl,pitch
 357+ C236 86                   add a,(hl)
 358+ C237 F5                   push af
 359+ C238 CD 33 AD             call get_sin
 360+ C23B 22 A4 CC             ld (sr_sin),hl
 361+ C23E F1                   pop af
 362+ C23F C6 40                add a,64
 363+ C241 CD 33 AD             call get_sin
 364+ C244 22 A6 CC             ld (sr_cos),hl
 365+ C247 2A A2 CC     .pt:    ld hl,(sr_len)                  ; horizontal distance per frame
 366+ C24A ED 5B A6 CC          ld de,(sr_cos)
 367+ C24E CD EA AD             call fmul
 368+ C251 DD 75 0D             ld (ix+B_S),l
 369+ C254 DD 74 0E             ld (ix+B_S+1),h
 370+ C257 2A A4 CC             ld hl,(sr_sin)                  ; slope = sin / cos  (S7.8)
 371+ C25A 7C                   ld a,h
 372+ C25B F5                   push af
 373+ C25C CD E5 AD             call abs_hl
 374+ C25F 7C                   ld a,h                          ; A:HL = |sin| << 8
 375+ C260 65                   ld h,l
 376+ C261 2E 00                ld l,0
 377+ C263 ED 5B A6 CC          ld de,(sr_cos)
 378+ C267 CD 61 CC             call udiv24
 379+ C26A F1                   pop af
 380+ C26B 17                   rla
 381+ C26C DC DE AD             call c,neg_hl
 382+ C26F DD 75 0B             ld (ix+B_SLOPE),l
 383+ C272 DD 74 0C             ld (ix+B_SLOPE+1),h
 384+ C275 C9                   ret
 385+ C276
 386+ C276              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 387+ C276              ; R / F: tilt the centre gun up / down (GUN_TILT_STEP a frame, to
 388+ C276              ; GUN_TILT_UP up, GUN_TILT_DN down); V: back towards level at GUN_CTR_STEP.
 389+ C276              ; Then sight_y: the gunsight's row for the selected weapon, VIEW_CY minus
 390+ C276              ; 128 * tan(tilt) for the centre slot (the projection's scale), kept
 391+ C276              ; SIGHT_MARGIN rows inside the view.
 392+ C276              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 393+ C276              gun_aim:
 394+ C276 3E 0A                ld a,K_GUN_UP
 395+ C278 CD BE E0             call key_held
 396+ C27B 28 0E                jr z,.dn
 397+ C27D 3A 4A AE             ld a,(gun_tilt)
 398+ C280 C6 80                add a,$80                       ; (signed compare)
 399+ C282 FE 8B                cp GUN_TILT_UP+$80
 400+ C284 30 05                jr nc,.dn                       ; at the top
 401+ C286 D6 7F                sub $80-GUN_TILT_STEP
 402+ C288 32 4A AE             ld (gun_tilt),a
 403+ C28B 3E 10        .dn:    ld a,K_GUN_DN
 404+ C28D CD BE E0             call key_held
 405+ C290 28 0E                jr z,.ct
 406+ C292 3A 4A AE             ld a,(gun_tilt)
 407+ C295 C6 80                add a,$80
 408+ C297 FE 61                cp $80-GUN_TILT_DN+1
 409+ C299 38 05                jr c,.ct                        ; at the bottom
 410+ C29B D6 81                sub $80+GUN_TILT_STEP
 411+ C29D 32 4A AE             ld (gun_tilt),a
 412+ C2A0 3E 11        .ct:    ld a,K_GUN_CTR
 413+ C2A2 CD BE E0             call key_held
 414+ C2A5 28 1A                jr z,.sight
 415+ C2A7 3A 4A AE             ld a,(gun_tilt)
 416+ C2AA B7                   or a
 417+ C2AB 28 14                jr z,.sight
 418+ C2AD FA B8 C2             jp m,.neg
 419+ C2B0 D6 02                sub GUN_CTR_STEP                ; tilted up: down towards 0
 420+ C2B2 F2 BE C2             jp p,.st
 421+ C2B5 AF                   xor a
 422+ C2B6 18 06                jr .st
 423+ C2B8 C6 02        .neg:   add a,GUN_CTR_STEP              ; tilted down: up towards 0
 424+ C2BA FA BE C2             jp m,.st
 425+ C2BD AF                   xor a
 426+ C2BE 32 4A AE     .st:    ld (gun_tilt),a
 427+ C2C1 0E 40        .sight: ld c,VIEW_CY
 428+ C2C3 3A 3A AE             ld a,(wpn_sel)
 429+ C2C6 FE 00                cp WPN_CENTRE
 430+ C2C8 20 29                jr nz,.sy                       ; a pylon: straight ahead
 431+ C2CA 3A 4A AE             ld a,(gun_tilt)
 432+ C2CD B7                   or a
 433+ C2CE 28 23                jr z,.sy
 434+ C2D0 FA DC C2             jp m,.sd
 435+ C2D3 21 F8 C2             ld hl,tilt_rows                 ; up: higher on the screen
 436+ C2D6 ED 31                add hl,a
 437+ C2D8 79                   ld a,c
 438+ C2D9 96                   sub (hl)
 439+ C2DA 18 09                jr .sc
 440+ C2DC ED 44        .sd:    neg                             ; down: lower
 441+ C2DE 21 F8 C2             ld hl,tilt_rows
 442+ C2E1 ED 31                add hl,a
 443+ C2E3 79                   ld a,c
 444+ C2E4 86                   add a,(hl)
 445+ C2E5 4F           .sc:    ld c,a
 446+ C2E6 FE 06                cp SIGHT_MARGIN
 447+ C2E8 30 02                jr nc,.lo
 448+ C2EA 0E 06                ld c,SIGHT_MARGIN
 449+ C2EC 79           .lo:    ld a,c
 450+ C2ED FE 7A                cp VIEW_H-SIGHT_MARGIN
 451+ C2EF 38 02                jr c,.sy
 452+ C2F1 0E 79                ld c,VIEW_H-SIGHT_MARGIN-1
 453+ C2F3 79           .sy:    ld a,c
 454+ C2F4 32 A6 AE             ld (sight_y),a
 455+ C2F7 C9                   ret
 456+ C2F8
 457+ C2F8              ; round(128 * tan(k * 360/256 deg)), k = 0..GUN_TILT_DN: the gunsight's
 458+ C2F8              ; rows from the centre for a tilt of k
 459+ C2F8 00 03 06 09  tilt_rows:  db 0, 3, 6, 9, 13, 16, 19, 22, 25, 29, 32, 35, 39, 42, 46, 49
 459+ C2FC 0D 10 13 16
 459+ C300 19 1D 20 23
 459+ C304 27 2A 2E 31
 460+ C308 35 39 3D 40              db 53, 57, 61, 64, 68, 73, 77, 81, 86, 90, 95, 100, 105, 110
 460+ C30C 44 49 4D 51
 460+ C310 56 5A 5F 64
 460+ C314 69 6E
 461+ C316 74 7A 80                 db 116, 122, 128
 462+ C319                      ASSERT $ - tilt_rows == GUN_TILT_DN + 1
 463+ C319                      ASSERT GUN_TILT_UP <= GUN_TILT_DN
 464+ C319
 465+ C319              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 466+ C319              ; Keys 1-5: select the weapon slot SPACE fires (an empty one isn't selected:
 467+ C319              ; the HUD flashes it instead)
 468+ C319              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 469+ C319              wpn_select:
 470+ C319 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; slot keys, newly pressed
 471+ C31B 79           .k:     ld a,c
 472+ C31C C6 0B                add a,K_SLOT1
 473+ C31E CD CF E0             call key_pressed
 474+ C321 20 07                jr nz,.sel
 475+ C323 0C                   inc c
 476+ C324 79                   ld a,c
 477+ C325 FE 05                cp WPN_SLOTS
 478+ C327 20 F2                jr nz,.k
 479+ C329 C9                   ret
 480+ C32A 79           .sel:   ld a,c
 481+ C32B 87                   add a,a
 482+ C32C 21 30 AE             ld hl,wpn_slots
 483+ C32F ED 31                add hl,a
 484+ C331 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 485+ C332 B7                   or a
 486+ C333 20 0A                jr nz,.fitted
 487+ C335 79                   ld a,c                          ; empty: keep the current slot and
 488+ C336 32 3B AE             ld (wflash_slot),a              ; have the HUD flash the asked-for one
 489+ C339 3E 1E                ld a,6*WFLASH
 490+ C33B 32 3C AE             ld (wflash_t),a
 491+ C33E C9                   ret
 492+ C33F              .fitted:
 493+ C33F 3A 3A AE             ld a,(wpn_sel)
 494+ C342 B9                   cp c
 495+ C343 C8                   ret z                           ; already selected
 496+ C344 79                   ld a,c
 497+ C345 32 3A AE             ld (wpn_sel),a
 498+ C348 C3 F4 C3             jp las_reset                    ; no beam carried to the new slot
 499+ C34B
 500+ C34B                  IF DEBUG_KILLALL
 501+ C34B              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 502+ C34B              ; Debug: key 8 changes the centre slot's gun, machine gun -> laser -> chain
 503+ C34B              ; gun -> machine gun (anything else there: the machine gun), with its full
 504+ C34B              ; (infinite) load, and selects the centre slot
 505+ C34B              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 506+ C34B              debug_gun_cycle:
 507+ C34B 21 04 04             ld hl,KB_8
 508+ C34E CD F1 E0             call key_code_pressed
 509+ C351 C8                   ret z
 510+ C352 21 30 AE             ld hl,wpn_slots                 ; the centre slot: type, ammo
 511+ C355 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 512+ C356 06 02                ld b,WPN_LASER
 513+ C358 FE 01                cp WPN_GUN
 514+ C35A 28 08                jr z,.set
 515+ C35C 06 03                ld b,WPN_CHAIN
 516+ C35E FE 02                cp WPN_LASER
 517+ C360 28 02                jr z,.set
 518+ C362 06 01                ld b,WPN_GUN
 519+ C364 70           .set:   ld (hl),b
 520+ C365 23                   inc hl
 521+ C366 36 FF                ld (hl),AMMO_INF
 522+ C368 AF                   xor a
 523+ C369 32 3A AE             ld (wpn_sel),a                  ; fire it
 524+ C36C C3 F4 C3             jp las_reset                    ; (no beam carried over)
 525+ C36F
 526+ C36F              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 527+ C36F              ; Debug: key 0 kills every monster at once (zombies, ghouls, ghosts and
 528+ C36F              ; banshees, none left fading) and counts every human still in the city or
 529+ C36F              ; aboard as rescued.  zombie_update then finds the wave wiped out: the next
 530+ C36F              ; wave, or after the last the level complete.
 531+ C36F              ; Key 9 does the same and ends the level, on to the next city's first level
 532+ C36F              ; (level 2 -> 6): level becomes this city's last (level_complete_screen
 533+ C36F              ; moves it on one, or after the last city the game is complete) and the
 534+ C36F              ; wave the level's last.
 535+ C36F              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 536+ C36F              debug_killall:
 537+ C36F 21 02 04             ld hl,KB_9
 538+ C372 CD F1 E0             call key_code_pressed
 539+ C375 28 18                jr z,.k0
 540+ C377 3A 3D AE             ld a,(level)                    ; (level-1) mod CITY_LEVELS - CITY_LEVELS
 541+ C37A 3D                   dec a
 542+ C37B D6 05        .m:     sub CITY_LEVELS
 543+ C37D 30 FC                jr nc,.m
 544+ C37F ED 44                neg                             ; CITY_LEVELS-1 - (level-1) mod
 545+ C381 3D                   dec a                           ; CITY_LEVELS: levels left in the city
 546+ C382 21 3D AE             ld hl,level
 547+ C385 86                   add a,(hl)
 548+ C386 77                   ld (hl),a                       ; the city's last level
 549+ C387 3A 65 A0             ld a,(level_cur+LV_WAVES)
 550+ C38A 32 46 AE             ld (wave_number),a              ; its last wave
 551+ C38D 18 07                jr .all
 552+ C38F 21 01 04     .k0:    ld hl,KB_0
 553+ C392 CD F1 E0             call key_code_pressed
 554+ C395 C8                   ret z
 555+ C396 DD 21 CB 2F  .all:   ld ix,zombies
 556+ C39A 06 26                ld b,NMON
 557+ C39C C5           .z:     push bc
 558+ C39D CD 11 3B             call zblip_off                  ; (map.asm)
 559+ C3A0 DD 36 0A 00          ld (ix+10),0                    ; gone (zflags unmarks its cell)
 560+ C3A4 11 1F 00             ld de,ZREC
 561+ C3A7 DD 19                add ix,de
 562+ C3A9 C1                   pop bc
 563+ C3AA 10 F0                djnz .z
 564+ C3AC 21 72 2F             ld hl,zatk_flags                ; nobody attacking
 565+ C3AF 06 26                ld b,NMON
 566+ C3B1 36 00        .a:     ld (hl),FALSE
 567+ C3B3 23                   inc hl
 568+ C3B4 10 FB                djnz .a
 569+ C3B6 DD 21 31 39          ld ix,humans
 570+ C3BA 06 14                ld b,HUMAN_MAX
 571+ C3BC C5           .h:     push bc
 572+ C3BD DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
 573+ C3C0 FE 01                cp HS_WAIT
 574+ C3C2 28 04                jr z,.s
 575+ C3C4 FE 02                cp HS_ABOARD
 576+ C3C6 20 0D                jr nz,.hn
 577+ C3C8 0E 00        .s:     ld c,0                          ; dot off,
 578+ C3CA CD 74 3B             call hum_dot
 579+ C3CD DD 36 06 03          ld (ix+6),HS_SAVED              ; rescued
 580+ C3D1 21 1F 39             ld hl,saved
 581+ C3D4 34                   inc (hl)
 582+ C3D5 11 0D 00     .hn:    ld de,HREC
 583+ C3D8 DD 19                add ix,de
 584+ C3DA C1                   pop bc
 585+ C3DB 10 DF                djnz .h
 586+ C3DD AF                   xor a
 587+ C3DE 32 1E 39             ld (passengers),a
 588+ C3E1 C3 B8 36             jp hum_flags                    ; (no waiting humans' cells marked)
 589+ C3E4                  ENDIF
 590+ C3E4
 591+ C3E4              ; New game: no gun cool-down, no beam
 592+ C3E4              weapons_reset:
 593+ C3E4 AF                   xor a
 594+ C3E5 32 98 CC             ld (gun_wait),a
 595+ C3E8 32 99 CC             ld (svc_on),a
 596+ C3EB 32 4A AE             ld (gun_tilt),a                 ; the centre gun level
 597+ C3EE 3E 40                ld a,VIEW_CY
 598+ C3F0 32 A6 AE             ld (sight_y),a
 599+ C3F3 AF                   xor a
 600+ C3F4                      ; fall through
 601+ C3F4
 602+ C3F4              ; Stop any laser beam or cool-down
 603+ C3F4              las_reset:
 604+ C3F4 AF                   xor a
 605+ C3F5 32 A0 CC             ld (las_on),a
 606+ C3F8 32 A1 CC             ld (las_cool),a
 607+ C3FB 32 6D B1             ld (laser_rec+B_STATE),a
 608+ C3FE C9                   ret
 609+ C3FF
 610+ C3FF              ; HL -> the selected weapon slot (type, then ammo)
 611+ C3FF              sel_slot:
 612+ C3FF 3A 3A AE             ld a,(wpn_sel)
 613+ C402 87                   add a,a
 614+ C403 21 30 AE             ld hl,wpn_slots
 615+ C406 ED 31                add hl,a
 616+ C408 C9                   ret
 617+ C409
 618+ C409              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 619+ C409              ; Landed (update_weapons): on the base pad, repair and reload.  The VTOL
 620+ C409              ; gains 1 hit point every REPAIR_FRAMES frames up to VTOL_HP; each slot
 621+ C409              ; whose weapon reloads (wpn_cfg, zpolice.asm: its full load and frames a
 622+ C409              ; shot) gains a
 623+ C409              ; shot every that many frames up to its full load.  The counts start afresh
 624+ C409              ; each landing (svc_on), so a shot or hit point takes the whole interval.
 625+ C409              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 626+ C409              pad_service:
 627+ C409 CD 8F A9             call cam_cell_ptr
 628+ C40C 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 629+ C40D E6 1F                and CELL_CODE
 630+ C40F FE 10                cp PAD_CODE
 631+ C411 28 05                jr z,.pad
 632+ C413 AF                   xor a                           ; landed elsewhere: nothing
 633+ C414 32 99 CC             ld (svc_on),a
 634+ C417 C9                   ret
 635+ C418 3A 99 CC     .pad:   ld a,(svc_on)
 636+ C41B B7                   or a
 637+ C41C 20 13                jr nz,.go
 638+ C41E 3C                   inc a                           ; just landed: start the counts
 639+ C41F 32 99 CC             ld (svc_on),a
 640+ C422 3E 05                ld a,REPAIR_FRAMES
 641+ C424 32 9A CC             ld (svc_rep),a
 642+ C427 21 9B CC             ld hl,svc_rel
 643+ C42A 06 05                ld b,WPN_SLOTS
 644+ C42C 36 00        .cl:    ld (hl),0                       ; (0: its interval starts next)
 645+ C42E 23                   inc hl
 646+ C42F 10 FB                djnz .cl
 647+ C431 3A B9 AE     .go:    ld a,(boost_fitted)             ; the booster: refuelled 1 a frame
 648+ C434 B7                   or a
 649+ C435 28 0B                jr z,.nf
 650+ C437 3A BA AE             ld a,(boost_fuel)
 651+ C43A FE C8                cp BOOST_FUEL_MAX
 652+ C43C 30 04                jr nc,.nf
 653+ C43E 3C                   inc a
 654+ C43F 32 BA AE             ld (boost_fuel),a
 655+ C442 21 9A CC     .nf:    ld hl,svc_rep                   ; repair
 656+ C445 35                   dec (hl)
 657+ C446 20 0D                jr nz,.rl
 658+ C448 36 05                ld (hl),REPAIR_FRAMES
 659+ C44A 3A 40 AE             ld a,(vtol_hp)
 660+ C44D FE 32                cp VTOL_HP
 661+ C44F 30 04                jr nc,.rl                       ; (undamaged)
 662+ C451 3C                   inc a
 663+ C452 32 40 AE             ld (vtol_hp),a
 664+ C455 DD 21 30 AE  .rl:    ld ix,wpn_slots                 ; reload, slot by slot
 665+ C459 21 9B CC             ld hl,svc_rel
 666+ C45C 06 05                ld b,WPN_SLOTS
 667+ C45E C5           .sl:    push bc
 668+ C45F DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+WPN_TYPE)
 669+ C462 E5                   push hl
 670+ C463 CD E3 A9             call wpn_cfg_ptr                ; (resident)
 671+ C466 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 672+ C467 4F                   ld c,a                          ; C = its full load (WCFG_LOAD)
 673+ C468 23                   inc hl
 674+ C469 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; frames a shot (WCFG_RELOAD)
 675+ C46A E1                   pop hl
 676+ C46B B7                   or a
 677+ C46C 28 16                jr z,.nx                        ; doesn't reload (or an empty slot)
 678+ C46E 47                   ld b,a                          ; B = frames a shot
 679+ C46F DD 7E 01             ld a,(ix+WPN_AMMO)
 680+ C472 B9                   cp c
 681+ C473 30 0D                jr nc,.full
 682+ C475 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 683+ C476 B7                   or a
 684+ C477 20 01                jr nz,.cnt
 685+ C479 70                   ld (hl),b                       ; a new interval
 686+ C47A 35           .cnt:   dec (hl)
 687+ C47B 20 07                jr nz,.nx
 688+ C47D DD 34 01             inc (ix+WPN_AMMO)               ; one more shot
 689+ C480 18 02                jr .nx
 690+ C482 36 00        .full:  ld (hl),0
 691+ C484 23           .nx:    inc hl
 692+ C485 DD 23                inc ix
 693+ C487 DD 23                inc ix
 694+ C489 C1                   pop bc
 695+ C48A 10 D2                djnz .sl
 696+ C48C C9                   ret
 697+ C48D
 698+ C48D              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 699+ C48D              ; Pulse laser: SPACE held with the laser in the centre slot fires a beam for
 700+ C48D              ; LASER_ON frames, then it cools for LASER_COOL frames.  Each beam frame the
 701+ C48D              ; beam is traced from the muzzle along the current view direction (the round
 702+ C48D              ; walk, LASER_RANGE long) to the first building, ground or zombie, killing a
 703+ C48D              ; zombie it reaches.  It is kept in laser_rec as a round (state 3) whose
 704+ C48D              ; path runs from the muzzle to where the beam stops; draw_bullets clips it
 705+ C48D              ; to the view, so it rises from the bottom edge of the screen.
 706+ C48D              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 707+ C48D              laser_update:
 708+ C48D 3A A0 CC             ld a,(las_on)
 709+ C490 B7                   or a
 710+ C491 20 25                jr nz,.beam
 711+ C493 32 6D B1             ld (laser_rec+B_STATE),a        ; no beam this frame
 712+ C496 3A A1 CC             ld a,(las_cool)
 713+ C499 B7                   or a
 714+ C49A 28 05                jr z,.ready
 715+ C49C 3D                   dec a                           ; cooling down
 716+ C49D 32 A1 CC             ld (las_cool),a
 717+ C4A0 C9                   ret
 718+ C4A1 CD FF C3     .ready: call sel_slot
 719+ C4A4 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 720+ C4A5 FE 02                cp WPN_LASER
 721+ C4A7 C0                   ret nz
 722+ C4A8 3E 09                ld a,K_FIRE
 723+ C4AA CD BE E0             call key_held
 724+ C4AD C8                   ret z
 725+ C4AE 3E 03                ld a,LASER_ON
 726+ C4B0 32 A0 CC             ld (las_on),a
 727+ C4B3 3E 02                ld a,SFX_LASER                  ; a new pulse: its zap
 728+ C4B5 32 6C 3F             ld (sfx_req),a
 729+ C4B8 21 A0 CC     .beam:  ld hl,las_on
 730+ C4BB 35                   dec (hl)
 731+ C4BC 20 05                jr nz,.on
 732+ C4BE 3E 08                ld a,LASER_COOL                 ; last beam frame: cool down next
 733+ C4C0 32 A1 CC             ld (las_cool),a
 734+ C4C3 DD 21 6D B1  .on:    ld ix,laser_rec
 735+ C4C7 21 00 06             ld hl,LASER_RANGE
 736+ C4CA CD B7 C1             call spawn_round                ; (start of path = the muzzle)
 737+ C4CD DD 36 19 01          ld (ix+B_DMG),LASER_DMG         ; per beam frame on a target
 738+ C4D1 CD 1C C6             call bullet_step                ; trace it to the first thing it meets
 739+ C4D4 DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+B_STATE)
 740+ C4D7 B7                   or a
 741+ C4D8 C8                   ret z                           ; off the map (can't, inside the wall)
 742+ C4D9 DD 36 00 03          ld (ix+B_STATE),3               ; beam
 743+ C4DD C9                   ret
 744+ C4DE
 745+ C4DE              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 746+ C4DE              ; Particles (pool `particles`, drawn by draw_particles): zombie bursts and
 747+ C4DE              ; rocket / missile bursts
 748+ C4DE              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 749+ C4DE              ; IX = a zombie just killed: spray NPART_BURST green particles from its body
 750+ C4DE              ; (PART_Y up), each with a random velocity: sideways -4..3 units a frame in
 751+ C4DE              ; x and z, upwards 2..5
 752+ C4DE              part_burst:
 753+ C4DE DD 6E 00             ld l,(ix+0)
 754+ C4E1 DD 66 01             ld h,(ix+1)
 755+ C4E4 22 E6 CC             ld (ps_x),hl
 756+ C4E7 21 0D 00             ld hl,PART_Y
 757+ C4EA 22 E8 CC             ld (ps_y),hl
 758+ C4ED DD 6E 02             ld l,(ix+2)
 759+ C4F0 DD 66 03             ld h,(ix+3)
 760+ C4F3 22 EA CC             ld (ps_z),hl
 761+ C4F6 21 3B 08             ld hl,PART_COL0 | PART_LIFE<<8
 762+ C4F9 22 EC CC             ld (ps_col),hl                  ; (and ps_life)
 763+ C4FC AF                   xor a
 764+ C4FD 32 EE CC             ld (ps_fire),a
 765+ C500 0E 0A                ld c,NPART_BURST
 766+ C502 18 21                jr part_spray
 767+ C504
 768+ C504              ; A rocket or missile burst at rb_x/y/z: rb_parts orange particles for
 769+ C504              ; FIRE_LIFE frames, flying out faster than a zombie's (x and z -8..7 units
 770+ C504              ; a frame, y -2..5)
 771+ C504              fire_burst:
 772+ C504 2A EF CC             ld hl,(rb_x)
 773+ C507 22 E6 CC             ld (ps_x),hl
 774+ C50A 2A F1 CC             ld hl,(rb_y)
 775+ C50D 22 E8 CC             ld (ps_y),hl
 776+ C510 2A F3 CC             ld hl,(rb_z)
 777+ C513 22 EA CC             ld (ps_z),hl
 778+ C516 21 83 0A             ld hl,FIRE_COL0 | FIRE_LIFE<<8
 779+ C519 22 EC CC             ld (ps_col),hl
 780+ C51C 3E 01                ld a,1
 781+ C51E 32 EE CC             ld (ps_fire),a
 782+ C521 3A E5 CC             ld a,(rb_parts)
 783+ C524 4F                   ld c,a
 784+ C525                      ; fall through
 785+ C525
 786+ C525              ; Spray C particles from ps_x/y/z, colour ps_col (age 1), lasting ps_life
 787+ C525              ; frames, velocities by ps_fire.  Uses free particles only; if the pool
 788+ C525              ; runs out, fewer.  Preserves IX.
 789+ C525              part_spray:
 790+ C525 FD E5                push iy
 791+ C527 FD 21 88 B1          ld iy,particles
 792+ C52B 06 3C                ld b,NPART
 793+ C52D FD 7E 00     .lp:    ld a,(iy+0)
 794+ C530 B7                   or a
 795+ C531 20 70                jr nz,.nx                       ; in use
 796+ C533 FD 36 00 01          ld (iy+0),1                     ; age 1: brightest
 797+ C537 2A E6 CC             ld hl,(ps_x)
 798+ C53A FD 75 01             ld (iy+1),l
 799+ C53D FD 74 02             ld (iy+2),h
 800+ C540 2A E8 CC             ld hl,(ps_y)
 801+ C543 FD 75 03             ld (iy+3),l
 802+ C546 FD 74 04             ld (iy+4),h
 803+ C549 2A EA CC             ld hl,(ps_z)
 804+ C54C FD 75 05             ld (iy+5),l
 805+ C54F FD 74 06             ld (iy+6),h
 806+ C552 2A EC CC             ld hl,(ps_col)
 807+ C555 FD 75 0A             ld (iy+10),l                    ; its colour and life
 808+ C558 FD 74 0B             ld (iy+11),h
 809+ C55B C5                   push bc
 810+ C55C CD B5 2C             call zrand                      ; (zombie page)
 811+ C55F 3A EE CC             ld a,(ps_fire)
 812+ C562 B7                   or a
 813+ C563 20 1E                jr nz,.fire
 814+ C565 7D                   ld a,l
 815+ C566 E6 07                and 7
 816+ C568 D6 04                sub 4
 817+ C56A FD 77 07             ld (iy+7),a                     ; vx -4..3
 818+ C56D 7C                   ld a,h
 819+ C56E E6 07                and 7
 820+ C570 D6 04                sub 4
 821+ C572 FD 77 09             ld (iy+9),a                     ; vz -4..3
 822+ C575 7D                   ld a,l
 823+ C576 0F                   rrca
 824+ C577 0F                   rrca
 825+ C578 0F                   rrca
 826+ C579 0F                   rrca
 827+ C57A E6 03                and 3
 828+ C57C C6 02                add a,2
 829+ C57E FD 77 08             ld (iy+8),a                     ; vy 2..5 (up)
 830+ C581 18 1C                jr .v
 831+ C583 7D           .fire:  ld a,l
 832+ C584 E6 0F                and 15
 833+ C586 D6 08                sub 8
 834+ C588 FD 77 07             ld (iy+7),a                     ; vx -8..7
 835+ C58B 7C                   ld a,h
 836+ C58C E6 0F                and 15
 837+ C58E D6 08                sub 8
 838+ C590 FD 77 09             ld (iy+9),a                     ; vz -8..7
 839+ C593 7D                   ld a,l
 840+ C594 0F                   rrca
 841+ C595 0F                   rrca
 842+ C596 0F                   rrca
 843+ C597 0F                   rrca
 844+ C598 E6 07                and 7
 845+ C59A D6 02                sub 2
 846+ C59C FD 77 08             ld (iy+8),a                     ; vy -2..5
 847+ C59F C1           .v:     pop bc
 848+ C5A0 0D                   dec c
 849+ C5A1 28 07                jr z,.done
 850+ C5A3 11 0C 00     .nx:    ld de,PREC
 851+ C5A6 FD 19                add iy,de
 852+ C5A8 10 83                djnz .lp
 853+ C5AA FD E1        .done:  pop iy
 854+ C5AC C9                   ret
 855+ C5AD
 856+ C5AD              ; Age every particle; past its life (+11) it is freed.  Move the rest by
 857+ C5AD              ; their velocity, with gravity, stopping on the ground.
 858+ C5AD              part_update:
 859+ C5AD DD 21 88 B1          ld ix,particles
 860+ C5B1 06 3C                ld b,NPART
 861+ C5B3 DD 7E 00     .lp:    ld a,(ix+0)
 862+ C5B6 B7                   or a
 863+ C5B7 28 5B                jr z,.nx
 864+ C5B9 3C                   inc a
 865+ C5BA 4F                   ld c,a
 866+ C5BB DD 7E 0B             ld a,(ix+11)
 867+ C5BE B9                   cp c
 868+ C5BF 79                   ld a,c
 869+ C5C0 30 01                jr nc,.live
 870+ C5C2 AF                   xor a                           ; gone
 871+ C5C3 DD 77 00     .live:  ld (ix+0),a
 872+ C5C6 B7                   or a
 873+ C5C7 28 4B                jr z,.nx
 874+ C5C9 DD 7E 07             ld a,(ix+7)                     ; x += vx
 875+ C5CC DD 6E 01             ld l,(ix+1)
 876+ C5CF DD 66 02             ld h,(ix+2)
 877+ C5D2 CD 8F C1             call add_sa
 878+ C5D5 DD 75 01             ld (ix+1),l
 879+ C5D8 DD 74 02             ld (ix+2),h
 880+ C5DB DD 7E 09             ld a,(ix+9)                     ; z += vz
 881+ C5DE DD 6E 05             ld l,(ix+5)
 882+ C5E1 DD 66 06             ld h,(ix+6)
 883+ C5E4 CD 8F C1             call add_sa
 884+ C5E7 DD 75 05             ld (ix+5),l
 885+ C5EA DD 74 06             ld (ix+6),h
 886+ C5ED DD 7E 08             ld a,(ix+8)                     ; y += vy, vy -= gravity
 887+ C5F0 DD 6E 03             ld l,(ix+3)
 888+ C5F3 DD 66 04             ld h,(ix+4)
 889+ C5F6 CD 8F C1             call add_sa
 890+ C5F9 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 891+ C5FB 28 09                jr z,.up
 892+ C5FD 21 00 00             ld hl,0                         ; on the ground: stays there
 893+ C600 DD 36 08 00          ld (ix+8),0
 894+ C604 18 08                jr .y
 895+ C606 DD 7E 08     .up:    ld a,(ix+8)
 896+ C609 D6 01                sub PART_GRAV
 897+ C60B DD 77 08             ld (ix+8),a
 898+ C60E DD 75 03     .y:     ld (ix+3),l
 899+ C611 DD 74 04             ld (ix+4),h
 900+ C614 11 0C 00     .nx:    ld de,PREC
 901+ C617 DD 19                add ix,de
 902+ C619 10 98                djnz .lp
 903+ C61B C9                   ret
 904+ C61C
 905+ C61C              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 906+ C61C              ; Move round IX one frame along its path, stopping at the first thing it hits
 907+ C61C              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 908+ C61C              bullet_step:
 909+ C61C                      ; start of this frame's path; kept for drawing (B_PX..), except on a
 910+ C61C                      ; fresh round's first step, which is drawn from the muzzle
 911+ C61C DD CB 00 7E          bit 7,(ix+B_STATE)
 912+ C620 DD CB 00 BE          res 7,(ix+B_STATE)
 913+ C624 20 24                jr nz,.fresh
 914+ C626 DD 6E 01             ld l,(ix+B_X)
 915+ C629 DD 66 02             ld h,(ix+B_X+1)
 916+ C62C DD 75 0F             ld (ix+B_PX),l
 917+ C62F DD 74 10             ld (ix+B_PX+1),h
 918+ C632 DD 6E 03             ld l,(ix+B_Y)
 919+ C635 DD 66 04             ld h,(ix+B_Y+1)
 920+ C638 DD 75 11             ld (ix+B_PY),l
 921+ C63B DD 74 12             ld (ix+B_PY+1),h
 922+ C63E DD 6E 05             ld l,(ix+B_Z)
 923+ C641 DD 66 06             ld h,(ix+B_Z+1)
 924+ C644 DD 75 13             ld (ix+B_PZ),l
 925+ C647 DD 74 14             ld (ix+B_PZ+1),h
 926+ C64A DD 6E 01     .fresh: ld l,(ix+B_X)
 927+ C64D DD 66 02             ld h,(ix+B_X+1)
 928+ C650 22 AC CC             ld (b_x0),hl
 929+ C653 DD 6E 03             ld l,(ix+B_Y)
 930+ C656 DD 66 04             ld h,(ix+B_Y+1)
 931+ C659 22 AE CC             ld (b_y0),hl
 932+ C65C DD 6E 05             ld l,(ix+B_Z)
 933+ C65F DD 66 06             ld h,(ix+B_Z+1)
 934+ C662 22 B0 CC             ld (b_z0),hl
 935+ C665 DD 6E 07             ld l,(ix+B_SIN)
 936+ C668 DD 66 08             ld h,(ix+B_SIN+1)
 937+ C66B 22 B2 CC             ld (b_sin),hl
 938+ C66E DD 6E 09             ld l,(ix+B_COS)
 939+ C671 DD 66 0A             ld h,(ix+B_COS+1)
 940+ C674 22 B4 CC             ld (b_cos),hl
 941+ C677 DD 6E 0B             ld l,(ix+B_SLOPE)
 942+ C67A DD 66 0C             ld h,(ix+B_SLOPE+1)
 943+ C67D 22 B6 CC             ld (b_slope),hl
 944+ C680 DD 6E 0D             ld l,(ix+B_S)
 945+ C683 DD 66 0E             ld h,(ix+B_S+1)
 946+ C686 22 B8 CC             ld (b_send),hl
 947+ C689 DD 7E 19             ld a,(ix+B_DMG)
 948+ C68C 32 DA CC             ld (b_dmg),a
 949+ C68F DD 7E 1A             ld a,(ix+B_KIND)                ; a proximity shell: monsters' hit
 950+ C692 FE 04                cp BK_PROX                      ; boxes PROX_R bigger every way
 951+ C694 3E 00                ld a,0
 952+ C696 20 02                jr nz,.pr
 953+ C698 3E 19                ld a,PROX_R
 954+ C69A 32 E0 CC     .pr:    ld (b_pr),a
 955+ C69D AF                   xor a
 956+ C69E 32 BA CC             ld (b_gnd),a
 957+ C6A1                      ; does it reach the ground this frame?  s = y0 / -slope
 958+ C6A1 2A B6 CC             ld hl,(b_slope)
 959+ C6A4 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 960+ C6A6 28 23                jr z,.nognd
 961+ C6A8 2A B8 CC             ld hl,(b_send)
 962+ C6AB CD 0E CC             call b_y_at                     ; HL = height at the end
 963+ C6AE CB 7C                bit 7,h
 964+ C6B0 28 19                jr z,.nognd
 965+ C6B2 2A B6 CC             ld hl,(b_slope)
 966+ C6B5 CD DE AD             call neg_hl
 967+ C6B8 EB                   ex de,hl                        ; DE = -slope
 968+ C6B9 2A AE CC             ld hl,(b_y0)
 969+ C6BC 7C                   ld a,h                          ; A:HL = y0 << 8
 970+ C6BD 65                   ld h,l
 971+ C6BE 2E 00                ld l,0
 972+ C6C0 CD 61 CC             call udiv24
 973+ C6C3 22 B8 CC             ld (b_send),hl
 974+ C6C6 3E 01                ld a,1
 975+ C6C8 32 BA CC             ld (b_gnd),a
 976+ C6CB              .nognd:
 977+ C6CB                      ; grid walk set-up.  The distance between cell boundaries depends only
 978+ C6CB                      ; on the direction: worked out (two divides) on the round's first step.
 979+ C6CB DD 7E 15             ld a,(ix+B_TDX)
 980+ C6CE DD B6 16             or (ix+B_TDX+1)
 981+ C6D1 20 18                jr nz,.tdok
 982+ C6D3 2A B2 CC             ld hl,(b_sin)
 983+ C6D6 CD E2 C7             call cell_span
 984+ C6D9 DD 75 15             ld (ix+B_TDX),l
 985+ C6DC DD 74 16             ld (ix+B_TDX+1),h
 986+ C6DF 2A B4 CC             ld hl,(b_cos)
 987+ C6E2 CD E2 C7             call cell_span
 988+ C6E5 DD 75 17             ld (ix+B_TDZ),l
 989+ C6E8 DD 74 18             ld (ix+B_TDZ+1),h
 990+ C6EB DD 6E 15     .tdok:  ld l,(ix+B_TDX)
 991+ C6EE DD 66 16             ld h,(ix+B_TDX+1)
 992+ C6F1 22 BF CC             ld (b_tdx),hl
 993+ C6F4 DD 6E 17             ld l,(ix+B_TDZ)
 994+ C6F7 DD 66 18             ld h,(ix+B_TDZ+1)
 995+ C6FA 22 C1 CC             ld (b_tdz),hl
 996+ C6FD 2A B2 CC             ld hl,(b_sin)                   ; first boundary, x then z
 997+ C700 3A AC CC             ld a,(b_x0)
 998+ C703 ED 5B BF CC          ld de,(b_tdx)
 999+ C707 CD F4 C7             call dda_axis
1000+ C70A 22 BB CC             ld (b_tmx),hl
1001+ C70D 32 C3 CC             ld (b_stx),a
1002+ C710 2A B4 CC             ld hl,(b_cos)
1003+ C713 3A B0 CC             ld a,(b_z0)
1004+ C716 ED 5B C1 CC          ld de,(b_tdz)
1005+ C71A CD F4 C7             call dda_axis
1006+ C71D 22 BD CC             ld (b_tmz),hl
1007+ C720 32 C4 CC             ld (b_stz),a
1008+ C723 3A AD CC             ld a,(b_x0+1)
1009+ C726 32 C5 CC             ld (b_ci),a
1010+ C729 3A B1 CC             ld a,(b_z0+1)
1011+ C72C 32 C6 CC             ld (b_cj),a
1012+ C72F 21 00 00             ld hl,0
1013+ C732 22 C7 CC             ld (b_sin_),hl                  ; s where the path enters this cell
1014+ C735              .cell:  ; s_out = min(tmx, tmz, send)
1015+ C735 2A BB CC             ld hl,(b_tmx)
1016+ C738 ED 5B BD CC          ld de,(b_tmz)
1017+ C73C CD 4D CC             call min_u
1018+ C73F ED 5B B8 CC          ld de,(b_send)
1019+ C743 CD 4D CC             call min_u
1020+ C746 22 C9 CC             ld (b_sout),hl
1021+ C749 CD 28 C8             call cell_hit
1022+ C74C DA D0 C7             jp c,.hit
1023+ C74F 2A C9 CC             ld hl,(b_sout)                  ; path ends in this cell?
1024+ C752 ED 5B B8 CC          ld de,(b_send)
1025+ C756 B7                   or a
1026+ C757 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1027+ C759 28 51                jr z,.end
1028+ C75B 2A BB CC             ld hl,(b_tmx)                   ; step into the next cell
1029+ C75E ED 5B BD CC          ld de,(b_tmz)
1030+ C762 B7                   or a
1031+ C763 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1032+ C765 30 1A                jr nc,.stz
1033+ C767 2A BB CC             ld hl,(b_tmx)                   ; x boundary first
1034+ C76A 22 C7 CC             ld (b_sin_),hl
1035+ C76D ED 5B BF CC          ld de,(b_tdx)
1036+ C771 CD 5B CC             call add_sat
1037+ C774 22 BB CC             ld (b_tmx),hl
1038+ C777 3A C3 CC             ld a,(b_stx)
1039+ C77A 21 C5 CC             ld hl,b_ci
1040+ C77D 86                   add a,(hl)
1041+ C77E 77                   ld (hl),a
1042+ C77F 18 18                jr .chk
1043+ C781 2A BD CC     .stz:   ld hl,(b_tmz)                   ; z boundary first
1044+ C784 22 C7 CC             ld (b_sin_),hl
1045+ C787 ED 5B C1 CC          ld de,(b_tdz)
1046+ C78B CD 5B CC             call add_sat
1047+ C78E 22 BD CC             ld (b_tmz),hl
1048+ C791 3A C4 CC             ld a,(b_stz)
1049+ C794 21 C6 CC             ld hl,b_cj
1050+ C797 86                   add a,(hl)
1051+ C798 77                   ld (hl),a
1052+ C799 3A C5 CC     .chk:   ld a,(b_ci)
1053+ C79C FE 40                cp GRID
1054+ C79E 30 07                jr nc,.gone                     ; left the map
1055+ C7A0 3A C6 CC             ld a,(b_cj)
1056+ C7A3 FE 40                cp GRID
1057+ C7A5 38 8E                jr c,.cell
1058+ C7A7 DD 36 00 00  .gone:  ld (ix+B_STATE),0
1059+ C7AB C9                   ret
1060+ C7AC 2A B8 CC     .end:   ld hl,(b_send)
1061+ C7AF 3A BA CC             ld a,(b_gnd)
1062+ C7B2 B7                   or a
1063+ C7B3 28 09                jr z,.fly
1064+ C7B5 CD 1B CC             call b_move                     ; reached the ground
1065+ C7B8 DD 36 00 02          ld (ix+B_STATE),2
1066+ C7BC 18 1C                jr .burst
1067+ C7BE CD 1B CC     .fly:   call b_move                     ; whole step, nothing hit
1068+ C7C1 DD 7E 02             ld a,(ix+B_X+1)                 ; still over the map?
1069+ C7C4 FE 40                cp GRID
1070+ C7C6 30 DF                jr nc,.gone
1071+ C7C8 DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+B_Z+1)
1072+ C7CB FE 40                cp GRID
1073+ C7CD 30 D8                jr nc,.gone
1074+ C7CF C9                   ret

1076+ C7D3 CD 1B CC             call b_move
1077+ C7D6 DD 36 00 02          ld (ix+B_STATE),2               ; show the impact, then free it

1080+ C7DE C8                   ret z

1082+ C7E2
1083+ C7E2              ; HL = S7.8 direction component -> HL = path distance s between cell
1084+ C7E2              ; boundaries along that axis, 256 * 256 / |c|  ($FFFF = never)
1085+ C7E2              cell_span:

1087+ C7E3 B5                   or l

1096+ C7F4
1097+ C7F4              ; HL = S7.8 direction component, A = low byte of the start coordinate,
1098+ C7F4              ; DE = cell_span for it.  -> HL = s to the first cell boundary ($FFFF =
1099+ C7F4              ; never), A = cell step (+1, -1, 0).  s = distance * span / 256, by MUL.
1100+ C7F4              dda_axis:

1103+ C7F6 B5                   or l

1109+ C7FF 32 D9 CC             ld (dd_sg),a
1110+ C802 17                   rla
1111+ C803 38 07                jr c,.neg
1112+ C805 AF                   xor a                           ; moving up: 256 - (x & 255)
1113+ C806 90                   sub b
1114+ C807 20 04                jr nz,.part
1115+ C809 EB                   ex de,hl                        ; (x & 255) = 0: a whole cell
1116+ C80A 18 12                jr .sgn
1117+ C80C 78           .neg:   ld a,b                          ; moving down: x & 255
1118+ C80D 4F           .part:  ld c,a                          ; HL = (dist * span) >> 8
1119+ C80E D5                   push de
1120+ C80F 57                   ld d,a
1121+ C810 ED 30                mul d,e                         ; dist * span low
1122+ C812 7A                   ld a,d
1123+ C813 D1                   pop de
1124+ C814 5A                   ld e,d
1125+ C815 51                   ld d,c
1126+ C816 ED 30                mul d,e                         ; dist * span high
1127+ C818 83                   add a,e
1128+ C819 6F                   ld l,a
1129+ C81A 7A                   ld a,d
1130+ C81B CE 00                adc a,0
1131+ C81D 67                   ld h,a
1132+ C81E 3A D9 CC     .sgn:   ld a,(dd_sg)

1134+ C822 3E 01                ld a,1
1135+ C824 D0                   ret nc
1136+ C825 3E FF                ld a,-1
1137+ C827 C9                   ret
1138+ C828
1139+ C828              ; Carry set if the path hits something in cell (b_ci, b_cj) between
1140+ C828              ; s = b_sin_ and b_sout; b_shit = where.

1219+ C8D4              ; Cell (b_ci, b_cj) marked as holding zombies or special monsters: the
1220+ C8D4              ; nearest whose box (ZHIT_R either side, ZHIT_H tall from its feet, +21;

1353+ C9F8              ; Zombie IY (index A) takes C damage; when its hit points run out it is

1393+ CA45              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1394+ CA45              ; Rocket, missile or Bofors shell IX has stopped (at B_X, B_Y, B_Z): its
1395+ CA45              ; damage (rb_dmg: BLAST_DMG, MISSILE_DMG, BOFORS_DMG) to every zombie, human

1638+ CC4D              min_u:

1640+ CC4E ED 52                sbc hl,de

1642+ CC51 D8                   ret c
1643+ CC52 EB                   ex de,hl
1644+ CC53 C9                   ret
1645+ CC54              max_u:
1646+ CC54 B7                   or a
1647+ CC55 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1648+ CC57 19                   add hl,de
1649+ CC58 D0                   ret nc

1651+ CC5A C9                   ret
1652+ CC5B
1653+ CC5B              ; HL = HL + DE, saturating at $FFFF
1654+ CC5B              add_sat:

1656+ CC5C D0                   ret nc
1657+ CC5D 21 FF FF             ld hl,$FFFF

1659+ CC61
1660+ CC61              ; A:HL / DE -> HL, unsigned, saturating at $FFFF.  DE < $8000.
1661+ CC61              udiv24:
1662+ CC61 47                   ld b,a
1663+ CC62 7A                   ld a,d                          ; quotient over $FFFF?
1664+ CC63 B7                   or a
1665+ CC64 20 08                jr nz,.go
1666+ CC66 78                   ld a,b
1667+ CC67 BB                   cp e
1668+ CC68 38 04                jr c,.go
1669+ CC6A 21 FF FF             ld hl,$FFFF
1670+ CC6D C9                   ret
1671+ CC6E 78           .go:    ld a,b                          ; A:H:L, shifted left 24 times;
1672+ CC6F 0E 18                ld c,24                         ; quotient bits come in at the bottom
1673+ CC71 DD E5                push ix
1674+ CC73 DD 21 00 00          ld ix,0                         ; remainder
1675+ CC77 29           .lp:    add hl,hl
1676+ CC78 17                   rla
1677+ CC79 F5                   push af
1678+ CC7A DD 29                add ix,ix
1679+ CC7C F1                   pop af
1680+ CC7D 30 02                jr nc,.n0
1681+ CC7F DD 23                inc ix

1683+ CC82 DD E5                push ix

1685+ CC85 B7                   or a

1687+ CC88 38 07                jr c,.n1

1689+ CC8B DD E1                pop ix
1690+ CC8D E1                   pop hl
1691+ CC8E 2C                   inc l
1692+ CC8F 18 01                jr .nx
1693+ CC91 E1           .n1:    pop hl
1694+ CC92 0D           .nx:    dec c
1695+ CC93 20 E2                jr nz,.lp

   1+ C000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ C000              ; Z-POLICE - main menu (8K page PAGE_MENU, runs at $C000 in slot 6)
   3+ C000              ;
   4+ C000              ; Shown after start-up.  Layer 2 is hidden and the menu is drawn on the ULA
   5+ C000              ; screen with opaque paper.  Every frame it advances the random number
   6+ C000              ; generator (zrand), so the game doesn't start from the same seed each time
   7+ C000              ; (the wait for a key press varies), then reads the keys:
   8+ C000              ;   1  New game   -> newGame (zpolice.asm): level 1
   9+ C000              ;   2  Continue   -> the save game (made by save and quit, from the pause:
  10+ C000              ;                 game_pause), its level at its wave (load_save,
  11+ C000              ;                 continue_game, zpolice.asm); NO SAVED GAME if there's none
  12+ C000              ;   9  Redefine keys -> keydef_screen (keydef.asm)
  13+ C000              ; game_over_screen (the landing pad destroyed) shows GAME OVER for 10
  14+ C000              ; seconds, then returns here.
  15+ C000              ;==============================================================================
  16+ C000
  17+ C000
  18+ C000              main_menu:
  19+ C000 ED 91 57 0C          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_INPUT     ; the input page at $E000 (this page: $C000)
  20+ C004 CD CC A0             call menu_clear
  21+ C007 21 64 C4             ld hl,menu_text                 ; title and options
  22+ C00A 7E           .txt:   ld a,(hl)
  23+ C00B FE FF                cp $FF
  24+ C00D 28 0B                jr z,.loop
  25+ C00F 57                   ld d,a                          ; row
  26+ C010 23                   inc hl
  27+ C011 5E                   ld e,(hl)                       ; column
  28+ C012 23                   inc hl
  29+ C013 4E                   ld c,(hl)                       ; attribute
  30+ C014 23                   inc hl
  31+ C015 CD 02 A1             call menu_print                 ; HL -> the text, past it after
  32+ C018 18 F0                jr .txt
  33+ C01A CD 4C A1     .loop:  call menu_frame                 ; one video frame
  34+ C01D CD B5 2C             call zrand                      ; stir the random number generator
  35+ C020 CD 00 E0             call kbd_scan
  36+ C023 21 01 03             ld hl,KB_1
  37+ C026 CD F1 E0             call key_code_pressed
  38+ C029 C2 B3 A7             jp nz,newGame
  39+ C02C 21 02 04             ld hl,KB_9                      ; redefine keys
  40+ C02F CD F1 E0             call key_code_pressed
  41+ C032 C2 01 AA             jp nz,keydef_go                 ; (zpolice.asm)
  42+ C035 21 02 03             ld hl,KB_2                      ; continue the save game
  43+ C038 CD F1 E0             call key_code_pressed
  44+ C03B 28 DD                jr z,.loop
  45+ C03D CD 33 AB             call load_save                  ; (zpolice.asm; pages the file
  46+ C040 D2 6F AB             jp nc,continue_game             ; handler in)
  47+ C043 ED 91 57 0C          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_INPUT     ; none (or not usable): say so
  48+ C047 11 09 12             ld de,18<<8 | 9
  49+ C04A 0E 0B                ld c,MENU_BAD
  50+ C04C 21 AE C4             ld hl,txt_no_save
  51+ C04F CD 02 A1             call menu_print
  52+ C052 18 C6                jr .loop
  53+ C054
  54+ C054              ; The landing pad has been destroyed: GAME OVER for GAME_OVER_FR frames
  55+ C054              ; (10 seconds), then the main menu
  56+ C054              game_over_screen:
  57+ C054 31 85 B5             ld sp,stack_top
  58+ C057 CD CC A0             call menu_clear
  59+ C05A 16 0B                ld d,11
  60+ C05C 1E 0B                ld e,11
  61+ C05E 0E 0A                ld c,MENU_TITLE
  62+ C060 21 79 C0             ld hl,txt_game_over
  63+ C063 CD 02 A1             call menu_print
  64+ C066 01 F4 01             ld bc,GAME_OVER_FR
  65+ C069 C5           .w:     push bc
  66+ C06A CD 4C A1             call menu_frame
  67+ C06D CD B5 2C             call zrand                      ; (keep stirring the seed)
  68+ C070 C1                   pop bc
  69+ C071 0B                   dec bc
  70+ C072 78                   ld a,b
  71+ C073 B1                   or c
  72+ C074 20 F3                jr nz,.w
  73+ C076 C3 00 C0             jp main_menu

  76+ C079 47 41 4D 45          db "GAME OVER", 0
  76+ C07D 20 4F 56 45
  76+ C081 52 00
  77+ C083
  78+ C083              ; The level's last wave is wiped out: LEVEL COMPLETE, until SPACE is pressed
  79+ C083              ; (newly: one held down for firing as the level ended doesn't count), then
  80+ C083              ; the load-out screen and the next level (next_level, zpolice.asm)
  81+ C083              LD_PROMPT_ROW   equ 23              ; "PRESS SPACE TO CONTINUE" (under the

  95+ C0A0 CD 85 37             call hum_unload_all             ; (human.asm) those aboard: rescued

  97+ C0A6 11 04 17             ld de,LD_PROMPT_ROW<<8 | 4

 115+ C0DC 53 20 53 50

 119+ C0F0              ; End of a level: every slot's weapon gets its full load again (wpn_cfg,
 120+ C0F0              ; WCFG_LOAD; AMMO_INF for the guns, 0 for an empty slot), and the screen
 121+ C0F0              ; says so
 122+ C0F0              rearm_all:

 124+ C0F4 06 05                ld b,WPN_SLOTS

 136+ C10F C3 02 A1             jp menu_print

 139+ C112              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 140+ C112              ; Salvage, at the end of a level: each human saved this level (saved) adds
 141+ C112              ; a salvage point (salvage_points).  Every point total in salvage_tab that
 142+ C112              ; the new total reaches (passed over from the old one) gives its rewards: a
 143+ C112              ; weapon unlocked (one more of that type in wpn_stock, for the load-out page)
 144+ C112              ; and / or (SA_* flags) a full repair (vtol_hp = VTOL_HP) or the booster
 145+ C112              ; module (fitted at once, its tank full).  After the table's last total,
 146+ C112              ; SALVAGE_TAIL, every SALVAGE_STEP more points is a full repair.  Shown on
 147+ C112              ; the LEVEL COMPLETE screen: the humans saved, the salvage, and a line for
 148+ C112              ; each reward (rows SA_ROW0..SA_ROW_MAX; any more aren't shown).
 149+ C112              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 150+ C112              SA_REPAIR       equ 1               ; salvage_tab rewards (flags): full repair,
 151+ C112              SA_BOOSTER      equ 2               ;   the booster module
 152+ C112              SALVAGE_TAIL    equ 145
 153+ C112              SALVAGE_STEP    equ 5
 154+ C112              SA_ROW0         equ 16
 155+ C112              SA_ROW_MAX      equ 22
 156+ C112              salvage_award:
 157+ C112 16 0B                ld d,11                         ; HUMANS SAVED n
 158+ C114 1E 07                ld e,7
 159+ C116 0E 0F                ld c,MENU_ATTR
 160+ C118 21 4A C3             ld hl,txt_sa_saved
 161+ C11B CD 02 A1             call menu_print
 162+ C11E 3A 1F 39             ld a,(saved)
 163+ C121 6F                   ld l,a
 164+ C122 26 00                ld h,0
 165+ C124 11 15 0B             ld de,11<<8 | 21
 166+ C127 CD 0B C2             call menu_num
 167+ C12A 2A 48 AE             ld hl,(salvage_points)          ; the new total
 168+ C12D 22 64 C2             ld (sa_old),hl
 169+ C130 3A 1F 39             ld a,(saved)
 170+ C133 ED 31                add hl,a
 171+ C135 22 48 AE             ld (salvage_points),hl
 172+ C138 16 0C                ld d,12                         ; SALVAGE n
 173+ C13A 1E 07                ld e,7
 174+ C13C 0E 0F                ld c,MENU_ATTR
 175+ C13E 21 57 C3             ld hl,txt_sa_points
 176+ C141 CD 02 A1             call menu_print
 177+ C144 2A 48 AE             ld hl,(salvage_points)
 178+ C147 11 15 0C             ld de,12<<8 | 21
 179+ C14A CD 0B C2             call menu_num
 180+ C14D 3E 10                ld a,SA_ROW0
 181+ C14F 32 66 C2             ld (sa_row),a
 182+ C152 21 67 C2             ld hl,salvage_tab
 183+ C155 5E           .lp:    ld e,(hl)                       ; DE = its points total
 184+ C156 23                   inc hl
 185+ C157 56                   ld d,(hl)
 186+ C158 23                   inc hl
 187+ C159 7A                   ld a,d
 188+ C15A A3                   and e
 189+ C15B 3C                   inc a
 190+ C15C 28 0F                jr z,.tail                      ; ($FFFF: the end)
 191+ C15E 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; B = weapon, C = SA_* rewards
 192+ C15F 47                   ld b,a
 193+ C160 23                   inc hl
 194+ C161 4E                   ld c,(hl)
 195+ C162 23                   inc hl
 196+ C163 E5                   push hl
 197+ C164 CD 8A C1             call sa_reached
 198+ C167 DC 9C C1             call c,sa_reward
 199+ C16A E1                   pop hl
 200+ C16B 18 E8                jr .lp
 201+ C16D 11 91 00     .tail:  ld de,SALVAGE_TAIL              ; then a repair every SALVAGE_STEP
 202+ C170 21 05 00     .tl:    ld hl,SALVAGE_STEP
 203+ C173 19                   add hl,de
 204+ C174 EB                   ex de,hl
 205+ C175 2A 48 AE             ld hl,(salvage_points)
 206+ C178 B7                   or a
 207+ C179 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 208+ C17B D8                   ret c                           ; beyond the new total
 209+ C17C 06 00                ld b,WPN_NONE
 210+ C17E 0E 01                ld c,SA_REPAIR
 211+ C180 D5                   push de
 212+ C181 CD 8A C1             call sa_reached
 213+ C184 DC 9C C1             call c,sa_reward
 214+ C187 D1                   pop de
 215+ C188 18 E6                jr .tl
 216+ C18A
 217+ C18A              ; Carry set if total DE is reached this time: sa_old < DE <= salvage_points.
 218+ C18A              ; Preserves BC, DE.
 219+ C18A              sa_reached:
 220+ C18A 2A 64 C2             ld hl,(sa_old)
 221+ C18D B7                   or a
 222+ C18E ED 52                sbc hl,de
 223+ C190 38 02                jr c,.lo
 224+ C192 B7                   or a                            ; old >= DE: reached before
 225+ C193 C9                   ret
 226+ C194 2A 48 AE     .lo:    ld hl,(salvage_points)
 227+ C197 B7                   or a
 228+ C198 ED 52                sbc hl,de                       ; new - DE: no carry if reached
 229+ C19A 3F                   ccf
 230+ C19B C9                   ret
 231+ C19C
 232+ C19C              ; Reward: B = weapon type (WPN_NONE: none) unlocked, C = SA_* flags: a full
 233+ C19C              ; repair, the booster module.  A line on the screen for each.
 234+ C19C              sa_reward:
 235+ C19C 78                   ld a,b
 236+ C19D B7                   or a
 237+ C19E 28 2F                jr z,.rep

 245+ C1AF 35                   dec (hl)                        ; (at most 255)
 246+ C1B0 C5           .nm:    push bc

 255+ C1BC E5                   push hl
 256+ C1BD 21 5F C3             ld hl,txt_sa_new
 257+ C1C0 1E 07                ld e,7

 260+ C1C6 1E 0B                ld e,11
 261+ C1C8 CD FF C1             call sa_line.at
 262+ C1CB CD FA C1             call sa_next
 263+ C1CE C1                   pop bc
 264+ C1CF CB 49        .rep:   bit 1,c                         ; the booster module: fitted, full
 265+ C1D1 28 17                jr z,.rp

 267+ C1D3 3E 01                ld a,1
 268+ C1D5 32 B9 AE             ld (boost_fitted),a

 270+ C1DA 32 BA AE             ld (boost_fuel),a

 272+ C1DE 21 6F C3             ld hl,txt_sa_booster
 273+ C1E1 1E 07                ld e,7
 274+ C1E3 CD FF C1             call sa_line
 275+ C1E6 CD FA C1             call sa_next
 276+ C1E9 C1                   pop bc
 277+ C1EA CB 41        .rp:    bit 0,c
 278+ C1EC C8                   ret z
 279+ C1ED                      ASSERT SA_REPAIR == 1<<0

 281+ C1EF 32 40 AE             ld (vtol_hp),a

 284+ C1F7 CD FF C1             call sa_line
 285+ C1FA                      ; fall through
 286+ C1FA
 287+ C1FA              ; The next reward line
 288+ C1FA              sa_next:
 289+ C1FA 21 66 C2             ld hl,sa_row
 290+ C1FD 34                   inc (hl)
 291+ C1FE C9                   ret

 293+ C1FF              ; HL -> text, E = column: print it on the reward row (if there is room)

# file opened: level_load.asm
   1+ C000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ C000              ; Z-POLICE - level loading (8K page PAGE_LEVEL, game logic: runs at $C000 in
   3+ C000              ; slot 6; it pages the file handler, PAGE_FILE, into slot 7)
   4+ C000              ;
   5+ C000              ; Each level is a file, ZPDATA/LEVELnn.ZPL, naming its city, ZPDATA/CITYnn.ZPC
   6+ C000              ; (both made by gentables.py; the formats are described there).  A city is
   7+ C000              ; played for several levels.  load_level reads both:
   8+ C000              ;   * the level record into level_cur (levels.asm): city name, zombie and
   9+ C000              ;     wave counts, the draw distance, ...;
  10+ C000              ;   * the city into the city bank (page 7, slot 3): city_col at $6000 and
  11+ C000              ;     city_map at $7000, one 8K read;
  12+ C000              ;   * the level's own map changes (x, z, cell code, city_col), for hand-made
  13+ C000              ;     layouts;
  14+ C000              ;   * the city's palette and sky colour into the Layer 2 palette;
  15+ C000              ;   * view_r / dot_r from the draw distance (2..VIEW_R tiles).
  16+ C000              ; Loading the map also clears every cell's zombie and human marks.  The
  17+ C000              ; files must be in the ZPDATA folder beside zpolice.nex on the SD card.
  18+ C000              ;==============================================================================
  19+ C000
  20+ C000              ; Level file
  21+ C000              LVF_CITY        equ 5               ; city number
  22+ C000              LVF_REC         equ 6               ; the level record (LV_SIZE bytes)
  23+ C000              LVF_NCHG        equ LVF_REC+LV_SIZE ; number of map changes,
  24+ C000              LVF_CHG         equ LVF_NCHG+1      ; then x, z, cell code, city_col each
  25+ C000              LVF_CHG_MAX     equ 64
  26+ C000              LVF_MAX         equ LVF_CHG+4*LVF_CHG_MAX
  27+ C000              ; City file
  28+ C000              CTF_SKY         equ 14              ; sky colour: RRRGGGBB, low blue bit
  29+ C000              CTF_PAL         equ 32              ; the palette, 256 pairs
  30+ C000              CTF_DATA        equ CTF_PAL+512     ; then city_col, city_map
  31+ C000              LEVEL_VERSION   equ 2               ; the files' format versions (+4): the
  32+ C000              CITY_VERSION    equ 1               ;   level file's 2 (specials by wave)
  33+ C000              LDERR_BADFILE   equ 255             ; error code: not a valid file
  34+ C000
  35+ C000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  36+ C000              ; A = level (1..99): load it.  Carry set on failure, A = the esxDOS error
  37+ C000              ; code or LDERR_BADFILE.  Leaves PAGE_FILE in slot 7.
  38+ C000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  39+ C000              load_level:
  40+ C000 0E 2F                ld c,'0'-1                      ; the level's two digits
  41+ C002 0C           .tens:  inc c
  42+ C003 D6 0A                sub 10
  43+ C005 30 FB                jr nc,.tens
  44+ C007 C6 3A                add a,'0'+10
  45+ C009 32 2F C1             ld (ll_level+13),a
  46+ C00C 79                   ld a,c
  47+ C00D 32 2E C1             ld (ll_level+12),a

  54+ C021 21 4F C1             ld hl,ll_lbuf
  55+ C024 11 47 C1             ld de,ll_lmagic
  56+ C027 0E 02                ld c,LEVEL_VERSION
  57+ C029 CD 11 C1             call ll_check
  58+ C02C D8                   ret c
  59+ C02D 21 55 C1             ld hl,ll_lbuf+LVF_REC           ; its record
  60+ C030 11 5B A0             ld de,level_cur

  64+ C03B 0E 2F                ld c,'0'-1

  66+ C03E D6 0A                sub 10
  67+ C040 30 FB                jr nc,.ctens
  68+ C042 C6 3A                add a,'0'+10
  69+ C044 32 41 C1             ld (ll_city+12),a
  70+ C047 79                   ld a,c
  71+ C048 32 40 C1             ld (ll_city+11),a
  72+ C04B 21 35 C1             ld hl,ll_city

  77+ C057 01 20 02             ld bc,CTF_DATA

  81+ C063 21 00 60             ld hl,city_col                  ; city bank, in one read
  82+ C066 01 00 20             ld bc,2*GRID*GRID

  84+ C06C DA 07 C1             jp c,.cerr
  85+ C06F 78                   ld a,b                          ; all of it?
  86+ C070 FE 20                cp high (2*GRID*GRID)
  87+ C072 3E FF                ld a,LDERR_BADFILE
  88+ C074 37                   scf
  89+ C075 C2 07 C1             jp nz,.cerr
  90+ C078 3A 17 E1             ld a,(fh_handle)

  92+ C07E D8                   ret c
  93+ C07F 21 C7 C2             ld hl,ll_cbuf
  94+ C082 11 4B C1             ld de,ll_cmagic
  95+ C085 0E 01                ld c,CITY_VERSION
  96+ C087 CD 11 C1             call ll_check
  97+ C08A D8                   ret c
  98+ C08B                      ; the level's map changes
  99+ C08B 3A C6 C1             ld a,(ll_lbuf+LVF_NCHG)
 100+ C08E FE 41                cp LVF_CHG_MAX+1
 101+ C090 3E FF                ld a,LDERR_BADFILE
 102+ C092 3F                   ccf
 103+ C093 D8                   ret c
 104+ C094 3A C6 C1             ld a,(ll_lbuf+LVF_NCHG)
 105+ C097 B7                   or a
 106+ C098 28 2C                jr z,.pal
 107+ C09A 47                   ld b,a
 108+ C09B DD 21 C7 C1          ld ix,ll_lbuf+LVF_CHG
 109+ C09F DD 6E 01     .chg:   ld l,(ix+1)                     ; row z
 110+ C0A2 26 00                ld h,0
 111+ C0A4                      ROWOFS
 111+ C0A4 29          >        add hl,hl
 111+ C0A5 29          >        add hl,hl
 111+ C0A6 29          >        add hl,hl
 111+ C0A7 29          >        add hl,hl
 111+ C0A8 29          >        add hl,hl
 111+ C0A9 29          >        add hl,hl
 112+ C0AA DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+0)                     ; column x
 113+ C0AD ED 31                add hl,a
 114+ C0AF 11 00 70             ld de,city_map
 115+ C0B2 19                   add hl,de
 116+ C0B3 DD 7E 02             ld a,(ix+2)
 117+ C0B6 77                   ld (hl),a                       ; the cell code
 118+ C0B7 11 00 F0             ld de,city_col-city_map
 119+ C0BA 19                   add hl,de
 120+ C0BB DD 7E 03             ld a,(ix+3)
 121+ C0BE 77                   ld (hl),a                       ; and its colour
 122+ C0BF 11 04 00             ld de,4
 123+ C0C2 DD 19                add ix,de
 124+ C0C4 10 D9                djnz .chg
 125+ C0C6              .pal:   ; the city's palette (the first Layer 2 palette), then its sky
 126+ C0C6 ED 91 43 10          nextreg $43,%00010000           ; edit Layer 2 palette 0, auto-inc
 127+ C0CA ED 91 40 00          nextreg $40,0
 128+ C0CE 21 E7 C2             ld hl,ll_cbuf+CTF_PAL
 129+ C0D1 06 00                ld b,0                          ; 256 entries
 130+ C0D3 7E           .pl:    ld a,(hl)
 131+ C0D4 23                   inc hl
 132+ C0D5 ED 92 44             nextreg $44,a                   ; RRRGGGBB
 133+ C0D8 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 134+ C0D9 23                   inc hl
 135+ C0DA ED 92 44             nextreg $44,a                   ; low blue bit
 136+ C0DD 10 F4                djnz .pl
 137+ C0DF ED 91 40 03          nextreg $40,SKY_COL
 138+ C0E3 3A D5 C2             ld a,(ll_cbuf+CTF_SKY)
 139+ C0E6 ED 92 44             nextreg $44,a
 140+ C0E9 3A D6 C2             ld a,(ll_cbuf+CTF_SKY+1)
 141+ C0EC ED 92 44             nextreg $44,a
 142+ C0EF                      ; the draw distance, 2..VIEW_R tiles; the ground dots one less
 143+ C0EF 3A 67 A0             ld a,(level_cur+LV_VIEW_R)
 144+ C0F2 FE 02                cp 2
 145+ C0F4 30 02                jr nc,.v1
 146+ C0F6 3E 02                ld a,2
 147+ C0F8 FE 07        .v1:    cp VIEW_R+1
 148+ C0FA 38 02                jr c,.v2
 149+ C0FC 3E 06                ld a,VIEW_R
 150+ C0FE 32 3E AE     .v2:    ld (view_r),a
 151+ C101 3D                   dec a
 152+ C102 32 3F AE             ld (dot_r),a
 153+ C105 B7                   or a                            ; carry clear: loaded
 154+ C106 C9                   ret
 155+ C107 F5           .cerr:  push af                         ; an error with the city file open:
 156+ C108 3A 17 E1             ld a,(fh_handle)                ; close it, keep the error
 157+ C10B CD 25 E0             call fh_close
 158+ C10E F1                   pop af
 159+ C10F 37                   scf
 160+ C110 C9                   ret
 161+ C111
 162+ C111              ; HL -> a file's first bytes, DE -> its 4-byte magic: carry clear if they
 163+ C111              ; match and the version byte after them is C (LEVEL_ / CITY_VERSION), else carry set
 164+ C111              ; and A = LDERR_BADFILE
 165+ C111              ll_check:
 166+ C111 06 04                ld b,4
 167+ C113 1A           .lp:    ld a,(de)
 168+ C114 BE                   cp (hl)

 174+ C11C B9                   cp c
 175+ C11D C8                   ret z                           ; (carry clear)

 177+ C120 37                   scf
 178+ C121 C9                   ret

# file opened: loadout.asm
   1+ C000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ C000              ; Z-POLICE - load-out screen (8K page PAGE_LOADOUT, runs at $C000 in slot 6)
   3+ C000              ;
   4+ C000              ; Before the first level (newGame) and between levels, after LEVEL COMPLETE
   5+ C000              ; (loadout_go pages it in).  On the
   6+ C000              ; left, the store: every weapon type unlocked (wpn_unlocked) with the number
   7+ C000              ; in store (wpn_stock), even 0 when the only one is fitted.  On the right,
   8+ C000              ; the VTOL's 5 weapon slots and what is fitted in each.
   9+ C000              ;   1-5    select a slot (yellow)
  10+ C000              ;   Q / A  move the highlight up / down the store list.  The highlighted
  11+ C000              ;          weapon is green if it can be fitted in the selected slot (the
  12+ C000              ;          slot allows it, wpn_fits, and one is in store), red if not; the
  13+ C000              ;          rest of the list is white.
  14+ C000              ;   SPACE  fit the highlighted weapon in the selected slot, if it can be:
  15+ C000              ;          one comes out of the store, with a full load (WCFG_LOAD), and
  16+ C000              ;          whatever was fitted there goes back into the store
  17+ C000              ;   0      done: start the level (next_level)
  18+ C000              ; Uses the resident text routines (text.asm) and the keys page in slot 7.
  19+ C000              ;==============================================================================
  20+ C000
  21+ C000              LO_ROW0         equ 5               ; first row of the lists
  22+ C000              LO_NAME_COL     equ 1               ; store: names,
  23+ C000              LO_CNT_COL      equ 13              ;   numbers in store
  24+ C000              LO_SLOT_COL     equ 18              ; slots: number, then the weapon
  25+ C000              LO_NAME_LEN     equ 11              ; names are at most this long
  26+ C000              LO_SLOT_STEP    equ 2               ; rows between the slots
  27+ C000
  28+ C000              loadout_screen:
  29+ C000 31 85 B5             ld sp,stack_top
  30+ C003 ED 91 57 0C          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_INPUT     ; the input page at $E000
  31+ C007 CD CC A0             call menu_clear

  33+ C00D 7E           .txt:   ld a,(hl)
  34+ C00E FE FF                cp $FF
  35+ C010 28 0B                jr z,.list
  36+ C012 57                   ld d,a
  37+ C013 23                   inc hl
  38+ C014 5E                   ld e,(hl)
  39+ C015 23                   inc hl
  40+ C016 4E                   ld c,(hl)
  41+ C017 23                   inc hl
  42+ C018 CD 02 A1             call menu_print
  43+ C01B 18 F0                jr .txt

  45+ C020 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; type order

  66+ C045 CD 13 C1     .draw:  call lo_draw
  67+ C048 CD 4C A1     .loop:  call menu_frame
  68+ C04B CD B5 2C             call zrand                      ; (keep stirring the seed)
  69+ C04E CD 00 E0             call kbd_scan
  70+ C051 21 01 04             ld hl,KB_0                      ; done
  71+ C054 CD F1 E0             call key_code_pressed
  72+ C057 C2 41 A8             jp nz,next_level
  73+ C05A 21 01 02             ld hl,KB_Q                      ; up the list
  74+ C05D CD F1 E0             call key_code_pressed

 119+ C0BA              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 120+ C0BA              ; Carry set if store weapon A can go in the selected slot (lo_slot): the
