# file opened: keydef.asm
   1+ C000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ C000              ; Z-POLICE - redefine keys screen (8K page PAGE_KEYDEF, runs at $C000 in
   3+ C000              ; slot 6)
   4+ C000              ;
   5+ C000              ; From the main menu (option 9, keydef_go pages it in).  Lists every control
   6+ C000              ; (K_ACTIONS of them) with its key (key_defs) and its joystick input
   7+ C000              ; (joy_defs, "-" for none; input.asm).
   8+ C000              ;   Q / A  move the highlight up / down the list (white; highlighted green)
   9+ C000              ;   SPACE  "PRESS A KEY OR JOYSTICK", a second's pause, then the next input
  10+ C000              ;          becomes the highlighted control's key, or its joystick input if
  11+ C000              ;          it is a joystick's, unless another control already uses it: then
  12+ C000              ;          "KEY ALREADY IN USE" (for a second) and no change
  13+ C000              ;   0      every key and joystick input back to its default (key_defaults,
  14+ C000              ;          joy_defaults)
  15+ C000              ;   1      back to the main menu (menu_go)
  16+ C000              ; These keys are fixed (key_code_pressed), whatever the controls are set to.
  17+ C000              ; Uses the resident text routines (text.asm) and the input page in slot 7.
  18+ C000              ;==============================================================================
  19+ C000
  20+ C000              KD_ROW0         equ 1               ; first row of the list (under the title)
  21+ C000              KD_NAME_COL     equ 1               ; control names,
  22+ C000              KD_KEY_COL      equ 16              ; their keys,
  23+ C000              KD_KEY_LEN      equ 7               ;   (key names at most this long)
  24+ C000              KD_JOY_COL      equ 24              ; their joystick inputs
  25+ C000              KD_JOY_LEN      equ 8               ;   (joystick names at most this long)
  26+ C000                      ASSERT KD_KEY_COL + KD_KEY_LEN < KD_JOY_COL && KD_JOY_COL + KD_JOY_LEN <= 32
  27+ C000              KD_MSG_ROW      equ 21              ; messages
  28+ C000              KD_PAUSE        equ 50              ; a second of 50 Hz frames
  29+ C000                      ASSERT KD_ROW0 + K_ACTIONS <= KD_MSG_ROW
  30+ C000
  31+ C000              keydef_screen:
  32+ C000 31 85 B5             ld sp,stack_top
  33+ C003 ED 91 57 0C          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_INPUT     ; the input page at $E000
  34+ C007 CD CC A0             call menu_clear
  35+ C00A 21 DD C1             ld hl,kd_text                   ; title and help
  36+ C00D 7E           .txt:   ld a,(hl)
  37+ C00E FE FF                cp $FF
  38+ C010 28 0B                jr z,.go
  39+ C012 57                   ld d,a
  40+ C013 23                   inc hl
  41+ C014 5E                   ld e,(hl)
  42+ C015 23                   inc hl
  43+ C016 4E                   ld c,(hl)
  44+ C017 23                   inc hl
  45+ C018 CD 02 A1             call menu_print
  46+ C01B 18 F0                jr .txt
  47+ C01D AF           .go:    xor a
  48+ C01E 32 B8 C5             ld (kd_cur),a
  49+ C021 CD 37 C1     .draw:  call kd_draw
  50+ C024 CD 4C A1     .loop:  call menu_frame
  51+ C027 CD B5 2C             call zrand                      ; (keep stirring the seed)
  52+ C02A CD 00 E0             call kbd_scan
  53+ C02D 21 01 03             ld hl,KB_1                      ; back to the main menu
  54+ C030 CD F1 E0             call key_code_pressed
  55+ C033 C2 08 AA             jp nz,menu_go                   ; (zpolice.asm)
  56+ C036 21 01 04             ld hl,KB_0                      ; the defaults
  57+ C039 CD F1 E0             call key_code_pressed
  58+ C03C 28 0D                jr z,.up
  59+ C03E 21 C0 E1             ld hl,key_defaults              ; (and joy_defaults, after it)
  60+ C041 11 70 E1             ld de,key_defs                  ; (and joy_defs)
  61+ C044 01 50 00             ld bc,2*K_ACTIONS*2
  62+ C047 ED B0                ldir
  63+ C049 18 D6                jr .draw
  64+ C04B 21 01 02     .up:    ld hl,KB_Q
  65+ C04E CD F1 E0             call key_code_pressed
  66+ C051 28 0C                jr z,.dn
  67+ C053 3A B8 C5             ld a,(kd_cur)
  68+ C056 B7                   or a
  69+ C057 28 06                jr z,.dn
  70+ C059 3D                   dec a
  71+ C05A 32 B8 C5             ld (kd_cur),a
  72+ C05D 18 C2                jr .draw
  73+ C05F 21 01 01     .dn:    ld hl,KB_A
  74+ C062 CD F1 E0             call key_code_pressed
  75+ C065 28 0D                jr z,.sp
  76+ C067 3A B8 C5             ld a,(kd_cur)
  77+ C06A 3C                   inc a
  78+ C06B FE 14                cp K_ACTIONS
  79+ C06D 30 05                jr nc,.sp
  80+ C06F 32 B8 C5             ld (kd_cur),a
  81+ C072 18 AD                jr .draw
  82+ C074 21 01 07     .sp:    ld hl,KB_SPACE                  ; change the highlighted one
  83+ C077 CD F1 E0             call key_code_pressed
  84+ C07A 28 A8                jr z,.loop
  85+ C07C CD 81 C0             call kd_change
  86+ C07F 18 A0                jr .draw
  87+ C081
  88+ C081              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  89+ C081              ; SPACE: "PRESS A KEY OR JOYSTICK", a second's pause, then the next input
  90+ C081              ; becomes the highlighted control's key (or its joystick input, if it is a
  91+ C081              ; joystick's), if no other control uses it
  92+ C081              kd_change:
  93+ C081 21 3F C2             ld hl,txt_kd_select
  94+ C084 0E 0A                ld c,MENU_TITLE
  95+ C086 CD 22 C1             call kd_msg
  96+ C089 06 32                ld b,KD_PAUSE                   ; a second (keys still read, so one
  97+ C08B C5           .p:     push bc                         ; held now isn't new after it)
  98+ C08C CD 4C A1             call menu_frame
  99+ C08F CD 00 E0             call kbd_scan
 100+ C092 C1                   pop bc
 101+ C093 10 F6                djnz .p
 102+ C095 CD 4C A1     .w:     call menu_frame                 ; wait for a key newly pressed
 103+ C098 CD B5 2C             call zrand
 104+ C09B CD 00 E0             call kbd_scan
 105+ C09E CD 03 C1             call kd_new_key
 106+ C0A1 28 F2                jr z,.w
 107+ C0A3 ED 53 BB C5          ld (kd_code),de
 108+ C0A7 21 70 E1             ld hl,key_defs                  ; a key, or a joystick input?
 109+ C0AA 7A                   ld a,d
 110+ C0AB FE 0A                cp JOY_BANK
 111+ C0AD 38 03                jr c,.t
 112+ C0AF 21 98 E1             ld hl,joy_defs
 113+ C0B2 22 BD C5     .t:     ld (kd_table),hl
 114+ C0B5 06 00                ld b,0                          ; in use by another control?
 115+ C0B7 3A B8 C5     .u:     ld a,(kd_cur)
 116+ C0BA B8                   cp b
 117+ C0BB 28 0A                jr z,.nu                        ; (its own key: fine)
 118+ C0BD 7E                   ld a,(hl)

 127+ C0C8 23                   inc hl
 128+ C0C9 04                   inc b
 129+ C0CA 78                   ld a,b
 130+ C0CB FE 14                cp K_ACTIONS
 131+ C0CD 20 E8                jr nz,.u
 132+ C0CF 3A B8 C5             ld a,(kd_cur)                   ; free: it's the control's key (or
 133+ C0D2 87                   add a,a                         ; joystick input) now
 134+ C0D3 2A BD C5             ld hl,(kd_table)
 135+ C0D6 ED 31                add hl,a
 136+ C0D8 ED 5B BB C5          ld de,(kd_code)
 137+ C0DC 73                   ld (hl),e
 138+ C0DD 23                   inc hl
 139+ C0DE 72                   ld (hl),d
 140+ C0DF 21 6A C2             ld hl,txt_kd_blank              ; (the message gone)
 141+ C0E2 0E 0F                ld c,MENU_ATTR
 142+ C0E4 C3 22 C1             jp kd_msg
 143+ C0E7 21 57 C2     .used:  ld hl,txt_kd_used               ; already a control's key: no change
 144+ C0EA 0E 0B                ld c,MENU_BAD
 145+ C0EC CD 22 C1             call kd_msg
 146+ C0EF 06 32                ld b,KD_PAUSE
 147+ C0F1 C5           .q:     push bc
 148+ C0F2 CD 4C A1             call menu_frame
 149+ C0F5 CD 00 E0             call kbd_scan
 150+ C0F8 C1                   pop bc
 151+ C0F9 10 F6                djnz .q
 152+ C0FB 21 6A C2             ld hl,txt_kd_blank
 153+ C0FE 0E 0F                ld c,MENU_ATTR
 154+ C100 C3 22 C1             jp kd_msg
 155+ C103
 156+ C103              ; NZ if an input (a key or a joystick's) went down since the last scan: DE =
 157+ C103              ; its code (bank in D, its bit in E; the lowest bank and bit if several)
 158+ C103              kd_new_key:
 159+ C103 21 56 E1             ld hl,key_now
 160+ C106 16 00                ld d,0
 161+ C108 E5           .b:     push hl
 162+ C109 3E 0D                ld a,KEY_BANKS                  ; key_prev, the same bank
 163+ C10B ED 31                add hl,a
 164+ C10D 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 165+ C10E 2F                   cpl                             ; not down before
 166+ C10F E1                   pop hl
 167+ C110 A6                   and (hl)                        ; and down now
 168+ C111 20 09                jr nz,.got
 169+ C113 23                   inc hl
 170+ C114 14                   inc d
 171+ C115 7A                   ld a,d
 172+ C116 FE 0D                cp KEY_BANKS
 173+ C118 20 EE                jr nz,.b
 174+ C11A AF                   xor a                           ; none (Z)
 175+ C11B C9                   ret
 176+ C11C 4F           .got:   ld c,a                          ; the lowest bit: A AND -A
 177+ C11D ED 44                neg
 178+ C11F A1                   and c
 179+ C120 5F                   ld e,a                          ; (NZ)
 180+ C121 C9                   ret
 181+ C122
 182+ C122              ; HL -> message, C = attribute: shown on the message row (cleared first)
 183+ C122              kd_msg:

 185+ C123 C5                   push bc

1735  C5C1                      MMU LOGIC_SLOT e, PAGE_SPECIAL, LOGIC_ADDR

1737  C000                      INCLUDE "specials.asm"
# file opened: specials.asm
   1+ C000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ C000              ; Z-POLICE - special monsters (8K page PAGE_SPECIAL, game logic: runs at
   3+ C000              ; $C000 in slot 6)
   4+ C000              ;
   5+ C000              ; Special monsters use the zombie records after the zombies' own (NZOMB ..
   6+ C000              ; NMON-1, zombie.asm), so they share the zombies' drawing (zombie_cell,
   7+ C000              ; fig_draw), map blips, cell marks (zflags), weapon hits and zdamage.  The
   8+ C000              ; record's type (+15) says which monster it is.  Each wave's specials come
   9+ C000              ; from the level file (level_cur, LV_WAVE_DATA: up to LV_SPEC_SLOTS types,
  10+ C000              ; SPEC_*, and how many of each), placed by spec_wave as the wave starts;
  11+ C000              ; the level's start is wave 1 (spec_init).  spec_update moves them each
  12+ C000              ; frame, after zombie_update.
  13+ C000              ;
  14+ C000              ; The ghost (ZT_GHOST, special monster id SPEC_GHOST): a stick man under a white
  15+ C000              ; sheet.  It can't attack the base.  It flies at twice the zombies' speed,
  16+ C000              ; at any angle, and up and down between the ground and the VTOL's ceiling
  17+ C000              ; (MAX_Y), over buildings lower than it.
  18+ C000              ;   * Roaming: it flies in a random direction (a random climb or descent
  19+ C000              ;     too) until something makes it turn: a building in the way or the map
  20+ C000              ;     edge (a new random direction at once), or, at its decision check
  21+ C000              ;     every GHOST_DECIDE (30) frames, a human or the VTOL within GHOST_SEE
  22+ C000              ;     (3 tiles) that it can see.
  23+ C000              ;   * Chasing: it flies straight at the nearest one it can see, aiming
  24+ C000              ;     again every GHOST_TRACK (5) frames.  If it loses sight of its target
  25+ C000              ;     (behind a taller building) or the target is gone, it roams again in a
  26+ C000              ;     random direction, back on the 30-frame cycle.
  27+ C000              ;   * Attacking: within ATTACK_R (0.1 tile) of its target on each axis it
  28+ C000              ;     stops and takes GHOST_DMG hit point every GHOST_HIT_FRAMES (10)
  29+ C000              ;     frames: from a human (HUMAN_HP) until it dies, or from the VTOL
  30+ C000              ;     (vtol_hp, VTOL_HP; at 0 it is destroyed and the game ends).  Its map blip is red
  31+ C000              ;     meanwhile (zatk_flags, zblip_red), as an attacking zombie's.  If the
  32+ C000              ;     target moves out of reach it chases it again; once the human is dead
  33+ C000              ;     it roams again.
  34+ C000              ;   * It has GHOST_HP (10) hit points; any weapon can shoot it.  Killed, it
  35+ C000              ;     fades out where it is, white to near black in 4 steps of 3 frames
  36+ C000              ;     (GM_DYING; zdamage starts it, zombie_draw colours it, g_dying ends
  37+ C000              ;     it), and can't be hit meanwhile.
  38+ C000              ; Ghost record fields, besides the zombies' (zombie.asm):
  39+ C000              ;   +11 frames to its next decision check, +16 mode (GM_*), +17 frames to
  40+ C000              ;   its next blow, +18 hit points, +21,+22 height of its feet, +23,+24 and
  41+ C000              ;   +25,+26 velocity x, z (signed 8.8 units a frame), +27,+28 the x and z
  42+ C000              ;   position fractions, +29 climb (signed units a frame), +30 target
  43+ C000              ;   (TG_NONE, TG_VTOL, or a human's index).
  44+ C000              ;==============================================================================
  45+ C000
  46+ C000              GHOST_HP        equ 10              ; hit points
  47+ C000              GHOST_DECIDE    equ 30              ; frames between decisions while roaming
  48+ C000              GHOST_TRACK     equ 5               ; and while chasing
  49+ C000              GHOST_SEE       equ 3*TILE_UNITS    ; it spots humans / the VTOL this near
  50+ C000              GHOST_HIT_FRAMES equ 10             ; frames between blows
  51+ C000              GHOST_DMG       equ 1               ; hit points a blow
  52+ C000              GHOST_CLIMB     equ 1               ; units a frame up or down
  53+ C000              GHOST_AIM_MIN   equ 80              ; (aiming: nearer counts as this far)
  54+ C000              GM_ROAM         equ 0               ; modes (+16)
  55+ C000              GM_CHASE        equ 1
  56+ C000              GM_ATTACK       equ 2
  57+ C000              GM_DYING        equ 3               ; killed: fading out (+17 frames left)
  58+ C000              GHOST_FADE_FRAMES equ 12            ; the fade: 4 steps of 3 frames
  59+ C000              TG_NONE         equ $FF             ; targets (+30)
  60+ C000              TG_VTOL         equ $FE
  61+ C000              TG_BASE         equ $C0             ; + a base tile's index (base_hp, 0..24)
  62+ C000              ; The banshee (ZT_BANSHEE, id SPEC_BANSHEE): a ghost that flies twice as
  63+ C000              ; fast, reaches BANSHEE_R (0.5 tile), does BANSHEE_DMG a blow and also
  64+ C000              ; attacks the base (the terrace and pad tiles).
  65+ C000              ; The ghoul (ZT_GHOUL, id SPEC_GHOUL, zombie.asm): a half-height zombie
  66+ C000              ; walking at five times the zombies' speed.  It is placed here (spec_wave)
  67+ C000              ; but moved, drawn and hit as a zombie.
  68+ C000              ; (Which waves have which is the level files' business: gentables.py.)
  69+ C000              BANSHEE_HP      equ 8
  70+ C000              BANSHEE_DMG     equ 5
  71+ C000              BANSHEE_R       equ TILE_UNITS/2
  72+ C000              BASE_TILES      equ (2*TERRACE_R+1)*(2*TERRACE_R+1)
  73+ C000
  74+ C000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  75+ C000              ; A level's start: every special record inactive, then wave 1's specials
  76+ C000              ; (the level's start is wave 1, as any other: spec_wave)
  77+ C000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  78+ C000              spec_init:
  79+ C000 DD 21 6D 33          ld ix,zombies+NZOMB*ZREC
  80+ C004 06 08                ld b,NSPEC
  81+ C006 0E 1E                ld c,NZOMB                      ; record index
  82+ C008 79           .lp:    ld a,c
  83+ C009 21 72 2F             ld hl,zatk_flags                ; not attacking
  84+ C00C ED 31                add hl,a
  85+ C00E 36 00                ld (hl),FALSE
  86+ C010 DD 36 0A 00          ld (ix+10),0                    ; inactive,
  87+ C014 DD 36 0E 00          ld (ix+14),0                    ; no blip,
  88+ C018 DD 36 13 FF          ld (ix+19),$FF                  ; no cell marked
  89+ C01C 11 1F 00             ld de,ZREC
  90+ C01F DD 19                add ix,de
  91+ C021 0C                   inc c
  92+ C022 10 E4                djnz .lp
  93+ C024 CD 0E C1             call spec_wave                  ; wave 1's specials (wave_number 1)
  94+ C027 C3 95 26             jp zflags                       ; (zombie.asm) mark their cells
  95+ C02A
  96+ C02A              ; Special monster IX (record sp_idx) of type sp_type: at a random street
  97+ C02A              ; position and height, roaming in a random direction
  98+ C02A              ghost_init:
  99+ C02A 3A 2E C7             ld a,(sp_type)
 100+ C02D DD 77 0F             ld (ix+15),a
 101+ C030 CD AA C1             call g_params                   ; (its speed for its first direction)
 102+ C033 CD B5 2C     .try:   call zrand                      ; (zombie page)
 103+ C036 7D                   ld a,l
 104+ C037 E6 3F                and GRID-1
 105+ C039 DD 77 01             ld (ix+1),a                     ; x cell
 106+ C03C 7C                   ld a,h
 107+ C03D E6 3F                and GRID-1
 108+ C03F DD 77 03             ld (ix+3),a                     ; z cell
 109+ C042 CD FA 2A             call zmap_ptr
 110+ C045 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 111+ C046 E6 1F                and CELL_CODE
 112+ C048 20 E9                jr nz,.try                      ; a building: pick again
 113+ C04A CD B5 2C             call zrand
 114+ C04D 7D                   ld a,l
 115+ C04E E6 7F                and 127
 116+ C050 C6 40                add a,64                        ; 64..191 inside the cell
 117+ C052 DD 77 00             ld (ix+0),a
 118+ C055 7C                   ld a,h
 119+ C056 E6 7F                and 127
 120+ C058 C6 40                add a,64
 121+ C05A DD 77 02             ld (ix+2),a
 122+ C05D CD B5 2C             call zrand
 123+ C060 7D                   ld a,l
 124+ C061 E6 7F                and 127                         ; height 0..127 (MAX_Y 192)
 125+ C063 DD 77 15             ld (ix+21),a
 126+ C066 DD 36 16 00          ld (ix+22),0
 127+ C06A AF                   xor a
 128+ C06B DD 77 1B             ld (ix+27),a                    ; position fractions
 129+ C06E DD 77 1C             ld (ix+28),a
 130+ C071 DD 77 05             ld (ix+5),a
 131+ C074 DD 77 07             ld (ix+7),a
 132+ C077 DD 36 10 00          ld (ix+16),GM_ROAM
 133+ C07B DD 36 12 0A          ld (ix+18),GHOST_HP
 134+ C07F DD 7E 0F             ld a,(ix+15)
 135+ C082 FE 04                cp ZT_BANSHEE
 136+ C084 20 04                jr nz,.hp
 137+ C086 DD 36 12 08          ld (ix+18),BANSHEE_HP
 138+ C08A              .hp:
 139+ C08A DD 36 1E FF          ld (ix+30),TG_NONE
 140+ C08E DD 36 0A 01          ld (ix+10),1                    ; active
 141+ C092 3A 2F C7             ld a,(sp_idx)                   ; first decisions spread over the
 142+ C095 5F                   ld e,a                          ; 30 frames
 143+ C096 16 07                ld d,7
 144+ C098 ED 30                mul d,e
 145+ C09A 7B                   ld a,e
 146+ C09B D6 1E        .mod:   sub GHOST_DECIDE
 147+ C09D 30 FC                jr nc,.mod
 148+ C09F C6 1F                add a,GHOST_DECIDE+1
 149+ C0A1 DD 77 0B             ld (ix+11),a
 150+ C0A4 CD 12 C2             call g_random
 151+ C0A7 C3 DB 3A             jp zblip                        ; (map.asm)
 152+ C0AA
 153+ C0AA              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 154+ C0AA              ; Per frame (after zombie_update): move every special monster, then mark
 155+ C0AA              ; the cells again
 156+ C0AA              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 157+ C0AA              spec_update:
 158+ C0AA 3A 6A 34             ld a,(zwave_new)                ; a new wave: its banshees or ghouls
 159+ C0AD B7                   or a
 160+ C0AE C4 0E C1             call nz,spec_wave
 161+ C0B1 DD 21 6D 33          ld ix,zombies+NZOMB*ZREC
 162+ C0B5 06 08                ld b,NSPEC
 163+ C0B7 0E 1E                ld c,NZOMB
 164+ C0B9 C5           .lp:    push bc
 165+ C0BA 79                   ld a,c
 166+ C0BB 32 2F C7             ld (sp_idx),a
 167+ C0BE DD CB 0A 46          bit 0,(ix+10)
 168+ C0C2 28 0D                jr z,.nx                        ; gone
 169+ C0C4 DD 7E 0F             ld a,(ix+15)
 170+ C0C7 FE 03                cp ZT_GHOST
 171+ C0C9 38 06                jr c,.nx                        ; (a ghoul walks: zombie_update)
 172+ C0CB CD AA C1             call g_params                   ; a ghost or banshee: its settings
 173+ C0CE CD DD C0             call ghost_move
 174+ C0D1 11 1F 00     .nx:    ld de,ZREC
 175+ C0D4 DD 19                add ix,de
 176+ C0D6 C1                   pop bc
 177+ C0D7 0C                   inc c
 178+ C0D8 10 DF                djnz .lp
 179+ C0DA C3 95 26             jp zflags                       ; their cells, as they are now
 180+ C0DD
 181+ C0DD              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 182+ C0DD              ; Ghost IX, one frame
 183+ C0DD              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 184+ C0DD              ghost_move:
 185+ C0DD DD 7E 10             ld a,(ix+16)
 186+ C0E0 FE 03                cp GM_DYING
 187+ C0E2 CA E4 C1             jp z,g_dying
 188+ C0E5 FE 02                cp GM_ATTACK
 189+ C0E7 CA 56 C3             jp z,g_attack
 190+ C0EA DD 35 0B             dec (ix+11)                     ; decision check due?
 191+ C0ED CC ED C1             call z,g_decide
 192+ C0F0 CD 49 C2             call g_step                     ; fly
 193+ C0F3 DD 7E 10             ld a,(ix+16)
 194+ C0F6 FE 01                cp GM_CHASE
 195+ C0F8 CC 33 C3             call z,g_reach                  ; close enough to attack?
 196+ C0FB DD 7E 10             ld a,(ix+16)
 197+ C0FE FE 02                cp GM_ATTACK
 198+ C100 C8                   ret z                           ; (its blip is red)
 199+ C101 3A 67 34             ld a,(zanim)                    ; its map blip, every 8 frames
 200+ C104 21 2F C7             ld hl,sp_idx
 201+ C107 86                   add a,(hl)
 202+ C108 E6 07                and 7
 203+ C10A C0                   ret nz
 204+ C10B C3 DB 3A             jp zblip
 205+ C10E
 206+ C10E              ; A new wave began (zwave_new, zombie.asm; spec_init for wave 1): its
 207+ C10E              ; specials from the level file (level_cur, LV_WAVE_DATA: wave_number's
 208+ C10E              ; record), each of its LV_SPEC_SLOTS types (SPEC_NONE: none) as many as it
 209+ C10E              ; says, into free special records.  Waves past LV_MAX_WAVES have none.
 210+ C10E              spec_wave:
 211+ C10E AF                   xor a
 212+ C10F 32 6A 34             ld (zwave_new),a
 213+ C112 3A 46 AE             ld a,(wave_number)
 214+ C115 3D                   dec a
 215+ C116 FE 0A                cp LV_MAX_WAVES
 216+ C118 D0                   ret nc
 217+ C119 5F                   ld e,a
 218+ C11A 16 0A                ld d,LV_WAVE_REC
 219+ C11C ED 30                mul d,e
 220+ C11E 21 68 A0             ld hl,level_cur+LV_WAVE_DATA
 221+ C121 19                   add hl,de
 222+ C122 22 31 C7             ld (sw_ptr),hl                  ; -> this wave's types
 223+ C125 06 05                ld b,LV_SPEC_SLOTS
 224+ C127 C5           .sl:    push bc
 225+ C128 2A 31 C7             ld hl,(sw_ptr)
 226+ C12B 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; a type
 227+ C12C FE 03                cp SPEC_IDS
 228+ C12E 30 14                jr nc,.ns                       ; (SPEC_NONE, or not a type)
 229+ C130 11 4F C1             ld de,spec_types
 230+ C133 ED 32                add de,a
 231+ C135 1A                   ld a,(de)
 232+ C136 32 2E C7             ld (sp_type),a                  ; its record type (ZT_*)
 233+ C139 3E 05                ld a,LV_SPECIAL-LV_SPECIAL_ID
 234+ C13B ED 31                add hl,a
 235+ C13D 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; how many
 236+ C13E 32 30 C7             ld (sp_left),a
 237+ C141 CD 52 C1             call spec_place
 238+ C144 2A 31 C7     .ns:    ld hl,(sw_ptr)
 239+ C147 23                   inc hl
 240+ C148 22 31 C7             ld (sw_ptr),hl
 241+ C14B C1                   pop bc
 242+ C14C 10 D9                djnz .sl
 243+ C14E C9                   ret
 244+ C14F
 245+ C14F              ; Special type (SPEC_*, the level files) -> record type (ZT_*)
 246+ C14F 03 04 02     spec_types: db ZT_GHOST, ZT_BANSHEE, ZT_GHOUL
 247+ C152                      ASSERT $ - spec_types == SPEC_IDS
 248+ C152                      ASSERT SPEC_GHOST == 0 && SPEC_BANSHEE == 1 && SPEC_GHOUL == 2
 249+ C152
 250+ C152              ; sp_left monsters of record type sp_type into free special records (as
 251+ C152              ; many as there's room for)
 252+ C152              spec_place:
 253+ C152 DD 21 6D 33          ld ix,zombies+NZOMB*ZREC
 254+ C156 06 08                ld b,NSPEC
 255+ C158 0E 1E                ld c,NZOMB
 256+ C15A 3A 30 C7     .lp:    ld a,(sp_left)
 257+ C15D B7                   or a
 258+ C15E C8                   ret z                           ; all placed
 259+ C15F DD CB 0A 46          bit 0,(ix+10)
 260+ C163 20 25                jr nz,.nx                       ; in use
 261+ C165 C5                   push bc
 262+ C166 79                   ld a,c
 263+ C167 32 2F C7             ld (sp_idx),a
 264+ C16A 21 72 2F             ld hl,zatk_flags
 265+ C16D ED 31                add hl,a
 266+ C16F 36 00                ld (hl),FALSE
 267+ C171 DD 36 0E 00          ld (ix+14),0                    ; no blip yet
 268+ C175 21 30 C7             ld hl,sp_left
 269+ C178 35                   dec (hl)
 270+ C179 3A 2E C7             ld a,(sp_type)
 271+ C17C FE 02                cp ZT_GHOUL
 272+ C17E 28 06                jr z,.gh
 273+ C180 CD 2A C0             call ghost_init
 274+ C183 C1                   pop bc
 275+ C184 18 04                jr .nx
 276+ C186 CD 93 C1     .gh:    call ghoul_init
 277+ C189 C1                   pop bc
 278+ C18A 11 1F 00     .nx:    ld de,ZREC
 279+ C18D DD 19                add ix,de
 280+ C18F 0C                   inc c
 281+ C190 10 C8                djnz .lp
 282+ C192 C9                   ret                             ; (no room for the rest)
 283+ C193
 284+ C193              ; A ghoul in record IX (sp_idx): placed as a zombie (zplace, zombie.asm),
 285+ C193              ; then made a ghoul; its first decision check spread by its index
 286+ C193              ghoul_init:
 287+ C193 CD 9F 25             call zplace
 288+ C196 DD 36 0F 02          ld (ix+15),ZT_GHOUL
 289+ C19A DD 36 12 05          ld (ix+18),GHOUL_HP
 290+ C19E 3A 2F C7             ld a,(sp_idx)
 291+ C1A1 E6 07                and 7
 292+ C1A3 3C                   inc a
 293+ C1A4 DD 77 0B             ld (ix+11),a
 294+ C1A7 C3 DB 3A             jp zblip                        ; (map.asm)
 295+ C1AA
 296+ C1AA              ; Special monster IX's settings, from its type (+15): g_speed (8.8 units a
 297+ C1AA              ; frame: a ghost twice the zombies' zspeed, a banshee four times), gp_range
 298+ C1AA              ; (its reach on each axis), gp_dmg (a blow), gp_base (1: attacks the base)
 299+ C1AA              g_params:
 300+ C1AA 3A 6B 2F             ld a,(zspeed)                   ; zspeed (4.4) * 32 = twice it, 8.8
 301+ C1AD 6F                   ld l,a
 302+ C1AE 26 00                ld h,0
 303+ C1B0 29                   add hl,hl
 304+ C1B1 29                   add hl,hl
 305+ C1B2 29                   add hl,hl
 306+ C1B3 29                   add hl,hl
 307+ C1B4 29                   add hl,hl
 308+ C1B5 DD 7E 0F             ld a,(ix+15)
 309+ C1B8 FE 04                cp ZT_BANSHEE
 310+ C1BA 28 13                jr z,.b
 311+ C1BC 22 28 C7             ld (g_speed),hl                 ; a ghost
 312+ C1BF 21 1A 00             ld hl,ATTACK_R
 313+ C1C2 22 2A C7             ld (gp_range),hl
 314+ C1C5 3E 01                ld a,GHOST_DMG
 315+ C1C7 32 2C C7             ld (gp_dmg),a
 316+ C1CA AF                   xor a
 317+ C1CB 32 2D C7             ld (gp_base),a
 318+ C1CE C9                   ret
 319+ C1CF 29           .b:     add hl,hl                       ; a banshee: twice as fast
 320+ C1D0 22 28 C7             ld (g_speed),hl
 321+ C1D3 21 80 00             ld hl,BANSHEE_R
 322+ C1D6 22 2A C7             ld (gp_range),hl
 323+ C1D9 3E 05                ld a,BANSHEE_DMG
 324+ C1DB 32 2C C7             ld (gp_dmg),a
 325+ C1DE 3E 01                ld a,1
 326+ C1E0 32 2D C7             ld (gp_base),a
 327+ C1E3 C9                   ret
 328+ C1E4
 329+ C1E4              ; Killed (zdamage): it hangs where it is, fading out (zombie_draw colours
 330+ C1E4              ; it by the frames left), then is gone
 331+ C1E4              g_dying:
 332+ C1E4 DD 35 11             dec (ix+17)
 333+ C1E7 C0                   ret nz
 334+ C1E8 DD 36 0A 00          ld (ix+10),0                    ; gone (zflags unmarks its cell)
 335+ C1EC C9                   ret
 336+ C1ED
 337+ C1ED              ; Decision check: look for a human or the VTOL to chase
 338+ C1ED              g_decide:
 339+ C1ED CD BA C4             call g_find                     ; carry: a target, at gt_x/z/y
 340+ C1F0 30 0B                jr nc,.none
 341+ C1F2 DD 36 10 01          ld (ix+16),GM_CHASE
 342+ C1F6 DD 36 0B 05          ld (ix+11),GHOST_TRACK
 343+ C1FA C3 88 C6             jp g_aim
 344+ C1FD DD 36 0B 1E  .none:  ld (ix+11),GHOST_DECIDE
 345+ C201 DD 7E 10             ld a,(ix+16)
 346+ C204 B7                   or a
 347+ C205 C8                   ret z                           ; roaming: fly on
 348+ C206                      ; lost it (gone, or out of sight): roam again, a new direction
 349+ C206 DD 36 10 00  g_lose: ld (ix+16),GM_ROAM
 350+ C20A DD 36 0B 1E          ld (ix+11),GHOST_DECIDE
 351+ C20E DD 36 1E FF          ld (ix+30),TG_NONE
 352+ C212                      ; fall through
 353+ C212
 354+ C212              ; A random direction: velocity g_speed at a random angle, and a random climb
 355+ C212              ; (-1, 0, +1, 0 units a frame)
 356+ C212              g_random:
 357+ C212 CD B5 2C             call zrand
 358+ C215 E5                   push hl
 359+ C216 7D                   ld a,l
 360+ C217 F5                   push af
 361+ C218 CD 33 AD             call get_sin                    ; (resident; pages the sine table)
 362+ C21B EB                   ex de,hl
 363+ C21C 2A 28 C7             ld hl,(g_speed)
 364+ C21F CD EA AD             call fmul                       ; vx = speed * sin (8.8)
 365+ C222 DD 75 17             ld (ix+23),l
 366+ C225 DD 74 18             ld (ix+24),h
 367+ C228 F1                   pop af
 368+ C229 C6 40                add a,64
 369+ C22B CD 33 AD             call get_sin
 370+ C22E EB                   ex de,hl
 371+ C22F 2A 28 C7             ld hl,(g_speed)
 372+ C232 CD EA AD             call fmul                       ; vz = speed * cos
 373+ C235 DD 75 19             ld (ix+25),l
 374+ C238 DD 74 1A             ld (ix+26),h
 375+ C23B E1                   pop hl
 376+ C23C 7C                   ld a,h
 377+ C23D E6 03                and 3
 378+ C23F 21 24 C7             ld hl,g_climbs
 379+ C242 ED 31                add hl,a
 380+ C244 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 381+ C245 DD 77 1D             ld (ix+29),a
 382+ C248 C9                   ret
 383+ C249
 384+ C249              ; Fly one frame: x, z by the velocity, unless that runs into a building
 385+ C249              ; taller than its feet or the map edge (then a new random direction, and a
 386+ C249              ; chase is given up); then the climb, kept between the roof or ground under
 387+ C249              ; it and MAX_Y
 388+ C249              g_step:
 389+ C249 DD 6E 1B             ld l,(ix+27)                    ; keep the old position
 390+ C24C DD 66 00             ld h,(ix+0)
 391+ C24F 22 33 C7             ld (gs_x),hl
 392+ C252 DD 7E 01             ld a,(ix+1)
 393+ C255 32 35 C7             ld (gs_x+2),a
 394+ C258 DD 6E 1C             ld l,(ix+28)
 395+ C25B DD 66 02             ld h,(ix+2)
 396+ C25E 22 36 C7             ld (gs_z),hl
 397+ C261 DD 7E 03             ld a,(ix+3)
 398+ C264 32 38 C7             ld (gs_z+2),a
 399+ C267 DD 5E 17             ld e,(ix+23)                    ; x: (fraction, lo, hi) += vx
 400+ C26A DD 56 18             ld d,(ix+24)
 401+ C26D 7A                   ld a,d
 402+ C26E 17                   rla
 403+ C26F 9F                   sbc a,a
 404+ C270 4F                   ld c,a                          ; sign
 405+ C271 DD 7E 1B             ld a,(ix+27)
 406+ C274 83                   add a,e
 407+ C275 DD 77 1B             ld (ix+27),a
 408+ C278 DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+0)
 409+ C27B 8A                   adc a,d
 410+ C27C DD 77 00             ld (ix+0),a
 411+ C27F DD 7E 01             ld a,(ix+1)
 412+ C282 89                   adc a,c
 413+ C283 DD 77 01             ld (ix+1),a
 414+ C286 DD 5E 19             ld e,(ix+25)                    ; z likewise
 415+ C289 DD 56 1A             ld d,(ix+26)
 416+ C28C 7A                   ld a,d
 417+ C28D 17                   rla
 418+ C28E 9F                   sbc a,a
 419+ C28F 4F                   ld c,a
 420+ C290 DD 7E 1C             ld a,(ix+28)
 421+ C293 83                   add a,e
 422+ C294 DD 77 1C             ld (ix+28),a
 423+ C297 DD 7E 02             ld a,(ix+2)
 424+ C29A 8A                   adc a,d
 425+ C29B DD 77 02             ld (ix+2),a
 426+ C29E DD 7E 03             ld a,(ix+3)
 427+ C2A1 89                   adc a,c
 428+ C2A2 DD 77 03             ld (ix+3),a
 429+ C2A5 CD 09 C3             call g_ground                   ; HL = roof under the new position,
 430+ C2A8 38 0C                jr c,.blk                       ; carry: off the map
 431+ C2AA DD 5E 15             ld e,(ix+21)
 432+ C2AD DD 56 16             ld d,(ix+22)
 433+ C2B0 EB                   ex de,hl
 434+ C2B1 B7                   or a
 435+ C2B2 ED 52                sbc hl,de                       ; feet - roof
 436+ C2B4 30 28                jr nc,.ok                       ; at or above it
 437+ C2B6 2A 33 C7     .blk:   ld hl,(gs_x)                    ; blocked: back, and turn
 438+ C2B9 DD 75 1B             ld (ix+27),l
 439+ C2BC DD 74 00             ld (ix+0),h
 440+ C2BF 3A 35 C7             ld a,(gs_x+2)
 441+ C2C2 DD 77 01             ld (ix+1),a
 442+ C2C5 2A 36 C7             ld hl,(gs_z)
 443+ C2C8 DD 75 1C             ld (ix+28),l
 444+ C2CB DD 74 02             ld (ix+2),h
 445+ C2CE 3A 38 C7             ld a,(gs_z+2)
 446+ C2D1 DD 77 03             ld (ix+3),a
 447+ C2D4 DD 7E 10             ld a,(ix+16)
 448+ C2D7 B7                   or a
 449+ C2D8 CA 12 C2             jp z,g_random                   ; roaming: a new direction
 450+ C2DB C3 06 C2             jp g_lose                       ; chasing: give it up
 451+ C2DE DD 7E 1D     .ok:    ld a,(ix+29)                    ; climb
 452+ C2E1 DD 6E 15             ld l,(ix+21)
 453+ C2E4 DD 66 16             ld h,(ix+22)
 454+ C2E7 5F                   ld e,a
 455+ C2E8 17                   rla
 456+ C2E9 9F                   sbc a,a
 457+ C2EA 57                   ld d,a
 458+ C2EB 19                   add hl,de
 459+ C2EC CB 7C                bit 7,h
 460+ C2EE 28 03                jr z,.pos
 461+ C2F0 21 00 00             ld hl,0
 462+ C2F3 11 C0 00     .pos:   ld de,MAX_Y                     ; no higher than the VTOL can go
 463+ C2F6 CD 25 C3             call g_min
 464+ C2F9 EB                   ex de,hl
 465+ C2FA D5                   push de
 466+ C2FB CD 09 C3             call g_ground                   ; nor below the roof under it
 467+ C2FE D1                   pop de
 468+ C2FF CD 2C C3             call g_max_de
 469+ C302 DD 75 15             ld (ix+21),l
 470+ C305 DD 74 16             ld (ix+22),h
 471+ C308 C9                   ret
 472+ C309
 473+ C309              ; HL = the roof height (0 on a road) of ghost IX's cell; carry set if the
 474+ C309              ; cell is off the map (then HL = $FFFF)
 475+ C309              g_ground:
 476+ C309 DD 7E 01             ld a,(ix+1)
 477+ C30C FE 40                cp GRID
 478+ C30E 30 10                jr nc,.off
 479+ C310 DD 7E 03             ld a,(ix+3)
 480+ C313 FE 40                cp GRID
 481+ C315 30 09                jr nc,.off
 482+ C317 CD FA 2A             call zmap_ptr                   ; (zombie page)
 483+ C31A 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 484+ C31B CD AA AC             call bld_height                 ; (masks the cell code)
 485+ C31E B7                   or a
 486+ C31F C9                   ret
 487+ C320 21 FF FF     .off:   ld hl,$FFFF
 488+ C323 37                   scf
 489+ C324 C9                   ret
 490+ C325
 491+ C325              ; HL = min(HL, DE), unsigned
 492+ C325 B7           g_min:  or a
 493+ C326 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 494+ C328 19                   add hl,de
 495+ C329 D8                   ret c
 496+ C32A EB                   ex de,hl
 497+ C32B C9                   ret
 498+ C32C              ; HL = max(HL, DE), unsigned
 499+ C32C              g_max_de:
 500+ C32C B7                   or a
 501+ C32D ED 52                sbc hl,de
 502+ C32F 19                   add hl,de
 503+ C330 D0                   ret nc
 504+ C331 EB                   ex de,hl
 505+ C332 C9                   ret
 506+ C333
 507+ C333              ; Chasing: aim at the target's place again (g_find left it in gt_*) is done
 508+ C333              ; at the decision checks; here, check whether it is within reach and if so
 509+ C333              ; start attacking.
 510+ C333              g_reach:
 511+ C333 CD D8 C3             call g_target                   ; gt_x/z/y = where it is now; carry:
 512+ C336 DA 06 C2             jp c,g_lose                     ; gone
 513+ C339 CD 54 C4             call g_near                     ; within ATTACK_R on each axis?
 514+ C33C D0                   ret nc
 515+ C33D DD 36 10 02          ld (ix+16),GM_ATTACK
 516+ C341 DD 36 11 0A          ld (ix+17),GHOST_HIT_FRAMES
 517+ C345 CD 4D C3             call g_flag                     ; attacking: map blip red
 518+ C348 36 01                ld (hl),TRUE
 519+ C34A C3 44 3B             jp zblip_red                    ; (map.asm)
 520+ C34D
 521+ C34D              ; HL -> ghost IX's zatk_flags entry
 522+ C34D 3A 2F C7     g_flag: ld a,(sp_idx)
 523+ C350 21 72 2F             ld hl,zatk_flags
 524+ C353 ED 31                add hl,a
 525+ C355 C9                   ret
 526+ C356
 527+ C356              ; Attacking: one blow every GHOST_HIT_FRAMES frames while the target is in
 528+ C356              ; reach
 529+ C356              g_attack:
 530+ C356 CD D8 C3             call g_target
 531+ C359 38 51                jr c,.done                      ; gone (dead, aboard, rescued)
 532+ C35B CD 54 C4             call g_near
 533+ C35E 38 10                jr c,.blow
 534+ C360 DD 36 10 01          ld (ix+16),GM_CHASE             ; it moved away: after it
 535+ C364 DD 36 0B 01          ld (ix+11),1                    ; (aim next frame)
 536+ C368 CD 4D C3             call g_flag
 537+ C36B 36 00                ld (hl),FALSE
 538+ C36D C3 DB 3A             jp zblip
 539+ C370 DD 35 11     .blow:  dec (ix+17)
 540+ C373 C0                   ret nz
 541+ C374 DD 36 11 0A          ld (ix+17),GHOST_HIT_FRAMES
 542+ C378 DD 7E 1E             ld a,(ix+30)
 543+ C37B FE FE                cp TG_VTOL
 544+ C37D 28 38                jr z,.vtol
 545+ C37F FE C0                cp TG_BASE
 546+ C381 30 3A                jr nc,.base
 547+ C383 DD E5                push ix                         ; a human: gp_dmg hit points
 548+ C385 CD C9 C3             call g_human                    ; IX -> it
 549+ C388 3A 2C C7             ld a,(gp_dmg)
 550+ C38B 4F                   ld c,a
 551+ C38C DD 7E 0A             ld a,(ix+10)
 552+ C38F 91                   sub c
 553+ C390 38 08                jr c,.dead
 554+ C392 DD 77 0A             ld (ix+10),a
 555+ C395 28 03                jr z,.dead
 556+ C397 DD E1                pop ix
 557+ C399 C9                   ret
 558+ C39A DD 36 0A 00  .dead:  ld (ix+10),0                    ; killed: dot off, dead
 559+ C39E 0E 00                ld c,0
 560+ C3A0 CD 74 3B             call hum_dot                    ; (map.asm)
 561+ C3A3 DD 36 06 04          ld (ix+6),HS_DEAD
 562+ C3A7 CD B8 36             call hum_flags                  ; (human.asm) its mark off
 563+ C3AA DD E1                pop ix
 564+ C3AC CD 4D C3     .done:  call g_flag                     ; back to roaming
 565+ C3AF 36 00                ld (hl),FALSE
 566+ C3B1 CD DB 3A             call zblip
 567+ C3B4 C3 06 C2             jp g_lose
 568+ C3B7 3A 2C C7     .vtol:  ld a,(gp_dmg)                   ; the VTOL (at 0 it is destroyed;
 569+ C3BA C3 A5 A9             jp vtol_damage                  ; zpolice.asm)
 570+ C3BD D6 C0        .base:  sub TG_BASE                     ; a base tile: E, D = its cell
 571+ C3BF CD 89 C4             call g_basecell
 572+ C3C2 3A 2C C7             ld a,(gp_dmg)
 573+ C3C5 4F                   ld c,a
 574+ C3C6 C3 84 28             jp base_damage                  ; (zombie.asm; the pad: game over)
 575+ C3C9
 576+ C3C9              ; IX -> the record of ghost IX's target human (+30).  (Push IX first.)
 577+ C3C9              g_human:
 578+ C3C9 DD 7E 1E             ld a,(ix+30)
 579+ C3CC 5F                   ld e,a
 580+ C3CD 16 0D                ld d,HREC
 581+ C3CF ED 30                mul d,e
 582+ C3D1 DD 21 31 39          ld ix,humans
 583+ C3D5 DD 19                add ix,de
 584+ C3D7 C9                   ret
 585+ C3D8
 586+ C3D8              ; gt_x, gt_z, gt_y = where ghost IX's target is now; carry set if it has
 587+ C3D8              ; none or it is gone (a human no longer waiting)
 588+ C3D8              g_target:
 589+ C3D8 DD 7E 1E             ld a,(ix+30)
 590+ C3DB FE FF                cp TG_NONE
 591+ C3DD 37                   scf
 592+ C3DE C8                   ret z
 593+ C3DF FE FE                cp TG_VTOL
 594+ C3E1 28 2E                jr z,.vtol
 595+ C3E3 FE C0                cp TG_BASE
 596+ C3E5 38 3E                jr c,.hum
 597+ C3E7 D6 C0                sub TG_BASE                     ; a base tile: still standing?
 598+ C3E9 21 AC 2F             ld hl,base_hp
 599+ C3EC ED 31                add hl,a
 600+ C3EE 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 601+ C3EF B7                   or a
 602+ C3F0 37                   scf
 603+ C3F1 C8                   ret z                           ; destroyed
 604+ C3F2 DD 7E 1E             ld a,(ix+30)
 605+ C3F5 D6 C0                sub TG_BASE
 606+ C3F7 CD 89 C4             call g_basecell
 607+ C3FA CD 98 C4             call g_tilepos                  ; its centre, its top
 608+ C3FD 2A 4F C7             ld hl,(gf_x)
 609+ C400 22 45 C7             ld (gt_x),hl
 610+ C403 2A 51 C7             ld hl,(gf_z)
 611+ C406 22 47 C7             ld (gt_z),hl
 612+ C409 2A 53 C7             ld hl,(gf_y)
 613+ C40C 22 49 C7             ld (gt_y),hl
 614+ C40F B7                   or a
 615+ C410 C9                   ret
 616+ C411              .vtol:
 617+ C411 2A 24 AE             ld hl,(cam_x+1)
 618+ C414 22 45 C7             ld (gt_x),hl
 619+ C417 2A 27 AE             ld hl,(cam_z+1)
 620+ C41A 22 47 C7             ld (gt_z),hl
 621+ C41D 2A 29 AE             ld hl,(cam_y)
 622+ C420 22 49 C7             ld (gt_y),hl
 623+ C423 B7                   or a
 624+ C424 C9                   ret
 625+ C425 DD E5        .hum:   push ix
 626+ C427 CD C9 C3             call g_human
 627+ C42A DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
 628+ C42D FE 01                cp HS_WAIT
 629+ C42F 20 1F                jr nz,.gone
 630+ C431 DD 6E 00             ld l,(ix+0)
 631+ C434 DD 66 01             ld h,(ix+1)
 632+ C437 22 45 C7             ld (gt_x),hl
 633+ C43A DD 6E 02             ld l,(ix+2)
 634+ C43D DD 66 03             ld h,(ix+3)
 635+ C440 22 47 C7             ld (gt_z),hl
 636+ C443 DD 6E 04             ld l,(ix+4)
 637+ C446 DD 66 05             ld h,(ix+5)
 638+ C449 22 49 C7             ld (gt_y),hl
 639+ C44C DD E1                pop ix
 640+ C44E B7                   or a
 641+ C44F C9                   ret
 642+ C450 DD E1        .gone:  pop ix
 643+ C452 37                   scf
 644+ C453 C9                   ret
 645+ C454
 646+ C454              ; Carry set if ghost IX is within ATTACK_R of gt_x/z/y on each axis
 647+ C454 DD 6E 00     g_near: ld l,(ix+0)
 648+ C457 DD 66 01             ld h,(ix+1)
 649+ C45A ED 5B 45 C7          ld de,(gt_x)
 650+ C45E CD 7A C4             call g_close
 651+ C461 D0                   ret nc
 652+ C462 DD 6E 02             ld l,(ix+2)
 653+ C465 DD 66 03             ld h,(ix+3)
 654+ C468 ED 5B 47 C7          ld de,(gt_z)
 655+ C46C CD 7A C4             call g_close
 656+ C46F D0                   ret nc
 657+ C470 DD 6E 15             ld l,(ix+21)
 658+ C473 DD 66 16             ld h,(ix+22)
 659+ C476 ED 5B 49 C7          ld de,(gt_y)
 660+ C47A                      ; fall through
 661+ C47A              ; Carry set if |HL - DE| <= gp_range (the monster's reach)
 662+ C47A              g_close:
 663+ C47A B7                   or a
 664+ C47B ED 52                sbc hl,de
 665+ C47D CD E5 AD             call abs_hl
 666+ C480 ED 5B 2A C7          ld de,(gp_range)
 667+ C484 13                   inc de
 668+ C485 B7                   or a
 669+ C486 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 670+ C488 C9                   ret
 671+ C489
 672+ C489              ; A = a base tile's index (row * 5 + column of the 5x5 square round the
 673+ C489              ; pad) -> E, D = its cell
 674+ C489              g_basecell:
 675+ C489 16 1E                ld d,PAD_Z-TERRACE_R
 676+ C48B FE 05        .r:     cp 2*TERRACE_R+1
 677+ C48D 38 05                jr c,.c
 678+ C48F D6 05                sub 2*TERRACE_R+1
 679+ C491 14                   inc d
 680+ C492 18 F7                jr .r
 681+ C494 C6 1E        .c:     add a,PAD_X-TERRACE_R
 682+ C496 5F                   ld e,a
 683+ C497 C9                   ret
 684+ C498
 685+ C498              ; gf_x/z/y = the centre of base tile (E, D) and its top (its height now)
 686+ C498              g_tilepos:
 687+ C498 63                   ld h,e
 688+ C499 2E 80                ld l,128
 689+ C49B 22 4F C7             ld (gf_x),hl
 690+ C49E 62                   ld h,d
 691+ C49F 22 51 C7             ld (gf_z),hl
 692+ C4A2 6A                   ld l,d
 693+ C4A3 26 00                ld h,0
 694+ C4A5                      ROWOFS
 694+ C4A5 29          >        add hl,hl
 694+ C4A6 29          >        add hl,hl
 694+ C4A7 29          >        add hl,hl
 694+ C4A8 29          >        add hl,hl
 694+ C4A9 29          >        add hl,hl
 694+ C4AA 29          >        add hl,hl
 695+ C4AB 7B                   ld a,e
 696+ C4AC ED 31                add hl,a
 697+ C4AE 11 00 70             ld de,city_map
 698+ C4B1 19                   add hl,de
 699+ C4B2 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 700+ C4B3 CD AA AC             call bld_height
 701+ C4B6 22 53 C7             ld (gf_y),hl
 702+ C4B9 C9                   ret
 703+ C4BA
 704+ C4BA              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 705+ C4BA              ; Look for the nearest human (waiting) or the VTOL within GHOST_SEE of ghost
 706+ C4BA              ; IX on each axis that it can see (g_sight).  Carry set if found: +30 = the
 707+ C4BA              ; target, gt_x/z/y = where it is.
 708+ C4BA              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 709+ C4BA              g_find:
 710+ C4BA 21 FF FF             ld hl,$FFFF
 711+ C4BD 22 4B C7             ld (gf_best),hl                 ; nearest so far
 712+ C4C0 3E FE                ld a,TG_VTOL                    ; the VTOL
 713+ C4C2 32 4E C7             ld (gf_who),a
 714+ C4C5 2A 24 AE             ld hl,(cam_x+1)
 715+ C4C8 22 4F C7             ld (gf_x),hl
 716+ C4CB 2A 27 AE             ld hl,(cam_z+1)
 717+ C4CE 22 51 C7             ld (gf_z),hl
 718+ C4D1 2A 29 AE             ld hl,(cam_y)
 719+ C4D4 22 53 C7             ld (gf_y),hl
 720+ C4D7 CD 5C C5             call g_cand
 721+ C4DA DD E5                push ix                         ; the humans still waiting
 722+ C4DC FD 21 31 39          ld iy,humans
 723+ C4E0 06 14                ld b,HUMAN_MAX
 724+ C4E2 0E 00                ld c,0
 725+ C4E4 C5           .hl:    push bc
 726+ C4E5 FD 7E 06             ld a,(iy+6)
 727+ C4E8 FE 01                cp HS_WAIT
 728+ C4EA 20 28                jr nz,.hn
 729+ C4EC 79                   ld a,c
 730+ C4ED 32 4E C7             ld (gf_who),a
 731+ C4F0 FD 6E 00             ld l,(iy+0)
 732+ C4F3 FD 66 01             ld h,(iy+1)
 733+ C4F6 22 4F C7             ld (gf_x),hl
 734+ C4F9 FD 6E 02             ld l,(iy+2)
 735+ C4FC FD 66 03             ld h,(iy+3)
 736+ C4FF 22 51 C7             ld (gf_z),hl
 737+ C502 FD 6E 04             ld l,(iy+4)
 738+ C505 FD 66 05             ld h,(iy+5)
 739+ C508 22 53 C7             ld (gf_y),hl
 740+ C50B C1                   pop bc
 741+ C50C C5                   push bc
 742+ C50D FD E5                push iy
 743+ C50F CD 5C C5             call g_cand
 744+ C512 FD E1                pop iy
 745+ C514 11 0D 00     .hn:    ld de,HREC
 746+ C517 FD 19                add iy,de
 747+ C519 C1                   pop bc
 748+ C51A 0C                   inc c
 749+ C51B 10 C7                djnz .hl
 750+ C51D DD E1                pop ix
 751+ C51F 3A 2D C7             ld a,(gp_base)                  ; a banshee: the base tiles too
 752+ C522 B7                   or a
 753+ C523 C4 37 C5             call nz,g_find_base
 754+ C526 2A 4B C7             ld hl,(gf_best)
 755+ C529 7C                   ld a,h
 756+ C52A A5                   and l
 757+ C52B 3C                   inc a
 758+ C52C 37                   scf
 759+ C52D 3F                   ccf
 760+ C52E C8                   ret z                           ; nobody (carry clear)
 761+ C52F 3A 4D C7             ld a,(gf_tgt)
 762+ C532 DD 77 1E             ld (ix+30),a
 763+ C535 37                   scf
 764+ C536 C9                   ret
 765+ C537
 766+ C537              ; The base tiles still standing (base_hp) as candidates for ghost IX
 767+ C537              g_find_base:
 768+ C537 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; tile index
 769+ C539 C5           .lp:    push bc
 770+ C53A 79                   ld a,c
 771+ C53B 21 AC 2F             ld hl,base_hp
 772+ C53E ED 31                add hl,a
 773+ C540 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 774+ C541 B7                   or a
 775+ C542 28 10                jr z,.nx                        ; destroyed (road now)
 776+ C544 79                   ld a,c
 777+ C545 C6 C0                add a,TG_BASE
 778+ C547 32 4E C7             ld (gf_who),a
 779+ C54A 79                   ld a,c
 780+ C54B CD 89 C4             call g_basecell
 781+ C54E CD 98 C4             call g_tilepos                  ; gf_x/z/y = the candidate
 782+ C551 CD 5C C5             call g_cand
 783+ C554 C1           .nx:    pop bc
 784+ C555 0C                   inc c
 785+ C556 79                   ld a,c
 786+ C557 FE 19                cp BASE_TILES
 787+ C559 20 DE                jr nz,.lp
 788+ C55B C9                   ret
 789+ C55C
 790+ C55C              ; Candidate gf_who at gf_x/z/y for ghost IX: if within GHOST_SEE on each
 791+ C55C              ; axis, nearer than the best so far and in sight, it becomes the best
 792+ C55C              ; (gf_best = its distance, gf_tgt, gt_x/z/y).  Preserves IX.
 793+ C55C              g_cand:
 794+ C55C DD 6E 00             ld l,(ix+0)                     ; dx = target - ghost
 795+ C55F DD 66 01             ld h,(ix+1)
 796+ C562 EB                   ex de,hl
 797+ C563 2A 4F C7             ld hl,(gf_x)
 798+ C566 B7                   or a
 799+ C567 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 800+ C569 22 55 C7             ld (gf_dx),hl
 801+ C56C CD E5 AD             call abs_hl
 802+ C56F 11 01 03             ld de,GHOST_SEE+1
 803+ C572 B7                   or a
 804+ C573 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 805+ C575 D0                   ret nc                          ; too far
 806+ C576 DD 6E 02             ld l,(ix+2)
 807+ C579 DD 66 03             ld h,(ix+3)
 808+ C57C EB                   ex de,hl
 809+ C57D 2A 51 C7             ld hl,(gf_z)
 810+ C580 B7                   or a
 811+ C581 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 812+ C583 22 57 C7             ld (gf_dz),hl
 813+ C586 CD E5 AD             call abs_hl
 814+ C589 11 01 03             ld de,GHOST_SEE+1
 815+ C58C B7                   or a
 816+ C58D ED 52                sbc hl,de
 817+ C58F D0                   ret nc
 818+ C590 2A 55 C7             ld hl,(gf_dx)                   ; distance: max + min/2 of |dx|, |dz|
 819+ C593 CD E5 AD             call abs_hl
 820+ C596 EB                   ex de,hl
 821+ C597 2A 57 C7             ld hl,(gf_dz)
 822+ C59A CD E5 AD             call abs_hl
 823+ C59D B7                   or a
 824+ C59E ED 52                sbc hl,de
 825+ C5A0 19                   add hl,de
 826+ C5A1 30 01                jr nc,.mx
 827+ C5A3 EB                   ex de,hl                        ; HL = max, DE = min
 828+ C5A4 CB 3A        .mx:    srl d
 829+ C5A6 CB 1B                rr e
 830+ C5A8 19                   add hl,de
 831+ C5A9 22 59 C7             ld (gf_d),hl
 832+ C5AC ED 5B 4B C7          ld de,(gf_best)
 833+ C5B0 B7                   or a
 834+ C5B1 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 835+ C5B3 D0                   ret nc                          ; not nearer
 836+ C5B4 CD D7 C5             call g_sight
 837+ C5B7 D8                   ret c                           ; hidden behind a building
 838+ C5B8 2A 59 C7             ld hl,(gf_d)
 839+ C5BB 22 4B C7             ld (gf_best),hl
 840+ C5BE 3A 4E C7             ld a,(gf_who)
 841+ C5C1 32 4D C7             ld (gf_tgt),a
 842+ C5C4 2A 4F C7             ld hl,(gf_x)
 843+ C5C7 22 45 C7             ld (gt_x),hl
 844+ C5CA 2A 51 C7             ld hl,(gf_z)
 845+ C5CD 22 47 C7             ld (gt_z),hl
 846+ C5D0 2A 53 C7             ld hl,(gf_y)
 847+ C5D3 22 49 C7             ld (gt_y),hl
 848+ C5D6 C9                   ret
 849+ C5D7
 850+ C5D7              ; Can ghost IX see gf_x/z/y?  Carry set if a building is in the way: the
 851+ C5D7              ; line between them is sampled at 7 points (1/8 .. 7/8 of the way), each
 852+ C5D7              ; checked against the roof of the cell it is over (not the target's own
 853+ C5D7              ; cell).  Preserves IX.
 854+ C5D7              g_sight:
 855+ C5D7 2A 55 C7             ld hl,(gf_dx)                   ; steps: d / 8
 856+ C5DA CD 7B C6             call g_div8
 857+ C5DD 22 39 C7             ld (gs_dx),hl
 858+ C5E0 2A 57 C7             ld hl,(gf_dz)
 859+ C5E3 CD 7B C6             call g_div8
 860+ C5E6 22 3B C7             ld (gs_dz),hl
 861+ C5E9 DD 6E 15             ld l,(ix+21)                    ; dy = target - ghost
 862+ C5EC DD 66 16             ld h,(ix+22)
 863+ C5EF EB                   ex de,hl
 864+ C5F0 2A 53 C7             ld hl,(gf_y)
 865+ C5F3 B7                   or a
 866+ C5F4 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 867+ C5F6 CD 7B C6             call g_div8
 868+ C5F9 22 3D C7             ld (gs_dy),hl
 869+ C5FC DD 6E 00             ld l,(ix+0)                     ; from the ghost
 870+ C5FF DD 66 01             ld h,(ix+1)
 871+ C602 22 3F C7             ld (gs_px),hl
 872+ C605 DD 6E 02             ld l,(ix+2)
 873+ C608 DD 66 03             ld h,(ix+3)
 874+ C60B 22 41 C7             ld (gs_pz),hl
 875+ C60E DD 6E 15             ld l,(ix+21)
 876+ C611 DD 66 16             ld h,(ix+22)
 877+ C614 22 43 C7             ld (gs_py),hl
 878+ C617 06 07                ld b,7
 879+ C619 C5           .lp:    push bc
 880+ C61A 2A 3F C7             ld hl,(gs_px)
 881+ C61D ED 5B 39 C7          ld de,(gs_dx)
 882+ C621 19                   add hl,de
 883+ C622 22 3F C7             ld (gs_px),hl
 884+ C625 2A 41 C7             ld hl,(gs_pz)
 885+ C628 ED 5B 3B C7          ld de,(gs_dz)
 886+ C62C 19                   add hl,de
 887+ C62D 22 41 C7             ld (gs_pz),hl
 888+ C630 2A 43 C7             ld hl,(gs_py)
 889+ C633 ED 5B 3D C7          ld de,(gs_dy)
 890+ C637 19                   add hl,de
 891+ C638 22 43 C7             ld (gs_py),hl
 892+ C63B 3A 40 C7             ld a,(gs_px+1)                  ; over the target's own cell: not in
 893+ C63E 21 50 C7             ld hl,gf_x+1                    ; the way (a base tile's top would
 894+ C641 BE                   cp (hl)                         ; hide itself)
 895+ C642 20 0C                jr nz,.cell
 896+ C644 3A 42 C7             ld a,(gs_pz+1)
 897+ C647 21 52 C7             ld hl,gf_z+1
 898+ C64A BE                   cp (hl)
 899+ C64B 20 03                jr nz,.cell
 900+ C64D C1                   pop bc
 901+ C64E 18 25                jr .nx
 902+ C650 3A 42 C7     .cell:  ld a,(gs_pz+1)                  ; the cell under this point
 903+ C653 6F                   ld l,a
 904+ C654 26 00                ld h,0
 905+ C656                      ROWOFS
 905+ C656 29          >        add hl,hl
 905+ C657 29          >        add hl,hl
 905+ C658 29          >        add hl,hl
 905+ C659 29          >        add hl,hl
 905+ C65A 29          >        add hl,hl
 905+ C65B 29          >        add hl,hl
 906+ C65C 3A 40 C7             ld a,(gs_px+1)
 907+ C65F ED 31                add hl,a
 908+ C661 11 00 70             ld de,city_map
 909+ C664 19                   add hl,de
 910+ C665 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 911+ C666 CD AA AC             call bld_height
 912+ C669 ED 5B 43 C7          ld de,(gs_py)
 913+ C66D B7                   or a
 914+ C66E ED 52                sbc hl,de                       ; roof - line height
 915+ C670 C1                   pop bc
 916+ C671 28 02                jr z,.nx
 917+ C673 30 04                jr nc,.hid                      ; the roof is above the line
 918+ C675 10 A2        .nx:    djnz .lp
 919+ C677 B7                   or a                            ; in sight
 920+ C678 C9                   ret
 921+ C679 37           .hid:   scf
 922+ C67A C9                   ret
 923+ C67B
 924+ C67B              ; HL = HL / 8, signed
 925+ C67B CB 2C        g_div8: sra h
 926+ C67D CB 1D                rr l
 927+ C67F CB 2C                sra h
 928+ C681 CB 1D                rr l
 929+ C683 CB 2C                sra h
 930+ C685 CB 1D                rr l
 931+ C687 C9                   ret
 932+ C688
 933+ C688              ; Aim ghost IX at gt_x/z/y: velocity g_speed straight at it, climbing or
 934+ C688              ; descending towards its height
 935+ C688              g_aim:
 936+ C688 DD 6E 00             ld l,(ix+0)                     ; dx, dz = target - ghost
 937+ C68B DD 66 01             ld h,(ix+1)
 938+ C68E EB                   ex de,hl
 939+ C68F 2A 45 C7             ld hl,(gt_x)
 940+ C692 B7                   or a
 941+ C693 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 942+ C695 22 55 C7             ld (gf_dx),hl
 943+ C698 DD 6E 02             ld l,(ix+2)
 944+ C69B DD 66 03             ld h,(ix+3)
 945+ C69E EB                   ex de,hl
 946+ C69F 2A 47 C7             ld hl,(gt_z)
 947+ C6A2 B7                   or a
 948+ C6A3 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 949+ C6A5 22 57 C7             ld (gf_dz),hl
 950+ C6A8 CD E5 AD             call abs_hl                     ; distance ~ max + 3/8 min
 951+ C6AB EB                   ex de,hl
 952+ C6AC 2A 55 C7             ld hl,(gf_dx)
 953+ C6AF CD E5 AD             call abs_hl
 954+ C6B2 B7                   or a
 955+ C6B3 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 956+ C6B5 19                   add hl,de
 957+ C6B6 30 01                jr nc,.mx
 958+ C6B8 EB                   ex de,hl
 959+ C6B9 E5           .mx:    push hl
 960+ C6BA EB                   ex de,hl
 961+ C6BB 54                   ld d,h
 962+ C6BC 5D                   ld e,l
 963+ C6BD 29                   add hl,hl
 964+ C6BE 19                   add hl,de
 965+ C6BF CB 3C                srl h
 966+ C6C1 CB 1D                rr l
 967+ C6C3 CB 3C                srl h
 968+ C6C5 CB 1D                rr l
 969+ C6C7 CB 3C                srl h
 970+ C6C9 CB 1D                rr l                            ; 3/8 min
 971+ C6CB D1                   pop de
 972+ C6CC 19                   add hl,de
 973+ C6CD 11 50 00             ld de,GHOST_AIM_MIN             ; (so the factor stays <= 1.0)
 974+ C6D0 CD 2C C3             call g_max_de
 975+ C6D3 EB                   ex de,hl                        ; DE = distance
 976+ C6D4 2A 28 C7             ld hl,(g_speed)                 ; factor = speed * 32 / distance
 977+ C6D7 29                   add hl,hl                       ; (at most 1.0, even for a banshee)
 978+ C6D8 29                   add hl,hl
 979+ C6D9 29                   add hl,hl
 980+ C6DA 29                   add hl,hl
 981+ C6DB 29                   add hl,hl
 982+ C6DC CD 05 39             call udiv16                     ; (human.asm) HL = HL / DE
 983+ C6DF 22 5B C7             ld (ga_t),hl
 984+ C6E2 2A 55 C7             ld hl,(gf_dx)                   ; v = d * factor * 8 (8.8)
 985+ C6E5 ED 5B 5B C7          ld de,(ga_t)
 986+ C6E9 CD EA AD             call fmul
 987+ C6EC 29                   add hl,hl
 988+ C6ED 29                   add hl,hl
 989+ C6EE 29                   add hl,hl
 990+ C6EF DD 75 17             ld (ix+23),l
 991+ C6F2 DD 74 18             ld (ix+24),h
 992+ C6F5 2A 57 C7             ld hl,(gf_dz)
 993+ C6F8 ED 5B 5B C7          ld de,(ga_t)
 994+ C6FC CD EA AD             call fmul
 995+ C6FF 29                   add hl,hl
 996+ C700 29                   add hl,hl

# file opened: zombie.asm
   1+ 2000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ 2000              ; Z-POLICE - zombie page (8K page PAGE_ZOMBIE, mapped at $2000 in MMU slot 1)
   3+ 2000              ;
   4+ 2000              ; Placeholder zombies: Layer 2 stick-man silhouettes walking the streets.
   5+ 2000              ; The page stays mapped at $2000 the whole time (slot 0 is used for paging
   6+ 2000              ; Layer 2 sections, slot 7 for the other code pages), so the city page can
   7+ 2000              ; call in here while it draws.
   8+ 2000              ;
   9+ 2000              ; Each zombie is a ZREC-byte record:
  10+ 2000              ;   +0,+1  x  8.8 fixed point in cell units (high byte = cell, low = fraction;
  11+ 2000              ;             the same as world units, one cell = 256)
  12+ 2000              ;   +2,+3  z  likewise
  13+ 2000              ;   +4     heading 0 = +z, 1 = +x, 2 = -z, 3 = -x  (yaw angle = heading*64;
  14+ 2000              ;             heading+1 turns right, heading+3 turns left)
  15+ 2000              ;   +5     walk animation phase 0..3
  16+ 2000              ;   +6     step accumulator: fraction of a world unit (4 bits)
  17+ 2000              ;   +7     wait: frames left standing still (0 = walking)
  18+ 2000              ;   +8,+9  cell (x, z) of the last turn decision ($FF = none)
  19+ 2000              ;   +10    active: 1 = in play, 0 = gone (not moved, linked or drawn)
  20+ 2000              ;   +11    frames until this zombie's next decision check
  21+ 2000              ;   +12,+13  cell (x, z) of its blip on the street map
  22+ 2000              ;   +14    1 = blip plotted
  23+ 2000              ;   +15    type: 0 = zombie, 1 = fast zombie (walks at twice the speed),
  24+ 2000              ;          ZT_GHOUL (a special: 5x, half height), ZT_GHOST, ZT_BANSHEE
  25+ 2000              ;   +16    mode: 0 = walking, 1 = going for a base tile, 2 = attacking it
  26+ 2000              ;   +17    frames to its next hit on the tile
  27+ 2000              ;   +18    hit points left (ZHP, ZHP_FAST; weapons.asm zdamage)
  28+ 2000              ;   +19,+20  cell (x, z) marked with CELL_ZOMBIE in city_map ($FF = none)
  29+ 2000              ;   +21,+22  height of its feet (0 for zombies; special monsters fly)
  30+ 2000              ;   +23..+30 special monsters' own fields (specials.asm)
  31+ 2000              ; Records 0..NZOMB-1 are the zombies, NZOMB..NMON-1 the special monsters
  32+ 2000              ; (specials.asm), which share the drawing, cell marks, map blips and weapon
  33+ 2000              ; hits; their type (+15) says which.
  34+ 2000              ; zatk_flags (one byte per zombie, by index): TRUE while it attacks a base
  35+ 2000              ; tile, when its map dot is red; FALSE again once the tile is destroyed.
  36+ 2000              ;
  37+ 2000              ; The base: at junctions a zombie leans towards the road that points at the
  38+ 2000              ; landing pad (zbias_t, from the level).  A base tile (terrace or pad) in its
  39+ 2000              ; way isn't avoided: it walks up to ATTACK_R from it and hits it every
  40+ 2000              ; ZHIT_FRAMES frames.  A destroyed terrace tile becomes road and the zombie
  41+ 2000              ; walks on; the pad destroyed sets game_over.
  42+ 2000              ;
  43+ 2000              ; Street map blips: zblip / zblip_off (map.asm) are called on the zombie's
  44+ 2000              ; decision-check frames.  Call zblip_off for a zombie before making it gone.
  45+ 2000              ;
  46+ 2000              ; Decisions are staggered: each active zombie only checks for junctions and
  47+ 2000              ; obstacles once every zsteps frames, with the zombies' check frames spread
  48+ 2000              ; out so only about NZOMB/zsteps of them check on any one frame.  In between
  49+ 2000              ; they just walk, so the look-ahead (zlook) covers zsteps frames of walking
  50+ 2000              ; plus a margin: ZMARGIN + zsteps*zspeed/16.
  51+ 2000              ;
  52+ 2000              ; Movement (zmove), every frame:
  53+ 2000              ;   * Standing still: count the wait down and do nothing else.
  54+ 2000              ;   * Not this zombie's check frame: step forwards (see the last point).
  55+ 2000              ;   * Junctions: once per cell, when the zombie is 0.25-0.75 of the way
  56+ 2000              ;     through the cell along its heading, look for a road (street cell) to
  57+ 2000              ;     the left and to the right.  Left is tested first: if there is a road,
  58+ 2000              ;     turn left at random (1 in 2); if not taken, the same for the right.
  59+ 2000              ;     The cell is then marked as decided, so it isn't tested again until the
  60+ 2000              ;     zombie has been somewhere else.
  61+ 2000              ;   * Blocked (building or map edge within zlook ahead): turn left if there
  62+ 2000              ;     is a road there, else right; otherwise turn round and stand still for
  63+ 2000              ;     ZWAIT frames before testing anything again.
  64+ 2000              ;   * Otherwise step forwards zspeed/16 world units (the remainder is kept,
  65+ 2000              ;     so slow speeds still move smoothly on average).
  66+ 2000              ;
  67+ 2000              ; Zombies are only ever on street (empty) cells.  They are drawn by the city
  68+ 2000              ; traversal when it reaches their cell (draw_cell jumps to zombie_cell for
  69+ 2000              ; empty cells), so the painter's algorithm hides them behind nearer buildings.
  70+ 2000              ; A cell holding zombies has bit 7 (CELL_ZOMBIE) of its city_map byte set,
  71+ 2000              ; by zflags every frame; zombie_cell then searches the records for them.
  72+ 2000              ;
  73+ 2000              ; Sprites (tables_zombie.inc, generated): 8 scales from 1x4 to 8x16 pixels,
  74+ 2000              ; forward and left facings, 4 walk frames.  Rear and right are the mirrored
  75+ 2000              ; forward and left.  One byte per row, bit 7 = leftmost pixel.
  76+ 2000              ;==============================================================================
  77+ 2000
  78+ 2000              NZOMB           equ 30          ; zombie records: the most in a wave
  79+ 2000              NSPEC           equ 8           ; most special monsters (5 ghosts, banshees)
  80+ 2000              NMON            equ NZOMB+NSPEC ; records: zombies, then special monsters
  81+ 2000              ZREC            equ 31          ; bytes per record
  82+ 2000              ZT_FAST         equ 1           ; +15: fast zombie
  83+ 2000              ZT_GHOUL        equ 2           ; +15: a ghoul: a special monster that walks
  84+ 2000                                              ; as a zombie (zmove), half height, 5x speed
  85+ 2000              ZT_GHOST        equ 3           ; +15: a ghost (specials.asm); types from
  86+ 2000              ZT_BANSHEE      equ 4           ; here on fly (specials.asm moves them)
  87+ 2000              ZHP             equ 5           ; hit points: a zombie (level 1)
  88+ 2000              ZHP_FAST        equ 4           ; and a fast zombie
  89+ 2000              GHOUL_HP        equ ZHP         ; and a ghoul
  90+ 2000              ZSPEED_INIT     equ 10          ; starting zspeed: 0.63 units (4.9 cm) per
  91+ 2000                                              ; frame, ~0.7 m/s at 14 fps
  92+ 2000              ZSTEPS_INIT     equ 10          ; starting zsteps: decide every 10 frames
  93+ 2000              ZMARGIN         equ 16          ; keep at least this far from walls: 1.25 m
  94+ 2000              ZWAIT           equ 100         ; frames to stand still when stuck
  95+ 2000              ZOMBIE_H        equ 26          ; height: 2 m (the same as the hit box, ZHIT_H)
  96+ 2000              GHOUL_H         equ ZOMBIE_H/2  ; a ghoul's height: 1 m
  97+ 2000                      ASSERT ZSPEED_INIT*5+15 < 256   ; a ghoul's step (zcalc_step) fits
  98+ 2000
  99+ 2000                      INCLUDE "tables_zombie.inc"     ; sprites, sizes, bitrev
# file opened: tables_zombie.inc
